const fs = require("fs");

const WASM = "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";
const bytes = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

const TARGET = 165;
const IMPORTED = 83;

function uleb(p) {
    let v = 0, s = 0;
    while (true) {
        const b = bytes[p++];
        v += (b & 127) * 2 ** s;
        if (!(b & 128)) return { value: v, next: p };
        s += 7;
    }
}

function readCode() {
    let p = 8;

    while (p < bytes.length) {
        const id = bytes[p++];
        const z = uleb(p);
        const start = z.next;
        const end = start + z.value;

        if (id === 10) return { start, end };

        p = end;
    }

    throw new Error("CODE SECTION NOT FOUND");
}

const code = readCode();

let p = code.start;
const n = uleb(p);
p = n.next;

let targetBody = null;

for (let i = 0; i < n.value; i++) {
    const z = uleb(p);
    const start = z.next;
    const end = start + z.value;

    if (IMPORTED + i === TARGET) {
        targetBody = { start, end, size: z.value };
        break;
    }

    p = end;
}

if (!targetBody) throw new Error("FUNC 165 NOT FOUND");

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FUNC 165 RETURN ANALYSIS");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("");
console.log("BODY :", "0x" + targetBody.start.toString(16),
    "..", "0x" + targetBody.end.toString(16));
console.log("SIZE :", targetBody.size);
console.log("");

const s = targetBody.start;
const e = targetBody.end;

console.log("LAST 32 BYTES OF BODY:");
console.log("");

for (let a = Math.max(s, e - 32); a < e; a++) {
    console.log(
        "0x" + a.toString(16).padStart(8, "0"),
        "0x" + bytes[a].toString(16).padStart(2, "0")
    );
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("CALL 165 SITE IN FUNC 9368");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("");

const site = 0x00320859;

for (let a = site - 12; a <= site + 12; a++) {
    console.log(
        "0x" + a.toString(16).padStart(8, "0"),
        "0x" + bytes[a].toString(16).padStart(2, "0")
    );
}

console.log("");
console.log("READ ONLY : YES");
console.log("NETWORK : NO");
console.log("TRANSACTIONS : NO");

FILE: scripts\analyzeFunc83Blocks.ts

import fs from "fs";

const wasmPath = "emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";
const targetFunc = 83;

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(wasmPath);

function readULEB(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = wasm[pos++];
        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) break;
        shift += 7;
    }

    return { value, next: pos };
}

function u32(pos: number): number {
    return (
        wasm[pos] |
        (wasm[pos + 1] << 8) |
        (wasm[pos + 2] << 16) |
        (wasm[pos + 3] << 24)
    ) >>> 0;
}

function hex(n: number, width = 8): string {
    return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(width, "0");
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FUNC 83 BASIC-BLOCK / CONTROL-FLOW ANALYSIS");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

console.log(`TARGET GLOBAL FUNC = ${targetFunc}`);

let pos = 8;

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Find sections
// ------------------------------------------------------------

let codeSectionStart = -1;
let codeSectionEnd = -1;
let codeSectionPayload = -1;

while (pos < wasm.length) {
    const id = wasm[pos++];
    const sizeInfo = readULEB(pos);
    const size = sizeInfo.value;
    pos = sizeInfo.next;

    const payload = pos;
    const end = payload + size;

    if (id === 10) {
        codeSectionStart = payload;
        codeSectionPayload = payload;
        codeSectionEnd = end;
        break;
    }

    pos = end;
}

if (codeSectionPayload < 0) {
    throw new Error("CODE SECTION NOT FOUND");
}

console.log(`CODE SECTION = ${hex(codeSectionStart)} .. ${hex(codeSectionEnd)}`);

let p = codeSectionPayload;

const countInfo = readULEB(p);
const functionCount = countInfo.value;
p = countInfo.next;

console.log(`DEFINED FUNCTIONS = ${functionCount}`);

let func83Start = -1;
let func83End = -1;

for (let ordinal = 0; ordinal < functionCount; ordinal++) {
    const bodySizeInfo = readULEB(p);
    const bodySize = bodySizeInfo.value;
    const bodySizeStart = p;

    p = bodySizeInfo.next;

    const bodyStart = p;
    const bodyEnd = bodyStart + bodySize;

    if (ordinal === targetFunc - 83) {
        func83Start = bodyStart;
        func83End = bodyEnd;
        console.log(`CODE ORDINAL = ${ordinal}`);
        console.log(`BODY        = ${hex(bodyStart)} .. ${hex(bodyEnd)}`);
        console.log(`SIZE        = ${bodySize}`);
        break;
    }

    p = bodyEnd;
}

if (func83Start < 0) {
    throw new Error("FUNC 83 BODY NOT FOUND");
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Read locals
// ------------------------------------------------------------

let q = func83Start;

const localDeclInfo = readULEB(q);
const localDeclCount = localDeclInfo.value;
q = localDeclInfo.next;

console.log(`LOCAL DECL GROUPS = ${localDeclCount}`);

let totalLocals = 0;

for (let i = 0; i < localDeclCount; i++) {
    const countInfo = readULEB(q);
    const count = countInfo.value;
    q = countInfo.next;

    const type = wasm[q++];

    totalLocals += count;

    console.log(
        `  LOCAL GROUP ${i}: COUNT=${count} TYPE=0x${type.toString(16)}`
    );
}

console.log(`TOTAL EXTRA LOCALS = ${totalLocals}`);
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Basic block discovery
// ------------------------------------------------------------

const blockStarts = new Set<number>();
const controlStack: { kind: string; start: number }[] = [];

blockStarts.add(q);

function addTarget(addr: number) {
    if (addr >= q && addr < func83End) {
        blockStarts.add(addr);
    }
}

for (let x = q; x < func83End;) {
    const opPos = x;
    const op = wasm[x++];

    switch (op) {

        // unreachable
        case 0x00:
            break;

        // nop
        case 0x01:
            break;

        // block / loop / if
        case 0x02:
        case 0x03:
        case 0x04: {
            controlStack.push({
                kind:
                    op === 0x02
                        ? "block"
                        : op === 0x03
                            ? "loop"
                            : "if",
                start: opPos
            });

            // blocktype
            x++;
            blockStarts.add(x);
            break;
        }

        // else
        case 0x05:
            blockStarts.add(x);
            break;

        // end
        case 0x0b:
            controlStack.pop();
            blockStarts.add(x);
            break;

        // br
        case 0x0c: {
            const r = readULEB(x);
            x = r.next;
            blockStarts.add(x);
            break;
        }

        // br_if
        case 0x0d: {
            const r = readULEB(x);
            x = r.next;
            blockStarts.add(x);
            break;
        }

        // br_table
        case 0x0e: {
            const r = readULEB(x);
            x = r.next;

            for (let i = 0; i < r.value; i++) {
                const t = readULEB(x);
                x = t.next;
            }

            const d = readULEB(x);
            x = d.next;

            blockStarts.add(x);
            break;
        }

        // return
        case 0x0f:
            blockStarts.add(x);
            break;

        // call
        case 0x10: {
            const r = readULEB(x);
            x = r.next;
            break;
        }

        // call_indirect
        case 0x11: {
            const r = readULEB(x);
            x = r.next;
            x++; // reserved/table byte
            break;
        }

        // local/global indices
        case 0x20:
        case 0x21:
        case 0x22:
        case 0x23:
        case 0x24: {
            const r = readULEB(x);
            x = r.next;
            break;
        }

        // memory loads/stores
        case 0x28:
        case 0x29:
        case 0x2a:
        case 0x2b:
        case 0x2c:
        case 0x2d:
        case 0x2e:
        case 0x2f:
        case 0x30:
        case 0x31:
        case 0x32:
        case 0x33:
        case 0x34:
        case 0x35:
        case 0x36:
        case 0x37:
        case 0x38:
        case 0x39:
        case 0x3a:
        case 0x3b:
        case 0x3c:
        case 0x3d:
        case 0x3e: {
            const align = readULEB(x);
            x = align.next;

            const offset = readULEB(x);
            x = offset.next;

            break;
        }

        // memory.size / grow
        case 0x3f:
        case 0x40:
            x++;
            break;

        // i32.const
        case 0x41: {
            const r = readULEB(x);
            x = r.next;
            break;
        }

        // i64.const
        case 0x42: {
            while (true) {
                const b = wasm[x++];
                if ((b & 0x80) === 0) break;
            }
            break;
        }

        // f32.const
        case 0x43:
            x += 4;
            break;

        // f64.const
        case 0x44:
            x += 8;
            break;

        // memory prefix
        case 0xfc: {
            const sub = readULEB(x);
            x = sub.next;

            // Most bulk-memory instructions have additional immediates.
            if (
                sub.value === 8 ||
                sub.value === 9 ||
                sub.value === 10 ||
                sub.value === 11
            ) {
                const a = readULEB(x);
                x = a.next;

                const b = readULEB(x);
                x = b.next;
            }

            break;
        }

        default:
            break;
    }

    // conditional/unconditional branch creates a new successor
    if (op === 0x0c || op === 0x0d || op === 0x0e || op === 0x0f) {
        if (x < func83End) blockStarts.add(x);
    }
}

const starts = [...blockStarts].sort((a, b) => a - b);

console.log(`BASIC BLOCK CANDIDATES = ${starts.length}`);
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

for (let i = 0; i < starts.length; i++) {
    const start = starts[i];
    const end = i + 1 < starts.length
        ? starts[i + 1]
        : func83End;

    console.log(
        `BLOCK ${i.toString().padStart(3, "0")}  ` +
        `${hex(start)} .. ${hex(end)}  SIZE=${end - start}`
    );
}

console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Interesting instruction scan
// ------------------------------------------------------------

console.log("INTERESTING CONTROL / MEMORY OPERATIONS");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

for (let x = q; x < func83End;) {
    const addr = x;
    const op = wasm[x++];

    let name = "";

    switch (op) {
        case 0x02: name = "block"; break;
        case 0x03: name = "loop"; break;
        case 0x04: name = "if"; break;
        case 0x05: name = "else"; break;
        case 0x0b: name = "end"; break;
        case 0x0c: name = "br"; break;
        case 0x0d: name = "br_if"; break;
        case 0x0e: name = "br_table"; break;
        case 0x0f: name = "return"; break;
        case 0x10: name = "call"; break;
        case 0x11: name = "call_indirect"; break;
        case 0x20: name = "local.get"; break;
        case 0x21: name = "local.set"; break;
        case 0x22: name = "local.tee"; break;
        case 0x23: name = "global.get"; break;
        case 0x24: name = "global.set"; break;
        case 0x28: name = "i32.load"; break;
        case 0x36: name = "i32.store"; break;
        case 0x37: name = "i64.store"; break;
        case 0x41: name = "i32.const"; break;
        case 0x42: name = "i64.const"; break;
        case 0x6a: name = "i32.add"; break;
        case 0x6b: name = "i32.sub"; break;
        case 0x71: name = "i32.and"; break;
        case 0x72: name = "i32.or"; break;
        case 0x73: name = "i32.xor"; break;
        case 0x74: name = "i32.shl"; break;
        case 0x75: name = "i32.shr_s"; break;
        case 0x76: name = "i32.shr_u"; break;
        case 0x77: name = "i32.rotl"; break;
    }

    if (name) {
        console.log(`${hex(addr)}  ${name}`);
    }

    switch (op) {
        case 0x20:
        case 0x21:
        case 0x22:
        case 0x23:
        case 0x24: {
            const r = readULEB(x);
            x = r.next;
            break;
        }

        case 0x10:
        case 0x0c:
        case 0x0d: {
            const r = readULEB(x);
            x = r.next;
            break;
        }

        case 0x11: {
            const r = readULEB(x);
            x = r.next + 1;
            break;
        }

        case 0x28:
        case 0x36:
        case 0x37: {
            const a = readULEB(x);
            x = a.next;

            const o = readULEB(x);
            x = o.next;
            break;
        }

        case 0x41: {
            const r = readULEB(x);
            x = r.next;
            break;
        }

        case 0x42:
            while ((wasm[x++] & 0x80) !== 0) {}
            break;

        case 0x43:
            x += 4;
            break;

        case 0x44:
            x += 8;
            break;
    }
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("END");
console.log("NO WASM EXECUTION");
console.log("============================================================");

FILE: scripts\analyzeFunc83Memory.mjs

import fs from "fs";

const b = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

const START = 0x6e81;
const END   = 0x74a6;

function uleb(p) {
  let v = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (true) {
    const x = b[p++];
    v += (x & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;

    if (!(x & 0x80))
      return { value: v >>> 0, next: p };

    shift += 7;
  }
}

function sleb(p) {
  let v = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (true) {
    const x = b[p++];
    v += (x & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;

    if (!(x & 0x80)) {
      if (shift < 32 && (x & 0x40))
        v -= 2 ** (shift + 7);

      return { value: v, next: p };
    }

    shift += 7;
  }
}

function hex(v) {
  return "0x" + v.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

let p = START;

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Skip local declarations
// ------------------------------------------------------------

const groups = uleb(p);
p = groups.next;

for (let i = 0; i < groups.value; i++) {
  const n = uleb(p);
  p = n.next;
  p++;
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Track simple constant stack values
// ------------------------------------------------------------

const stack = [];

const loads = [];
const stores = [];
const globals = [];
const constants = [];
const branches = [];

function pop() {
  return stack.length ? stack.pop() : null;
}

function push(x) {
  stack.push(x);
}

while (p < END) {
  const at = p;
  const op = b[p++];

  // ----------------------------------------------------------
  // Constants
  // ----------------------------------------------------------

  if (op === 0x41) {
    const x = sleb(p);
    p = x.next;

    push(x.value);

    constants.push({
      at,
      kind: "i32.const",
      value: x.value
    });

    continue;
  }

  if (op === 0x42) {
    const x = sleb(p);
    p = x.next;

    push(x.value);

    constants.push({
      at,
      kind: "i64.const",
      value: x.value
    });

    continue;
  }

  // ----------------------------------------------------------
  // locals
  // ----------------------------------------------------------

  if (op === 0x20) {
    const x = uleb(p);
    p = x.next;

    push(`L${x.value}`);
    continue;
  }

  if (op === 0x21) {
    const x = uleb(p);
    p = x.next;

    const v = pop();

    continue;
  }

  if (op === 0x22) {
    const x = uleb(p);
    p = x.next;

    const v = stack.length ? stack[stack.length - 1] : null;

    push(`L${x.value}`);

    continue;
  }

  // ----------------------------------------------------------
  // globals
  // ----------------------------------------------------------

  if (op === 0x23) {
    const x = uleb(p);
    p = x.next;

    push(`G${x.value}`);

    globals.push({
      at,
      op: "global.get",
      index: x.value
    });

    continue;
  }

  if (op === 0x24) {
    const x = uleb(p);
    p = x.next;

    const v = pop();

    globals.push({
      at,
      op: "global.set",
      index: x.value,
      value: v
    });

    continue;
  }

  // ----------------------------------------------------------
  // memory load/store
  // ----------------------------------------------------------

  if (op >= 0x28 && op <= 0x35) {
    const align = uleb(p);
    p = align.next;

    const offset = uleb(p);
    p = offset.next;

    const address = stack.length
      ? stack[stack.length - 1]
      : null;

    const value = pop();

    loads.push({
      at,
      opcode: op,
      offset: offset.value,
      address
    });

    // load result
    push(`LOAD(${address ?? "?"}+${hex(offset.value)})`);

    continue;
  }

  if (op >= 0x36 && op <= 0x3e) {
    const align = uleb(p);
    p = align.next;

    const offset = uleb(p);
    p = offset.next;

    const value = pop();
    const address = pop();

    stores.push({
      at,
      opcode: op,
      offset: offset.value,
      address,
      value
    });

    continue;
  }

  // ----------------------------------------------------------
  // Branches
  // ----------------------------------------------------------

  if (op === 0x0c || op === 0x0d) {
    const x = uleb(p);
    p = x.next;

    branches.push({
      at,
      opcode: op,
      target: x.value
    });

    continue;
  }

  if (op === 0x0e) {
    const n = uleb(p);
    p = n.next;

    const targets = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < n.value + 1; i++) {
      const x = uleb(p);
      p = x.next;
      targets.push(x.value);
    }

    branches.push({
      at,
      opcode: op,
      targets
    });

    continue;
  }

  // ----------------------------------------------------------
  // Memory.size/grow
  // ----------------------------------------------------------

  if (op === 0x3f || op === 0x40) {
    p++;
    continue;
  }

  // ----------------------------------------------------------
  // Comparisons / arithmetic
  // ----------------------------------------------------------

  if (
    (op >= 0x45 && op <= 0x4f) ||
    (op >= 0x50 && op <= 0x5b) ||
    (op >= 0x67 && op <= 0x78)
  ) {
    pop();
    pop();
    push("EXPR");
    continue;
  }

  // ----------------------------------------------------------
  // block / loop / if
  // ----------------------------------------------------------

  if (op === 0x02 || op === 0x03 || op === 0x04) {
    p++;
    continue;
  }

  // ----------------------------------------------------------
  // drop
  // ----------------------------------------------------------

  if (op === 0x1a) {
    pop();
    continue;
  }

  // ----------------------------------------------------------
  // select
  // ----------------------------------------------------------

  if (op === 0x1b) {
    pop();
    pop();
    pop();
    push("SELECT");
    continue;
  }

  // ----------------------------------------------------------
  // Misc stack operators
  // ----------------------------------------------------------

  if (op === 0x0f || op === 0x0b) {
    continue;
  }

  // For unhandled instructions, don't attempt dangerous
  // immediate parsing. We only need the access inventory.
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Output
// ------------------------------------------------------------

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FUNC 83 MEMORY / GLOBAL ANALYSIS");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`BODY : ${hex(START)} .. ${hex(END)}`);
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

console.log("CONSTANTS:");

for (const x of constants) {
  if (
    x.value === 0x195348 ||
    x.value === 0x19534c ||
    x.value === 0x195350 ||
    x.value === 0x195354 ||
    x.value === 0x195358 ||
    x.value === 0x19535c ||
    x.value === 0x195360
  ) {
    console.log(
      `  ${hex(x.at)}  ${x.kind} ${hex(x.value >>> 0)}`
    );
  }
}

console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
console.log("MEMORY ACCESSES:");

for (const x of loads) {
  console.log(
    `  LOAD  ${hex(x.at)}  opcode=0x${x.opcode.toString(16)}` +
    ` offset=${x.offset} base=${x.address ?? "?"}`
  );
}

for (const x of stores) {
  console.log(
    `  STORE ${hex(x.at)}  opcode=0x${x.opcode.toString(16)}` +
    ` offset=${x.offset} base=${x.address ?? "?"}`
  );
}

console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
console.log("GLOBALS:");

for (const x of globals) {
  console.log(
    `  ${hex(x.at)}  ${x.op} ${x.index}` +
    (x.value !== undefined ? ` value=${x.value}` : "")
  );
}

console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
console.log("BRANCHES:");

for (const x of branches) {
  if (x.targets)
    console.log(`  ${hex(x.at)}  br_table -> ${x.targets.join(",")}`);
  else
    console.log(`  ${hex(x.at)}  opcode=0x${x.opcode.toString(16)} target=${x.target}`);
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("DONE");
console.log("============================================================");

FILE: scripts\analyzeFunction3550LimbsV55.ts

import fs from "fs";

const path =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const b = fs.readFileSync(path);

const START = 0x001243d3;
const END   = 0x00124729;

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" FUNCTION 3550 LIMB ANALYSIS v55");
console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

console.log(`WASM size : ${b.length.toLocaleString()}`);
console.log(`Range     : 0x${START.toString(16)} .. 0x${END.toString(16)}`);
console.log("");

function hex(n: number, width = 16): string {
    return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(width, "0");
}

function readU32(p: number): number {
    return (
        b[p] |
        (b[p + 1] << 8) |
        (b[p + 2] << 16) |
        (b[p + 3] << 24)
    ) >>> 0;
}

function readULEB(p: number): { value: number; next: number } {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const x = b[p++];
        value |= (x & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((x & 0x80) === 0) {
            return { value: value >>> 0, next: p };
        }

        shift += 7;
    }
}

function readSLEB(p: number): { value: bigint; next: number } {
    let result = 0n;
    let shift = 0n;
    let byte = 0;

    while (true) {
        byte = b[p++];

        result |=
            BigInt(byte & 0x7f) << shift;

        shift += 7n;

        if ((byte & 0x80) === 0) {
            break;
        }
    }

    if (
        shift < 64n &&
        (byte & 0x40)
    ) {
        result |=
            (-1n) << shift;
    }

    return {
        value: result,
        next: p
    };
}

console.log("============================================================");

for (let p = START; p < END; p++) {
    if (b[p] === 0x42) {
        const r = readSLEB(p + 1);
        const key = r.value.toString();

        constants.set(
            key,
            (constants.get(key) ?? 0) + 1
        );
    }
}

for (const [value, count] of constants) {
    let n: bigint;

    try {
        n = BigInt(value);
    } catch {
        continue;
    }

    console.log(
        `i64.const ${value.padStart(22)}  ` +
        `hex=${n >= 0n ? hex(Number(n)) : "negative"}  ` +
        `count=${count}`
    );
}

import * as fs from "fs";

const file =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const b = fs.readFileSync(file);

const CODE_START = 0x00006e7d;
const CODE_END   = 0x00324ef5;

const TARGET_SLOT = 7697;

const CANDIDATES = [
  0x002bacae,
  0x002bbfa9,
  0x002bf7e6,
  0x002ced71,
  0x0030211a,
  0x0030229c,
];

function uleb(p: number) {
  let v = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (true) {
    const x = b[p++];

    v += (x & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;

    if (!(x & 0x80))
      return { v, p };

    shift += 7;
  }
}

function hex(n: number) {
  return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

/*
   Build function-body ranges.

   There are 83 imported functions.
   Therefore:

      global function = body ordinal + 83
*/

function getCodeBodies() {

  let p = CODE_START;

  const bodyCountR = uleb(p);
  const bodyCount = bodyCountR.v;
  p = bodyCountR.p;

  const bodies: {
    ordinal: number;
    global: number;
    start: number;
    end: number;
  }[] = [];

  for (let ordinal = 0; ordinal < bodyCount; ordinal++) {

    const sizeR = uleb(p);
    p = sizeR.p;

    const start = p;
    const end = p + sizeR.v;

    bodies.push({
      ordinal,
      global: ordinal + 83,
      start,
      end,
    });

    p = end;
  }

  return bodies;
}

const bodies = getCodeBodies();

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FUNCTION 5079 DISPATCH ANALYSIS");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

console.log("");
console.log("TARGET");
console.log(`  Function       : 5079`);
console.log(`  Element index  : 7696`);
console.log(`  Table slot     : ${TARGET_SLOT}`);
console.log("");

console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
console.log("CALL_INDIRECT CANDIDATE FUNCTIONS");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

for (const addr of CANDIDATES) {

  const body = bodies.find(
    x => addr >= x.start && addr < x.end
  );

  if (!body) {
    console.log(`${hex(addr)}  NOT FOUND`);
    continue;
  }

  console.log(
    `${hex(addr)}  body=${body.ordinal}  globalFunction=${body.global}`
  );

  console.log(
    `  body range : ${hex(body.start)} .. ${hex(body.end)}`
  );
}

console.log("");

console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
console.log("ELEMENT TABLE AROUND SLOT 7697");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

let elementStart = -1;
let elementEnd = -1;

let p = 8;

while (p < b.length) {

  const id = b[p++];

  const sizeR = uleb(p);
  p = sizeR.p;

  const start = p;
  const end = p + sizeR.v;

  if (id === 9) {
    elementStart = start;
    elementEnd = end;
    break;
  }

  p = end;
}

if (elementStart < 0)
  throw new Error("Element section not found");

p = elementStart;

const segCountR = uleb(p);
p = segCountR.p;

for (let seg = 0; seg < segCountR.v; seg++) {

  const flagsR = uleb(p);
  const flags = flagsR.v;
  p = flagsR.p;

  if (flags !== 0)
    throw new Error(`Unexpected element flags ${flags}`);

  const offsetOpcode = b[p++];

  if (offsetOpcode !== 0x41)
    throw new Error("Unexpected offset expression");

  const offsetR = uleb(p);
  const tableOffset = offsetR.v;
  p = offsetR.p;

  if (b[p] !== 0x0b)
    throw new Error("Missing end of offset expression");

  p++;

  const countR = uleb(p);
  const count = countR.v;
  p = countR.p;

  const firstElement = TARGET_SLOT - tableOffset;

  const fromIndex = Math.max(0, firstElement - 12);
  const toIndex = Math.min(count - 1, firstElement + 12);

  console.log(
    `Segment #${seg}  table offset=${tableOffset}  count=${count}`
  );

  console.log("");

  for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {

    const valuePos = p;

    const valueR = uleb(p);
    const fn = valueR.v;
    p = valueR.p;

    if (i < fromIndex || i > toIndex)
      continue;

    const tableSlot = tableOffset + i;

    console.log(
      `element=${i.toString().padStart(5)}  ` +
      `slot=${tableSlot.toString().padStart(5)}  ` +
      `function=${fn.toString().padStart(5)}  ` +
      `@${hex(valuePos)}`
    );
  }

  console.log("");
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("END");
console.log("============================================================");

FILE: scripts\analyzeFunction96Input.js

const fs = require("fs");

const wasmPath =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const bytes = fs.readFileSync(wasmPath);

const START = 0x00008111;
const END   = 0x00008174;

function uleb(p) {
  let v = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (true) {
    const b = bytes[p++];

    v += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;

    if (!(b & 0x80))
      return { value: v, next: p };

    shift += 7;
  }
}

function sleb(p) {
  let v = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (true) {
    const b = bytes[p++];

    v |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
    shift += 7;

    if (!(b & 0x80)) {

      if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40))
        v |= (~0 << shift);

      return {
        value: v | 0,
        next: p
      };
    }
  }
}

function hex(n) {
  return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function main() {

  let p = START;

  console.log("============================================================");
  console.log("FUNCTION 96 INPUT / LOCAL1 ANALYSIS");
  console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
  console.log("============================================================");
  console.log(`RANGE : ${hex(START)} .. ${hex(END)}`);
  console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

  let step = 0;

  while (p < END) {

    const at = p;
    const op = bytes[p++];

    step++;

    if (op === 0x20) {

      const x = uleb(p);
      p = x.next;

      console.log(
        `${hex(at)}  local.get ${x.value}`
      );
    }

    else if (op === 0x21) {

      const x = uleb(p);
      p = x.next;

      console.log(
        `${hex(at)}  local.set ${x.value}`
      );
    }

    else if (op === 0x22) {

      const x = uleb(p);
      p = x.next;

      console.log(
        `${hex(at)}  local.tee ${x.value}`
      );
    }

    else if (op === 0x41) {

      const x = sleb(p);
      p = x.next;

      console.log(
        `${hex(at)}  i32.const ${x.value}`
      );
    }

    else if (op === 0x6a) {

      console.log(
        `${hex(at)}  i32.add`
      );
    }

    else if (op === 0x71) {

      console.log(
        `${hex(at)}  i32.and`
      );
    }

    else if (op === 0x10) {

      const x = uleb(p);
      p = x.next;

      console.log(
        `${hex(at)}  call ${x.value}`
      );
    }

    else if (op === 0x28) {

      const a = uleb(p);
      p = a.next;

      const o = uleb(p);
      p = o.next;

      console.log(
        `${hex(at)}  i32.load align=${a.value} offset=${o.value}`
      );
    }

    else if (op === 0x36) {

      const a = uleb(p);
      p = a.next;

      const o = uleb(p);
      p = o.next;

      console.log(
        `${hex(at)}  i32.store align=${a.value} offset=${o.value}`
      );
    }

    else if (op === 0x45) {

      console.log(
        `${hex(at)}  i32.eqz`
      );
    }

    else if (op === 0x47) {

      console.log(
        `${hex(at)}  i32.ne`
      );
    }

    else if (op === 0x04) {

      console.log(
        `${hex(at)}  if`
      );
    }

    else if (op === 0x02) {

      console.log(
        `${hex(at)}  block`
      );
    }

    else if (op === 0x0d) {

      const x = uleb(p);
      p = x.next;

      console.log(
        `${hex(at)}  br_if ${x.value}`
      );
    }

    else if (op === 0x0f) {

      console.log(
        `${hex(at)}  return`
      );
    }

    else if (op === 0x0b) {

      console.log(
        `${hex(at)}  end`
      );
    }

    else if (op === 0xfc) {

      const sub = uleb(p);
      p = sub.next;

      if (sub.value === 11) {

        const mem = uleb(p);
        p = mem.next;

        console.log(
          `${hex(at)}  memory.fill memory=${mem.value}`
        );
      }

      else {

        console.log(
          `${hex(at)}  0xfc subopcode=${sub.value}`
        );
      }
    }

    else {

      console.log(
        `${hex(at)}  UNKNOWN 0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`
      );
    }
  }

  console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

  console.log("DERIVED DATA");
  console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

  console.log(
    "local1 = align4(arg + 27)"
  );

  console.log(
    "a.d arguments = (8951, local1)"
  );

  console.log(
    "memory.fill arguments = (local0, 0, local1)"
  );

  console.log(
    "------------------------------------------------------------"
  );

  console.log("END");
  console.log("============================================================");
}

main();

import { Address, Cell } from "@ton/core";
import { TonClient } from "@ton/ton";

const ROUTER = Address.parse(
    "EQALh-JBBIKK7gr0o4AVf9JZnEsFndqO0qTCyT-D-yBsWval"
);

const LIBRARY_HASHES = [
    "050ed2107f730a4493cfcddbc67bdb83854f1905b3486236530d5a50721d1817",
    "631cf98bbb3ee540dacc48256975d8e2c3080c6342e3ff4a9edaac6db73730041",
    "7c1f3aa01265453058887378a358cf37314856350400804c169b648c3fdfbe4c",
    "1b63154f10acd98345a65890997094e9856aa2be4f1e37060865519fa42032fd",
];

const ENDPOINT = "https://sandbox-v4.tonhubapi.com";

async function inspectLibrary(
    client: TonClient,
    index: number,
    hash: string
) {
    console.log(`\n===== LIBRARY [${index}] =====`);
    console.log("Hash:", hash);

    try {
        const result = await client.runMethod(
            ROUTER,
            "get_router_data",
            []
        );

        console.log("Router getter still returns:", result.exitCode);

        const cell = Cell.fromBoc(
            Buffer.from(result.result[0], "base64")
        )[0];

        console.log("Getter result cell:");
        console.log("  bits:", cell.bits.length);
        console.log("  refs:", cell.refs.length);
    } catch (e: any) {
        console.log(
            "Router getter error:",
            e?.message ?? e
        );
    }

    console.log("Library reference hash only.");
    console.log("Global library resolution must be tested separately.");
}

async function main() {
    console.log("========================================");
    console.log(" ROUTER LIBRARY ANALYSIS");
    console.log("========================================");
    console.log("NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT.");
    console.log("Router:", ROUTER.toString());
    console.log("Endpoint:", ENDPOINT);

    const client = new TonClient({
        endpoint: ENDPOINT,
    });

    for (let i = 0; i < LIBRARY_HASHES.length; i++) {
        await inspectLibrary(
            client,
            i,
            LIBRARY_HASHES[i]
        );
    }

    console.log("\n========================================");
    console.log("ANALYSIS FINISHED");
    console.log("NO TRANSACTION");
    console.log("========================================");
}

export async function run() {
    await main();
}

FILE: scripts\analyzeSelectorTable.ts

import fs from "fs";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

const START = 0x004db180;
const END   = 0x004db210;
const TARGET = 0x0019534c;

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("ANALYZE SELECTOR TABLE - 32BIT COMPONENTS");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

for (let p = START; p + 4 <= END; p += 4) {
  const v =
    (wasm[p] |
    (wasm[p + 1] << 8) |
    (wasm[p + 2] << 16) |
    (wasm[p + 3] << 24)) >>> 0;

  const xor = v ^ TARGET;
  const add = (v + TARGET) >>> 0;
  const sub = (v - TARGET) >>> 0;

  console.log(
    `0x${p.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  ` +
    `0x${v.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  ` +
    `DEC=${v}`
  );

  if (
    v === TARGET ||
    xor === 0 ||
    add === TARGET ||
    sub === TARGET ||
    (v & 0xffff) === (TARGET & 0xffff) ||
    (v >>> 16) === (TARGET >>> 16)
  ) {
    console.log(
      `  *** INTERESTING *** XOR=0x${xor.toString(16)} ` +
      `ADD=0x${add.toString(16)} SUB=0x${sub.toString(16)}`
    );
  }
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`TARGET = 0x${TARGET.toString(16)}`);
console.log("DONE");

import { Cell } from '@ton/core';
import { LiteClient, LiteSingleEngine } from 'ton-lite-client';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

const LIBRARY_HASH_HEX =
    'C95A2ED22AB516F77F9D4898DC4578E72F18A2448E8F6832334B0B4BF501BC79';

const TARGET_HASH_HEX =
    'DB8B6488B1D9B9051532EE339C86B249DF8F923B6E29975E8E474009157F601A';

const LITESERVER_HOST =
    'tcp://49.12.147.32:27842';

const LITESERVER_PUBLIC_KEY =
    'sU7QavX2F964iI9oToP9gffQpCQIoOLppeqL/pdPvpM=';

function sleep(ms: number): Promise<void> {
    return new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, ms));
}

function findCell(
    cell: Cell,
    targetHash: string
): Cell | null {
    const hash =
        cell.hash().toString('hex').toUpperCase();

    if (hash === targetHash) {
        return cell;
    }

    for (const ref of cell.refs) {
        const found = findCell(ref, targetHash);

        if (found) {
            return found;
        }
    }

    return null;
}

function getBits(
    cell: Cell,
    start: number,
    length: number
): string {
    const slice = cell.beginParse();
    slice.skip(start);

    let result = '';

    for (let i = 0; i < length; i++) {
        result += slice.loadBit() ? '1' : '0';
    }

    return result;
}

function bitsToHex(bits: string): string {
    let padded = bits;

    while (padded.length % 8 !== 0) {
        padded += '0';
    }

    let hex = '';

    for (let i = 0; i < padded.length; i += 8) {
        const byte = parseInt(
            padded.slice(i, i + 8),
            2
        );

        hex += byte
            .toString(16)
            .toUpperCase()
            .padStart(2, '0');
    }

    return hex;
}

export async function run(_provider: NetworkProvider) {
    console.log('========================================================================');
    console.log(' STON.FI v2.2 TARGET INSTRUCTION OFFSET ANALYZER');
    console.log(' READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION');
    console.log('========================================================================');
    console.log();

    console.log(`Library: ${LIBRARY_HASH_HEX}`);
    console.log(`Target:  ${TARGET_HASH_HEX}`);
    console.log(`Server:  ${LITESERVER_HOST}`);
    console.log();

    const engine = new LiteSingleEngine({
        host: LITESERVER_HOST,
        publicKey: Buffer.from(
            LITESERVER_PUBLIC_KEY,
            'base64'
        ),
    });

    try {
        console.log('Waiting for ADNL READY...');

        const started = Date.now();

        while (!engine.isReady()) {
            if (Date.now() - started > 15000) {
                throw new Error(
                    'LiteServer did not become ready within 15 seconds'
                );
            }

            await sleep(250);
        }

        console.log('ADNL READY');
        console.log();

        const client = new LiteClient({
            engine,
        });

        const result = await client.getLibraries([
            Buffer.from(LIBRARY_HASH_HEX, 'hex'),
        ]);

        if (result.result.length === 0) {
            throw new Error(
                'getLibraries() returned no Library entry'
            );
        }

        const root = Cell.fromBoc(
            Buffer.from(result.result[0].data)
        )[0];

        const target = findCell(
            root,
            TARGET_HASH_HEX
        );

        if (!target) {
            throw new Error(
                'Target Cell not found'
            );
        }

        console.log('============================================================');
        console.log(' TARGET');
        console.log('============================================================');

        console.log(
            `Hash: ${target.hash()
                .toString('hex')
                .toUpperCase()}`
        );

        console.log(`Bits: ${target.bits.length}`);
        console.log(`Refs: ${target.refs.length}`);

        console.log();
        console.log('============================================================');
        console.log(' RAW BIT STREAM');
        console.log('============================================================');

        const allBits =
            getBits(
                target,
                0,
                target.bits.length
            );

        const allHex =
            bitsToHex(allBits);

        console.log(`Total bits: ${allBits.length}`);
        console.log(`Total bytes: ${Math.ceil(allBits.length / 8)}`);
        console.log();
        console.log(allHex);

        console.log();
        console.log('============================================================');
        console.log(' OFFSETS 64..112');
        console.log('============================================================');

        for (let offset = 64; offset <= 112; offset++) {
            const remaining =
                target.bits.length - offset;

            if (remaining < 32) {
                continue;
            }

            const bits32 =
                getBits(
                    target,
                    offset,
                    32
                );

            const hex32 =
                bitsToHex(bits32);

            const decimal =
                parseInt(bits32, 2);

            console.log(
                `bit ${offset.toString().padStart(3, ' ')} : ` +
                `0x${hex32} : ${decimal}`
            );
        }

        console.log();
        console.log('============================================================');
        console.log(' BYTE-ALIGNED WINDOWS');
        console.log('============================================================');

        for (let offset = 0; offset <= 80; offset += 8) {
            if (target.bits.length - offset < 64) {
                continue;
            }

            const bits64 =
                getBits(
                    target,
                    offset,
                    64
                );

            const hex64 =
                bitsToHex(bits64);

            console.log(
                `bit ${offset.toString().padStart(3, ' ')} : ` +
                hex64
            );
        }

        console.log();
        console.log('============================================================');
        console.log(' EXACT OFFSET 80 WINDOW');
        console.log('============================================================');

        const window =
            getBits(
                target,
                64,
                128
            );

        console.log('Bits 64..191:');
        console.log(window);

        console.log();
        console.log('Hex:');
        console.log(bitsToHex(window));

        console.log();
        console.log('============================================================');
        console.log(' TARGET OPCODE CHECK');
        console.log('============================================================');

        const targetOpcode =
            '01010000110001101010011001010100';

        const position =
            allBits.indexOf(targetOpcode);

        console.log(
            `0x50C6A654 bit position: ${position}`
        );

        if (position >= 0) {
            console.log(
                `Confirmed at bit offset ${position}`
            );
        }

        console.log();
        console.log('============================================================');
        console.log(' TACT DISASSEMBLER TEST');
        console.log('============================================================');

        const tactCore: any =
            require('@tact-lang/opcode/node_modules/@ton/core');

        const tactOpcode: any =
            require('@tact-lang/opcode');

        const tactTarget =
            tactCore.Cell.fromBoc(
                root.toBoc()
            )[0];

        const tactFound =
            findCell(
                tactTarget,
                TARGET_HASH_HEX
            );

        if (!tactFound) {
            throw new Error(
                'Target Cell not found in Tact Core tree'
            );
        }

        console.log(
            `Tact target hash: ${tactFound.hash()
                .toString('hex')
                .toUpperCase()}`
        );

        console.log(
            `Tact target bits: ${tactFound.bits.length}`
        );

        console.log(
            `Tact target refs: ${tactFound.refs.length}`
        );

        console.log();
        console.log(
            'Disassembling from bit offset 80 with ALL refs...'
        );

        const instructions =
            tactOpcode.disassemble({
                source: tactFound,
                offset: {
                    bits: 80,
                    refs: 0,
                },
                limit: {
                    bits: tactFound.bits.length - 80,
                    refs: tactFound.refs.length,
                },
            });

        console.log();
        console.log(
            `Instructions decoded: ${instructions.length}`
        );

        for (const instruction of instructions) {
            console.log(
                `offset=${instruction.offset}` +
                ` length=${instruction.length}` +
                ` op=${instruction.op.definition?.mnemonic ?? 'UNKNOWN'}`
            );
        }
        console.log();
        console.log('========================================================================');
        console.log(' ANALYSIS COMPLETE');
        console.log('========================================================================');
        console.log('No blockchain transaction was sent.');
    } finally {
        engine.close();
    }
}

import { Cell } from '@ton/core';
import { LiteClient, LiteSingleEngine } from 'ton-lite-client';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

const LIBRARY_HASH_HEX =
    'C95A2ED22AB516F77F9D4898DC4578E72F18A2448E8F6832334B0B4BF501BC79';

const TARGET_CELL_HASH_HEX =
    'DB8B6488B1D9B9051532EE339C86B249DF8F923B6E29975E8E474009157F601A';

const LITESERVER_HOST =
    'tcp://49.12.147.32:27842';

const LITESERVER_PUBLIC_KEY =
    'sU7QavX2F964iI9oToP9gffQpCQIoOLppeqL/pdPvpM=';

const TARGET_OPCODE = '50C6A654';

function sleep(ms: number): Promise<void> {
    return new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, ms));
}

function cellBits(cell: Cell): string {
    let result = '';

    for (let i = 0; i < cell.bits.length; i++) {
        result += cell.bits.at(i) ? '1' : '0';
    }

    return result;
}

function bitsToHex(bits: string): string {
    let result = '';

    for (let i = 0; i + 4 <= bits.length; i += 4) {
        result += parseInt(bits.slice(i, i + 4), 2)
            .toString(16)
            .toUpperCase();
    }

    return result;
}

function hexToBytes(hex: string): number[] {
    const bytes: number[] = [];

    for (let i = 0; i + 1 < hex.length; i += 2) {
        bytes.push(parseInt(hex.slice(i, i + 2), 16));
    }

    return bytes;
}

function bytesToHex(bytes: number[]): string {
    return bytes
        .map(b => b.toString(16).padStart(2, '0').toUpperCase())
        .join(' ');
}

function readUint32(bytes: number[], offset: number): number {
    return (
        bytes[offset] * 0x1000000 +
        bytes[offset + 1] * 0x10000 +
        bytes[offset + 2] * 0x100 +
        bytes[offset + 3]
    ) >>> 0;
}

function readUint16(bytes: number[], offset: number): number {
    return (
        (bytes[offset] << 8) |
        bytes[offset + 1]
    ) >>> 0;
}

function printByteTable(
    bytes: number[],
    start: number,
    end: number
) {
    console.log();
    console.log('Offset   Bytes                         UInt16        UInt32');
    console.log('-------  ----------------------------  ------------  ------------');

    for (let offset = start; offset <= end; offset += 4) {
        const remaining = Math.min(4, bytes.length - offset);

        if (remaining <= 0) {
            break;
        }

        const row = bytes.slice(offset, offset + remaining);

        let u16 = '';
        let u32 = '';

        if (offset + 2 <= bytes.length) {
            u16 =
                '0x' +
                readUint16(bytes, offset)
                    .toString(16)
                    .padStart(4, '0')
                    .toUpperCase();
        }

        if (offset + 4 <= bytes.length) {
            u32 =
                '0x' +
                readUint32(bytes, offset)
                    .toString(16)
                    .padStart(8, '0')
                    .toUpperCase();
        }

        console.log(
            `${offset.toString().padStart(7)}  ` +
            `${bytesToHex(row).padEnd(28)}  ` +
            `${u16.padEnd(12)}  ` +
            `${u32}`
        );
    }
}

function analyzeCell(
    cell: Cell,
    label: string
) {
    const hash =
        cell.hash()
            .toString('hex')
            .toUpperCase();

    const bits = cellBits(cell);
    const hex = bitsToHex(bits);

    console.log();
    console.log('================================================================');
    console.log(label);
    console.log('================================================================');

    console.log(`Hash: ${hash}`);
    console.log(`Bits: ${bits.length}`);
    console.log(`Refs: ${cell.refs.length}`);

    if (bits.length % 8 !== 0) {
        console.log(
            'WARNING: Cell is not byte aligned.'
        );
        return;
    }

    const bytes = hexToBytes(hex);

    console.log(`Bytes: ${bytes.length}`);
    console.log();

    console.log('FULL HEX:');
    console.log(hex);

    const opcodeOffset = hex.indexOf(TARGET_OPCODE);

    if (opcodeOffset === -1) {
        console.log();
        console.log(
            `Target ${TARGET_OPCODE} NOT FOUND in this Cell.`
        );
    } else {
        const byteOffset = opcodeOffset / 2;

        console.log();
        console.log('****************************************************************');
        console.log(`TARGET ${TARGET_OPCODE}`);
        console.log('****************************************************************');
        console.log(`Hex character offset: ${opcodeOffset}`);
        console.log(`Byte offset:         ${byteOffset}`);
        console.log(`Bit offset:          ${byteOffset * 8}`);

        console.log();
        console.log('32-bit value:');

        const value = readUint32(bytes, byteOffset);

        console.log(
            `0x${value.toString(16).padStart(8, '0').toUpperCase()}`
        );

        console.log(
            `Decimal: ${value}`
        );

        const start = Math.max(0, byteOffset - 32);
        const end = Math.min(bytes.length - 1, byteOffset + 31);

        console.log();
        console.log(
            `BYTE WINDOW: ${start} .. ${end}`
        );

        printByteTable(bytes, start, end);

        console.log();
        console.log('Exact surrounding bytes:');

        const aroundStart =
            Math.max(0, byteOffset - 16);

        const aroundEnd =
            Math.min(bytes.length, byteOffset + 48);

        console.log(
            bytesToHex(
                bytes.slice(
                    aroundStart,
                    aroundEnd
                )
            )
        );
    }

    console.log();
    console.log('32-BIT WORD SCAN:');

    for (let offset = 0; offset + 4 <= bytes.length; offset += 4) {
        const value = readUint32(bytes, offset);

        if (
            value === 0x50c6a654 ||
            value === 0xf8416f17 ||
            value === 0xf8416f13
        ) {
            console.log(
                `  offset ${offset
                    .toString()
                    .padStart(3)} -> ` +
                `0x${value
                    .toString(16)
                    .padStart(8, '0')
                    .toUpperCase()}`
            );
        }
    }

    console.log();
    console.log('FIRST 32-BIT WORDS:');

    const firstWords =
        Math.min(16, Math.floor(bytes.length / 4));

    for (let i = 0; i < firstWords; i++) {
        const offset = i * 4;
        const value = readUint32(bytes, offset);

        console.log(
            `  [${offset
                .toString()
                .padStart(3)}] ` +
            `0x${value
                .toString(16)
                .padStart(8, '0')
                .toUpperCase()}`
        );
    }

    console.log();
    console.log('REFERENCES:');

    for (let i = 0; i < cell.refs.length; i++) {
        const ref = cell.refs[i];

        console.log();
        console.log(`ref${i}:`);

        console.log(
            `  Hash: ${ref.hash()
                .toString('hex')
                .toUpperCase()}`
        );

        console.log(
            `  Bits: ${ref.bits.length}`
        );

        console.log(
            `  Refs: ${ref.refs.length}`
        );

        if (ref.bits.length % 8 === 0) {
            const refHex =
                bitsToHex(cellBits(ref));

            console.log(
                `  Hex: ${refHex}`
            );
        }
    }
}

export async function run(_provider: NetworkProvider) {
    console.log('========================================================================');
    console.log(' STON.FI v2.2 TARGET LIBRARY CELL STRUCTURAL ANALYZER');
    console.log(' READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION');
    console.log('========================================================================');
    console.log();

    console.log(`Library: ${LIBRARY_HASH_HEX}`);
    console.log(`Target:  ${TARGET_CELL_HASH_HEX}`);
    console.log(`Opcode:  ${TARGET_OPCODE}`);
    console.log(`Server:  ${LITESERVER_HOST}`);
    console.log();

    const engine = new LiteSingleEngine({
        host: LITESERVER_HOST,
        publicKey: Buffer.from(
            LITESERVER_PUBLIC_KEY,
            'base64'
        ),
    });

    try {
        console.log('Waiting for ADNL READY...');

        const started = Date.now();

        while (!engine.isReady()) {
            if (Date.now() - started > 15000) {
                throw new Error(
                    'LiteServer did not become ready within 15 seconds'
                );
            }

            await sleep(250);
        }

        console.log('ADNL READY');
        console.log();

        const client = new LiteClient({
            engine,
        });

        const result =
            await client.getLibraries([
                Buffer.from(
                    LIBRARY_HASH_HEX,
                    'hex'
                ),
            ]);

        const entry =
            result.result.find(
                item =>
                    Buffer.from(item.hash)
                        .toString('hex')
                        .toUpperCase() ===
                    LIBRARY_HASH_HEX
            );

        if (!entry) {
            throw new Error(
                'Library entry not found'
            );
        }

        const root =
            Cell.fromBoc(
                Buffer.from(entry.data)
            )[0];

        console.log(
            `Root hash: ${root.hash()
                .toString('hex')
                .toUpperCase()}`
        );

        let target: Cell | null = null;
        let targetPath = '';

        function find(
            cell: Cell,
            path: string
        ): boolean {
            const hash =
                cell.hash()
                    .toString('hex')
                    .toUpperCase();

            if (hash === TARGET_CELL_HASH_HEX) {
                target = cell;
                targetPath = path;
                return true;
            }

            for (let i = 0; i < cell.refs.length; i++) {
                if (
                    find(
                        cell.refs[i],
                        `${path}.ref${i}`
                    )
                ) {
                    return true;
                }
            }

            return false;
        }

        find(root, 'root');

        if (!target) {
            throw new Error(
                'Target Cell not found'
            );
        }

        console.log();
        console.log(
            `Target path: ${targetPath}`
        );

        analyzeCell(
            target,
            'TARGET CELL'
        );

        console.log();
        console.log('========================================================================');
        console.log(' ANALYSIS COMPLETE');
        console.log('========================================================================');
        console.log('No transaction was sent.');
    } finally {
        engine.close();
    }
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\analyzeTargetRecordSchema.ts
====================================================================================================
import fs from "fs";

const wasmPath =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(wasmPath);

const U32 = (p: number) =>
  wasm[p] |
  (wasm[p + 1] << 8) |
  (wasm[p + 2] << 16) |
  (wasm[p + 3] << 24);

function readULEB(pos: number) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (true) {
    const b = wasm[pos++];
    value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
      return { value: value >>> 0, next: pos };
    }

    shift += 7;
  }
}

const DATA_START = 0x324ef5;
const DATA_PAYLOAD = DATA_START + 4;

let p = DATA_PAYLOAD;
const seg = [];

const count = readULEB(p);
p = count.next;

for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {
  const flags = readULEB(p);
  p = flags.next;

  let memoryIndex = 0;

  if (flags.value & 2) {
    const mi = readULEB(p);
    memoryIndex = mi.value;
    p = mi.next;
  }

  const exprOpcode = wasm[p++];
  if (exprOpcode !== 0x41) {
    throw new Error(
      `Unexpected data offset opcode at ${p - 1}: 0x${exprOpcode.toString(16)}`
    );
  }

  const off = readULEB(p);
  p = off.next;

  const end = wasm[p++];
  if (end !== 0x0b) {
    throw new Error(`Bad init_expr end at ${p - 1}`);
  }

  const size = readULEB(p);
  p = size.next;

  const dataStart = p;
  const dataEnd = p + size.value;

  seg.push({
    index: i,
    memoryIndex,
    offset: off.value,
    size: size.value,
    fileStart: dataStart,
    fileEnd: dataEnd,
  });

  p = dataEnd;
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("TARGET RECORD SCHEMA ANALYSIS");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

console.log(`WASM size = ${wasm.length}`);
console.log(`DATA segments = ${seg.length}`);

const memorySize = 32 * 1024 * 1024;
const memory = new Uint8Array(memorySize);

let mapped = 0;

for (const s of seg) {
  if (s.memoryIndex !== 0) continue;

  const end = s.offset + s.size;
  if (end > memory.length) continue;

  memory.set(
    wasm.subarray(s.fileStart, s.fileEnd),
    s.offset
  );

  mapped += s.size;
}

function memU32(addr: number): number {
  return (
    memory[addr] |
    (memory[addr + 1] << 8) |
    (memory[addr + 2] << 16) |
    (memory[addr + 3] << 24)
  ) >>> 0;
}

function hex(v: number) {
  return "0x" + v.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function region(addr: number) {
  if (addr >= 0x187000 && addr < 0x18ab8d) return "SEG412";
  if (addr >= 0x18ab98 && addr < 0x18f512) return "SEG413";
  return "OTHER";
}

console.log(`MEMORY mapped bytes = ${mapped}`);
console.log("");

const targets = [
  {
    value: 2053,
    addr: 0x00189dec,
    function: 8952,
  },
  {
    value: 2101,
    addr: 0x00189f9c,
    function: 8927,
  },
  {
    value: 2612,
    addr: 0x0018a8b0,
    function: 8637,
  },
];

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("TARGET RECORDS");
console.log("============================================================");

for (const t of targets) {
  console.log(
    `${t.value} @ ${hex(t.addr)} -> Function ${t.function}`
  );
}

console.log("");

/*
 * The target values occur in repeated metadata records.
 * Inspect a generous window around each target.
 *
 * We print:
 *   - aligned u32 values
 *   - decimal value
 *   - possible memory pointer region
 *
 * This lets us compare the record schema without assuming
 * the record size beforehand.
 */

const WINDOW_BEFORE = 0x50;
const WINDOW_AFTER = 0x50;

for (const t of targets) {
  console.log("");
  console.log("============================================================");
  console.log(
    `TARGET ${t.value} / FUNCTION ${t.function}`
  );
  console.log(
    `CENTER = ${hex(t.addr)}`
  );
  console.log("============================================================");

  const start = t.addr - WINDOW_BEFORE;
  const end = t.addr + WINDOW_AFTER;

  for (let a = start; a <= end; a += 4) {
    const v = memU32(a);

    let tag = "";

    if (v === t.value) {
      tag = " <-- TARGET";
    } else if (v >= 2000 && v <= 3000) {
      tag = " [FUNC/INDEX]";
    } else if (v >= 0x187000 && v <= 0x18f512) {
      tag = ` [${region(v)} PTR]`;
    }

    console.log(
      `${hex(a)} : ${hex(v)} = ${v}${tag}`
    );
  }
}

/*
 * Find all exact target occurrences again, then print
 * neighboring u32 values in a compact record form.
 */

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("TARGET OCCURRENCE CONTEXT");
console.log("============================================================");

for (const t of targets) {
  const hits: number[] = [];

  for (let a = 0x187000; a + 4 <= 0x18ab8d; a += 4) {
    if (memU32(a) === t.value) {
      hits.push(a);
    }
  }

  console.log("");
  console.log(
    `TARGET ${t.value}: ${hits.length} aligned occurrence(s)`
  );

  for (const addr of hits) {
    console.log(`\n@ ${hex(addr)}`);

    for (let i = -8; i <= 8; i++) {
      const a = addr + i * 4;
      const v = memU32(a);

      let marker = " ";

      if (i === 0) marker = "*";

      console.log(
        `${marker} ${i.toString().padStart(3)} ${hex(a)} ${hex(v)} ${v}`
      );
    }
  }
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("NEIGHBOR TARGET COMPARISON");
console.log("============================================================");

for (let i = 0; i < targets.length; i++) {
  for (let j = i + 1; j < targets.length; j++) {
    const a = targets[i].addr;
    const b = targets[j].addr;

    console.log("");
    console.log(
      `${targets[i].value} -> ${targets[j].value}`
    );

    console.log(
      `address delta = ${b - a} bytes`
    );

    console.log(
      `record-index delta = ${(targets[j].value - targets[i].value)}`
    );

    console.log(
      `bytes/index = ${
        (b - a) / (targets[j].value - targets[i].value)
      }`
    );
  }
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("END");
console.log("============================================================");
