const fs = require("fs");

const WASM = "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const buf = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("TYPE 4 + TABLE 0 DISPATCH ANALYSIS");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`WASM SIZE : ${buf.length}`);
console.log("TARGET   : FUNCTION 8952");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

let p = 8; // wasm magic + version

function u8() {
    return buf[p++];
}

function u32() {
    let result = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = buf[p++];
        result |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0)
            return result >>> 0;

        shift += 7;
    }
}

function bytes(n) {
    const start = p;
    p += n;
    return buf.subarray(start, p);
}

function readVec(fn) {
    const n = u32();
    const out = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < n; i++)
        out.push(fn());

    return out;
}

function readName() {
    const n = u32();
    const s = Buffer.from(bytes(n));
    return s.toString("utf8");
}

function hex(x, width = 8) {
    return "0x" + x.toString(16).padStart(width, "0");
}

function signedI32(x) {
    return x | 0;
}

function valTypeName(v) {
    const map = {
        0x7f: "i32",
        0x7e: "i64",
        0x7d: "f32",
        0x7c: "f64",
        0x7b: "v128",
        0x70: "funcref",
        0x6f: "externref"
    };

    return map[v] || `0x${v.toString(16)}`;
}

function parseLimits() {
    const flags = u8();
    const min = u32();

    let max = null;

    if (flags & 0x01)
        max = u32();

    return { flags, min, max };
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// STORAGE
// ------------------------------------------------------------

const types = [];
const imports = [];
const functionTypeIndices = [];
const tableInfo = [];
const table0Segments = [];

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// SECTION SCAN
// ------------------------------------------------------------

const sections = [];

p = 8;

while (p < buf.length) {
    const sectionStart = p;
    const id = u8();
    const size = u32();

    const payloadStart = p;
    const payloadEnd = p + size;

    sections.push({
        id,
        start: sectionStart,
        payloadStart,
        payloadEnd,
        size
    });

    p = payloadEnd;
}

console.log("\nSECTION MAP");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

for (const s of sections) {
    console.log(
        `SECTION ${s.id.toString().padStart(2)} ` +
        `${hex(s.payloadStart)} .. ${hex(s.payloadEnd)} ` +
        `SIZE ${s.size}`
    );
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// TYPE SECTION
// ------------------------------------------------------------

const typeSection = sections.find(s => s.id === 1);

if (!typeSection)
    throw new Error("TYPE SECTION NOT FOUND");

p = typeSection.payloadStart;

const typeCount = u32();

for (let i = 0; i < typeCount; i++) {

    const form = u8();

    if (form !== 0x60) {
        throw new Error(
            `Unsupported type form at type ${i}: 0x${form.toString(16)}`
        );
    }

    const params = readVec(() => valTypeName(u8()));
    const results = readVec(() => valTypeName(u8()));

    types.push({
        index: i,
        params,
        results
    });
}

console.log("\n============================================================");
console.log("TYPE SECTION");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`TYPE COUNT : ${types.length}`);

const type4 = types[4];

if (!type4) {
    throw new Error("TYPE 4 NOT FOUND");
}

console.log("\nTYPE 4");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
console.log(`PARAM COUNT  : ${type4.params.length}`);
console.log(`PARAMS       : (${type4.params.join(", ")})`);
console.log(`RESULT COUNT : ${type4.results.length}`);
console.log(`RESULTS      : (${type4.results.join(", ")})`);

console.log("\nSIGNATURE");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
console.log(
    `(${type4.params.join(", ")}) -> (${type4.results.join(", ")})`
);

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// IMPORT SECTION
// ------------------------------------------------------------

const importSection = sections.find(s => s.id === 2);

if (importSection) {

    p = importSection.payloadStart;

    const importCount = u32();

    for (let i = 0; i < importCount; i++) {

        const module = readName();
        const name = readName();
        const kind = u8();

        let typeIndex = null;

        if (kind === 0x00) {
            typeIndex = u32();
        }
        else if (kind === 0x01) {
            u8(); // elem type
            parseLimits();
        }
        else if (kind === 0x02) {
            parseLimits();
        }
        else if (kind === 0x03) {
            u8();
            u8();
        }
        else {
            throw new Error(`Unknown import kind: ${kind}`);
        }

        imports.push({
            index: i,
            module,
            name,
            kind,
            typeIndex
        });
    }
}

console.log("\n============================================================");
console.log("IMPORTS");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`IMPORT COUNT : ${imports.length}`);

let importedFunctionCount = 0;

for (const imp of imports) {
    if (imp.kind === 0x00)
        importedFunctionCount++;
}

console.log(`IMPORTED FUNCTIONS : ${importedFunctionCount}`);

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// FUNCTION SECTION
// ------------------------------------------------------------

const functionSection = sections.find(s => s.id === 3);

if (!functionSection)
    throw new Error("FUNCTION SECTION NOT FOUND");

p = functionSection.payloadStart;

const definedFunctionCount = u32();

for (let i = 0; i < definedFunctionCount; i++) {
    functionTypeIndices.push(u32());
}

console.log("\n============================================================");
console.log("FUNCTION TYPE MAP");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`DEFINED FUNCTIONS : ${definedFunctionCount}`);
console.log(`TOTAL FUNCTIONS   : ${importedFunctionCount + definedFunctionCount}`);

function functionType(functionIndex) {

    if (functionIndex < importedFunctionCount) {
        const imp = imports[functionIndex];

        return imp ? imp.typeIndex : null;
    }

    const definedIndex = functionIndex - importedFunctionCount;

    return functionTypeIndices[definedIndex] ?? null;
}

function isType4(functionIndex) {
    return functionType(functionIndex) === 4;
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// TYPE 4 FUNCTION ENUMERATION
// ------------------------------------------------------------

const type4Functions = [];

for (
    let functionIndex = 0;
    functionIndex < importedFunctionCount + definedFunctionCount;
    functionIndex++
) {
    if (isType4(functionIndex))
        type4Functions.push(functionIndex);
}

console.log("\nTYPE 4 FUNCTION COUNT");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
console.log(`TYPE 4 FUNCTIONS : ${type4Functions.length}`);

console.log("\nFIRST TYPE 4 FUNCTIONS");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

for (const f of type4Functions.slice(0, 100)) {
    console.log(
        `FUNC ${f.toString().padStart(5)} ` +
        `TYPE ${functionType(f)}`
    );
}

if (type4Functions.length > 100)
    console.log(`... ${type4Functions.length - 100} more`);

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// TABLE SECTION
// ------------------------------------------------------------

const tableSection = sections.find(s => s.id === 4);

if (!tableSection)
    throw new Error("TABLE SECTION NOT FOUND");

p = tableSection.payloadStart;

const tableCount = u32();

for (let i = 0; i < tableCount; i++) {

    const elemType = u8();
    const limits = parseLimits();

    tableInfo.push({
        index: i,
        elemType,
        min: limits.min,
        max: limits.max
    });
}

console.log("\n============================================================");
console.log("TABLE SECTION");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`TABLE COUNT : ${tableInfo.length}`);

for (const t of tableInfo) {
    console.log(
        `TABLE ${t.index} ` +
        `TYPE=${valTypeName(t.elemType)} ` +
        `MIN=${t.min} ` +
        `MAX=${t.max === null ? "none" : t.max}`
    );
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// ELEMENT SECTION
// ------------------------------------------------------------

const elementSection = sections.find(s => s.id === 9);

if (!elementSection)
    throw new Error("ELEMENT SECTION NOT FOUND");

p = elementSection.payloadStart;

const elementCount = u32();

console.log("\n============================================================");
console.log("ELEMENT SEGMENTS");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`ELEMENT COUNT : ${elementCount}`);

for (let seg = 0; seg < elementCount; seg++) {

    const flags = u32();

    let tableIndex = 0;
    let offset = null;
    let functionIndices = [];

    /*
       flags 0:
         active table 0
         offset expr
         vec(funcidx)

       flags 2:
         active explicit table
         offset expr
         elemkind
         vec(funcidx)

       flags 1 / 3:
         passive/declarative legacy forms

       flags 4 / 6:
         expression-based active segments
    */

    if (flags === 0) {

        // offset expression
        const opcode = u8();

        if (opcode !== 0x41)
            throw new Error(
                `Segment ${seg}: expected i32.const, got 0x${opcode.toString(16)}`
            );

        offset = signedI32(u32());

        const end = u8();

        if (end !== 0x0b)
            throw new Error(
                `Segment ${seg}: expected end`
            );

        const n = u32();

        for (let i = 0; i < n; i++)
            functionIndices.push(u32());
    }

    else if (flags === 2) {

        tableIndex = u32();

        const opcode = u8();

        if (opcode !== 0x41)
            throw new Error(
                `Segment ${seg}: expected i32.const`
            );

        offset = signedI32(u32());

        const end = u8();

        if (end !== 0x0b)
            throw new Error(
                `Segment ${seg}: expected end`
            );

        const elemKind = u8();

        if (elemKind !== 0x00)
            throw new Error(
                `Segment ${seg}: unsupported elemkind 0x${elemKind.toString(16)}`
            );

        const n = u32();

        for (let i = 0; i < n; i++)
            functionIndices.push(u32());
    }

    else if (flags === 1 || flags === 3) {

        if (flags === 1) {
            u8(); // elemkind
        }
        else {
            u32(); // table index
            u8();  // elemkind
        }

        const n = u32();

        for (let i = 0; i < n; i++)
            u32();

        continue;
    }

    else if (flags === 4 || flags === 6) {

        if (flags === 6)
            tableIndex = u32();

        // offset expression
        const opcode = u8();

        if (opcode !== 0x41)
            throw new Error(
                `Segment ${seg}: unsupported offset expression opcode 0x${opcode.toString(16)}`
            );

        offset = signedI32(u32());

        const end = u8();

        if (end !== 0x0b)
            throw new Error(
                `Segment ${seg}: expected end`
            );

        // elemtype
        u8();

        const n = u32();

        /*
          expression form:
             vec(expr)
             each expr = ref.func funcidx end
        */

        for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {

            const refFunc = u8();

            if (refFunc !== 0xd2) {
                throw new Error(
                    `Segment ${seg}: expected ref.func, got 0x${refFunc.toString(16)}`
                );
            }

            const f = u32();

            functionIndices.push(f);

            const endExpr = u8();

            if (endExpr !== 0x0b) {
                throw new Error(
                    `Segment ${seg}: expected expr end`
                );
            }
        }
    }

    else {
        throw new Error(
            `Unsupported element segment flags: ${flags}`
        );
    }

    const info = {
        segment: seg,
        flags,
        tableIndex,
        offset,
        count: functionIndices.length,
        functionIndices
    };

    table0Segments.push(info);

    console.log(
        `SEG ${seg.toString().padStart(3)} ` +
        `FLAGS=${flags} ` +
        `TABLE=${tableIndex} ` +
        `OFFSET=${offset} ` +
        `COUNT=${functionIndices.length}`
    );
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// TABLE 0 MAP
// ------------------------------------------------------------

console.log("\n============================================================");
console.log("TABLE 0 FUNCTION MAP");
console.log("============================================================");

const table0 = [];

for (const seg of table0Segments) {

    if (seg.tableIndex !== 0)
        continue;

    if (seg.offset === null)
        continue;

    for (let i = 0; i < seg.functionIndices.length; i++) {

        const tableIndex = seg.offset + i;
        const functionIndex = seg.functionIndices[i];

        table0[tableIndex] = functionIndex;
    }
}

const table0Defined = table0.filter(x => x !== undefined).length;

console.log(`TABLE 0 POPULATED ENTRIES : ${table0Defined}`);

const targetTableIndex = 2053;
const targetFunction = table0[targetTableIndex];

console.log("\nTARGET VERIFICATION");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
console.log(`TABLE[${targetTableIndex}] : FUNC ${targetFunction}`);

if (targetFunction !== 8952) {
    console.log("WARNING: TARGET DOES NOT MATCH 8952");
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// TYPE 4 TABLE TARGETS
// ------------------------------------------------------------

console.log("\n============================================================");
console.log("TABLE 0 -> TYPE 4 TARGETS");
console.log("============================================================");

const type4TableTargets = [];

for (let i = 0; i < table0.length; i++) {

    const f = table0[i];

    if (f === undefined)
        continue;

    if (isType4(f)) {

        type4TableTargets.push({
            tableIndex: i,
            functionIndex: f
        });
    }
}

console.log(`TYPE 4 TABLE TARGET COUNT : ${type4TableTargets.length}`);

console.log("\nTYPE 4 TABLE TARGETS");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

for (const x of type4TableTargets) {

    console.log(
        `TABLE[${x.tableIndex}] -> FUNC ${x.functionIndex}`
    );
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// NEIGHBORHOOD AROUND 8952
// ------------------------------------------------------------

console.log("\n============================================================");
console.log("TABLE NEIGHBORHOOD AROUND FUNC 8952");
console.log("============================================================");

const pos = table0.findIndex(f => f === 8952);

console.log(`FUNC 8952 TABLE POSITION : ${pos}`);

if (pos >= 0) {

    for (
        let i = Math.max(0, pos - 15);
        i <= Math.min(table0.length - 1, pos + 15);
        i++
    ) {

        const f = table0[i];

        if (f === undefined)
            continue;

        console.log(
            `TABLE[${i}] -> FUNC ${f} ` +
            `TYPE=${functionType(f)}`
        );
    }
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------

console.log("============================================================");

for (const x of type4TableTargets) {

    if (Math.abs(x.tableIndex - 2053) <= 50) {

        console.log(

FILE: scripts\analyzeType4Families.js

const fs = require("fs");

const WASM =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

function readU32(buf, p) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = buf[p++];

        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return {
                value: value >>> 0,
                next: p
            };
        }

        shift += 7;
    }
}

function readName(buf, p) {
    const n = readU32(buf, p);
    return {
        next: n.next + n.value
    };
}

function skipLimits(buf, p) {
    const flags = buf[p++];

    const min = readU32(buf, p);
    p = min.next;

    if (flags & 1) {
        const max = readU32(buf, p);
        p = max.next;
    }

    return p;
}

function getSections(buf) {
    const sections = [];
    let p = 8;

    while (p < buf.length) {
        const id = buf[p++];

        const sizeInfo = readU32(buf, p);
        p = sizeInfo.next;

        const start = p;
        const end = start + sizeInfo.value;

        sections.push({
            id,
            start,
            end
        });

        p = end;
    }

    return sections;
}

function parseTypes(buf, section) {
    let p = section.start;

    const countInfo = readU32(buf, p);
    p = countInfo.next;

    const types = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < countInfo.value; i++) {
        const form = buf[p++];

        if (form !== 0x60) {
            throw new Error(
                `Unexpected type form 0x${form.toString(16)} at type ${i}`
            );
        }

        const pc = readU32(buf, p);
        p = pc.next;

        const params = [];

        for (let j = 0; j < pc.value; j++) {
            params.push(buf[p++]);
        }

        const rc = readU32(buf, p);
        p = rc.next;

        const results = [];

        for (let j = 0; j < rc.value; j++) {
            results.push(buf[p++]);
        }

        types.push({
            params,
            results
        });
    }

    return types;
}

function parseImports(buf, section) {
    let p = section.start;

    const countInfo = readU32(buf, p);
    p = countInfo.next;

    const importedTypes = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < countInfo.value; i++) {
        const module = readName(buf, p);
        p = module.next;

        const name = readName(buf, p);
        p = name.next;

        const kind = buf[p++];

        if (kind === 0) {
            const t = readU32(buf, p);
            p = t.next;

            importedTypes.push(t.value);
        }

        else if (kind === 1) {
            p += 1;
            p = skipLimits(buf, p);
        }

        else if (kind === 2) {
            p = skipLimits(buf, p);
        }

        else if (kind === 3) {
            p += 2;
        }

        else {
            throw new Error(`Unknown import kind ${kind}`);
        }
    }

    return importedTypes;
}

function parseFunctionTypes(buf, section) {
    let p = section.start;

    const countInfo = readU32(buf, p);
    p = countInfo.next;

    const result = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < countInfo.value; i++) {
        const t = readU32(buf, p);
        p = t.next;

        result.push(t.value);
    }

    return result;
}

function typeSignature(types, typeIndex) {
    const t = types[typeIndex];

    if (!t) {
        return "UNKNOWN";
    }

    const p = t.params
        .map(x => `0x${x.toString(16)}`)
        .join(",");

    const r = t.results
        .map(x => `0x${x.toString(16)}`)
        .join(",");

    return `(${p})->(${r})`;
}

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("ANALYZE TYPE 4 FAMILIES");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

console.log();
console.log("WASM SIZE :", wasm.length);

const sections = getSections(wasm);

const typeSection = sections.find(x => x.id === 1);
const importSection = sections.find(x => x.id === 2);
const functionSection = sections.find(x => x.id === 3);
const elementSection = sections.find(x => x.id === 9);

if (!typeSection || !functionSection || !elementSection) {
    throw new Error("Required WASM sections missing");
}

const types = parseTypes(wasm, typeSection);

const importedTypes = importSection
    ? parseImports(wasm, importSection)
    : [];

const definedTypes =
    parseFunctionTypes(wasm, functionSection);

const functionTypes =
    importedTypes.concat(definedTypes);

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// REAL ELEMENT TABLE
// ------------------------------------------------------------

let p = elementSection.start;

const seg = readU32(wasm, p);
p = seg.next;

if (seg.value !== 1) {
    throw new Error(
        `Expected 1 element segment, got ${seg.value}`
    );
}

const flags = readU32(wasm, p);
p = flags.next;

if (flags.value !== 0) {
    throw new Error(
        `Expected flags 0, got ${flags.value}`
    );
}

if (wasm[p++] !== 0x41) {
    throw new Error("Expected i32.const");
}

let offset = 0;
let shift = 0;

while (true) {
    const b = wasm[p++];

    offset |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
        break;
    }

    shift += 7;
}

if (wasm[p++] !== 0x0b) {
    throw new Error("Expected end opcode");
}

const countInfo = readU32(wasm, p);
p = countInfo.next;

const count = countInfo.value;

if (count !== 8992) {
    throw new Error(
        `Expected 8992 table entries, got ${count}`
    );
}

const table = new Array(offset + count);

for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
    const f = readU32(wasm, p);
    p = f.next;

    table[offset + i] = f.value;
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// COLLECT TYPE 4 TARGETS
// ------------------------------------------------------------

const type4 = [];

for (let tableIndex = 0; tableIndex < table.length; tableIndex++) {
    const func = table[tableIndex];

    if (func === undefined) {
        continue;
    }

    const typeIndex = functionTypes[func];

    if (typeIndex === 4) {
        type4.push({
            tableIndex,
            func,
            typeIndex
        });
    }
}

console.log();
console.log("TABLE ENTRIES :", count);
console.log("TABLE OFFSET  :", offset);
console.log("TYPE 4 COUNT  :", type4.length);
console.log(
    "ELEMENT END VERIFIED :",
    p === elementSection.end ? "YES" : "NO"
);

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// GROUP CONSECUTIVE TYPE 4 TABLE ENTRIES
// ------------------------------------------------------------

const groups = [];

let current = null;

for (const item of type4) {

    if (!current) {
        current = {
            start: item.tableIndex,
            end: item.tableIndex,
            funcs: [item.func]
        };
        continue;
    }

    if (item.tableIndex === current.end + 1) {
        current.end = item.tableIndex;
        current.funcs.push(item.func);
    }
    else {
        groups.push(current);

        current = {
            start: item.tableIndex,
            end: item.tableIndex,
            funcs: [item.func]
        };
    }
}

if (current) {
    groups.push(current);
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// GROUP STATISTICS
// ------------------------------------------------------------

const lengthDistribution = new Map();

for (const g of groups) {
    const len = g.end - g.start + 1;

    lengthDistribution.set(
        len,
        (lengthDistribution.get(len) || 0) + 1
    );
}

console.log();
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("CONSECUTIVE TYPE 4 GROUPS");
console.log("============================================================");

console.log("GROUP COUNT :", groups.length);

console.log();

    .sort((a, b) => a.func - b.func);

    if (!fg) {
        fg = {
            start: item.func,
            end: item.func,
            items: [item]
        };
        continue;
    }

    if (item.func === fg.end + 1) {
        fg.end = item.func;
        fg.items.push(item);
    }
    else {
        funcGroups.push(fg);

        fg = {
            start: item.func,
            end: item.func,
            items: [item]
        };
    }
}

if (fg) {
    funcGroups.push(fg);
}

console.log("FUNCTION CLUSTER COUNT :", funcGroups.length);

    const len = g.end - g.start + 1;

    if (len < 3) {
        continue;
    }

    console.log(
        `FUNC ${g.start}..${g.end} ` +
        `LENGTH=${len}`
    );

====================================================================================================

import fs from "fs";

const file = "emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";
const targetFunc = 656;

const b = fs.readFileSync(file);
let p = 8;

function u8() {
  return b[p++];
}

function readULEB() {
  let v = 0;
  let s = 0;

  while (true) {
    const x = u8();
    v += (x & 0x7f) * 2 ** s;

    if (!(x & 0x80)) return v >>> 0;

    s += 7;
  }
}

function readSLEB() {
  let v = 0;
  let s = 0;

  while (true) {
    const x = u8();
    v += (x & 0x7f) * 2 ** s;
    s += 7;

    if (!(x & 0x80)) {
      if (s < 32 && (x & 0x40)) {
        v -= 2 ** s;
      }
      return v | 0;
    }

    s += 7;
  }
}

function hex(v, n = 8) {
  return "0x" + (v >>> 0).toString(16).padStart(n, "0");
}

function readName() {
  const n = readULEB();
  const start = p;
  p += n;
  return b.subarray(start, start + n).toString("utf8");
}

function skipLimits() {
  const flags = readULEB();
  readULEB();

  if (flags & 1) {
    readULEB();
  }
}

// ============================================================
// PARSE MODULE
// ============================================================

let importedFunctions = 0;
let funcTypeIndices = [];
let codeBodies = [];
let types = [];

while (p < b.length) {
  const sectionId = u8();
  const sectionSize = readULEB();
  const sectionEnd = p + sectionSize;

  // TYPE
  if (sectionId === 1) {
    const count = readULEB();

    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
      const form = u8();

      if (form !== 0x60) {
        throw new Error(
          `Unexpected type form ${hex(form, 2)}`
        );
      }

      const np = readULEB();
      const params = [];

      for (let j = 0; j < np; j++) {
        params.push(u8());
      }

      const nr = readULEB();
      const results = [];

      for (let j = 0; j < nr; j++) {
        results.push(u8());
      }

      types.push({
        params,
        results
      });
    }
  }

  // IMPORT
  else if (sectionId === 2) {
    const count = readULEB();

    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
      readName();
      readName();

      const kind = u8();

      if (kind === 0) {
        readULEB();
        importedFunctions++;
      }

      else if (kind === 1) {
        u8();
        skipLimits();
      }

      else if (kind === 2) {
        skipLimits();
      }

      else if (kind === 3) {
        u8();
        u8();
      }
    }
  }

  // FUNCTION
  else if (sectionId === 3) {
    const count = readULEB();

    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
      funcTypeIndices.push(readULEB());
    }
  }

  // CODE
  else if (sectionId === 10) {
    const count = readULEB();

    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
      const size = readULEB();

      const start = p;
      const end = p + size;

      codeBodies.push({
        start,
        end,
        size
      });

      p = end;
    }
  }

  p = sectionEnd;
}

// ============================================================
// RESOLVE FUNC 656
// ============================================================

const definedIndex =
  targetFunc - importedFunctions;

if (
  definedIndex < 0 ||
  definedIndex >= codeBodies.length
) {
  throw new Error(
    `FUNC ${targetFunc} not found`
  );
}

const body = codeBodies[definedIndex];

const typeIndex =
  funcTypeIndices[definedIndex];

const type = types[typeIndex];

const names = {
  0x00: "UNREACHABLE",
  0x01: "NOP",
  0x02: "BLOCK",
  0x03: "LOOP",
  0x04: "IF",

  0x6b: "I32.SUB",
  0x6c: "I32.MUL",
  0x6d: "I32.DIV_S",
  0x6e: "I32.DIV_U",

  0x71: "I32.AND",
  0x72: "I32.OR",
  0x73: "I32.XOR",

  0x74: "I32.SHL",
  0x75: "I32.SHR_S",
  0x76: "I32.SHR_U",

  0x79: "I32.ROTL",
  0x7a: "I32.ROTR",

  0xa7: "I32.WRAP_I64",
  0xad: "I64.EXTEND_I32_S"
};

function blockType() {
  const x = u8();

  if (x === 0x40) return "0x40";
  if (x === 0x7f) return "i32";
  if (x === 0x7e) return "i64";
  if (x === 0x7d) return "f32";
  if (x === 0x7c) return "f64";

  return hex(x, 2);
}

function memArg() {
  const align = readULEB();
  const offset = readULEB();

  return {
    align,
    offset
  };
}

// DECODE
// ============================================================

const instructions = [];

while (p < body.end) {
  const at = p;
  const op = u8();

  let line =
    names[op] ||
    `opcode=0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`;

  const rec = {
    at,
    op,
    line
  };

  switch (op) {

    case 0x02:
    case 0x03:
    case 0x04: {
      const bt = blockType();

      line += ` blocktype=${bt}`;
      rec.blockType = bt;

      break;
    }

    case 0x0c:
    case 0x0d: {
      const depth = readULEB();

      line += ` ${depth}`;
      rec.depth = depth;

      break;
    }

    case 0x0e: {
      const n = readULEB();
      const labels = [];

      for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
        labels.push(readULEB());
      }

      const def = readULEB();

      line +=
        ` labels=[${labels.join(",")}]` +
        ` default=${def}`;

      break;
    }

    case 0x10: {
      const func = readULEB();

      line += ` FUNC ${func}`;
      rec.func = func;

      break;
    }

    case 0x11: {
      const ti = readULEB();
      const table = readULEB();

      line +=
        ` TYPE ${ti}` +
        ` TABLE ${table}`;

      rec.typeIndex = ti;
      rec.tableIndex = table;

      break;
    }

    case 0x20:
    case 0x21:
    case 0x22: {
      const local = readULEB();

      line += ` L${local}`;
      rec.local = local;

      break;
    }

    case 0x23:
    case 0x24: {
      const global = readULEB();

      line += ` G${global}`;
      rec.global = global;

      break;
    }

    case 0x28:
    case 0x29:
    case 0x2d:
    case 0x2f:
    case 0x36:
    case 0x37:
    case 0x3a:
    case 0x3b: {
      const m = memArg();

      line +=
        ` align=${m.align}` +
        ` offset=${m.offset}`;

      rec.align = m.align;
      rec.offset = m.offset;

      break;
    }

    case 0x41: {
      const value = readSLEB();

      line +=
        ` ${value}` +
        ` (${hex(value)})`;

      rec.value = value;

      break;
    }

    case 0x42: {
      const value = readSLEB();

      line += ` ${value}`;
      rec.value = value;

      break;
    }

    case 0xfc: {
      const sub = readULEB();

      line +=
        ` subopcode=0x${sub.toString(16)}`;

      rec.subopcode = sub;

      break;
    }

    default:
      break;
  }

  rec.line = line;
  instructions.push(rec);

  console.log(
    `${hex(at)}  ${line}`
  );
}

  );

  console.log(

  const begin = Math.max(0, idx - 10);

  for (let i = begin; i < idx; i++) {
    console.log(

    );
  }

  console.log(
    "NEXT INSTRUCTIONS:"
  );

  const end = Math.min(
    instructions.length,
    idx + 6
  );

  for (let i = idx + 1; i < end; i++) {
    console.log(
      `  ${hex(instructions[i].at)}  ${instructions[i].line}`
    );
  }
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\auditFunc6919.ts

import fs from "fs";

const file =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const b = fs.readFileSync(file);

const START = 0x23a188;
const END   = 0x23a21b;

function readULEB(p: number) {
  let v = 0;
  let s = 0;

  while (true) {
    const x = b[p++];

    if (x === undefined)
      throw new Error(`ULEB out of range @ 0x${(p - 1).toString(16)}`);

    v += (x & 0x7f) * Math.pow(2, s);

    if (!(x & 0x80))
      return { value: v >>> 0, next: p };

    s += 7;

    if (s > 35)
      throw new Error(`ULEB too long @ 0x${p.toString(16)}`);
  }
}

function readSLEB(p: number) {
  let v = 0;
  let s = 0;

  while (true) {
    const x = b[p++];

    if (x === undefined)
      throw new Error(`SLEB out of range @ 0x${(p - 1).toString(16)}`);

    v += (x & 0x7f) * Math.pow(2, s);

    if (!(x & 0x80)) {
      if (s < 32 && (x & 0x40))
        v -= Math.pow(2, s + 7);

      return {
        value: v | 0,
        next: p
      };
    }

    s += 7;

    if (s > 35)
      throw new Error(`SLEB too long @ 0x${p.toString(16)}`);
  }
}

function hex(x: number) {
  return "0x" + (x >>> 0).toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function blockType(p: number) {
  const x = b[p];

  if (x === undefined)
    throw new Error(`BLOCKTYPE out of range @ ${hex(p)}`);

  /*
   * MVP block types:
   * 0x40 = empty result
   * 0x7f = i32
   * 0x7e = i64
   * 0x7d = f32
   * 0x7c = f64
   */

  if (x === 0x40)
    return { text: "type=void", next: p + 1 };

  if (x === 0x7f)
    return { text: "type=i32", next: p + 1 };

  if (x === 0x7e)
    return { text: "type=i64", next: p + 1 };

  if (x === 0x7d)
    return { text: "type=f32", next: p + 1 };

  if (x === 0x7c)
    return { text: "type=f64", next: p + 1 };

  const r = readSLEB(p);

  return {
    text: `type=typeidx(${r.value})`,
    next: r.next
  };
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FUNC 6919 EXACT DECODER / AUDIT");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`RANGE : ${hex(START)} .. ${hex(END)}`);
console.log("TARGET: MEM[0x25100C]");
console.log("============================================================");

let p = START;
let depth = 0;

while (p < END) {

  const at = p;
  const op = b[p++];

  if (op === undefined)
    break;

  let s = "";

  switch (op) {

    case 0x00:
      s = "unreachable";
      break;

    case 0x01:
      s = "nop";
      break;

    case 0x02: {
      const r = blockType(p);
      p = r.next;
      depth++;
      s = `block ${r.text}  depth=${depth}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x03: {
      const r = blockType(p);
      p = r.next;
      depth++;
      s = `loop ${r.text}  depth=${depth}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x04: {
      const r = blockType(p);
      p = r.next;
      depth++;
      s = `if ${r.text}  depth=${depth}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x05:
      s = `else depth=${depth}`;
      break;

    case 0x0b:
      s = `end depth=${depth}`;
      if (depth > 0)
        depth--;
      break;

    case 0x0c: {
      const r = readULEB(p);
      p = r.next;
      s = `br ${r.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x0d: {
      const r = readULEB(p);
      p = r.next;
      s = `br_if ${r.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x0e: {
      const n = readULEB(p);
      p = n.next;

      const labels: number[] = [];

      for (let i = 0; i < n.value; i++) {
        const r = readULEB(p);
        p = r.next;
        labels.push(r.value);
      }

      const d = readULEB(p);
      p = d.next;

      s = `br_table [${labels.join(",")}] default=${d.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x0f:
      s = "return";
      break;

    case 0x10: {
      const r = readULEB(p);
      p = r.next;
      s = `call FUNC ${r.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x11: {
      const t = readULEB(p);
      p = t.next;

      const table = readULEB(p);
      p = table.next;

      s = `CALL_INDIRECT type=${t.value} table=${table.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x1a:
      s = "drop";
      break;

    case 0x1b:
      s = "select";
      break;

    case 0x20: {
      const r = readULEB(p);
      p = r.next;
      s = `local.get ${r.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x21: {
      const r = readULEB(p);
      p = r.next;
      s = `local.set ${r.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x22: {
      const r = readULEB(p);
      p = r.next;
      s = `local.tee ${r.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x23: {
      const r = readULEB(p);
      p = r.next;
      s = `global.get ${r.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x24: {
      const r = readULEB(p);
      p = r.next;
      s = `global.set ${r.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x28: {
      const align = readULEB(p);
      p = align.next;

      const off = readULEB(p);
      p = off.next;

      s = `i32.load align=${align.value} offset=${off.value}`;

      if (off.value === 0x25100c)
        s += "  <<< TARGET ADDRESS AS OFFSET";

      break;
    }

    case 0x36: {
      const align = readULEB(p);
      p = align.next;

      const off = readULEB(p);
      p = off.next;

      s = `i32.store align=${align.value} offset=${off.value}`;

      if (off.value === 0x25100c)
        s += "  <<< TARGET ADDRESS AS OFFSET";

      break;
    }

    case 0x41: {
      const r = readSLEB(p);
      p = r.next;

      s = `i32.const ${r.value} (${hex(r.value)})`;

      if ((r.value >>> 0) === 0x25100c)
        s += "  <<< TARGET ADDRESS";

      break;
    }

    case 0x45:
      s = "i32.eqz";
      break;

    case 0x46:
      s = "i32.eq";
      break;

    case 0x47:
      s = "i32.ne";
      break;

    case 0x48:
      s = "i32.lt_s";
      break;

    case 0x49:
      s = "i32.lt_u";
      break;

    case 0x4a:
      s = "i32.gt_s";
      break;

    case 0x4b:
      s = "i32.gt_u";
      break;

    case 0x4c:
      s = "i32.le_s";
      break;

    case 0x4d:
      s = "i32.le_u";
      break;

    case 0x4e:
      s = "i32.ge_s";
      break;

    case 0x4f:
      s = "i32.ge_u";
      break;

    case 0x6a:
      s = "i32.add";
      break;

    case 0x6b:
      s = "i32.sub";
      break;

    case 0x71:
      s = "i32.and";
      break;

    case 0x72:
      s = "i32.or";
      break;

    case 0x73:
      s = "i32.xor";
      break;

    case 0x74:
      s = "i32.shl";
      break;

    case 0x75:
      s = "i32.shr_s";
      break;

    case 0x76:
      s = "i32.shr_u";
      break;

    case 0x7c:
      s = "i32.rotl";
      break;

    case 0x7d:
      s = "i32.rotr";
      break;

    default:
      s = `UNKNOWN opcode=0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`;
      break;
  }

  console.log(`${hex(at)}  ${s}`);
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`FINAL POSITION : ${hex(p)}`);
console.log(`EXPECTED END   : ${hex(END)}`);
console.log(`REMAINING      : ${END - p}`);
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("DONE");

import fs from "fs";

const file =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const b = fs.readFileSync(file);

const ranges = [
  { fn: 821, start: 0x0003899d, end: 0x00038a21 },
  { fn: 822, start: 0x00038a22, end: 0x00038a47 },
];

function readULEB(p: number) {
  let v = 0;
  let s = 0;

  while (true) {
    const x = b[p++];

    if (x === undefined)
      throw new Error(`ULEB OOB @ 0x${(p - 1).toString(16)}`);

    v += (x & 0x7f) * 2 ** s;

    if (!(x & 0x80))
      return { value: v >>> 0, next: p };

    s += 7;
  }
}

function readSLEB(p: number) {
  let v = 0;
  let s = 0;

  while (true) {
    const x = b[p++];

    if (x === undefined)
      throw new Error(`SLEB OOB @ 0x${(p - 1).toString(16)}`);

    v += (x & 0x7f) * 2 ** s;

    if (!(x & 0x80)) {
      if (s < 32 && (x & 0x40))
        v -= 2 ** (s + 7);

      return { value: v | 0, next: p };
    }

    s += 7;
  }
}

function hex(x: number) {
  return "0x" + (x >>> 0).toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function blockType(p: number) {
  const x = b[p];

  if (x === 0x40) return { text: "void", next: p + 1 };
  if (x === 0x7f) return { text: "i32", next: p + 1 };
  if (x === 0x7e) return { text: "i64", next: p + 1 };
  if (x === 0x7d) return { text: "f32", next: p + 1 };
  if (x === 0x7c) return { text: "f64", next: p + 1 };

  const r = readSLEB(p);

  return {
    text: `typeidx(${r.value})`,
    next: r.next,
  };
}

function decode(start: number, end: number, fn: number) {
  console.log("");
  console.log("############################################################");
  console.log(`FUNC ${fn}`);
  console.log("############################################################");
  console.log(`BODY : ${hex(start)} .. ${hex(end)}`);
  console.log(`SIZE : ${end - start}`);
  console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

  let p = start;
  let depth = 0;

  while (p < end) {
    const at = p;
    const op = b[p++];

    if (op === undefined)
      break;

    let s = "";

    switch (op) {
      case 0x00:
        s = "unreachable";
        break;

      case 0x01:
        s = "nop";
        break;

      case 0x02: {
        const r = blockType(p);
        p = r.next;
        depth++;
        s = `block ${r.text} depth=${depth}`;
        break;
      }

      case 0x03: {
        const r = blockType(p);
        p = r.next;
        depth++;
        s = `loop ${r.text} depth=${depth}`;
        break;
      }

      case 0x04: {
        const r = blockType(p);
        p = r.next;
        depth++;
        s = `if ${r.text} depth=${depth}`;
        break;
      }

      case 0x05:
        s = `else depth=${depth}`;
        break;

      case 0x0b:
        s = `end depth=${depth}`;
        if (depth > 0) depth--;
        break;

      case 0x0c: {
        const r = readULEB(p);
        p = r.next;
        s = `br ${r.value}`;
        break;
      }

      case 0x0d: {
        const r = readULEB(p);
        p = r.next;
        s = `br_if ${r.value}`;
        break;
      }

      case 0x0f:
        s = "return";
        break;

      case 0x10: {
        const r = readULEB(p);
        p = r.next;
        s = `CALL FUNC ${r.value}`;
        break;
      }

      case 0x11: {
        const t = readULEB(p);
        p = t.next;

        const table = readULEB(p);
        p = table.next;

        s = `CALL_INDIRECT type=${t.value} table=${table.value}`;
        break;
      }

      case 0x1a:
        s = "drop";
        break;

      case 0x1b:
        s = "select";
        break;

      case 0x20: {
        const r = readULEB(p);
        p = r.next;
        s = `local.get ${r.value}`;
        break;
      }

      case 0x21: {
        const r = readULEB(p);
        p = r.next;
        s = `local.set ${r.value}`;
        break;
      }

      case 0x22: {
        const r = readULEB(p);
        p = r.next;
        s = `local.tee ${r.value}`;
        break;
      }

      case 0x23: {
        const r = readULEB(p);
        p = r.next;
        s = `global.get ${r.value}`;
        break;
      }

      case 0x24: {
        const r = readULEB(p);
        p = r.next;
        s = `global.set ${r.value}`;
        break;
      }

      case 0x28: {
        const a = readULEB(p);
        p = a.next;

        const o = readULEB(p);
        p = o.next;

        s = `i32.load align=${a.value} offset=${o.value}`;
        break;
      }

      case 0x36: {
        const a = readULEB(p);
        p = a.next;

        const o = readULEB(p);
        p = o.next;

        s = `i32.store align=${a.value} offset=${o.value}`;
        break;
      }

      case 0x41: {
        const r = readSLEB(p);
        p = r.next;
        s = `i32.const ${r.value} (${hex(r.value)})`;
        break;
      }

      case 0x45:
        s = "i32.eqz";
        break;

      case 0x46:
        s = "i32.eq";
        break;

      case 0x47:
        s = "i32.ne";
        break;

      case 0x48:
        s = "i32.lt_s";
        break;

      case 0x49:
        s = "i32.lt_u";
        break;

      case 0x4a:
        s = "i32.gt_s";
        break;

      case 0x4b:
        s = "i32.gt_u";
        break;

      case 0x4c:
        s = "i32.le_s";
        break;

      case 0x4d:
        s = "i32.le_u";
        break;

      case 0x4e:
        s = "i32.ge_s";
        break;

      case 0x4f:
        s = "i32.ge_u";
        break;

      case 0x6a:
        s = "i32.add";
        break;

      case 0x6b:
        s = "i32.sub";
        break;

      case 0x71:
        s = "i32.and";
        break;

      case 0x72:
        s = "i32.or";
        break;

      case 0x73:
        s = "i32.xor";
        break;

      case 0x74:
        s = "i32.shl";
        break;

      case 0x75:
        s = "i32.shr_s";
        break;

      case 0x76:
        s = "i32.shr_u";
        break;

      case 0x70:
        s = "i32.wrap_i64";
        break;

      case 0x7c:
        s = "i32.rotl";
        break;

      case 0x7d:
        s = "i32.rotr";
        break;

      default:
        s = `UNKNOWN opcode=0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`;
        break;
    }

    console.log(`${hex(at)}  ${s}`);
  }

  console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
  console.log(`FINAL : ${hex(p)}`);
  console.log(`EXPECT: ${hex(end)}`);
  console.log(`REMAINING: ${end - p}`);
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FUNC 821 / 822 EXACT DECODER");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

for (const r of ranges)
  decode(r.start, r.end, r.fn);

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("DONE");
console.log("============================================================");

import fs from "fs";

const file =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const b = fs.readFileSync(file);

const START = 0x0003899d;
const END   = 0x00038a21;

function readULEB(p: number) {
  let v = 0;
  let s = 0;

  while (true) {
    const x = b[p++];

    if (x === undefined)
      throw new Error(`ULEB OOB @ 0x${(p - 1).toString(16)}`);

    v += (x & 0x7f) * 2 ** s;

    if (!(x & 0x80))
      return { value: v >>> 0, next: p };

    s += 7;
  }
}

function readSLEB(p: number, bits = 32) {
  let v = 0;
  let s = 0;

  while (true) {
    const x = b[p++];

    if (x === undefined)
      throw new Error(`SLEB OOB @ 0x${(p - 1).toString(16)}`);

    v += (x & 0x7f) * 2 ** s;

    if (!(x & 0x80)) {
      if (s < bits && (x & 0x40))
        v -= 2 ** (s + 7);

      return { value: v, next: p };
    }

    s += 7;

    if (s > bits + 7)
      throw new Error(`SLEB too long @ 0x${p.toString(16)}`);
  }
}

function hex(x: number | bigint) {
  if (typeof x === "bigint")
    return "0x" + x.toString(16);

  return "0x" + (x >>> 0).toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function blockType(p: number) {
  const x = b[p];

  if (x === 0x40) return { text: "void", next: p + 1 };
  if (x === 0x7f) return { text: "i32", next: p + 1 };
  if (x === 0x7e) return { text: "i64", next: p + 1 };
  if (x === 0x7d) return { text: "f32", next: p + 1 };
  if (x === 0x7c) return { text: "f64", next: p + 1 };

  const r = readSLEB(p);

  return {
    text: `typeidx(${r.value})`,
    next: r.next,
  };
}

function readF64(p: number) {
  if (p + 8 > b.length)
    throw new Error("f64 OOB");

  return { next: p + 8 };
}

      s = `global.set ${r.value}`;

      const o = readULEB(p);

      p = a.next;

      const o = readULEB(p);
      p = o.next;

      s = `i32.store align=${a.value} offset=${o.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x37: {
      const a = readULEB(p);
      p = a.next;

      const o = readULEB(p);
      p = o.next;

      s = `i64.store align=${a.value} offset=${o.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x3a: {
      const a = readULEB(p);
      p = a.next;

      const o = readULEB(p);
      p = o.next;

      s = `i64.store8 align=${a.value} offset=${o.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x3b: {
      const a = readULEB(p);
      p = a.next;

      const o = readULEB(p);
      p = o.next;

      s = `i64.store16 align=${a.value} offset=${o.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x3c: {
      const a = readULEB(p);
      p = a.next;

      const o = readULEB(p);
      p = o.next;

      s = `i64.store32 align=${a.value} offset=${o.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x41: {
      const r = readSLEB(p, 32);
      p = r.next;
      s = `i32.const ${r.value} (${hex(r.value)})`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x42: {
      const r = readSLEB(p, 64);
      p = r.next;

      s = `i64.const ${r.value}`;

      if (Math.abs(r.value) > 0xffffffff)
        s += "  [64-BIT]";

      break;

    case 0x43:
      p += 4;
      s = "f32.const";
      break;

    case 0x44:
      p += 8;
      s = "f64.const";
      break;

    case 0x45:
      s = "i32.eqz";
      break;

    case 0x46:
      s = "i32.eq";
      break;

    case 0x47:
      s = "i32.ne";

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\auditStatePath.mjs
====================================================================================================
import fs from "fs";

const file = "emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";
const b = fs.readFileSync(file);

let p = 8;

function u8() {
  return b[p++];
}

function u32() {
  const v = b.readUInt32LE(p);
  p += 4;
  return v >>> 0;
}

function readULEB() {
  let v = 0;
  let s = 0;

  while (true) {
    const x = u8();
    v += (x & 0x7f) * 2 ** s;

    if (!(x & 0x80)) return v >>> 0;

    s += 7;
  }
}

function readSLEB() {
  let v = 0;
  let s = 0;

  while (true) {
    const x = u8();
    v += (x & 0x7f) * 2 ** s;
    s += 7;

    if (!(x & 0x80)) {
      if (s < 32 && (x & 0x40)) {
        v -= 2 ** s;
      }
      return v | 0;
    }
  }
}

function hex(v, n = 8) {
  return "0x" + (v >>> 0).toString(16).padStart(n, "0");
}

function readName() {
  const n = readULEB();
  const start = p;
  p += n;
  return b.subarray(start, start + n).toString("utf8");
}

function skipLimits() {
  const flags = readULEB();
  readULEB();

  if (flags & 1) {
    readULEB();
  }
}

const types = [];
const importedFuncTypes = [];
const funcTypeIndices = [];
const codeBodies = [];

let dataSegments = [];
let tableSegments = [];

let importedFunctions = 0;

// ============================================================
// PARSE MODULE
// ============================================================

while (p < b.length) {
  const sectionId = u8();
  const sectionSize = readULEB();
  const sectionStart = p;
  const sectionEnd = sectionStart + sectionSize;

  // ----------------------------------------------------------
  // TYPE
  // ----------------------------------------------------------
  if (sectionId === 1) {
    const count = readULEB();

    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
      const form = u8();

      if (form !== 0x60) {
        throw new Error(
          `Unexpected type form ${hex(form, 2)}`
        );
      }

      const np = readULEB();
      const params = [];

      for (let j = 0; j < np; j++) {
        params.push(u8());
      }

      const nr = readULEB();
      const results = [];

      for (let j = 0; j < nr; j++) {
        results.push(u8());
      }

      types.push({
        params,
        results
      });
    }
  }

  // ----------------------------------------------------------
  // IMPORT
  // ----------------------------------------------------------
  else if (sectionId === 2) {
    const count = readULEB();

    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
      readName();
      readName();

      const kind = u8();

      if (kind === 0) {
        const typeIndex = readULEB();

        importedFuncTypes.push(typeIndex);
        importedFunctions++;
      }

      else if (kind === 1) {
        u8();
        skipLimits();
      }

      else if (kind === 2) {
        skipLimits();
      }

      else if (kind === 3) {
        u8();
        u8();
      }
    }
  }

  // ----------------------------------------------------------
  // FUNCTION
  // ----------------------------------------------------------
  else if (sectionId === 3) {
    const count = readULEB();

    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
      funcTypeIndices.push(readULEB());
    }
  }

  // ----------------------------------------------------------
  // TABLE
  // ----------------------------------------------------------
  else if (sectionId === 4) {
    const count = readULEB();

    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
      const elemType = u8();
      const flags = readULEB();
      const min = readULEB();

      let max = null;

      if (flags & 1) {
        max = readULEB();
      }

      tableSegments.push({
        elemType,
        flags,
        min,
        max
      });
    }
  }

  // ----------------------------------------------------------
  // ELEMENT
  // ----------------------------------------------------------
  else if (sectionId === 9) {
    const count = readULEB();

    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
      const segStart = p;

      const flags = readULEB();

      let tableIndex = 0;

      if (flags & 2) {
        tableIndex = readULEB();
      }

      // Active segment offset expression
      if ((flags & 1) === 0 || (flags & 2)) {
        const op = u8();

        if (op === 0x41) {
          readSLEB();
        }
        else if (op === 0x23) {
          readULEB();
        }
        else {
          throw new Error(
            `Unexpected element offset opcode ${hex(op, 2)}`
          );
        }

        const end = u8();

        if (end !== 0x0b) {
          throw new Error("Missing element offset END");
        }
      }

      const elemKind = flags & 4;

      if (elemKind) {
        // expression elements
        const n = readULEB();

        for (let j = 0; j < n; j++) {
          while (u8() !== 0x0b) {}
        }
      }
      else {
        const n = readULEB();
        const funcs = [];

        for (let j = 0; j < n; j++) {
          funcs.push(readULEB());
        }

        tableSegments.push({
          elementSegment: i,
          tableIndex,
          start: segStart,
          funcs
        });
      }
    }
  }

  // ----------------------------------------------------------
  // CODE
  // ----------------------------------------------------------
  else if (sectionId === 10) {
    const count = readULEB();

    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
      const bodySize = readULEB();

      const bodyStart = p;
      const bodyEnd = p + bodySize;

      codeBodies.push({
        bodyStart,
        bodyEnd,
        bodySize
      });

      p = bodyEnd;
    }
  }

  // ----------------------------------------------------------
  // DATA
  // ----------------------------------------------------------
  else if (sectionId === 11) {
    const count = readULEB();

    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
      const flags = readULEB();

      let memoryIndex = 0;
      let offset = null;

      if ((flags & 1) === 0) {
        const op = u8();

        if (op === 0x41) {
          offset = readSLEB();
        }
        else if (op === 0x23) {
          readULEB();
          offset = null;
        }
        else {
          throw new Error(
            `Unexpected data offset opcode ${hex(op, 2)}`
          );
        }

        const end = u8();

        if (end !== 0x0b) {
          throw new Error("Missing data offset END");
        }
      }
      else if (flags === 2) {
        memoryIndex = readULEB();

        const op = u8();

        if (op === 0x41) {
          offset = readSLEB();
        }
        else if (op === 0x23) {
          readULEB();
          offset = null;
        }

        const end = u8();

        if (end !== 0x0b) {
          throw new Error("Missing data offset END");
        }
      }

      const size = readULEB();

      const dataStart = p;
      const dataEnd = p + size;

      dataSegments.push({
        memoryIndex,
        offset,
        dataStart,
        dataEnd,
        size
      });

      p = dataEnd;
    }
  }

  p = sectionEnd;
}

// ============================================================
// FUNCTION RESOLUTION
// ============================================================

function functionInfo(funcIndex) {
  if (funcIndex < importedFunctions) {
    const typeIndex = importedFuncTypes[funcIndex];

    return {
      funcIndex,
      imported: true,
      typeIndex,
      body: null
    };
  }

  const definedIndex = funcIndex - importedFunctions;

  if (
    definedIndex < 0 ||
    definedIndex >= codeBodies.length
  ) {
    return null;
  }

  const typeIndex = funcTypeIndices[definedIndex];

  return {
    funcIndex,
    imported: false,
    typeIndex,
    body: codeBodies[definedIndex]
  };
}

function signature(funcIndex) {
  const info = functionInfo(funcIndex);

  if (!info) return "UNKNOWN";

  const t = types[info.typeIndex];

  if (!t) return `TYPE ${info.typeIndex} UNKNOWN`;

  const map = {
    0x7f: "i32",
    0x7e: "i64",
    0x7d: "f32",
    0x7c: "f64"
  };

  return (
    "(" +
    t.params.map(x => map[x] || hex(x, 2)).join(",") +
    ") -> (" +
    t.results.map(x => map[x] || hex(x, 2)).join(",") +
    ")"
  );
}

function scanIndirect(funcIndex) {
  const info = functionInfo(funcIndex);

  if (!info || !info.body) return;

  let q = info.body.bodyStart;

  // skip locals
  const groups = (() => {
    let v = 0;
    let s = 0;

    while (true) {
      const x = b[q++];

      v += (x & 0x7f) * 2 ** s;

      if (!(x & 0x80)) return v;

      s += 7;
    }
  })();

  for (let i = 0; i < groups; i++) {
    // local count
    let done = false;

    while (!done) {
      const x = b[q++];

      if (!(x & 0x80)) done = true;
    }

    q++;
  }

  while (q < info.body.bodyEnd) {
    const at = q;
    const op = b[q++];

    if (op === 0x11) {
      let typeIndex = 0;
      let shift = 0;

      while (true) {
        const x = b[q++];

        typeIndex += (x & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;

        if (!(x & 0x80)) break;

        shift += 7;
      }

      let tableIndex = 0;
      shift = 0;

      while (true) {
        const x = b[q++];

        tableIndex += (x & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;

        if (!(x & 0x80)) break;

        shift += 7;
      }

      console.log(
        `FUNC ${funcIndex} CALL_INDIRECT @ ${hex(at)}` +
        ` TYPE ${typeIndex} TABLE ${tableIndex}`
      );
    }

    // enough for this audit:
    // stop at body end; unknown operands are not interpreted here
  }
}

scanIndirect(122);
scanIndirect(656);

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("AUDIT DONE");
console.log("============================================================");

FILE: scripts\backtrace113Selector.ts

import fs from "fs";

const b = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

function uleb(p:number) {
  let v=0,s=0;
  while(true){
    const x=b[p++];
    v |= (x&127)<<s;
    if(!(x&128)) return {v:v>>>0,p};
    s+=7;
  }
}

function sleb(p:number) {
  let v=0,s=0,x=0;
  while(true){
    x=b[p++];
    v |= (x&127)<<s;
    s+=7;
    if(!(x&128)) break;
  }
  if(s<32 && (x&64)) v |= (~0<<s);
  return {v:v|0,p};
}

function str(p:number) {
  const x=uleb(p);
  return x.p+x.v;
}

let types:any[]=[];
let imported=0;
let defTypes:number[]=[];

// ---------------- TYPES / IMPORTS / FUNCTION SECTION ----------------

let p=8;

while(p<b.length){
  const id=b[p++];
  const z=uleb(p);
  const start=z.p;
  const end=start+z.v;

  if(id===1){
    let q=start;
    const n=uleb(q); q=n.p;

    for(let i=0;i<n.v;i++){
      q++; // 0x60

      const np=uleb(q); q=np.p;
      const params:number[]=[];
      for(let j=0;j<np.v;j++) params.push(b[q++]);

      const nr=uleb(q); q=nr.p;
      const results:number[]=[];
      for(let j=0;j<nr.v;j++) results.push(b[q++]);

      types.push({params,results});
    }
  }

  if(id===2){
    let q=start;
    const n=uleb(q); q=n.p;

    for(let i=0;i<n.v;i++){
      q=str(q);
      q=str(q);

      const kind=b[q++];

      if(kind===0){
        q=uleb(q).p;
        imported++;
      }
      else if(kind===1){
        q++;
        const fl=uleb(q); q=fl.p;
        q=uleb(q).p;
        if(fl.v&1) q=uleb(q).p;
      }
      else if(kind===2){
        q=uleb(q).p;
      }
      else if(kind===3){
        q+=2;
      }
    }
  }

  if(id===3){
    let q=start;
    const n=uleb(q); q=n.p;

    for(let i=0;i<n.v;i++){
      const x=uleb(q);
      defTypes.push(x.v);
      q=x.p;
    }
  }

  p=end;
}

function ftype(fn:number){
  const i=fn-imported;
  if(i<0 || i>=defTypes.length) return null;
  return types[defTypes[i]];
}

// ---------------- CODE ----------------

type F={fn:number,start:number,end:number,code:number};

const funcs:F[]=[];

p=8;

while(p<b.length){
  const id=b[p++];
  const z=uleb(p);
  const start=z.p;
  const end=start+z.v;

  if(id===10){
    let q=start;
    const n=uleb(q); q=n.p;

    for(let i=0;i<n.v;i++){
      const bs=uleb(q);
      const body=bs.p;
      const bend=body+bs.v;

      let x=body;
      const nl=uleb(x);
      x=nl.p;

      for(let k=0;k<nl.v;k++){
        const c=uleb(x);
        x=c.p+1;
      }

      funcs.push({
        fn:imported+i,
        start:body,
        end:bend,
        code:x
      });

      q=bend;
    }
  }

  p=end;
}

function getFunc(fn:number){
  return funcs.find(x=>x.fn===fn);
}

function decode(f:F){
  const ins:any[]=[];
  let q=f.code;

  while(q<f.end-1){
    const off=q;
    const op=b[q++];

    let text=`op 0x${op.toString(16)}`;

    try{
      if(op===0x20){
        const x=uleb(q); q=x.p;
        text=`local.get ${x.v}`;
      }
      else if(op===0x21){
        const x=uleb(q); q=x.p;
        text=`local.set ${x.v}`;
      }
      else if(op===0x22){
        const x=uleb(q); q=x.p;
        text=`local.tee ${x.v}`;
      }
      else if(op===0x41){
        const x=sleb(q); q=x.p;
        text=`i32.const ${x.v} (0x${(x.v>>>0).toString(16)})`;
      }
      else if(op===0x28){
        const a=uleb(q); q=a.p;
        const o=uleb(q); q=o.p;
        text=`i32.load offset=${o.v}`;
      }
      else if(op===0x29){
        const a=uleb(q); q=a.p;
        const o=uleb(q); q=o.p;
        text=`i64.load offset=${o.v}`;
      }
      else if(op===0x36){
        const a=uleb(q); q=a.p;
        const o=uleb(q); q=o.p;
        text=`i32.store offset=${o.v}`;
      }
      else if(op===0x6a){
        text="i32.add";
      }
      else if(op===0x6b){
        text="i32.sub";
      }
      else if(op===0x6c){
        text="i32.mul";
      }
      else if(op===0x10){
        const x=uleb(q); q=x.p;
        text=`call ${x.v}`;
      }
      else if(op===0x11){
        const x=uleb(q); q=x.p;
        const y=uleb(q); q=y.p;
        text=`call_indirect type=${x.v}`;
      }
      else if(op===0x1a){
        text="drop";
      }
      else if(op===0x45){
        text="i32.eqz";
      }
      else if(op===0x47){
        text="i32.lt_u";
      }
      else if(op===0x04){
        q=uleb(q).p;
        text="if";
      }
      else if(op===0x05){
        text="else";
      }
      else if(op===0x0b){
        text="end";
      }
      else if(op===0x0c){
        const x=uleb(q); q=x.p;
        text=`br ${x.v}`;
      }
      else if(op===0x0d){
        const x=uleb(q); q=x.p;
        text=`br_if ${x.v}`;
      }
      else{
        // known no-immediate opcodes are left as-is
      }
    }catch{
      break;
    }

    ins.push({off,op,text});
  }

  return ins;
}

        x.op===0x10
      ){
        console.log(
          `  0x${x.off.toString(16).padStart(8,"0")}  ${x.text}`
        );
      }

  }

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\backtraceIndirectTarget4154.ts
