import fs from "fs";

const WASM = "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const TARGET_FUNC = 4154;
const TARGET_SLOT = 114;
const TARGET_TYPE = 0;
const TARGET_TABLE = 0;

type Instr = {
    off: number;
    op: number;
    imm: number[];
    size: number;
    text: string;
};

type Value =
    | { kind: "const"; value: number; origin: string }
    | { kind: "local"; index: number; origin: string }
    | { kind: "unknown"; origin: string };

type Body = {
    func: number;
    start: number;
    end: number;
    bytes: Buffer;
};

function uleb(buf: Buffer, p: number): { value: number; next: number } {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (p < buf.length) {
        const b = buf[p++];
        value += (b & 0x7f) * Math.pow(2, shift);

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return { value, next: p };
        }

        shift += 7;
    }

    throw new Error("ULEB overflow");
}

function sleb(buf: Buffer, p: number): { value: number; next: number } {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let b = 0;

    while (p < buf.length) {
        b = buf[p++];
        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
        shift += 7;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) break;
    }

    if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40)) {
        value |= (-1 << shift);
    }

    return { value, next: p };
}

function readName(buf: Buffer, p: number): { name: string; next: number } {
    const n = uleb(buf, p);
    const end = n.next + n.value;
    return {
        name: buf.subarray(n.next, end).toString("utf8"),
        next: end,
    };
}

function readVec(buf: Buffer, p: number): { count: number; next: number } {
    const r = uleb(buf, p);

    return {
        count: r.value,
        next: r.next,
    };
}
function decodeInstructions(bytes: Buffer, base: number): Instr[] {
    const out: Instr[] = [];
    let p = 0;

    while (p < bytes.length) {
        const off = base + p;
        const start = p;
        const op = bytes[p++];

        const imm: number[] = [];
        let text = `op=0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`;

        if (op === 0x41) {
            const r = sleb(bytes, p);
            imm.push(r.value);
            p = r.next;
            text = `i32.const ${r.value}`;
        } else if (op === 0x42) {
            const r = sleb(bytes, p);
            imm.push(r.value);
            p = r.next;
            text = `i64.const ${r.value}`;
        } else if (op === 0x20) {
            const r = uleb(bytes, p);
            imm.push(r.value);
            p = r.next;
            text = `local.get ${r.value}`;
        } else if (op === 0x21) {
            const r = uleb(bytes, p);
            imm.push(r.value);
            p = r.next;
            text = `local.set ${r.value}`;
        } else if (op === 0x22) {
            const r = uleb(bytes, p);
            imm.push(r.value);
            p = r.next;
            text = `local.tee ${r.value}`;
        } else if (op === 0x10) {
            const r = uleb(bytes, p);
            imm.push(r.value);
            p = r.next;
            text = `call ${r.value}`;
        } else if (op === 0x11) {
            const a = uleb(bytes, p);
            p = a.next;

            const b = uleb(bytes, p);
            p = b.next;

            imm.push(a.value, b.value);
            text = `call_indirect type=${a.value} table=${b.value}`;
        } else if (op === 0x1a) {
            text = "drop";
        } else if (op === 0x45) {
            text = "i32.eqz";
        } else if (op === 0x46) {
            text = "i32.eq";
        } else if (op === 0x47) {
            text = "i32.ne";
        } else if (op === 0x48) {
            text = "i32.lt_s";
        } else if (op === 0x49) {
            text = "i32.lt_u";
        } else if (op === 0x4a) {
            text = "i32.gt_s";
        } else if (op === 0x4b) {
            text = "i32.gt_u";
        } else if (op === 0x4c) {
            text = "i32.le_s";
        } else if (op === 0x4d) {
            text = "i32.le_u";
        } else if (op === 0x4e) {
            text = "i32.ge_s";
        } else if (op === 0x4f) {
            text = "i32.ge_u";
        } else if (op >= 0x6a && op <= 0x78) {
            text = `numeric op 0x${op.toString(16)}`;
        } else if (op === 0x0b) {
            text = "end";
        } else if (op === 0x0f) {
            text = "return";
        } else if (op === 0x02 || op === 0x03 || op === 0x04) {
            if (p >= bytes.length) throw new Error("Missing blocktype");

            const first = bytes[p++];

            if (first === 0x40) {
                imm.push(first);
            } else if (first >= 0x40) {
                imm.push(first);
            } else {
                let val = first & 0x7f;
                let shift = 7;
                let b = first;

                while (b & 0x80) {
                    if (p >= bytes.length) throw new Error("Blocktype overflow");
                    b = bytes[p++];
                    val |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
                    shift += 7;
                }

                imm.push(val);
            }

            text = op === 0x02 ? "block" : op === 0x03 ? "loop" : "if";
        } else if (op === 0x05) {
            text = "else";
        } else if (op === 0x0c) {
            const r = uleb(bytes, p);
            imm.push(r.value);
            p = r.next;
            text = `br ${r.value}`;
        } else if (op === 0x0d) {
            const r = uleb(bytes, p);
            imm.push(r.value);
            p = r.next;
            text = `br_if ${r.value}`;
        } else if (op === 0x0e) {
            const r = uleb(bytes, p);
            p = r.next;

            for (let i = 0; i < r.value + 1; i++) {
                const x = uleb(bytes, p);
                p = x.next;
                imm.push(x.value);
            }

            text = "br_table";
        } else if (op === 0x28 || op === 0x29 ||
                   op === 0x2c || op === 0x2d ||
                   op === 0x2e || op === 0x2f ||
                   op === 0x30 || op === 0x31 ||
                   op === 0x32 || op === 0x33 ||
                   op === 0x34 || op === 0x35 ||
                   op === 0x36 || op === 0x37 ||
                   op === 0x38 || op === 0x39 ||
                   op === 0x3a || op === 0x3b ||
                   op === 0x3c || op === 0x3d ||
                   op === 0x3e || op === 0x3f ||
                   op === 0x40) {
            const a = uleb(bytes, p);
            p = a.next;

            const b = uleb(bytes, p);
            p = b.next;

            imm.push(a.value, b.value);
            text = `memory op 0x${op.toString(16)}`;
        } else if (op === 0x25 || op === 0x26) {
            const r = uleb(bytes, p);
            imm.push(r.value);
            p = r.next;
            text = op === 0x25 ? `table.get ${r.value}` : `table.set ${r.value}`;
        } else if (op === 0x24) {
            const r = uleb(bytes, p);
            imm.push(r.value);
            p = r.next;
            text = `global.set ${r.value}`;
        } else if (op === 0x23) {
            const r = uleb(bytes, p);
            imm.push(r.value);
            p = r.next;
            text = `global.get ${r.value}`;
        } else if (op === 0x28 || op === 0x29) {
            const a = uleb(bytes, p);
            p = a.next;
            const b = uleb(bytes, p);
            p = b.next;
            imm.push(a.value, b.value);
        }

        out.push({
            off,
            op,
            imm,
            size: p - start,
            text,
        });
    }

    return out;
}

function parseSections(wasm: Buffer) {
    let p = 8;

    let typeSec: { start: number; size: number } | undefined;
    let importSec: { start: number; size: number } | undefined;
    let funcSec: { start: number; size: number } | undefined;
    let codeSec: { start: number; size: number } | undefined;

    while (p < wasm.length) {
        const id = wasm[p++];

        const s = uleb(wasm, p);
        const size = s.value;
        p = s.next;

        const start = p;

        if (id === 1) typeSec = { start, size };
        if (id === 2) importSec = { start, size };
        if (id === 3) funcSec = { start, size };
        if (id === 10) codeSec = { start, size };

        p += size;
    }

    return { typeSec, importSec, funcSec, codeSec };
}

function parseTypes(wasm: Buffer, sec: { start: number; size: number }) {
    let p = sec.start;
    const end = p + sec.size;

    const v = readVec(wasm, p);
    const count = v.count;
    p = v.next;

    const types: { params: number[]; results: number[] }[] = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
        const form = wasm[p++];

        if (form !== 0x60) {
            throw new Error(`Unsupported type form ${form}`);
        }

        const pc = uleb(wasm, p);
        p = pc.next;

        const params = Array.from(wasm.subarray(p, p + pc.value));
        p += pc.value;

        const rc = uleb(wasm, p);
        p = rc.next;

        const results = Array.from(wasm.subarray(p, p + rc.value));
        p += rc.value;

        types.push({ params, results });
    }

    if (p !== end) {
        throw new Error(`Type section cursor mismatch ${p} != ${end}`);
    }

    return types;
}

function parseImports(wasm: Buffer, sec: { start: number; size: number }) {
    let p = sec.start;

    const v = readVec(wasm, p);
    const count = v.count;
    p = v.next;

    let importedFuncs = 0;

    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
        const mod = readName(wasm, p);
        p = mod.next;

        const name = readName(wasm, p);
        p = name.next;

        const kind = wasm[p++];

        if (kind === 0) {
            const r = uleb(wasm, p);
            p = r.next;
            importedFuncs++;
        } else if (kind === 1) {
            p += 4;
        } else if (kind === 2) {
            p += 1;
            const a = uleb(wasm, p);
            p = a.next;

            if (a.value === 0x40) {
                const b = uleb(wasm, p);
                p = b.next;
            }
        } else if (kind === 3) {
            p += 2;
        } else {
            throw new Error(`Unknown import kind ${kind}`);
        }
    }

    return importedFuncs;
}

function parseFunctionTypes(
    wasm: Buffer,
    sec: { start: number; size: number }
) {
    let p = sec.start;

    const v = readVec(wasm, p);
    const count = v.count;
    p = v.next;

    const types: number[] = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
        const r = uleb(wasm, p);
        types.push(r.value);
        p = r.next;
    }

    return types;
}

function parseCodeBodies(
    wasm: Buffer,
    sec: { start: number; size: number },
    importedFuncs: number
): Body[] {
    let p = sec.start;

    const v = readVec(wasm, p);
    const count = v.count;
    p = v.next;

    const bodies: Body[] = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
        const s = uleb(wasm, p);
        const size = s.value;
        p = s.next;

        const start = p;
        const end = p + size;

        bodies.push({
            func: importedFuncs + i,
            start,
            end,
            bytes: wasm.subarray(start, end),
        });

        p = end;
    }

    return bodies;
}

function cloneValue(v: Value): Value {
    if (v.kind === "const") {
        return {
            kind: "const",
            value: v.value,
            origin: v.origin,
        };
    }

    if (v.kind === "local") {
        return {
            kind: "local",
            index: v.index,
            origin: v.origin,
        };
    }

    return {
        kind: "unknown",
        origin: v.origin,
    };
}

function valueText(v: Value): string {
    if (v.kind === "const") {
        return `CONST(${v.value})`;
    }

    if (v.kind === "local") {
        return `LOCAL(${v.index})`;
    }

    return "UNKNOWN";
}

export async function run() {
    const wasm = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(" BACKWARD INDIRECT TARGET TRACE -> FUNCTION 4154");
    console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log();
    console.log(`WASM size       : ${wasm.length}`);
    console.log(`Target function : ${TARGET_FUNC}`);
    console.log(`Target slot     : 0x${TARGET_SLOT.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")} = ${TARGET_SLOT}`);
    console.log(`Target type     : ${TARGET_TYPE}`);
    console.log(`Target table    : ${TARGET_TABLE}`);
    console.log();

    const sections = parseSections(wasm);

    if (!sections.typeSec ||
        !sections.importSec ||
        !sections.funcSec ||
        !sections.codeSec) {
        throw new Error("Required sections missing");
    }

    const types = parseTypes(wasm, sections.typeSec);
    const importedFuncs = parseImports(wasm, sections.importSec);

    const funcTypes = parseFunctionTypes(wasm, sections.funcSec);

    const bodies = parseCodeBodies(
        wasm,
        sections.codeSec,
        importedFuncs
    );

    console.log(`Types          : ${types.length}`);
    console.log(`Imported funcs : ${importedFuncs}`);
    console.log(`Defined funcs  : ${funcTypes.length}`);
    console.log(`Code bodies    : ${bodies.length}`);
    console.log();

    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log("TARGET FUNCTION TYPE");
    console.log("============================================================");

    const targetDefinedIndex = TARGET_FUNC - importedFuncs;

    if (
        targetDefinedIndex < 0 ||
        targetDefinedIndex >= funcTypes.length
    ) {
        throw new Error("Target function outside defined function range");
    }

    const targetType = funcTypes[targetDefinedIndex];

    console.log(`Function ${TARGET_FUNC} type index: ${targetType}`);
    console.log(`Expected target type             : ${TARGET_TYPE}`);
    console.log();

    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log("DECODING CALL_INDIRECT SITES");
    console.log("============================================================");

    let decodedBodies = 0;
    let decodeErrors = 0;

    type Site = {
        func: number;
        body: Body;
        instrs: Instr[];
        index: number;
    };

    const sites: Site[] = [];

    for (const body of bodies) {
        try {
            const instrs = decodeInstructions(body.bytes, body.start);

            decodedBodies++;

            for (let i = 0; i < instrs.length; i++) {
                const ins = instrs[i];

                if (
                    ins.op === 0x11 &&
                    ins.imm[0] === TARGET_TYPE &&
                    ins.imm[1] === TARGET_TABLE
                ) {
                    sites.push({
                        func: body.func,
                        body,
                        instrs,
                        index: i,
                    });
                }
            }
        } catch {
            decodeErrors++;
        }
    }

    console.log(`Decoded bodies       : ${decodedBodies}`);
    console.log(`Decode errors        : ${decodeErrors}`);
    console.log(`Type-0/table-0 sites : ${sites.length}`);
    console.log();

    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log("BACKWARD TRACE");
    console.log("============================================================");

    let exact = 0;
    let localPaths = 0;
    let unknown = 0;

    for (const site of sites) {
        const instrs = site.instrs;
        const ci = site.index;

        let value: Value = {
            kind: "unknown",
            origin: "call_indirect-index",
        };

        let found = false;
        let trace: string[] = [];

        /*
         * The table index is the top stack value immediately
         * before call_indirect.
         *
         * We walk backwards through stack-producing instructions.
         */
        let need = 1;

        for (let i = ci - 1; i >= 0 && i >= ci - 80; i--) {
            const ins = instrs[i];

            if (ins.op === 0x41) {
                if (need === 1) {
                    value = {
                        kind: "const",
                        value: ins.imm[0],
                        origin: `0x${ins.off.toString(16)}`,
                    };

                    trace.push(
                        `${ins.text} @ 0x${ins.off.toString(16)}`
                    );

                    found = true;
                    break;
                }
            }

            if (ins.op === 0x20) {
                if (need === 1) {
                    const idx = ins.imm[0];

                    value = {
                        kind: "local",
                        index: idx,
                        origin: `0x${ins.off.toString(16)}`,
                    };

                    trace.push(
                        `${ins.text} @ 0x${ins.off.toString(16)}`
                    );

                    /*
                     * Continue backward searching for the latest
                     * local.set / tee of this local.
                     */
                    for (
                        let j = i - 1;
                        j >= 0 && j >= i - 120;
                        j--
                    ) {
                        const prev = instrs[j];

                        if (
                            (prev.op === 0x21 || prev.op === 0x22) &&
                            prev.imm[0] === idx
                        ) {
                            trace.push(
                                `${prev.text} @ 0x${prev.off.toString(16)}`
                            );

                            for (
                                let k = j - 1;
                                k >= 0 && k >= j - 80;
                                k--
                            ) {
                                const src = instrs[k];

                                if (src.op === 0x41) {
                                    value = {
                                        kind: "const",
                                        value: src.imm[0],
                                        origin: `0x${src.off.toString(16)}`,
                                    };

                                    trace.push(
                                        `${src.text} @ 0x${src.off.toString(16)}`
                                    );

                                    found = true;
                                    break;
                                }

                                if (src.op === 0x20) {
                                    trace.push(
                                        `${src.text} @ 0x${src.off.toString(16)}`
                                    );
                                    break;
                                }

                                if (
                                    src.op === 0x10 ||
                                    src.op === 0x11
                                ) {
                                    break;
                                }
                            }

                            break;
                        }
                    }

                    break;
                }
            }

            /*
             * A direct call can produce an i32 index, but without
             * a signature-aware interprocedural analysis we mark it
             * unknown rather than guessing.
             */
            if (ins.op === 0x10 || ins.op === 0x11) {
                break;
            }
        }

        if (found && value.kind === "const") {
            if (value.value === TARGET_SLOT) {
                exact++;

                console.log();
                console.log(
                    `*** EXACT TARGET CANDIDATE ***`
                );
                console.log(
                    `Caller function : ${site.func}`
                );
                console.log(
                    `call_indirect   : 0x${instrs[ci].off.toString(16)}`
                );
                console.log(
                    `Index value     : ${valueText(value)}`
                );

                for (const t of trace) {
                    console.log(`  <- ${t}`);
                }
            }
        } else if (value.kind === "local") {
            localPaths++;
        } else {
            unknown++;
        }
    }

    console.log();
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log("RESULT");
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(`Decoded bodies          : ${decodedBodies}`);
    console.log(`Decode errors           : ${decodeErrors}`);
    console.log(`Type-0/table-0 sites    : ${sites.length}`);
    console.log(`Exact CONST(114) paths  : ${exact}`);
    console.log(`Local-dependent paths   : ${localPaths}`);
    console.log(`Unknown paths           : ${unknown}`);
    console.log();

    if (exact > 0) {
        console.log(
            "TARGET FUNCTION 4154 HAS AT LEAST ONE BACKWARD-TRACE CANDIDATE."
        );
    } else {
        console.log(
            "NO EXACT CONST(114) BACKWARD PATH FOUND."
        );
        console.log(
            "This is NOT proof that Function 4154 has no indirect caller."
        );
        console.log(
            "Local/interprocedural or memory-derived indices remain."
        );
    }

    console.log();
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log("SAFETY / MODE");
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log("No transaction sent.");
    console.log("No WASM modification.");
    console.log("Read-only / offline.");
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\backtraceLocal4154.ts

import fs from "fs";

const WASM =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const TARGET = 114;

function uleb(b: Buffer, p: number) {
  let v = 0, s = 0;

  while (p < b.length) {
    const x = b[p++];

    v += (x & 127) * 2 ** s;

    if (!(x & 128))
      return { value: v, next: p };

    s += 7;
  }

  throw Error("ULEB");
}

function sleb(b: Buffer, p: number) {
  let v = 0, s = 0, x = 0;

  while (p < b.length) {
    x = b[p++];

    v |= (x & 127) << s;
    s += 7;

    if (!(x & 128))
      break;
  }

  if (s < 32 && (x & 64))
    v |= -1 << s;

  return { value: v, next: p };
}

function skipName(b: Buffer, p: number) {
  const n = uleb(b, p);
  return n.next + n.value;
}

function sections(b: Buffer): any {
  let p = 8;
  const r: any = {};

  while (p < b.length) {
    const id = b[p++];

    const s = uleb(b, p);
    p = s.next;

    r[id] = {
      start: p,
      size: s.value
    };

    p += s.value;
  }

  return r;
}

function importedFuncs(b: Buffer, sec: any) {
  let p = sec.start;

  const n = uleb(b, p);
  p = n.next;

  let count = 0;

  for (let i = 0; i < n.value; i++) {
    p = skipName(b, p);
    p = skipName(b, p);

    const kind = b[p++];

    if (kind === 0) {
      p = uleb(b, p).next;
      count++;
    }
    else if (kind === 1 || kind === 2) {
      const f = uleb(b, p);
      p = f.next;

      p = uleb(b, p).next;

      if (f.value & 1)
        p = uleb(b, p).next;
    }
    else if (kind === 3) {
      p += 2;
    }
  }

  return count;
}

function getBodies(
  b: Buffer,
  sec: any,
  imported: number
) {
  let p = sec.start;

  const n = uleb(b, p);
  p = n.next;

  const out: any[] = [];

  for (let i = 0; i < n.value; i++) {
    const s = uleb(b, p);
    p = s.next;

    out.push({
      func: imported + i,
      start: p,
      end: p + s.value,
      bytes: b.subarray(p, p + s.value)
    });

    p += s.value;
  }

  return out;
}

type I = {
  off: number;
  op: number;
  imm: number[];
  text: string;
};

function decode(b: Buffer, base: number): I[] {
  const a: I[] = [];

  let p = 0;

  while (p < b.length) {
    const off = base + p;
    const op = b[p++];

    const imm: number[] = [];
    let text = `op=0x${op.toString(16)}`;

    if (op === 0x41) {
      const r = sleb(b, p);
      p = r.next;

      imm.push(r.value);
      text = `i32.const ${r.value}`;
    }

    else if (
      op === 0x20 ||
      op === 0x21 ||
      op === 0x22
    ) {
      const r = uleb(b, p);
      p = r.next;

      imm.push(r.value);

      text =
        op === 0x20
          ? `local.get ${r.value}`
          : op === 0x21
          ? `local.set ${r.value}`
          : `local.tee ${r.value}`;
    }

    else if (op === 0x23 || op === 0x24) {
      const r = uleb(b, p);
      p = r.next;

      imm.push(r.value);

      text =
        op === 0x23
          ? `global.get ${r.value}`
          : `global.set ${r.value}`;
    }

    else if (op === 0x10) {
      const r = uleb(b, p);
      p = r.next;

      imm.push(r.value);
      text = `call ${r.value}`;
    }

    else if (op === 0x11) {
      const t = uleb(b, p);
      p = t.next;

      const tab = uleb(b, p);
      p = tab.next;

      imm.push(t.value, tab.value);

      text =
        `call_indirect type=${t.value} table=${tab.value}`;
    }

    else if (
      op >= 0x28 &&
      op <= 0x3e
    ) {
      const al = uleb(b, p);
      p = al.next;

      const of = uleb(b, p);
      p = of.next;

      imm.push(al.value, of.value);

      text = `load offset=${of.value}`;
    }

    else if (op === 0x6a)
      text = "i32.add";

    else if (op === 0x6b)
      text = "i32.sub";

    else if (op === 0x6c)
      text = "i32.mul";

    else if (op === 0x1a)
      text = "drop";

    else if (op === 0x0f)
      text = "return";

    else if (op === 0x0b)
      text = "end";

    else if (op === 0x05)
      text = "else";

    else if (
      op === 0x02 ||
      op === 0x03 ||
      op === 0x04
    ) {
      if (p < b.length)
        p++;

      text =
        op === 0x02
          ? "block"
          : op === 0x03
          ? "loop"
          : "if";
    }

    else if (
      op === 0x0c ||
      op === 0x0d
    ) {
      const r = uleb(b, p);
      p = r.next;

      imm.push(r.value);

      text =
        op === 0x0c
          ? `br ${r.value}`
          : `br_if ${r.value}`;
    }

    a.push({
      off,
      op,
      imm,
      text
    });
  }

  return a;
}

function show(
  a: I[],
  i: number
) {
  for (
    let j = Math.max(0, i - 10);
    j <= Math.min(a.length - 1, i + 2);
    j++
  ) {
    console.log(
      `${j === i ? " >>>" : "    "} ` +
      `0x${a[j].off.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")} ` +
      a[j].text
    );
  }
}

export async function run() {
  const wasm = fs.readFileSync(WASM);
  const sec = sections(wasm);

  const imported = importedFuncs(
    wasm,
    sec[2]
  );

  const bodies = getBodies(
    wasm,
    sec[10],
    imported
  );

  console.log(
    "============================================================"
  );
  console.log(
    " BACKTRACE LOCAL -> INDIRECT INDEX 114"
  );
  console.log(
    " READ ONLY - OFFLINE"
  );
  console.log(
    "============================================================"
  );

  console.log(`WASM size : ${wasm.length}`);
  console.log(`Target    : ${TARGET}`);
  console.log();

  let sites = 0;
  let locals = 0;
  let const114 = 0;
  let constOther = 0;
  let loadSource = 0;
  let localSource = 0;
  let callSource = 0;
  let arithmeticSource = 0;
  let unknownSource = 0;

  for (const body of bodies) {
    let a: I[];

    try {
      a = decode(
        body.bytes,
        body.start
      );
    }
    catch {
      continue;
    }

    for (let i = 0; i < a.length; i++) {
      const c = a[i];

      if (
        c.op !== 0x11 ||
        c.imm[0] !== 0 ||
        c.imm[1] !== 0
      )
        continue;

      if (i < 1)
        continue;

      const outer = a[i - 1];

      if (
        outer.op < 0x28 ||
        outer.op > 0x3e
      )
        continue;

      if (i < 3)
        continue;

      const inner = a[i - 2];
      const base = a[i - 3];

      if (
        inner.op < 0x28 ||
        inner.op > 0x3e ||
        base.op !== 0x20
      )
        continue;

      sites++;
      locals++;

      const localId = base.imm[0];

      /*
       * Search backward for the nearest definition
       * of this local.
       */
      let def = -1;

      for (
        let j = i - 4;
        j >= 0;
        j--
      ) {
        if (
          (a[j].op === 0x21 ||
           a[j].op === 0x22) &&
          a[j].imm[0] === localId
        ) {
          def = j;
          break;
        }
      }

      if (def < 0) {
        unknownSource++;

        console.log(
          `NO LOCAL DEF: func=${body.func} ` +
          `local=${localId} ` +
          `call=0x${c.off.toString(16)}`
        );

        continue;
      }

      /*
       * The value assigned to local is normally
       * the stack value immediately before local.set/tee.
       */
      const src = def - 1;

      if (src < 0) {
        unknownSource++;
        continue;
      }

      const s = a[src];

      if (
        s.op === 0x41 &&
        s.imm[0] === TARGET
      ) {
        const114++;

        console.log(
          `FOUND 114: func=${body.func} ` +
          `local=${localId} ` +
          `def=0x${a[def].off.toString(16)} ` +
          `call=0x${c.off.toString(16)}`
        );

        show(a, def);
        continue;
      }

      if (s.op === 0x41) {
        constOther++;

        console.log(
          `CONST SOURCE: func=${body.func} ` +
          `local=${localId} ` +
          `value=${s.imm[0]} ` +
          `call=0x${c.off.toString(16)}`
        );

        continue;
      }

      if (
        s.op >= 0x28 &&
        s.op <= 0x3e
      ) {
        loadSource++;

        console.log(
          `LOAD SOURCE: func=${body.func} ` +
          `local=${localId} ` +
          `offset=${s.imm[1]} ` +
          `call=0x${c.off.toString(16)}`
        );

        continue;
      }

      if (s.op === 0x20) {
        localSource++;

        console.log(
          `LOCAL SOURCE: func=${body.func} ` +
          `local=${localId} ` +
          `fromLocal=${s.imm[0]} ` +
          `call=0x${c.off.toString(16)}`
        );

        continue;
      }

      if (s.op === 0x10) {
        callSource++;

        console.log(
          `CALL SOURCE: func=${body.func} ` +
          `local=${localId} ` +
          `callFunc=${s.imm[0]} ` +
          `call=0x${c.off.toString(16)}`
        );

        continue;
      }

      if (
        s.op === 0x6a ||
        s.op === 0x6b ||
        s.op === 0x6c
      ) {
        arithmeticSource++;

        console.log(
          `ARITH SOURCE: func=${body.func} ` +
          `local=${localId} ` +
          `op=${s.text} ` +
          `call=0x${c.off.toString(16)}`
        );

        continue;
      }

      unknownSource++;
    }
  }

  console.log();

  console.log(
    "============================================================"
  );
  console.log("SUMMARY");
  console.log(
    "============================================================"
  );

  console.log(
    `Local-base sites     : ${sites}`
  );

  console.log(
    `CONST(114) source    : ${const114}`
  );

  console.log(
    `Other CONST source  : ${constOther}`
  );

  console.log(
    `LOAD source         : ${loadSource}`
  );

  console.log(
    `LOCAL source        : ${localSource}`
  );

  console.log(
    `CALL source         : ${callSource}`
  );

  console.log(
    `Arithmetic source   : ${arithmeticSource}`
  );

  console.log(
    `Unknown source      : ${unknownSource}`
  );

  console.log();

  console.log(
    "NEXT: resolve LOAD/CALL sources that feed the local."
  );
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\backtraceMemoryIndex4154.ts
====================================================================================================
import fs from "fs";

const WASM =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const TARGET_TYPE = 0;
const TARGET_TABLE = 0;

type I = {
  off: number;
  op: number;
  imm: number[];
  text: string;
};

function uleb(b: Buffer, p: number) {
  let v = 0, s = 0;
  while (p < b.length) {
    const x = b[p++];
    v += (x & 127) * 2 ** s;
    if (!(x & 128)) return { value: v, next: p };
    s += 7;
  }
  throw new Error("ULEB");
}

function sleb(b: Buffer, p: number) {
  let v = 0, s = 0, x = 0;
  while (p < b.length) {
    x = b[p++];
    v |= (x & 127) << s;
    s += 7;
    if (!(x & 128)) break;
  }
  if (s < 32 && (x & 64)) v |= -1 << s;
  return { value: v, next: p };
}

function name(b: Buffer, p: number) {
  const n = uleb(b, p);
  return {
    next: n.next + n.value,
    value: b.subarray(n.next, n.next + n.value).toString()
  };
}

function sections(b: Buffer) {
  let p = 8;
  const r: any = {};

  while (p < b.length) {
    const id = b[p++];
    const s = uleb(b, p);
    p = s.next;

    r[id] = { start: p, size: s.value };
    p += s.value;
  }

  return r;
}

function imports(b: Buffer, sec: any) {
  let p = sec.start;
  const n = uleb(b, p);
  p = n.next;

  let count = 0;

  for (let i = 0; i < n.value; i++) {
    p = name(b, p).next;
    p = name(b, p).next;

    const kind = b[p++];

    if (kind === 0) {
      p = uleb(b, p).next;
      count++;
    } else if (kind === 1) {
      p++;
      const f = uleb(b, p); p = f.next;
      p = uleb(b, p).next;
      if (f.value & 1) p = uleb(b, p).next;
    } else if (kind === 2) {
      const f = uleb(b, p); p = f.next;
      p = uleb(b, p).next;
      if (f.value & 1) p = uleb(b, p).next;
    } else if (kind === 3) {
      p += 2;
    }
  }

  return count;
}

function funcTypes(b: Buffer, sec: any) {
  let p = sec.start;
  const n = uleb(b, p);
  p = n.next;

  const a: number[] = [];

  for (let i = 0; i < n.value; i++) {
    const r = uleb(b, p);
    a.push(r.value);
    p = r.next;
  }

  return a;
}

function bodies(b: Buffer, sec: any, imported: number) {
  let p = sec.start;
  const n = uleb(b, p);
  p = n.next;

  const a: any[] = [];

  for (let i = 0; i < n.value; i++) {
    const s = uleb(b, p);
    p = s.next;

    a.push({
      func: imported + i,
      start: p,
      end: p + s.value,
      bytes: b.subarray(p, p + s.value)
    });

    p += s.value;
  }

  return a;
}

function decode(b: Buffer, base: number): I[] {
  const out: I[] = [];
  let p = 0;

  while (p < b.length) {
    const off = base + p;
    const op = b[p++];
    const imm: number[] = [];
    let text = `op=0x${op.toString(16)}`;

    if (op === 0x41) {
      const r = sleb(b, p);
      p = r.next;
      imm.push(r.value);
      text = `i32.const ${r.value}`;
    }
    else if (op === 0x20 || op === 0x21 || op === 0x22) {
      const r = uleb(b, p);
      p = r.next;
      imm.push(r.value);
      text =
        op === 0x20 ? `local.get ${r.value}` :
        op === 0x21 ? `local.set ${r.value}` :
        `local.tee ${r.value}`;
    }
    else if (op === 0x10) {
      const r = uleb(b, p);
      p = r.next;
      imm.push(r.value);
      text = `call ${r.value}`;
    }
    else if (op === 0x11) {
      const a = uleb(b, p); p = a.next;
      const t = uleb(b, p); p = t.next;
      imm.push(a.value, t.value);
      text = `call_indirect type=${a.value} table=${t.value}`;
    }
    else if (op >= 0x28 && op <= 0x3e) {
      const a = uleb(b, p); p = a.next;
      const o = uleb(b, p); p = o.next;
      imm.push(a.value, o.value);

      const names: Record<number,string> = {
        0x28:"i32.load",
        0x29:"i64.load",
        0x2c:"i32.load8_s",
        0x2d:"i32.load8_u",
        0x2e:"i32.load16_s",
        0x2f:"i32.load16_u"
      };

      text = `${names[op] ?? "memory"} offset=${o.value}`;
    }
    else if (op === 0x23) {
      const r = uleb(b, p);
      p = r.next;
      imm.push(r.value);
      text = `global.get ${r.value}`;
    }
    else if (op === 0x24) {
      const r = uleb(b, p);
      p = r.next;
      imm.push(r.value);
      text = `global.set ${r.value}`;
    }
    else if (op === 0x6a) text = "i32.add";
    else if (op === 0x6b) text = "i32.sub";
    else if (op === 0x6c) text = "i32.mul";
    else if (op === 0x1a) text = "drop";
    else if (op === 0x0b) text = "end";
    else if (op === 0x05) text = "else";
    else if (op === 0x04) {
      if (p >= b.length) throw new Error("if");
      p++;
      text = "if";
    }
    else if (op === 0x02 || op === 0x03) {
      if (p >= b.length) throw new Error("block");
      p++;
      text = op === 0x02 ? "block" : "loop";
    }
    else if (op === 0x0c || op === 0x0d) {
      const r = uleb(b, p);
      p = r.next;
      imm.push(r.value);
      text = op === 0x0c ? `br ${r.value}` : `br_if ${r.value}`;
    }
    else if (op === 0x0f) text = "return";
    else if (op >= 0x45 && op <= 0x4f)
      text = "i32.compare";
    else if (op >= 0x6d && op <= 0x78)
      text = "i32.arith";

    if (p <= off - base) throw new Error("stalled");

    out.push({ off, op, imm, text });
  }

  return out;
}

function window(a: I[], i: number, n = 6) {
  for (
    let j = Math.max(0, i - n);
    j <= Math.min(a.length - 1, i + n);
    j++
  ) {
    console.log(
      `${j === i ? " >>>" : "    "} ` +
      `0x${a[j].off.toString(16).padStart(8,"0")} ${a[j].text}`
    );
  }
}

export async function run() {
  const wasm = fs.readFileSync(WASM);
  const s = sections(wasm);

  const imported = imports(wasm, s[2]);
  const types = funcTypes(wasm, s[3]);
  const bs = bodies(wasm, s[10], imported);

  console.log("============================================================");
  console.log(" BACKTRACE MEMORY INDEX -> TYPE 0 / TABLE 0");
  console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
  console.log("============================================================");
  console.log(`WASM size      : ${wasm.length}`);
  console.log(`Imported funcs : ${imported}`);
  console.log(`Defined funcs  : ${types.length}`);
  console.log(`Bodies         : ${bs.length}`);
  console.log();

  let sites = 0;
  let memorySites = 0;
  let const114 = 0;
  let localSources = 0;
  let arithmeticSources = 0;

  const results: any[] = [];

  for (const body of bs) {
    let a: I[];

    try {
      a = decode(body.bytes, body.start);
    } catch {
      continue;
    }

    for (let i = 0; i < a.length; i++) {
      if (
        a[i].op !== 0x11 ||
        a[i].imm[0] !== TARGET_TYPE ||
        a[i].imm[1] !== TARGET_TABLE
      ) continue;

      sites++;

      const prev = a[i - 1];

      if (!prev) continue;

      if (prev.op === 0x41 && prev.imm[0] === 114) {
        const114++;
        continue;
      }

      if (
        prev.op >= 0x28 &&
        prev.op <= 0x3e
      ) {
        memorySites++;

        let source = "UNKNOWN";

        const before = a[i - 2];

        if (before) {
          if (before.op === 0x20 || before.op === 0x22)
            source = `local ${before.imm[0]}`;

          else if (
            before.op === 0x41
          )
            source = `const ${before.imm[0]}`;

          else if (
            before.op === 0x6a ||
            before.op === 0x6b ||
            before.op === 0x6c
          )
            source = before.text;
        }

        if (source.startsWith("local"))
          localSources++;

        if (
          source === "i32.add" ||
          source === "i32.sub" ||
          source === "i32.mul"
        )
          arithmeticSources++;

        results.push({
          func: body.func,
          call: a[i].off,
          load: prev.off,
          offset: prev.imm[1],
          source
        });
      }
    }
  }

  console.log("SUMMARY");
  console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
  console.log(`call_indirect sites : ${sites}`);
  console.log(`direct CONST(114)   : ${const114}`);
  console.log(`memory index loads  : ${memorySites}`);
  console.log(`local-derived base  : ${localSources}`);
  console.log(`arithmetic base     : ${arithmeticSources}`);
  console.log();

  console.log("FIRST 50 MEMORY INDEX CHAINS");
  console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

  for (const r of results.slice(0, 50)) {
    console.log(
      `func=${r.func} ` +
      `load=0x${r.load.toString(16)} ` +
      `offset=${r.offset} ` +
      `call=0x${r.call.toString(16)} ` +
      `base=${r.source}`
    );
  }

  console.log();
  console.log("DETAILED FIRST 10");
  console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

  let shown = 0;

  for (const body of bs) {
    if (shown >= 10) break;

    let a: I[];

    try {
      a = decode(body.bytes, body.start);
    } catch {
      continue;
    }

    for (let i = 0; i < a.length && shown < 10; i++) {
      if (
        a[i].op === 0x11 &&
        a[i].imm[0] === 0 &&
        a[i].imm[1] === 0 &&
        i > 0 &&
        a[i-1].op >= 0x28 &&
        a[i-1].op <= 0x3e
      ) {
        console.log();
        console.log(`FUNCTION ${body.func}`);
        window(a, i);
        shown++;
      }
    }
  }

  console.log();
  console.log("============================================================");
  console.log("READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION - NO WASM MODIFICATION");
  console.log("============================================================");
}

import { Address } from '@ton/core';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';
import { DEX } from '@ston-fi/sdk';

const ROOSTER_MASTER = Address.parse(
    'EQCyuv-cYXFibA05bhiL3seM3Ur2B_bpsc364Zb8F3NMtrYx'
);

const PTON_MASTER = Address.parse(
    'EQBnGWMCf3-FZZq1W4IWcWiGAc3PHuZ0_H-7sad2oY00o83S'
);

const ROUTER = Address.parse(
    'EQCS4UEa5UaJLzOyyKieqQOQ2P9M-7kXpkO5HnP3Bv250cN3'
);

const USER = Address.parse(
    'EQDxn5MLbFyV649-OBviQrMp5KqWjGHNVwFM6GTeaLl8N0Ix'
);

const EXPECTED_USER_JETTON_WALLET = Address.parse(
    'EQBzQOOJu9qQVli2R0o6p3pXvi_wj9O2ncMhv3UyAjvMiMOh'
);

const EXPECTED_ROUTER_PTON_WALLET = Address.parse(
    'EQCSIMGBps_qzRG3uPYhON8bucyCtu0mYdL1-u4gSz77IBa3'
);

const ROOSTER_AMOUNT = 200073535827652n;
const MIN_LP_OUT = 300329052918n;

const GAS_AMOUNT = 300000000n;
const FORWARD_GAS_AMOUNT = 240000000n;

const TRANSFER_OPCODE = 0x0f8a7ea5;
const PROVIDE_LP_OPCODE = 0x37c096df;

function assertEqual(
    label: string,
    actual: string,
    expected: string
) {
    if (actual !== expected) {
        throw new Error(
            `${label} FAILED\n` +
            `Expected: ${expected}\n` +
            `Actual:   ${actual}`
        );
    }

    console.log(`${label}: PASS`);
}

function assertBigInt(
    label: string,
    actual: bigint,
    expected: bigint
) {
    if (actual !== expected) {
        throw new Error(
            `${label} FAILED\n` +
            `Expected: ${expected}\n` +
            `Actual:   ${actual}`
        );
    }

    console.log(`${label}: PASS`);
}

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {
    console.log('=================================================');
    console.log(' STON.fi v2.2 SDK LIQUIDITY TX BUILDER');
    console.log(' BUILD + DECODE ONLY');
    console.log(' READ-ONLY - NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT');
    console.log('=================================================');

    console.log('\n===== ADDRESSES =====');

    console.log('Router:');
    console.log(ROUTER.toString());

    console.log('\nROOSTER Master:');
    console.log(ROOSTER_MASTER.toString());

    console.log('\npTON Master:');
    console.log(PTON_MASTER.toString());

    console.log('\nUser:');
    console.log(USER.toString());

    console.log('\nExpected user ROOSTER wallet:');
    console.log(
        EXPECTED_USER_JETTON_WALLET.toString()
    );

    console.log('\nExpected router pTON wallet:');
    console.log(
        EXPECTED_ROUTER_PTON_WALLET.toString()
    );

    console.log('\n===== SDK ROUTER =====');

    const router =
        DEX.v2_2.Router.CPI.create(ROUTER);

    console.log('SDK Router created: PASS');

    console.log('\n===== FRESH DEADLINE =====');

    const now = Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000);
    const deadline = now + 900;

    console.log(
        'Current Unix time:',
        now
    );

    console.log(
        'Deadline:',
        deadline
    );

    console.log('\n===== BUILD TRANSACTION =====');

    const routerProvider =
        provider.provider(ROUTER);

    const txParams =
        await router.getProvideLiquidityJettonTxParams(
            routerProvider,
            {
                userWalletAddress: USER,
                sendTokenAddress: ROOSTER_MASTER,
                otherTokenAddress: PTON_MASTER,
                sendAmount: ROOSTER_AMOUNT,
                minLpOut: MIN_LP_OUT,
                deadline,
                gasAmount: GAS_AMOUNT,
                forwardGasAmount: FORWARD_GAS_AMOUNT,
                transferExcessAddress: USER,
            }
        );

    console.log(
        'SDK transaction built: PASS'
    );

    console.log('\n===== TX PARAMS =====');

    console.log(
        'Destination:',
        txParams.to.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'Value:',
        BigInt(txParams.value).toString()
    );

    if (!txParams.body) {
        throw new Error(
            'Transaction body is missing.'
        );
    }

    const body = txParams.body;

    console.log(
        'Body bits:',
        body.bits.length
    );

    console.log(
        'Body refs:',
        body.refs.length
    );

    console.log('\n===== BASIC GUARDS =====');

    assertEqual(
        'Destination',
        txParams.to.toRawString(),
        EXPECTED_USER_JETTON_WALLET.toRawString()
    );

    assertBigInt(
        'Gas',
        BigInt(txParams.value),
        GAS_AMOUNT
    );

    if (body.bits.length !== 720) {
        throw new Error(
            `Unexpected body bits: ${body.bits.length}`
        );
    }

    if (body.refs.length !== 1) {
        throw new Error(
            `Unexpected body refs: ${body.refs.length}`
        );
    }

    console.log(
        'Body structure: PASS'
    );

    console.log('\n===== DECODE TRANSFER =====');

    const parser = body.beginParse();

    const transferOpcode =
        parser.loadUint(32);

    const queryId =
        parser.loadUintBig(64);

    const amount =
        parser.loadCoins();

    const destination =
        parser.loadAddress();

    const responseDestination =
        parser.loadAddress();

    const transferCustomPayloadPresent =
        parser.loadBit();

    if (transferCustomPayloadPresent) {
        parser.loadRef();
    }

    const forwardAmount =
        parser.loadCoins();

    const forwardPayload =
        parser.loadRef();

    console.log(
        'Transfer opcode:',
        `0x${transferOpcode.toString(16)}`
    );

    console.log(
        'Query ID:',
        queryId.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'Amount:',
        amount.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'Destination:',
        destination.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'Response destination:',
        responseDestination.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'Custom payload:',
        transferCustomPayloadPresent
    );

    console.log(
        'Forward TON:',
        forwardAmount.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        '\n===== DECODE PROVIDE_LP ====='
    );

    const provideParser =
        forwardPayload.beginParse();

    const provideOpcode =
        provideParser.loadUint(32);

    const routerWallet =
        provideParser.loadAddress();

    const refundAddress =
        provideParser.loadAddress();

    const excessesAddress =
        provideParser.loadAddress();

    const encodedDeadline =
        provideParser.loadUintBig(64);

    /*
     * STON.fi v2 provide_lp_body is stored
     * in a separate reference.
     */
    const provideBody =
        provideParser.loadRef();

    const provideBodyParser =
        provideBody.beginParse();

    const minLpOut =
        provideBodyParser.loadCoins();

    const receiver =
        provideBodyParser.loadAddress();

    const bothPositive =
        provideBodyParser.loadBit();

    const forwardGas =
        provideBodyParser.loadCoins();

    const provideCustomPayloadPresent =
        provideBodyParser.loadBit();

    if (provideCustomPayloadPresent) {
        provideBodyParser.loadRef();
    }

    console.log(
        'Provide LP opcode:',
        `0x${provideOpcode.toString(16)}`
    );

    console.log(
        'Router pTON wallet:',
        routerWallet.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'Refund:',
        refundAddress.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'Excesses:',
        excessesAddress.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'Deadline:',
        encodedDeadline.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'minLpOut:',
        minLpOut.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'Receiver:',
        receiver.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'bothPositive:',
        bothPositive
    );

    console.log(
        'Forward gas:',
        forwardGas.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'Provide custom payload:',
        provideCustomPayloadPresent
    );

    console.log(
        '\n===== FINAL CONTENT GUARDS ====='
    );

    assertEqual(
        'Transfer opcode',
        `0x${transferOpcode.toString(16)}`,
        `0x${TRANSFER_OPCODE.toString(16)}`
    );

    assertBigInt(
        'Query ID',
        queryId,
        0n
    );

    assertBigInt(
        'ROOSTER amount',
        amount,
        ROOSTER_AMOUNT
    );

    assertEqual(
        'Destination Router',
        destination.toRawString(),
        ROUTER.toRawString()
    );

    assertEqual(
        'Response destination',
        responseDestination.toRawString(),
        USER.toRawString()
    );

    assertBigInt(
        'Forward TON',
        forwardAmount,
        FORWARD_GAS_AMOUNT
    );

    assertEqual(
        'Provide LP opcode',
        `0x${provideOpcode.toString(16)}`,
        `0x${PROVIDE_LP_OPCODE.toString(16)}`
    );

    assertEqual(
        'Router pTON wallet',
        routerWallet.toRawString(),
        EXPECTED_ROUTER_PTON_WALLET.toRawString()
    );

    assertEqual(
        'Refund address',
        refundAddress.toRawString(),
        USER.toRawString()
    );

    assertEqual(
        'Excesses address',
        excessesAddress.toRawString(),
        USER.toRawString()
    );

    assertBigInt(
        'Deadline',
        encodedDeadline,
        BigInt(deadline)
    );

    assertBigInt(
        'minLpOut',
        minLpOut,
        MIN_LP_OUT
    );

    assertEqual(
        'Receiver',
        receiver.toRawString(),
        USER.toRawString()
    );

    if (bothPositive !== true) {
        throw new Error(
            'bothPositive FAILED'
        );
    }

    console.log(
        'bothPositive: PASS'
    );

    console.log(
        '\nForward gas decoded:',
        forwardGas.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        '\nProvide custom payload:',
        provideCustomPayloadPresent
    );

    console.log('\n=================================================');
    console.log(' ALL SDK TX GUARDS PASSED');
    console.log('=================================================');

    console.log(
        '\nNO TRANSACTION WAS SENT.'
    );

    console.log(
        'NO sender.send() EXISTS IN THIS FILE.'
    );

    console.log(
        '================================================='
    );
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\buildDirectAddLiquidity.ts
====================================================================================================
import { Address } from '@ton/core';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';
import { DEX } from '@ston-fi/sdk';
import * as fs from 'fs';

const POOL = Address.parse(
    'EQD5ALaCuEqJ7Mi9qlXOA7BHMrTq8e2fZ5B_teWN17MZq-cf'
);

const USER = Address.parse(
    'EQDxn5MLbFyV649-OBviQrMp5KqWjGHNVwFM6GTeaLl8N0Ix'
);

const AMOUNT_ROOSTER = 500000000000000n;
const AMOUNT_PTON = 500000000n;

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {
    console.log('');
    console.log('==========================================');
    console.log(' DIRECT ADD LIQUIDITY BUILD ONLY');
    console.log(' READ-ONLY / NO TRANSACTION SENT');
    console.log('==========================================');
    console.log('');

    const pool = provider.open(
        DEX.v2_2.Pool.CPI.create(POOL)
    );

    const lpAccount = await pool.getLpAccount({
        ownerAddress: USER
    });

    console.log('LP Account:');
    console.log(lpAccount.address.toString());
    console.log('');

    const contractProvider = provider.provider(
        lpAccount.address
    );

    const data = await lpAccount.getLpAccountData(
        contractProvider
    );

    console.log('===== CURRENT LP ACCOUNT DATA =====');
    console.log('User:', data.userAddress.toString());
    console.log('Pool:', data.poolAddress.toString());
    console.log('Amount 0:', data.amount0.toString());
    console.log('Amount 1:', data.amount1.toString());
    console.log('');

    if (data.amount0 !== AMOUNT_ROOSTER) {
        throw new Error(
            `Unexpected amount0. Expected ${AMOUNT_ROOSTER}, got ${data.amount0}`
        );
    }

    if (data.amount1 !== AMOUNT_PTON) {
        throw new Error(
            `Unexpected amount1. Expected ${AMOUNT_PTON}, got ${data.amount1}`
        );
    }

    const tx = await lpAccount.getDirectAddLiquidityTxParams(
        contractProvider,
        {
            amount0: AMOUNT_ROOSTER,
            amount1: AMOUNT_PTON,
            minimumLpToMint: 1n,
            userWalletAddress: USER,
            refundAddress: USER,
            excessesAddress: USER
        }
    );

    const boc = tx.body
        ? tx.body.toBoc().toString('base64')
        : '';

    fs.writeFileSync(
        'current_direct_add_liquidity.boc',
        boc
    );

    console.log('===== TRANSACTION BUILD =====');
    console.log('TO:', tx.to.toString());
    console.log('VALUE:', tx.value.toString());
    console.log(
        'VALUE TON:',
        Number(tx.value) / 1e9
    );
    console.log('');

    console.log('Amount 0:', AMOUNT_ROOSTER.toString());
    console.log(
        'Amount 0 ROOSTER:',
        Number(AMOUNT_ROOSTER) / 1e9
    );

    console.log('Amount 1:', AMOUNT_PTON.toString());
    console.log(
        'Amount 1 pTON:',
        Number(AMOUNT_PTON) / 1e9
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log('Minimum LP to mint: 1');
    console.log('');
    console.log('BODY BOC:');
    console.log(boc);
    console.log('');

    console.log(
        'Saved: current_direct_add_liquidity.boc'
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log('==========================================');
    console.log(' BUILD COMPLETED');
    console.log(' NO TRANSACTION SENT');
    console.log('==========================================');
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\buildExactSdkMatchedBocV22.ts
====================================================================================================
import { Address, beginCell, Cell } from "@ton/core";
import * as fs from "fs";

const USER_WALLET =
  "EQDxn5MLbFyV649-OBviQrMp5KqWjGHNVwFM6GTeaLl8N0Ix";

const ROUTER =
  "EQCS4UEa5UaJLzOyyKieqQOQ2P9M-7kXpkO5HnP3Bv250cN3";

const ROUTER_PTON_WALLET =
  "EQCSIMGBps_qzRG3uPYhON8bucyCtu0mYdL1-u4gSz77IBa3";

const ROOSTER_AMOUNT = 200073535827652n;
const MIN_LP_OUT = 300329052918n;

const FORWARD_TON_AMOUNT = 240000000n;

const DEADLINE = 1790586870;

const JETTON_TRANSFER_OPCODE = 0x0f8a7ea5;
const PROVIDE_LP_OPCODE = 0x37c096df;

const OUTPUT_FILE =
  "./exact_sdk_matched_v22_rooster_liquidity.boc";

function hashCell(cell: Cell): string {
  return cell.hash().toString("hex");
}

export async function run(provider: any) {
  console.log("=================================================");
  console.log(" STON.fi v2.2 EXACT SDK-MATCHED BOC BUILDER");
  console.log(" READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT");
  console.log("=================================================");

  const user = Address.parse(USER_WALLET);
  const router = Address.parse(ROUTER);
  const routerPtonWallet =
    Address.parse(ROUTER_PTON_WALLET);

  console.log("\n===== VALUES =====");

  console.log("ROOSTER amount:");
  console.log(ROOSTER_AMOUNT.toString());

  console.log("minLpOut:");
  console.log(MIN_LP_OUT.toString());

  console.log("forwardTonAmount:");
  console.log(FORWARD_TON_AMOUNT.toString());

  console.log("deadline:");
  console.log(DEADLINE.toString());

  console.log("router:");
  console.log(router.toString());

  console.log("routerWalletAddress:");
  console.log(routerPtonWallet.toString());

  console.log("user:");
  console.log(user.toString());

  /*
   * SDK createProvideLiquidityBody():
   *
   * opcode
   * routerWalletAddress
   * refundAddress
   * excessesAddress
   * deadline
   * reference:
   *   minLpOut
   *   receiverAddress
   *   bothPositive
   *   dexCustomPayloadForwardGasAmount
   *   dexCustomPayload
   */

  const provideLpParameters = beginCell()
    .storeCoins(MIN_LP_OUT)
    .storeAddress(user)
    .storeUint(1, 1)
    .storeCoins(0n)
    .storeMaybeRef(null)
    .endCell();

  const provideLpBody = beginCell()
    .storeUint(PROVIDE_LP_OPCODE, 32)
    .storeAddress(routerPtonWallet)
    .storeAddress(user)
    .storeAddress(user)
    .storeUint(DEADLINE, 64)
    .storeRef(provideLpParameters)
    .endCell();

  /*
   * SDK createJettonTransferMessage():
   *
   * opcode
   * queryId
   * amount
   * destination
   * responseDestination
   * customPayload
   * forwardTonAmount
   * forwardPayload as reference
   */

  const jettonTransferBody = beginCell()
    .storeUint(JETTON_TRANSFER_OPCODE, 32)
    .storeUint(0, 64)
    .storeCoins(ROOSTER_AMOUNT)
    .storeAddress(router)
    .storeAddress(user)
    .storeMaybeRef(null)
    .storeCoins(FORWARD_TON_AMOUNT)
.storeBit(1)
.storeRef(provideLpBody)
.endCell();

  const boc = jettonTransferBody.toBoc();
  const base64 = boc.toString("base64");

  fs.writeFileSync(
    OUTPUT_FILE,
    base64,
    "utf8"
  );

  console.log("\n===== RESULT =====");

  console.log("Root bits:");
  console.log(jettonTransferBody.bits.length);

  console.log("Root refs:");
  console.log(jettonTransferBody.refs.length);

  console.log("Root SHA256:");
  console.log(hashCell(jettonTransferBody));

  console.log("BOC bytes:");
  console.log(boc.length);

  console.log("BOC chars:");
  console.log(base64.length);

  console.log("\nBOC saved:");
  console.log(OUTPUT_FILE);

  console.log("\nBOC base64:");
  console.log(base64);

  console.log("\n===== SDK COMPARISON TARGET =====");

  console.log(
    "SDK SHA256:"
  );
  console.log(
    "1a1651ed7a231143443f7cb52b6170f7cebe6a6da217d5519bf2ea9b35affe79"
  );

  console.log(
    "SDK bytes: 266"
  );

  console.log(
    "SDK chars: 356"
  );

  console.log("\n=================================================");
  console.log(" BOC BUILD COMPLETE");
  console.log(" NO TRANSACTION WAS SENT");
  console.log("=================================================");
}
