FILE: scripts\buildFinalLiquidityV22.ts
====================================================================================================
import { Address, TonClient4 } from '@ton/ton';
import { DEX } from '@ston-fi/sdk';
import * as fs from 'fs';

const RPC_ENDPOINT = 'https://sandbox-v4.tonhubapi.com';

const ROUTER = Address.parse(
    'EQCS4UEa5UaJLzOyyKieqQOQ2P9M-7kXpkO5HnP3Bv250cN3'
);

const ROOSTER_MASTER = Address.parse(
    'EQCyuv-cYXFibA05bhiL3seM3Ur2B_bpsc364Zb8F3NMtrYx'
);

const PTON_MASTER = Address.parse(
    'EQBnGWMCf3-FZZq1W4IWcWiGAc3PHuZ0_H-7sad2oY00o83S'
);

const KNOWN_POOL = Address.parse(
    'EQD5ALaCuEqJ7Mi9qlXOA7BHMrTq8e2fZ5B_teWN17MZq-cf'
);

const USER = Address.parse(
    'EQDxn5MLbFyV649-OBviQrMp5KqWjGHNVwFM6GTeaLl8N0Ix'
);

const EXPECTED_ROOSTER_WALLET = Address.parse(
    'EQCePy3CW0qsWj03tvCwBxpavtYcu27LURfEy7H6tRhTbwsq'
);

const EXPECTED_PTON_WALLET = Address.parse(
    'EQCSIMGBps_qzRG3uPYhON8bucyCtu0mYdL1-u4gSz77IBa3'
);

const EXPECTED_USER_ROOSTER_WALLET = Address.parse(
    'EQBzQOOJu9qQVli2R0o6p3pXvi_wj9O2ncMhv3UyAjvMiMOh'
);

const PTON_AMOUNT = 500_000_000n;

const TX_DEADLINE =
    Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000) + 900;

export async function run() {
    console.log(
        '======================================================================'
    );
    console.log(
        ' STON.FI v2.2 FINAL LIQUIDITY BUILD'
    );
    console.log(
        '======================================================================'
    );
    console.log(
        'BUILD ONLY - NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT.'
    );

    const client = new TonClient4({
        endpoint: RPC_ENDPOINT,
    });

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------
    // 1. CREATE ROUTER
    // ------------------------------------------------------------------

    console.log('\n===== 1. CREATE ROUTER =====');

    const router =
        DEX.v2_2.Router.CPI.create(ROUTER);

    const routerProvider =
        client.provider(ROUTER);

    console.log(
        'Router:',
        ROUTER.toString()
    );

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------
    // 2. VERIFY KNOWN POOL
    // ------------------------------------------------------------------

    console.log('\n===== 2. VERIFY POOL =====');

    const pool =
        DEX.v2_2.Pool.CPI.create(KNOWN_POOL);

    const poolProvider =
        client.provider(KNOWN_POOL);

    console.log(
        'Known Pool:',
        KNOWN_POOL.toString()
    );

    const data =
        await pool.getPoolData(poolProvider);

    console.log(
        'Reserve0 ROOSTER:',
        data.reserve0.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'Reserve1 pTON:',
        data.reserve1.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'Total LP:',
        data.totalSupplyLP.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'Token0 Wallet:',
        data.token0WalletAddress.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'Token1 Wallet:',
        data.token1WalletAddress.toString()
    );

    if (
        !data.token0WalletAddress.equals(
            EXPECTED_ROOSTER_WALLET
        )
    ) {
        throw new Error(
            'TOKEN0 ROOSTER WALLET CHECK FAILED'
        );
    }

    if (
        !data.token1WalletAddress.equals(
            EXPECTED_PTON_WALLET
        )
    ) {
        throw new Error(
            'TOKEN1 pTON WALLET CHECK FAILED'
        );
    }

    console.log(
        'POOL TOKEN WALLETS: PASS'
    );

    if (data.reserve0 <= 0n) {
        throw new Error(
            'ROOSTER RESERVE INVALID'
        );
    }

    if (data.reserve1 <= 0n) {
        throw new Error(
            'pTON RESERVE INVALID'
        );
    }

    if (data.totalSupplyLP <= 0n) {
        throw new Error(
            'LP SUPPLY INVALID'
        );
    }

    console.log(
        'POOL DATA: PASS'
    );

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------
    // 3. FRESH POOL DATA
    // ------------------------------------------------------------------

    console.log('\n===== 3. FRESH POOL DATA =====');

    console.log(
        'Reserve0 ROOSTER:',
        data.reserve0.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'Reserve1 pTON:',
        data.reserve1.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'Total LP:',
        data.totalSupplyLP.toString()
    );

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------
    // 4. LIQUIDITY CALCULATION
    // ------------------------------------------------------------------

    console.log('\n===== 4. LIQUIDITY CALCULATION =====');

    console.log(
        'pTON amount:',
        PTON_AMOUNT.toString(),
        '=',
        Number(PTON_AMOUNT) / 1e9,
        'TON'
    );

    const roosterAmount =
        PTON_AMOUNT *
        data.reserve0 /
        data.reserve1;

    const expectedLpFromRooster =
        roosterAmount *
        data.totalSupplyLP /
        data.reserve0;

    const expectedLpFromPton =
        PTON_AMOUNT *
        data.totalSupplyLP /
        data.reserve1;

    console.log(
        'LP from ROOSTER side:',
        expectedLpFromRooster.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'LP from pTON side:',
        expectedLpFromPton.toString()
    );

    if (
        expectedLpFromRooster !==
        expectedLpFromPton
    ) {
        throw new Error(
            'LP CALCULATION MISMATCH'
        );
    }

    const expectedLp =
        expectedLpFromPton;

    const minLpOut =
        expectedLp *
        95n /
        100n;

    console.log(
        'ROOSTER amount:',
        roosterAmount.toString(),
        '=',
        Number(roosterAmount) / 1e9,
        'ROOSTER'
    );

    console.log(
        'Expected LP:',
        expectedLp.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'minLpOut:',
        minLpOut.toString()
    );

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------
    // 5. BUILD ROOSTER TRANSACTION
    // ------------------------------------------------------------------

    console.log(
        '\n===== 5. BUILD ROOSTER TRANSACTION ====='
    );

    const roosterTx =
        await router.getProvideLiquidityJettonTxParams(
            routerProvider,
            {
                userWalletAddress: USER,

                sendTokenAddress:
                    ROOSTER_MASTER,

                sendAmount:
                    roosterAmount,

                otherTokenAddress:
                    PTON_MASTER,

                minLpOut:
                    minLpOut,

                receiverAddress:
                    USER,

                refundAddress:
                    USER,

          excessesAddress:
    USER,

deadline:
    TX_DEADLINE,
            }
        );

    console.log(
        'ROOSTER destination:',
        roosterTx.to.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'ROOSTER value:',
        roosterTx.value.toString(),
        'nanoTON'
    );

    if (!roosterTx.body) {
        throw new Error(
            'ROOSTER BODY IS EMPTY'
        );
    }

    const roosterBoc =
        roosterTx.body
            .toBoc()
            .toString('base64');

    if (!roosterBoc) {
        throw new Error(
            'ROOSTER BOC GENERATION FAILED'
        );
    }

    fs.writeFileSync(
        'rooster_v22_generated.boc',
        roosterBoc
    );

    console.log(
        'ROOSTER BOC saved:',
        'rooster_v22_generated.boc'
    );

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------
    // 6. BUILD pTON TRANSACTION
    // ------------------------------------------------------------------

    console.log(
        '\n===== 6. BUILD pTON TRANSACTION ====='
    );

    /*
     * SDK v2.2 exposes pTON as PtonV2_1.
     *
     * The installed SDK declaration confirms:
     *
     * DEX.v2_2.pTON === PtonV2_1
     *
     * and constructor:
     *
     * constructor(address: AddressType, ...)
     */

    const proxyTon =
        new DEX.v2_2.pTON(
            PTON_MASTER
        );

    console.log(
        'pTON master:',
        PTON_MASTER.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'pTON SDK version:',
        proxyTon.version
    );

    const ptonTx =
        await router.getProvideLiquidityTonTxParams(
            routerProvider,
            {
                userWalletAddress:
                    USER,

                receiverAddress:
                    USER,

                proxyTon:
                    proxyTon,

                otherTokenAddress:
                    ROOSTER_MASTER,

                sendAmount:
                    PTON_AMOUNT,

                minLpOut:
                    minLpOut,

                refundAddress:
                    USER,

                excessesAddress:
                    USER,

                bothPositive:
    true,

deadline:
    TX_DEADLINE,
            }
        );

    console.log(
        'pTON destination:',
        ptonTx.to.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'pTON value:',
        ptonTx.value.toString(),
        'nanoTON'
    );

   if (ptonTx.body == null) {
    throw new Error(
        'PTON BODY IS EMPTY'
    );
}

const ptonBoc =
    ptonTx.body
        .toBoc()
        .toString('base64');

    if (!ptonBoc) {
        throw new Error(
            'PTON BOC GENERATION FAILED'
        );
    }

    fs.writeFileSync(
        'pton_v22_generated.boc',
        ptonBoc
    );

    console.log(
        'pTON BOC saved:',
        'pton_v22_generated.boc'
    );

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------
    // 7. DESTINATION CHECKS
    // ------------------------------------------------------------------

    console.log(
        '\n===== 7. DESTINATION CHECKS ====='
    );

    /*
     * ROOSTER:
     * The Jetton liquidity builder targets the user's ROOSTER wallet.
     */

    if (
        !roosterTx.to.equals(
            EXPECTED_USER_ROOSTER_WALLET
        )
    ) {
        throw new Error(
            'ROOSTER DESTINATION CHECK FAILED'
        );
    }

    console.log(
        'ROOSTER destination:',
        roosterTx.to.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'Expected ROOSTER wallet:',
        EXPECTED_USER_ROOSTER_WALLET.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'ROOSTER DESTINATION: PASS'
    );

   /*
 * pTON destination check:
 * The SDK-generated destination is the Router's pTON wallet.
 */

if (
    !ptonTx.to.equals(
        EXPECTED_PTON_WALLET
    )
) {
    throw new Error(
        'pTON DESTINATION CHECK FAILED'
    );
}

console.log(
    'pTON destination:',
    ptonTx.to.toString()
);

console.log(
    'Expected pTON wallet:',
    EXPECTED_PTON_WALLET.toString()
);

console.log(
    'pTON DESTINATION: PASS'
);
 

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------
    // 8. BOC CHECKS
    // ------------------------------------------------------------------

    console.log(
        '\n===== 8. BOC CHECKS ====='
    );

    if (
        roosterBoc.length === 0
    ) {
        throw new Error(
            'ROOSTER BOC EMPTY'
        );
    }

    if (
        ptonBoc.length === 0
    ) {
        throw new Error(
            'pTON BOC EMPTY'
        );
    }

    console.log(
        'ROOSTER BOC: PASS'
    );

    console.log(
        'pTON BOC: PASS'
    );

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------
    // 9. FINAL SUMMARY
    // ------------------------------------------------------------------

    console.log(
        '\n===== 9. FINAL SUMMARY ====='
    );

    console.log(
        'Router:',
        ROUTER.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'Pool:',
        KNOWN_POOL.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'ROOSTER master:',
        ROOSTER_MASTER.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'pTON master:',
        PTON_MASTER.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'User:',
        USER.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'ROOSTER wallet:',
        EXPECTED_ROOSTER_WALLET.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'pTON wallet:',
        EXPECTED_PTON_WALLET.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'User ROOSTER wallet:',
        EXPECTED_USER_ROOSTER_WALLET.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'ROOSTER amount:',
        roosterAmount.toString(),
        '=',
        Number(roosterAmount) / 1e9,
        'ROOSTER'
    );

    console.log(
        'pTON amount:',
        PTON_AMOUNT.toString(),
        '=',
        Number(PTON_AMOUNT) / 1e9,
        'TON'
    );

    console.log(
        'Expected LP:',
        expectedLp.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'minLpOut:',
        minLpOut.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'ROOSTER tx value:',
        roosterTx.value.toString(),
        'nanoTON'
    );

    console.log(
        'pTON tx value:',
        ptonTx.value.toString(),
        'nanoTON'
    );

    console.log(
        'Pool identity: PASS'
    );

    console.log(
        'Pool wallets: PASS'
    );

    console.log(
        'Liquidity calculation: PASS'
    );

    console.log(
        'ROOSTER transaction build: PASS'
    );

    console.log(
        'pTON transaction build: PASS'
    );

    console.log(
        'Destination checks: PASS'
    );

    console.log(
        'BOC bodies generated: PASS'
    );

    console.log(
        '\n======================================================================'
    );

    console.log(
        ' BUILD COMPLETE'
    );

    console.log(
        '======================================================================'
    );

    console.log(
        'FINAL 0.5 TON SCENARIO BUILT.'
    );

    console.log(
        'NO TRANSACTION WAS SENT.'
    );
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\buildFinalTonConnectRequestV22.ts
====================================================================================================
import { Address, beginCell } from '@ton/core';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';
import { DEX } from '@ston-fi/sdk';

const USER = Address.parse(
    'EQDxn5MLbFyV649-OBviQrMp5KqWjGHNVwFM6GTeaLl8N0Ix'
);

const ROOSTER_MASTER = Address.parse(
    'EQCyuv-cYXFibA05bhiL3seM3Ur2B_bpsc364Zb8F3NMtrYx'
);

const PTON_MASTER = Address.parse(
    'EQBnGWMCf3-FZZq1W4IWcWiGAc3PHuZ0_H-7sad2oY00o83S'
);

const EXPECTED_USER_ROOSTER_WALLET = Address.parse(
    'EQBzQOOJu9qQVli2R0o6p3pXvi_wj9O2ncMhv3UyAjvMiMOh'
);

const EXPECTED_POOL_PTON_WALLET = Address.parse(
    'EQCSIMGBps_qzRG3uPYhON8bucyCtu0mYdL1-u4gSz77IBa3'
);

const ROUTER = Address.parse(
    'EQCS4UEa5UaJLzOyyKieqQOQ2P9M-7kXpkO5HnP3Bv250cN3'
);

const ROOSTER_AMOUNT = 100_000_000_000n;
const PTON_AMOUNT = 249_908n;
const MIN_LP_OUT = 150_109_265n;

const ROOSTER_OUTER_VALUE = 300_000_000n;
const PTON_OUTER_VALUE = 310_249_908n;

function sameAddress(a: Address, b: Address): boolean {
    return a.equals(b);
}

function printSeparator() {
    console.log('='.repeat(72));
}

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {
    printSeparator();
    console.log(' STON.FI v2.2 FINAL TONCONNECT REQUEST BUILDER');
    console.log(' READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT');
    printSeparator();

    const wallet = provider.sender().address;

    if (!wallet) {
        throw new Error('Connected wallet address is unavailable.');
    }

    console.log(`Connected wallet: ${wallet.toString()}`);

    const router = provider.open(
        DEX.v2_2.Router.CPI.create(ROUTER)
    );

    const proxyTon = DEX.v2_1.pTON.create(PTON_MASTER);

    /*
     * Fresh deadline.
     *
     * We deliberately create it at runtime so the resulting
     * TonConnect request cannot contain an expired deadline.
     */
    const deadline = Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000) + 600;

    console.log(`Deadline: ${deadline}`);
    console.log(`ROOSTER amount: ${ROOSTER_AMOUNT}`);
    console.log(`pTON amount: ${PTON_AMOUNT}`);
    console.log(`minLpOut: ${MIN_LP_OUT}`);

    /*
     * ------------------------------------------------------------
     * 1. ROOSTER LEG
     * ------------------------------------------------------------
     *
     * This is exactly the SDK call already passed by
     * finalTwoMessageGateV22.ts.
     */
    const roosterTx =
        await router.getProvideLiquidityJettonTxParams({
            userWalletAddress: USER,
            sendTokenAddress: ROOSTER_MASTER,
            otherTokenAddress: PTON_MASTER,
            sendAmount: ROOSTER_AMOUNT,
            minLpOut: MIN_LP_OUT,
            receiverAddress: USER,
            refundAddress: USER,
            excessesAddress: USER,
            deadline,
        });

    /*
     * ------------------------------------------------------------
     * 2. pTON LEG
     * ------------------------------------------------------------
     *
     * This is exactly the SDK call already passed by
     * finalTwoMessageGateV22.ts.
     */
    const ptonTx =
        await router.getProvideLiquidityTonTxParams({
            userWalletAddress: USER,
            otherTokenAddress: ROOSTER_MASTER,
            sendAmount: PTON_AMOUNT,
            minLpOut: MIN_LP_OUT,
            receiverAddress: USER,
            refundAddress: USER,
            excessesAddress: USER,
            deadline,
            proxyTon,
        });

    /*
     * ------------------------------------------------------------
     * 3. DESTINATION SAFETY CHECKS
     * ------------------------------------------------------------
     */

    console.log('');
    console.log('DESTINATION CHECKS');

    if (
        !sameAddress(
            roosterTx.to,
            EXPECTED_USER_ROOSTER_WALLET
        )
    ) {
        throw new Error(
            `ROOSTER destination mismatch:\n` +
            `Expected: ${EXPECTED_USER_ROOSTER_WALLET}\n` +
            `Actual:   ${roosterTx.to}`
        );
    }

    if (
        !sameAddress(
            ptonTx.to,
            EXPECTED_POOL_PTON_WALLET
        )
    ) {
        throw new Error(
            `pTON destination mismatch:\n` +
            `Expected: ${EXPECTED_POOL_PTON_WALLET}\n` +
            `Actual:   ${ptonTx.to}`
        );
    }

    if (BigInt(roosterTx.value) !== ROOSTER_OUTER_VALUE) {
        throw new Error(
            `ROOSTER outer value mismatch: ${roosterTx.value}`
        );
    }

    if (BigInt(ptonTx.value) !== PTON_OUTER_VALUE) {
        throw new Error(
            `pTON outer value mismatch: ${ptonTx.value}`
        );
    }

    console.log('ROOSTER destination: PASS');
    console.log('pTON destination: PASS');
    console.log('ROOSTER outer value: PASS');
    console.log('pTON outer value: PASS');

    /*
     * ------------------------------------------------------------
     * 4. BUILD EXACT TONCONNECT MESSAGE OBJECTS
     * ------------------------------------------------------------
     *
     * IMPORTANT:
     * We only serialize the already-built SDK transaction bodies.
     *
     * connector.sendTransaction() is NOT called.
     * provider.sender().send() is NOT called.
     */
    if (!roosterTx.body) {
    throw new Error('ROOSTER body was not produced.');
}

if (!ptonTx.body) {
    throw new Error('pTON body was not produced.');
}

const roosterPayload = roosterTx.body
    .toBoc()
    .toString('base64');

const ptonPayload = ptonTx.body
    .toBoc()
    .toString('base64');
    const tonConnectRequest = {
        validUntil: deadline,
        network: -3,
        messages: [
            {
                address: roosterTx.to.toString(),
                amount: roosterTx.value.toString(),
                payload: roosterPayload,
            },
            {
                address: ptonTx.to.toString(),
                amount: ptonTx.value.toString(),
                payload: ptonPayload,
            },
        ],
    };

    /*
     * ------------------------------------------------------------
     * 5. DISPLAY REQUEST
     * ------------------------------------------------------------
     */

    console.log('');
    printSeparator();
    console.log(' TONCONNECT REQUEST');
    printSeparator();

    console.log(JSON.stringify(
        tonConnectRequest,
        null,
        2
    ));

    /*
     * ------------------------------------------------------------
     * 6. BASIC FINAL CHECKS
     * ------------------------------------------------------------
     */

    if (tonConnectRequest.messages.length !== 2) {
        throw new Error('TonConnect message count is not 2.');
    }

    if (
        tonConnectRequest.messages[0].address !==
        roosterTx.to.toString()
    ) {
        throw new Error('Message 1 address mismatch.');
    }

    if (
        tonConnectRequest.messages[1].address !==
        ptonTx.to.toString()
    ) {
        throw new Error('Message 2 address mismatch.');
    }

    if (!roosterPayload) {
        throw new Error('ROOSTER payload is empty.');
    }

    if (!ptonPayload) {
        throw new Error('pTON payload is empty.');
    }

    console.log('');
    printSeparator();
    console.log(' FINAL RESULT');
    printSeparator();

    console.log('Two messages: PASS');
    console.log('ROOSTER BOC: PASS');
    console.log('pTON BOC: PASS');
    console.log('validUntil: PASS');
    console.log('network = -3: PASS');

    console.log('');
    console.log('SAFETY');
    console.log('connector.sendTransaction(): NOT CALLED');
    console.log('sender.send(): NOT CALLED');
    console.log('wallet.send(): NOT CALLED');
    console.log('Tonkeeper approval: NOT REQUESTED');
    console.log('Transaction broadcast: NO');
    console.log('Wallet state change: NO');
    console.log('Pool state change: NO');

    console.log('');
    printSeparator();
    console.log(' FINAL TONCONNECT REQUEST BUILT');
    printSeparator();
}

import { beginCell, Address } from "@ton/core";
import fs from "fs";

export async function run() {
    console.log("================================================");
    console.log(" STON.fi v2.2 FINAL ROOSTER LIQUIDITY BOC");
    console.log(" SDK-MATCHED / READ ONLY");
    console.log(" NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT");
    console.log("================================================");

    // =================================================
    // FINAL TARGET VALUES
    // =================================================

    // 200073.535827652 ROOSTER
    const ROOSTER_AMOUNT = 200_073_535_827_652n;

    // Required minimum LP tokens
    const MIN_LP_OUT = 300_329_052_918n;

    const ROUTER = Address.parse(
        "EQCS4UEa5UaJLzOyyKieqQOQ2P9M-7kXpkO5HnP3Bv250cN3"
    );

    const USER_WALLET = Address.parse(
        "EQDxn5MLbFyV649-OBviQrMp5KqWjGHNVwFM6GTeaLl8N0Ix"
    );

    // Router's pTON Jetton Wallet
    const ROUTER_PTON_WALLET = Address.parse(
        "EQCSIMGBps_qzRG3uPYhON8bucyCtu0mYdL1-u4gSz77IBa3"
    );

    // Keep the same SDK-matched value used by the
    // previously verified builder until independently
    // confirmed against the installed SDK gas constants.
    const FORWARD_GAS_AMOUNT = 240_000_000n;

    // Outer transaction value for the ROOSTER Jetton wallet.
    const GAS_AMOUNT = 300_000_000n;

    const JETTON_TRANSFER_OPCODE = 0x0f8a7ea5;
    const PROVIDE_LP_OPCODE = 0x37c096df;

    const QUERY_ID = 0n;

    // =================================================
    // 1. PROVIDE_LP BODY
    //
    // Current @ston-fi/sdk structure:
    //
    //   uint32  PROVIDE_LP
    //   address routerWalletAddress
    //   coins   minLpOut
    //
    // No deadline.
    // No refund address.
    // No excesses address.
    // =================================================

    const provideLpPayload = beginCell()
        .storeUint(PROVIDE_LP_OPCODE, 32)
        .storeAddress(ROUTER_PTON_WALLET)
        .storeCoins(MIN_LP_OUT)
        .endCell();

    // =================================================
    // 2. JETTON TRANSFER BODY
    // =================================================

    const body = beginCell()
        .storeUint(JETTON_TRANSFER_OPCODE, 32)

        // query_id
        .storeUint(QUERY_ID, 64)

        // amount
        .storeCoins(ROOSTER_AMOUNT)

        // destination = STON.fi Router
        .storeAddress(ROUTER)

        // response destination = user
        .storeAddress(USER_WALLET)

        // custom payload = none
        .storeBit(false)

        // forward TON
        .storeCoins(FORWARD_GAS_AMOUNT)

        // forward payload = reference
        .storeBit(true)

        // PROVIDE_LP body
        .storeRef(provideLpPayload)

        .endCell();

    // =================================================
    // 3. SERIALIZE BOC
    // =================================================

    const boc = body.toBoc({
        idx: false
    });

    const base64 = boc.toString("base64");

    const outputFile =
        "./final_v22_rooster_liquidity.boc";

    fs.writeFileSync(
        outputFile,
        base64,
        "utf8"
    );

    // =================================================
    // 4. REPORT
    // =================================================

    console.log("");
    console.log("===== FINAL TARGET VALUES =====");

    console.log(
        "ROOSTER nano:",
        ROOSTER_AMOUNT.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        "ROOSTER display:",
        Number(ROOSTER_AMOUNT) / 1e9
    );

    console.log(
        "minLpOut:",
        MIN_LP_OUT.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        "Router:",
        ROUTER.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        "Router pTON wallet:",
        ROUTER_PTON_WALLET.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        "Response destination:",
        USER_WALLET.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        "Forward TON:",
        FORWARD_GAS_AMOUNT.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        "Forward TON display:",
        Number(FORWARD_GAS_AMOUNT) / 1e9
    );

    console.log(
        "Gas value:",
        GAS_AMOUNT.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        "Gas value display:",
        Number(GAS_AMOUNT) / 1e9
    );

    // =================================================
    // PROVIDE_LP
    // =================================================

    console.log("");
    console.log("===== PROVIDE_LP STRUCTURE =====");

    console.log(
        "Opcode: 0x37c096df"
    );

    console.log(
        "routerWalletAddress:",
        ROUTER_PTON_WALLET.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        "minLpOut:",
        MIN_LP_OUT.toString()
    );

    // =================================================
    // OUTER TRANSFER
    // =================================================

    console.log("");
    console.log("===== OUTER JETTON TRANSFER =====");

    console.log(
        "Opcode: 0x0f8a7ea5"
    );

    console.log(
        "query_id:",
        QUERY_ID.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        "amount:",
        ROOSTER_AMOUNT.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        "destination:",
        ROUTER.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        "response:",
        USER_WALLET.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        "custom payload: none"
    );

    console.log(
        "forward TON:",
        FORWARD_GAS_AMOUNT.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        "forward payload: reference"
    );

    // =================================================
    // BOC
    // =================================================

    console.log("");
    console.log("===== BOC =====");

    console.log(
        "Output:",
        outputFile
    );

    console.log(
        "Base64 chars:",
        base64.length
    );

    console.log(
        "BOC bytes:",
        boc.length
    );

    console.log("");
    console.log(base64);

    console.log("");
    console.log("================================================");
    console.log(" FINAL TARGET BOC BUILD COMPLETE");
    console.log(" READ ONLY");
    console.log(" NO TRANSACTION SENT");
    console.log("================================================");
}

import { Address, beginCell } from "@ton/core";

export async function run() {
  console.log("========================================");
  console.log(" STON.fi v2.2 FINAL TON TX BUILD CHECK");
  console.log(" BUILD ONLY — NO NETWORK");
  console.log(" NO TRANSACTION SENT");
  console.log("========================================");

  const routerAddress = Address.parse(
    "EQCS4UEa5UaJLzOyyKieqQOQ2P9M-7kXpkO5HnP3Bv250cN3"
  );

  const ptonWalletAddress = Address.parse(
    "EQCSIMGBps_qzRG3uPYhON8bucyCtu0mYdL1-u4gSz77IBa3"
  );

  const userAddress = Address.parse(
    "EQDxn5MLbFyV649-OBviQrMp5KqWjGHNVwFM6GTeaLl8N0Ix"
  );

  const sendAmount = 249908n;
  const forwardGasAmount = 300000000n;
  const tonTransferGas = 10000000n;

  const totalValue =
    sendAmount +
    forwardGasAmount +
    tonTransferGas;

  const deadline = 1790531314;

  const minLpOut = 150109265n;

  const provideLpPayload = beginCell()
    .storeUint(0x37c096df, 32)
    .storeAddress(ptonWalletAddress)
    .storeAddress(userAddress)
    .storeAddress(userAddress)
    .storeUint(deadline, 64)
    .storeRef(
      beginCell()
        .storeCoins(minLpOut)
        .storeAddress(userAddress)
        .storeUint(1, 1)
        .storeCoins(0n)
        .storeMaybeRef(null)
        .endCell()
    )
    .endCell();

  const tonTransferBody = beginCell()
    .storeUint(0x01f3835d, 32)
    .storeUint(0, 64)
    .storeCoins(sendAmount)
    .storeAddress(userAddress)
    .storeBit(true)
    .storeRef(provideLpPayload)
    .endCell();

  console.log("");
  console.log("DESTINATION:");
  console.log(ptonWalletAddress.toString());

  console.log("");
  console.log("ROUTER:");
  console.log(routerAddress.toString());

  console.log("");
  console.log("USER / REFUND:");
  console.log(userAddress.toString());

  console.log("");
  console.log("SEND AMOUNT:", sendAmount.toString());
  console.log("FORWARD GAS:", forwardGasAmount.toString());
  console.log("TRANSFER GAS:", tonTransferGas.toString());
  console.log("TOTAL VALUE:", totalValue.toString());
  console.log("TOTAL TON:", Number(totalValue) / 1e9);

  console.log("");
  console.log("PROVIDE_LP OPCODE: 0x37c096df");
  console.log("TON_TRANSFER OPCODE: 0x01f3835d");
  console.log("MIN LP OUT:", minLpOut.toString());
  console.log("DEADLINE:", deadline);

  console.log("");
  console.log("BODY BITS:", tonTransferBody.bits.length);
  console.log("BODY REFS:", tonTransferBody.refs.length);
  console.log(
    "BODY HASH:",
    tonTransferBody.hash().toString("hex")
  );

  console.log("");
  console.log("BODY BOC:");
  console.log(
    tonTransferBody.toBoc().toString("base64")
  );

  console.log("");
  console.log("========================================");
  console.log(" BUILD COMPLETE");
  console.log(" NO NETWORK");
  console.log(" NO WALLET");
  console.log(" NO TRANSACTION SENT");
  console.log("========================================");
}

run().catch(console.error);

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\buildFunction122CFGv1.ts
====================================================================================================
import fs from "fs";

const WASM =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const START = 0x1226d;
const END   = 0x1295b;

const TARGETS = new Set([
    0x12680,
    0x12681,
    0x126f6,
    0x126fa,
    0x12700,
    0x1270b,
    0x12713,
    0x1273d,
    0x12791,
    0x127bd,
    0x1283e,
    0x1289c,
    0x128b4,
    0x128fb,
    0x1293b,
    0x12950,
]);

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

function u8(p: number): number {
    return wasm[p] ?? 0;
}

function uleb(p: number): { value: number; next: number } {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = u8(p++);
        value += (b & 0x7f) * Math.pow(2, shift);

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0)
            break;

        shift += 7;
    }

    return { value, next: p };
}

function sleb(p: number): { value: number; next: number } {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let b = 0;

    while (true) {
        b = u8(p++);
        value += (b & 0x7f) * Math.pow(2, shift);
        shift += 7;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0)
            break;
    }

    if ((b & 0x40) !== 0 && shift < 32)
        value -= Math.pow(2, shift);

    return { value, next: p };
}

function hex(x: number): string {
    return "0x" + x.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

type Insn = {
    addr: number;
    end: number;
    op: number;
    mnemonic: string;
    targetDepth?: number;
    targets?: number[];
    defaultDepth?: number;
    immediate?: number;
};

type Frame = {
    kind: "block" | "loop" | "if";
    start: number;
    label: number;
};

function blocktype(p: number): number {
    return u8(p);
}

/*
 * Decode only instructions needed for CFG construction,
 * but advance over all common WASM immediates correctly.
 */
function decodeOne(p0: number): Insn {
    const addr = p0;
    const op = u8(p0++);
    let mnemonic = `op_0x${op.toString(16)}`;
    let immediate: number | undefined;

    switch (op) {

        case 0x00:
            mnemonic = "unreachable";
            break;

        case 0x01:
            mnemonic = "nop";
            break;

        case 0x02:
            mnemonic = "block";
            p0++;
            break;

        case 0x03:
            mnemonic = "loop";
            p0++;
            break;

        case 0x04:
            mnemonic = "if";
            p0++;
            break;

        case 0x05:
            mnemonic = "else";
            break;

        case 0x0b:
            mnemonic = "end";
            break;

        case 0x0c: {
            const r = uleb(p0);
            p0 = r.next;
            immediate = r.value;
            mnemonic = `br depth=${r.value}`;
            break;
        }

        case 0x0d: {
            const r = uleb(p0);
            p0 = r.next;
            immediate = r.value;
            mnemonic = `br_if depth=${r.value}`;
            break;
        }

        case 0x0e: {
            const c = uleb(p0);
            p0 = c.next;

            const ds: number[] = [];

            for (let i = 0; i < c.value; i++) {
                const r = uleb(p0);
                p0 = r.next;
                ds.push(r.value);
            }

            const d = uleb(p0);
            p0 = d.next;

            return {
                addr,
                end: p0,
                op,
                mnemonic: `br_table count=${c.value}`,
                targets: ds,
                defaultDepth: d.value,
            };
        }

        case 0x0f:
            mnemonic = "return";
            break;

        case 0x10: {
            const r = uleb(p0);
            p0 = r.next;
            immediate = r.value;
            mnemonic = `call ${r.value}`;
            break;
        }

        case 0x11: {
            const a = uleb(p0);
            p0 = a.next;

            const b = uleb(p0);
            p0 = b.next;

            mnemonic = `call_indirect type=${a.value} table=${b.value}`;
            break;
        }

        case 0x1a:
            mnemonic = "drop";
            break;

        case 0x1b:
            mnemonic = "select";
            break;

        case 0x20: {
            const r = uleb(p0);
            p0 = r.next;
            mnemonic = `local.get ${r.value}`;
            break;
        }

        case 0x21: {
            const r = uleb(p0);
            p0 = r.next;
            mnemonic = `local.set ${r.value}`;
            break;
        }

        case 0x22: {
            const r = uleb(p0);
            p0 = r.next;
            mnemonic = `local.tee ${r.value}`;
            break;
        }

        case 0x23: {
            const r = uleb(p0);
            p0 = r.next;
            mnemonic = `global.get ${r.value}`;
            break;
        }

        case 0x24: {
            const r = uleb(p0);
            p0 = r.next;
            mnemonic = `global.set ${r.value}`;
            break;
        }

        /*
         * Loads/stores.
         */
        case 0x28: case 0x29: case 0x2a: case 0x2b:
        case 0x2c: case 0x2d: case 0x2e: case 0x2f:
        case 0x30: case 0x31: case 0x32: case 0x33:
        case 0x34: case 0x35:
        case 0x36: case 0x37: case 0x38: case 0x39:
        case 0x3a: case 0x3b: case 0x3c: case 0x3d:
        case 0x3e: case 0x3f: case 0x40: {
            const a = uleb(p0);
            p0 = a.next;

            const b = uleb(p0);
            p0 = b.next;

            mnemonic = `mem op=0x${op.toString(16)} align=${a.value} offset=${b.value}`;
            break;
        }

        case 0x41: {
            const r = sleb(p0);
            p0 = r.next;
            immediate = r.value;
            mnemonic = `i32.const ${r.value}`;
            break;
        }

        case 0x42: {
            /*
             * i64.const is signed LEB128.
             * We only need its length here.
             */
            const r = sleb(p0);
            p0 = r.next;
            mnemonic = "i64.const";
            break;
        }

        /*
         * 0xfc prefixed instructions.
         */
        case 0xfc: {
            const sub = uleb(p0);
            p0 = sub.next;

            mnemonic = `0xfc.${sub.value}`;

            /*
             * Saturating conversions 0-7: no extra immediate.
             * Memory bulk operations have memory/table immediates.
             */
            if (sub.value >= 8 && sub.value <= 11) {
                const a = uleb(p0);
                p0 = a.next;

                const b = uleb(p0);
                p0 = b.next;

                mnemonic += ` ${a.value} ${b.value}`;
            }
            break;
        }

        /*
         * Reference types.
         */
        case 0xd0:
            p0++;
            mnemonic = "ref.null";
            break;

        case 0xd1:
            mnemonic = "ref.is_null";
            break;

        case 0xd2: {
            const r = uleb(p0);
            p0 = r.next;
            mnemonic = `ref.func ${r.value}`;
            break;
        }

        default:
            /*
             * Most numeric opcodes have no immediate.
             * This is sufficient for this function's CFG once
             * the known instruction boundaries are verified.
             */
            break;
    }

    return {
        addr,
        end: p0,
        op,
        mnemonic,
        immediate,
    };
}

const insns: Insn[] = [];

let p = START;

while (p < END) {
    const ins = decodeOne(p);

    if (ins.end <= p) {
        console.log(`DECODER STALL @ ${hex(p)}`);
        break;
    }

    insns.push(ins);
    p = ins.end;
}

const byAddr = new Map<number, Insn>();

for (const x of insns)
    byAddr.set(x.addr, x);

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" FUNCTION 122 REAL INSTRUCTION / CFG ANALYSIS");
console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`Range: ${hex(START)} .. ${hex(END)}`);
console.log(`Decoded instructions: ${insns.length}`);
console.log();

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" TARGET INSTRUCTION BOUNDARIES");
console.log("============================================================");

for (const t of [...TARGETS].sort((a, b) => a - b)) {
    const x = byAddr.get(t);

    if (x) {
        console.log(
            `${hex(t)}  VALID  ${x.mnemonic}  end=${hex(x.end)}`
        );
    } else {
        console.log(
            `${hex(t)}  NOT-INSTRUCTION-BOUNDARY`
        );
    }
}

console.log();

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" CONTROL FLOW");
console.log("============================================================");

const frames: Frame[] = [];

function resolveDepth(depth: number): Frame | null {
    if (depth < 0 || depth >= frames.length)
        return null;

    return frames[frames.length - 1 - depth] ?? null;
}

for (const x of insns) {

    if (
        x.op === 0x02 ||
        x.op === 0x03 ||
        x.op === 0x04
    ) {
        const kind =
            x.op === 0x02 ? "block" :
            x.op === 0x03 ? "loop" :
            "if";

        frames.push({
            kind,
            start: x.addr,
            label: x.op === 0x03 ? x.end : x.end,
        });

        continue;
    }

    if (x.op === 0x05) {
        /*
         * ELSE keeps the same IF frame.
         */
        continue;
    }

    if (x.op === 0x0b) {
        if (frames.length > 0)
            frames.pop();

        continue;
    }

    if (x.op === 0x0c || x.op === 0x0d) {

        const depth = x.immediate ?? -1;
        const target = resolveDepth(depth);

        console.log(
            `${hex(x.addr)}  ${x.mnemonic}` +
            `  => ${target ? hex(target.label) : "UNRESOLVED"}` +
            `${target ? ` (${target.kind} @ ${hex(target.start)})` : ""}`
        );

        continue;
    }

    if (x.op === 0x0e) {

        const targets: string[] = [];

        for (const d of x.targets ?? []) {
            const f = resolveDepth(d);
            targets.push(
                f
                    ? `${d}->${hex(f.label)}`
                    : `${d}->UNRESOLVED`
            );
        }

        const d = x.defaultDepth ?? -1;
        const df = resolveDepth(d);

        console.log(
            `${hex(x.addr)}  br_table` +
            ` cases=[${targets.join(", ")}]` +
            ` default=${d}->${df ? hex(df.label) : "UNRESOLVED"}`
        );
    }
}

console.log();

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" PREDECESSORS OF TARGETS");
console.log("============================================================");

for (const target of [...TARGETS].sort((a, b) => a - b)) {

    const preds: string[] = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < insns.length; i++) {

        const x = insns[i];
        const next = insns[i + 1];

        /*
         * Direct fall-through.
         */
        if (
            next &&
            next.addr === target &&
            x.op !== 0x00 &&
            x.op !== 0x0f &&
            x.op !== 0x0c &&
            x.op !== 0x0e
        ) {
            preds.push(
                `${hex(x.addr)} FALLTHROUGH (${x.mnemonic})`
            );
        }

        /*
         * br_if also has fall-through, handled above.
         */
        if (x.op === 0x0d) {
            const depth = x.immediate ?? -1;
            // Exact target is printed in CONTROL FLOW section.
            void depth;
        }
    }

    console.log(
        `${hex(target)} <- ` +
        (preds.length ? preds.join(" | ") : "NO DIRECT FALLTHROUGH")
    );
}

console.log();

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" INSTRUCTION WINDOW AROUND 0x12680");
console.log("============================================================");

const idx = insns.findIndex(x => x.addr >= 0x12670);

if (idx >= 0) {
    for (
        let i = Math.max(0, idx - 5);
        i < Math.min(insns.length, idx + 35);
        i++
    ) {
        const x = insns[i];

        console.log(
            `${hex(x.addr)} .. ${hex(x.end - 1)}  ${x.mnemonic}`
        );
    }
}

console.log();
console.log("DONE");

FILE: scripts\buildInitialLiquidityTx.ts
====================================================================================================

function addressToCell(address: Address): Cell {
    return beginCell()
        .storeAddress(address)
        .endCell();
}

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {

    // ==========================================

        'EQCS4UEa5UaJLzOyyKieqQOQ2P9M-7kXpkO5HnP3Bv250cN3'
    );

        'EQCyuv-cYXFibA05bhiL3seM3Ur2B_bpsc364Zb8F3NMtrYx'
    );

        'EQBnGWMCf3-FZZq1W4IWcWiGAc3PHuZ0_H-7sad2oY00o83S'
    );

        '0QDxn5MLbFyV649-OBviQrMp5KqWjGHNVwFM6GTeaLl8N6R-'
    );

const ROOSTER_MASTER = Address.parse(

function printCellInfo(name: string, cell: Cell) {
    console.log('');
    console.log('='.repeat(78));
    console.log(name);
    console.log('='.repeat(78));

    console.log('BITS:', cell.bits.length);
    console.log('REFS:', cell.refs.length);

    console.log('BOC:');
    console.log(cell.toBoc().toString('base64'));

    console.log('HEX:');
    console.log(cell.toBoc().toString('hex'));
}

function checkAddress(
    name: string,
    actual: Address,
    expected: Address
) {
    const ok = actual.equals(expected);

    console.log(`${name}:`, ok ? 'YES' : 'NO');

    if (!ok) {
        console.log('  ACTUAL  :', actual.toString());
        console.log('  EXPECTED:', expected.toString());
    }
}

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {

    console.log('');
    console.log('======================================================================');
    console.log(' STON.FI v2.2 BOTH-SIDES PROVIDE LP INSPECTOR');
    console.log(' READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT');
    console.log('======================================================================');

    console.log('');
    console.log('ROUTER :', ROUTER.toString());
    console.log('USER   :', USER.toString());
    console.log('ROOSTER:', ROOSTER_MASTER.toString());
    console.log('pTON   :', PTON_MASTER.toString());

    // ================================================================
    // ROUTER
    // ================================================================

    const router = DEX.v2_2.Router.CPI.create(ROUTER);

    const routerProvider = provider.provider(ROUTER);

    // ================================================================
    // 1. POOL VERIFICATION
    // ================================================================

    console.log('');
    console.log('------------------------------------------------------------------');
    console.log('1. POOL VERIFICATION');
    console.log('------------------------------------------------------------------');

    const pool = await router.getPoolAddressByJettonMinters(
        routerProvider,
        {
            token0: ROOSTER_MASTER,
            token1: PTON_MASTER,
        }
    );

    console.log('SDK POOL:', pool.toString());

    checkAddress(
        'POOL MATCH',
        pool,
        EXPECTED_POOL
    );

    if (!pool.equals(EXPECTED_POOL)) {
        throw new Error(
            'STOP: SDK pool does not match expected v2.2 pool.'
        );
    }

    // ================================================================
    // 2. pTON v2.1
    // ================================================================

    console.log('');
    console.log('------------------------------------------------------------------');
    console.log('2. pTON v2.1');
    console.log('------------------------------------------------------------------');

    const proxyTon = new DEX.v2_2.pTON(PTON_MASTER);

    console.log(
        'pTON VERSION:',
        proxyTon.version
    );

    console.log(
        'pTON ADDRESS:',
        proxyTon.address.toString()
    );

    if (proxyTon.version !== 'v2_1') {
        throw new Error(
            'STOP: Router v2.2 requires pTON v2.1.'
        );
    }

    if (!proxyTon.address.equals(PTON_MASTER)) {
        throw new Error(
            'STOP: pTON address mismatch.'
        );
    }

    console.log('pTON VERSION MATCH: YES');
    console.log('pTON ADDRESS MATCH: YES');

    // ================================================================
    // 3. ROOSTER SIDE
    // ================================================================

    console.log('');
    console.log('------------------------------------------------------------------');
    console.log('3. ROOSTER SIDE');
    console.log('------------------------------------------------------------------');

    console.log(
        'ROOSTER AMOUNT:',
        ROOSTER_AMOUNT.toString(),
        'nano-ROOSTER'
    );

    console.log(
        'ROOSTER DISPLAY:',
        Number(ROOSTER_AMOUNT) / 1_000_000_000,
        'ROOSTER'
    );

    console.log(
        'MIN LP OUT:',
        MIN_LP_OUT.toString()
    );

    const roosterTx =
        await router.getProvideLiquidityJettonTxParams(
            routerProvider,
            {
                userWalletAddress: USER,
                sendTokenAddress: ROOSTER_MASTER,
                otherTokenAddress: PTON_MASTER,
                sendAmount: ROOSTER_AMOUNT,
                minLpOut: MIN_LP_OUT,
                receiverAddress: USER,
                refundAddress: USER,
                excessesAddress: USER,
                transferExcessAddress: USER,
            }
        );

    const roosterBody = roosterTx.body;

    if (!roosterBody) {
        throw new Error(
            'STOP: SDK returned empty ROOSTER transaction body.'
        );
    }

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        'ROOSTER TX TO:',
        roosterTx.to.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'ROOSTER TX VALUE:',
        roosterTx.value.toString(),
        'nanoTON'
    );

    console.log(
        'ROOSTER TX VALUE:',
        Number(roosterTx.value) / 1_000_000_000,
        'TON'
    );

    checkAddress(
        'ROOSTER DESTINATION MATCH',
        roosterTx.to,
        USER_ROOSTER_WALLET
    );

    writeFileSync(
        './rooster_v22_generated.boc',
        roosterBody.toBoc()
    );

    console.log(
        'ROOSTER BOC SAVED:',
        './rooster_v22_generated.boc'
    );

    printCellInfo(
        'ROOSTER TRANSACTION BODY',
        roosterBody
    );

    // ================================================================
    // 4. pTON SIDE
    // ================================================================

    console.log('');
    console.log('------------------------------------------------------------------');
    console.log('4. pTON SIDE');
    console.log('------------------------------------------------------------------');

    console.log(
        'pTON AMOUNT:',
        PTON_AMOUNT.toString(),
        'nano-pTON'
    );

    console.log(
        'pTON DISPLAY:',
        Number(PTON_AMOUNT) / 1_000_000_000,
        'pTON'
    );

    const ptonTx =
        await router.getProvideLiquidityTonTxParams(
            routerProvider,
            {
                userWalletAddress: USER,
                proxyTon,
                otherTokenAddress: ROOSTER_MASTER,
                sendAmount: PTON_AMOUNT,
                minLpOut: MIN_LP_OUT,
                receiverAddress: USER,
                refundAddress: USER,
                excessesAddress: USER,
            }
        );

    const ptonBody = ptonTx.body;

    if (!ptonBody) {
        throw new Error(
            'STOP: SDK returned empty pTON transaction body.'
        );
    }

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        'pTON TX TO:',
        ptonTx.to.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'pTON TX VALUE:',
        ptonTx.value.toString(),
        'nanoTON'
    );

    console.log(
        'pTON TX VALUE:',
        Number(ptonTx.value) / 1_000_000_000,
        'TON'
    );

    writeFileSync(
        './pton_v22_generated.boc',
        ptonBody.toBoc()
    );

    console.log(
        'pTON BOC SAVED:',
        './pton_v22_generated.boc'
    );

    printCellInfo(
        'pTON TRANSACTION BODY',
        ptonBody
    );

    // ================================================================
    // 5. pTON VALUE
    // ================================================================

    console.log('');
    console.log('------------------------------------------------------------------');
    console.log('5. GENERATED pTON VALUE');
    console.log('------------------------------------------------------------------');

    console.log(
        'pTON WRAPPED AMOUNT:',
        PTON_AMOUNT.toString(),
        'nano'
    );

    console.log(
        'TOTAL TX VALUE:',
        ptonTx.value.toString(),
        'nanoTON'
    );

    console.log(
        'TOTAL TX VALUE:',
        Number(ptonTx.value) / 1_000_000_000,
        'TON'
    );

    console.log(
        'pTON EXTRA VALUE:',
        (ptonTx.value - PTON_AMOUNT).toString(),
        'nanoTON'
    );

    console.log(
        'pTON EXTRA VALUE:',
        Number(ptonTx.value - PTON_AMOUNT) / 1_000_000_000,
        'TON'
    );

    // ================================================================
    // 6. FINAL SUMMARY
    // ================================================================

    console.log('');
    console.log('======================================================================');
    console.log(' FINAL READ-ONLY SUMMARY');
    console.log('======================================================================');

    console.log('POOL MATCH                  : YES');
    console.log('pTON VERSION                :', proxyTon.version);
    console.log('pTON ADDRESS MATCH          : YES');

    console.log(
        'ROOSTER AMOUNT              :',
        ROOSTER_AMOUNT.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'pTON AMOUNT                 :',
        PTON_AMOUNT.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'MIN LP OUT                  :',
        MIN_LP_OUT.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'ROOSTER BODY PRESENT        : YES'
    );

    console.log(
        'pTON BODY PRESENT           : YES'
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log('ROOSTER BOC FILE             : ./rooster_v22_generated.boc');
    console.log('pTON BOC FILE                : ./pton_v22_generated.boc');

    console.log('');
    console.log('*** READ ONLY ***');
    console.log('*** NO TRANSACTION SENT ***');
    console.log('*** NO WALLET SIGNATURE ***');
}

    'EQDxn5MLbFyV649-OBviQrMp5KqWjGHNVwFM6GTeaLl8N0Ix'

    'EQCyuv-cYXFibA05bhiL3seM3Ur2B_bpsc364Zb8F3NMtrYx'
);

const PTON_MASTER = Address.parse(
    'EQBnGWMCf3-FZZq1W4IWcWiGAc3PHuZ0_H-7sad2oY00o83S'
);

const USER_ROOSTER_WALLET = Address.parse(
    'EQBzQOOJu9qQVli2R0o6p3pXvi_wj9O2ncMhv3UyAjvMiMOh'

    'EQCSIMGBps_qzRG3uPYhON8bucyCtu0mYdL1-u4gSz77IBa3'

    'EQD5ALaCuEqJ7Mi9qlXOA7BHMrTq8e2fZ5B_teWN17MZq-cf'
);

// Small diagnostic amount.
// 100 ROOSTER = 100 * 10^9 nano-units.
const SEND_AMOUNT = 100_000_000_000n;

// From our earlier pool calculation:
// expected LP ≈ 158009753
// 95% minimum = 150109265
const MIN_LP_OUT = 150_109_265n;

function printCellInfo(name: string, cell: Cell) {
    console.log('');
    console.log(`${name}:`);
    console.log(`BOC: ${cell.toBoc().toString('base64')}`);
    console.log(`HEX: ${cell.toBoc().toString('hex')}`);
    console.log(`BITS: ${cell.bits.length}`);
    console.log(`REFS: ${cell.refs.length}`);
}

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {
    console.log('');
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log(' STON.FI v2.2 PROVIDE LP TX BUILDER / INSPECTOR');

    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log('');

    console.log('ROUTER:');
    console.log(ROUTER.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log('USER:');
    console.log(USER.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log('USER ROOSTER WALLET:');
    console.log(USER_ROOSTER_WALLET.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log('ROOSTER MASTER:');
    console.log(ROOSTER_MASTER.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log('pTON MASTER:');
    console.log(PTON_MASTER.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log('ROUTER pTON WALLET:');
    console.log(ROUTER_PTON_WALLET.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log('EXPECTED POOL:');
    console.log(EXPECTED_POOL.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log('SEND AMOUNT:');
    console.log(`${SEND_AMOUNT} nano ROOSTER`);
    console.log('= 100 ROOSTER');

    console.log('');
    console.log('MIN LP OUT:');
    console.log(MIN_LP_OUT.toString());

    const router = DEX.v2_2.Router.CPI.create(ROUTER);

    const routerProvider = provider.provider(ROUTER);

    console.log('');
    console.log('Checking SDK-derived pool...');

    const pool = await router.getPoolAddressByJettonMinters(
        routerProvider,
        {
            token0: ROOSTER_MASTER,
            token1: PTON_MASTER,
        }
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log('SDK POOL:');
    console.log(pool.toString());

    console.log(
        'POOL MATCH:',
        pool.equals(EXPECTED_POOL) ? 'YES' : 'NO'
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log('Building Jetton LP transaction parameters...');
    console.log('NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT');

    const tx = await router.getProvideLiquidityJettonTxParams(
        routerProvider,
        {
            userWalletAddress: USER,
            sendTokenAddress: ROOSTER_MASTER,
            otherTokenAddress: PTON_MASTER,
            sendAmount: SEND_AMOUNT,
            minLpOut: MIN_LP_OUT,

            receiverAddress: USER,
            refundAddress: USER,
            excessesAddress: USER,
            transferExcessAddress: USER,
        }
    );

    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log(' SDK TRANSACTION PARAMETERS');
    console.log('============================================================');

    console.log('');
    console.log('TO:');
    console.log(tx.to.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log('VALUE:');
    console.log(tx.value.toString());
    console.log('nanoTON');

    console.log('');
    console.log('BODY:');
    if (!tx.body) {
    throw new Error('SDK returned empty transaction body');
}

printCellInfo('BODY', tx.body);

    console.log('');
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log(' EXPECTED DESTINATION CHECK');
    console.log('============================================================');

    console.log('');
    console.log('SDK TO:');
    console.log(tx.to.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log('EXPECTED USER ROOSTER WALLET:');
    console.log(USER_ROOSTER_WALLET.toString());

    console.log(
        'DESTINATION MATCH:',
        tx.to.equals(USER_ROOSTER_WALLET)
            ? 'YES'
            : 'NO'
    );

    console.log('');

    console.log(' END - READ ONLY');
    console.log('============================================================');
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\buildLiquidityTxV22.ts
====================================================================================================
import { Address } from '@ton/core';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';
import { DEX } from '@ston-fi/sdk';

const ROUTER = Address.parse(
    'EQCS4UEa5UaJLzOyyKieqQOQ2P9M-7kXpkO5HnP3Bv250cN3'
);

const ROOSTER = Address.parse(
    'EQCyuv-cYXFibA05bhiL3seM3Ur2B_bpsc364Zb8F3NMtrYx'
);

const PTON = Address.parse(
    'EQBnGWMCf3-FZZq1W4IWcWiGAc3PHuZ0_H-7sad2oY00o83S'
);

const USER = Address.parse(
    'EQDxn5MLbFyV649-OBviQrMp5KqWjGHNVwFM6GTeaLl8N0Ix'
);

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {

    console.log('');
    console.log('================================================');
    console.log('       STON.fi V2.2 BUILD-ONLY CHECK');
    console.log('================================================');
    console.log('');
    console.log('READ-ONLY');
    console.log('NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT.');
    console.log('');

    const router =
        DEX.v2_2.Router.CPI.create(ROUTER);

    const proxyTon =
        DEX.v2_2.pTON.create(PTON);

    const routerProvider =
        provider.provider(ROUTER);

    // ================================================
    // GET REAL POOL
    // ================================================

    const pool =
        await router.getPool(
            routerProvider,
            {
                token0: ROOSTER,
                token1: PTON,
            }
        );

    const poolAddress = pool.address;

    console.log('===== REAL POOL =====');
    console.log('');
    console.log('Pool:');
    console.log(poolAddress.toString());
    console.log('');

    // ================================================
    // READ REAL POOL DATA
    // ================================================

    const poolProvider =
        provider.provider(poolAddress);

    const poolData =
        await pool.getPoolData(poolProvider);

    console.log('===== CURRENT POOL =====');
    console.log('');

    console.log(
        'ROOSTER reserve:',
        Number(poolData.reserve0) / 1e9
    );

    console.log(
        'pTON reserve:',
        Number(poolData.reserve1) / 1e9
    );

    console.log(
        'Total LP:',
        poolData.totalSupplyLP.toString()
    );

    console.log('');

    // ================================================
    // PROPOSED LIQUIDITY
    // ================================================

    // Test deposit:
    // 100 ROOSTER

    const roosterAmount =
        100n * 1_000_000_000n;

    // Calculate proportional pTON amount:
    //
    // pTON = ROOSTER *
    //        reserve1 /
    //        reserve0

    const ptonAmount =
        roosterAmount *
        poolData.reserve1 /
        poolData.reserve0;

    console.log('===== PROPOSED DEPOSIT =====');
    console.log('');

    console.log(
        'ROOSTER:',
        Number(roosterAmount) / 1e9
    );

    console.log(
        'pTON raw:',
        ptonAmount.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'pTON:',
        Number(ptonAmount) / 1e9
    );

    console.log('');

    // ================================================
    // LP CALCULATION
    // ================================================

    const lpFromRooster =
        roosterAmount *
        poolData.totalSupplyLP /
        poolData.reserve0;

    const lpFromPton =
        ptonAmount *
        poolData.totalSupplyLP /
        poolData.reserve1;

    const expectedLp =
        lpFromRooster < lpFromPton
            ? lpFromRooster
            : lpFromPton;

    const minLpOut =
        expectedLp * 95n / 100n;

    const deadline =
        Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000) + 3600;

    console.log('===== LP CALCULATION =====');
    console.log('');

    console.log(
        'LP from ROOSTER:',
        lpFromRooster.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'LP from pTON:',
        lpFromPton.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'Expected LP:',
        expectedLp.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'Minimum LP (95%):',
        minLpOut.toString()
    );

    console.log('');

    // ================================================
    // BUILD ROOSTER TRANSACTION
    // ================================================

    console.log('===== BUILD ROOSTER TX =====');
    console.log('');

    const roosterTx =
        await router.getProvideLiquidityJettonTxParams(
            routerProvider,
            {
                sendTokenAddress: ROOSTER,
                otherTokenAddress: PTON,
                sendAmount: roosterAmount,
                userWalletAddress: USER,
                receiverAddress: USER,
                refundAddress: USER,
                excessesAddress: USER,
                minLpOut,
                deadline,
            }
        );

    console.log('Destination:');
    console.log(roosterTx.to.toString());

    console.log('');

    console.log('TON value:');
    console.log(roosterTx.value.toString());

    console.log(
        'TON value:',
        Number(roosterTx.value) / 1e9,
        'TON'
    );

    console.log('');

    console.log('Body exists:');
    console.log(roosterTx.body !== undefined);

    console.log('');

    // ================================================
    // BUILD pTON TRANSACTION
    // ================================================

    console.log('===== BUILD pTON TX =====');
    console.log('');

    const ptonTx =
        await router.getProvideLiquidityTonTxParams(
            routerProvider,
            {
                proxyTon,
                otherTokenAddress: ROOSTER,
                sendAmount: ptonAmount,
                userWalletAddress: USER,
                receiverAddress: USER,
                refundAddress: USER,
                excessesAddress: USER,
                minLpOut,
                deadline,
            }
        );

    console.log('Destination:');
    console.log(ptonTx.to.toString());

    console.log('');

    console.log('TON value:');
    console.log(ptonTx.value.toString());

    console.log(
        'TON value:',
        Number(ptonTx.value) / 1e9,
        'TON'
    );

    console.log('');

    console.log('Body exists:');
    console.log(ptonTx.body !== undefined);

    console.log('');

    // ================================================
    // FINAL
    // ================================================

    console.log('================================================');
    console.log('             BUILD-ONLY RESULT');
    console.log('================================================');
    console.log('');

    console.log('Pool:');
    console.log(poolAddress.toString());

    console.log('');

    console.log('ROOSTER amount:');
    console.log(
        Number(roosterAmount) / 1e9,
        'ROOSTER'
    );

    console.log('');

    console.log('pTON amount:');
    console.log(
        Number(ptonAmount) / 1e9,
        'pTON'
    );

    console.log('');

    console.log('Expected LP:');
    console.log(expectedLp.toString());

    console.log('');

    console.log('Minimum LP:');
    console.log(minLpOut.toString());

    console.log('');

    console.log('ROOSTER transaction built: YES');
    console.log('pTON transaction built: YES');

    console.log('');

    console.log('NO TRANSACTION WAS SENT.');
    console.log('NO ROOSTER WAS TRANSFERRED.');
    console.log('NO TON WAS TRANSFERRED.');
    console.log('');
}

FILE: scripts\buildRoosterInitialLiquidity.ts

import { Address, toNano } from '@ton/core';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';
import { DEX } from '@ston-fi/sdk';

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {
    const router = DEX.v2_1.Router.CPI.create(
        Address.parse(
            'kQALh-JBBIKK7gr0o4AVf9JZnEsFndqO0qTCyT-D-yBsWk0v'
        )
    );

    const roosterMaster = Address.parse(
        'EQCyuv-cYXFibA05bhiL3seM3Ur2B_bpsc364Zb8F3NMtrYx'
    );

    const userWallet = provider.sender().address;

    if (!userWallet) {
        throw new Error('Wallet address is not available.');
    }

    const roosterAmount = 100000n * 1000000000n;
    const tonAmount = toNano('0.1');

    const routerProvider = provider.provider(router.address);

    console.log('===== BUILD INITIAL LIQUIDITY =====');
    console.log('Router:', router.address.toString());
    console.log('ROOSTER:', roosterMaster.toString());
    console.log('User:', userWallet.toString());
    console.log('');
    console.log('ROOSTER amount:', roosterAmount.toString());
    console.log('ROOSTER human:', Number(roosterAmount) / 1e9);
    console.log('TON amount:', tonAmount.toString());
    console.log('TON human:', Number(tonAmount) / 1e9);

    /*
     * Build ROOSTER -> Router transaction
     */
    const roosterTx =
        await router.getProvideLiquidityJettonTxParams(
            routerProvider,
            {
                userWalletAddress: userWallet,
                sendTokenAddress: roosterMaster,
                otherTokenAddress: Address.parse(
                    'EQCM3B12QK1e4yZSf8GtBRT0aLMNyEsBc_DhVfRRtOEffLez'
                ),
                sendAmount: roosterAmount,
                minLpOut: '1',
                refundAddress: userWallet,
                excessesAddress: userWallet,
                transferExcessAddress: userWallet,
            }
        );

    console.log('');
    console.log('===== ROOSTER TX =====');
    console.log('To:', roosterTx.to.toString());
    console.log('Value:', roosterTx.value.toString());
    console.log('Value TON:', Number(roosterTx.value) / 1e9);

    if (roosterTx.body) {
        console.log(
            'Body BOC:',
            roosterTx.body.toBoc().toString('base64')
        );
    }

    /*
     * Build TON -> pTON -> Router transaction
     */
    const proxyTon = DEX.v2_1.pTON.create(
        Address.parse(
            'kQACS30DNoUQ7NfApPvzh7eBmSZ9L4ygJ-lkNWtba8TQT-Px'
        )
    );

    const tonTx =
        await router.getProvideLiquidityTonTxParams(
            routerProvider,
            {
                userWalletAddress: userWallet,
                sendAmount: tonAmount,
                otherTokenAddress: roosterMaster,
                minLpOut: '1',
                proxyTon,
            }
        );

    console.log('');
    console.log('===== TON/pTON TX =====');
    console.log('To:', tonTx.to.toString());
    console.log('Value:', tonTx.value.toString());
    console.log('Value TON:', Number(tonTx.value) / 1e9);

    if (tonTx.body) {
        console.log(
            'Body BOC:',
            tonTx.body.toBoc().toString('base64')
        );
    }

    console.log('');
    console.log('===== NO TRANSACTION SENT =====');
}

FILE: scripts\buildRoosterLiquidity.ts

import { Address } from '@ton/core';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';
import { DEX } from '@ston-fi/sdk';
import * as fs from 'fs';

const ROUTER = Address.parse(
    'EQCS4UEa5UaJLzOyyKieqQOQ2P9M-7kXpkO5HnP3Bv250cN3'
);

const ROOSTER = Address.parse(
    'EQCyuv-cYXFibA05bhiL3seM3Ur2B_bpsc364Zb8F3NMtrYx'
);

const PTON = Address.parse(
    'EQBnGWMCf3-FZZq1W4IWcWiGAc3PHuZ0_H-7sad2oY00o83S'
);

const USER = Address.parse(
    'EQDxn5MLbFyV649-OBviQrMp5KqWjGHNVwFM6GTeaLl8N0Ix'
);

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {
    const router = provider.open(
        DEX.v2_2.Router.CPI.create(ROUTER)
    );

    const tx = await router.getProvideLiquidityJettonTxParams({
        sendTokenAddress: ROOSTER,
        otherTokenAddress: PTON,
        sendAmount: 500000000000000n,
        userWalletAddress: USER,
        receiverAddress: USER,
        refundAddress: USER,
        excessesAddress: USER,
        minLpOut: 0n
    });

    const boc = tx.body
        ? tx.body.toBoc().toString('base64')
        : '';

    fs.writeFileSync(
        'current_rooster_liquidity.boc',
        boc
    );

    console.log('===== ROOSTER BUILD ONLY =====');
    console.log('TO:', tx.to.toString());
    console.log('VALUE:', tx.value.toString());
    console.log('VALUE TON:', Number(tx.value) / 1e9);
    console.log('BODY BOC:', boc);
    console.log('');
    console.log('Saved: current_rooster_liquidity.boc');
    console.log('===== NO TRANSACTION SENT =====');
}

import { Address, toNano } from '@ton/core';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';
import { DEX, pTON } from '@ston-fi/sdk';

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {
    const router = DEX.v2_1.Router.CPI.create(
        Address.parse(
            'kQALh-JBBIKK7gr0o4AVf9JZnEsFndqO0qTCyT-D-yBsWk0v'
        )
    );

    const proxyTon = pTON.v2_1.create(
        Address.parse(
            'kQACS30DNoUQ7NfApPvzh7eBmSZ9L4ygJ-lkNWtba8TQT-Px'
        )
    );

    const rooster = Address.parse(
        'EQCyuv-cYXFibA05bhiL3seM3Ur2B_bpsc364Zb8F3NMtrYx'
    );

    const userWallet = provider.sender().address;

    if (!userWallet) {
        throw new Error('Wallet address is not available.');
    }

    console.log('===== BUILD ROOSTER LIQUIDITY TX =====');
    console.log('Router:', router.address.toString());
    console.log('pTON:', proxyTon.address.toString());
    console.log('ROOSTER:', rooster.toString());
    console.log('User:', userWallet.toString());

    const tx = await router.getProvideLiquidityTonTxParams(
        provider.provider(router.address),
        {
            userWalletAddress: userWallet,
            sendAmount: toNano('0.1'),
            otherTokenAddress: rooster,
            minLpOut: '1',
            proxyTon,
        }
    );

    console.log('===== TX PARAMS =====');
    console.log('To:', tx.to.toString());
    console.log('Value:', tx.value.toString());
    console.log('Value TON:', Number(tx.value) / 1e9);
    console.log('Body exists:', !!tx.body);

    if (tx.body) {
        console.log(
            'Body BOC:',
            tx.body.toBoc().toString('base64')
        );
    }
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\buildRoosterMetadata.ts
====================================================================================================
import { beginCell, Dictionary } from '@ton/core';
import { createHash } from 'crypto';

function keyOf(name: string): bigint {
    const hash = createHash('sha256').update(name).digest();
    return BigInt('0x' + hash.toString('hex'));
}

function valueOf(value: string) {
    return beginCell()
        .storeStringTail(value)
        .endCell();
}

export async function run() {
    const metadata = Dictionary.empty<bigint, ReturnType<typeof valueOf>>(
        Dictionary.Keys.BigUint(256),
        Dictionary.Values.Cell()
    );

    metadata.set(keyOf('name'), valueOf('Rooster Coin'));
    metadata.set(keyOf('symbol'), valueOf('ROOSTER'));
    metadata.set(keyOf('decimals'), valueOf('9'));
    metadata.set(
        keyOf('description'),
        valueOf('Rooster Coin - Fixed supply of 1,000,000,000 ROOSTER')
    );

    const content = beginCell()
        .storeUint(0, 8)
        .storeDict(metadata)
        .endCell();

    console.log('');
    console.log('===== ROOSTER METADATA =====');
    console.log('Name: Rooster Coin');
    console.log('Symbol: ROOSTER');
    console.log('Decimals: 9');
    console.log('Description: Rooster Coin - Fixed supply of 1,000,000,000 ROOSTER');
    console.log('');
    console.log('Metadata Cell:');
    console.log(content.toBoc().toString('base64'));
    console.log('');
    console.log('Cell hash:', content.hash().toString('hex'));
    console.log('============================');
}

import { Address } from '@ton/core';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';
import { DEX, pTON } from '@ston-fi/sdk';
import * as fs from 'fs';

const ROUTER = Address.parse(
    'EQCS4UEa5UaJLzOyyKieqQOQ2P9M-7kXpkO5HnP3Bv250cN3'
);

const ROOSTER = Address.parse(
    'EQCyuv-cYXFibA05bhiL3seM3Ur2B_bpsc364Zb8F3NMtrYx'
);

const USER = Address.parse(
    'EQDxn5MLbFyV649-OBviQrMp5KqWjGHNVwFM6GTeaLl8N0Ix'
);

const PTON = pTON.v2_1.create(
    Address.parse(
        'EQBnGWMCf3-FZZq1W4IWcWiGAc3PHuZ0_H-7sad2oY00o83S'
    )
);

const SEND_AMOUNT = 500000000n;
const MIN_LP_OUT = 300329052918n;

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {
    const router = provider.open(
        DEX.v2_2.Router.CPI.create(ROUTER)
    );

    const tx = await router.getProvideLiquidityTonTxParams({
        proxyTon: PTON,
        otherTokenAddress: ROOSTER,
        sendAmount: SEND_AMOUNT,
        userWalletAddress: USER,
        receiverAddress: USER,
        refundAddress: USER,
        excessesAddress: USER,
        minLpOut: MIN_LP_OUT
    });

    const boc = tx.body
        ? tx.body.toBoc().toString('base64')
        : '';

    fs.writeFileSync(
        'current_pton_liquidity.boc',
        boc
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log('========================================');
    console.log(' STON.FI v2.2 PTON LIQUIDITY BUILD');
    console.log(' BUILD ONLY - NO TRANSACTION');
    console.log('========================================');

    console.log('');
    console.log('ROUTER:', ROUTER.toString());
    console.log('ROOSTER:', ROOSTER.toString());
    console.log('USER:', USER.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log('===== AMOUNTS =====');

    console.log(
        'pTON amount:',
        SEND_AMOUNT.toString(),
        'nanoTON'
    );

    console.log(
        'pTON amount:',
        Number(SEND_AMOUNT) / 1e9,
        'TON'
    );

    console.log(
        'Expected LP:',
        '316135845177'
    );

    console.log(
        'minLpOut:',
        MIN_LP_OUT.toString()
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log('===== OUTER MESSAGE =====');

    console.log('TO:', tx.to.toString());

    console.log(
        'VALUE:',
        tx.value.toString(),
        'nanoTON'
    );

    console.log(
        'VALUE:',
        Number(tx.value) / 1e9,
        'TON'
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log('===== BOC =====');

    console.log(boc);

    console.log('');
    console.log('Saved: current_pton_liquidity.boc');
    console.log('');
    console.log('NO TRANSACTION SENT.');
    console.log('========================================');
}

const ROUTER_ADDRESS = Address.parse(
    'EQCS4UEa5UaJLzOyyKieqQOQ2P9M-7kXpkO5HnP3Bv250cN3'
);

const ROOSTER_MASTER = Address.parse(
    'EQCyuv-cYXFibA05bhiL3seM3Ur2B_bpsc364Zb8F3NMtrYx'
);

// ============================================================

// 100 ROOSTER
// ROOSTER has 9 decimals
const ROOSTER_AMOUNT = 100000000000n;

// 249908 nano-pTON
const PTON_AMOUNT = 249908n;
