// ============================================================
// CELL PRINTER
// ============================================================

function printCell(
    label: string,
    body: Cell | null | undefined
) {
    console.log('');
    console.log(`--- ${label} ---`);

    if (!body) {
        console.log('EMPTY');
        return;
    }

    console.log('BOC:');
    console.log(
        body.toBoc().toString('base64')
    );

    console.log('Hash:');
    console.log(
        body.hash().toString('hex')
    );

    console.log('Bits:', body.bits.length);
    console.log('Refs:', body.refs.length);
}

FILE: scripts\buildSdkV22TonLiquidity.ts

import { Address } from "@ton/core";
import { TonClient4 } from "@ton/ton";
import { DEX } from "@ston-fi/sdk";
import { writeFileSync } from "fs";

export async function run() {
  console.log("========================================");
  console.log(" SDK v2.2 TON LIQUIDITY BODY CHECK");
  console.log(" READ ONLY — NO TRANSACTION SENT");
  console.log("========================================");

  const client = new TonClient4({
    endpoint: "https://sandbox-v4.tonhubapi.com",
  });

  const router = DEX.v2_2.Router.CPI.create(
    Address.parse(
    "EQCS4UEa5UaJLzOyyKieqQOQ2P9M-7kXpkO5HnP3Bv250cN3"
    )
  );

  const user = Address.parse(
    "EQDxn5MLbFyV649-OBviQrMp5KqWjGHNVwFM6GTeaLl8N0Ix"
  );

  const proxyTon = DEX.v2_2.pTON.create(
    Address.parse(
      "EQBnGWMCf3-FZZq1W4IWcWiGAc3PHuZ0_H-7sad2oY00o83S"
    )
  );

  const result = await router.getProvideLiquidityTonTxParams(
    client.provider(router.address),
    {
      proxyTon,
      userWalletAddress: user,
      otherTokenAddress: Address.parse(
        "EQCyuv-cYXFibA05bhiL3seM3Ur2B_bpsc364Zb8F3NMtrYx"
      ),
      sendAmount: 249908000000000n,
      minLpOut: 150109265n,
      receiverAddress: user,
      refundAddress: user,
      excessesAddress: user,
      deadline: 1790531314,
    }
  );

  const body = result.body;

if (!body) {
  throw new Error("SDK returned empty transaction body");
}

console.log("");
console.log("===== SDK RESULT =====");
console.log("to:", result.to.toString());
console.log("value:", result.value.toString());
console.log("bits:", body.bits.length);
console.log("refs:", body.refs.length);
console.log("body hash:", body.hash().toString("hex"));

const boc = body.toBoc();

  console.log("BOC:", boc.toString("base64"));

  writeFileSync(
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\scripts\\sdk_v22_ton_body.boc",
    boc
  );

  console.log("");
  console.log("BOC saved.");
  console.log("NO TRANSACTION SENT.");
}

import { Address, beginCell } from "@ton/core";
import { DEX } from "@ston-fi/sdk";

const ROUTER = Address.parse(
    "EQCS4UEa5UaJLzOyyKieqQOQ2P9M-7kXpkO5HnP3Bv250cN3"
);

const ROOSTER = Address.parse(
    "EQCyuv-cYXFibA05bhiL3seM3Ur2B_bpsc364Zb8F3NMtrYx"
);

const PTON = Address.parse(
    "EQBnGWMCf3-FZZq1W4IWcWiGAc3PHuZ0_H-7sad2oY00o83S"
);

const USER = Address.parse(
    "EQDxn5MLbFyV649-OBviQrMp5KqWjGHNVwFM6GTeaLl8N0Ix"
);

const DEADLINE = 1790588068n;
const MIN_LP_OUT = 300329052918n;

console.log("==============================================");
console.log(" STON.FI v2.2 SINGLE-SIDE ROOSTER PREFLIGHT");
console.log(" READ-ONLY ONLY");
console.log(" NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT");
console.log("==============================================");

console.log("\nEXPECTED:");
console.log("Router:", ROUTER.toString());
console.log("ROOSTER:", ROOSTER.toString());
console.log("pTON:", PTON.toString());
console.log("User:", USER.toString());

console.log("\nPARAMETERS:");
console.log("Send token: ROOSTER");
console.log("Other token: pTON");
console.log("Send amount:", "200073535827652");
console.log("Min LP:", MIN_LP_OUT.toString());
console.log("Deadline:", DEADLINE.toString());
console.log("bothPositive: false");

const routerWalletAddress = Address.parse(
    "EQCSIMGBps_qzRG3uPYhON8bucyCtu0mYdL1-u4gSz77IBa3"
);

console.log("\nOTHER TOKEN WALLET AT ROUTER:");
console.log(routerWalletAddress.toString());

const payload = beginCell()
    .storeUint(0x37c096df, 32)
    .storeAddress(routerWalletAddress)
    .storeAddress(USER)
    .storeAddress(USER)
    .storeUint(DEADLINE, 64)
    .storeRef(
        beginCell()
            .storeCoins(MIN_LP_OUT)
            .storeAddress(USER)
            .storeUint(0, 1)
            .storeCoins(0)
            .storeMaybeRef(null)
            .endCell()
    )
    .endCell();

console.log("\n===== PROVIDE_LP PAYLOAD =====");

console.log("Opcode: 0x37c096df");
console.log("Router wallet:", routerWalletAddress.toString());
console.log("Refund:", USER.toString());
console.log("Excesses:", USER.toString());
console.log("Deadline:", DEADLINE.toString());
console.log("Min LP:", MIN_LP_OUT.toString());
console.log("Receiver:", USER.toString());
console.log("bothPositive: false");
console.log("Custom payload: NONE");

console.log("\nPAYLOAD BOC:");
console.log(payload.toBoc().toString("base64"));

console.log("\nPAYLOAD SIZE:");
console.log("bits:", payload.bits.length);
console.log("refs:", payload.refs.length);

console.log("\n==============================================");
console.log(" IMPORTANT");
console.log(" This is only the forward PROVIDE_LP payload.");
console.log(" No Jetton Transfer is constructed.");
console.log(" No wallet transaction is sent.");
console.log("==============================================");

    TonConnect,

} from '@tonconnect/sdk';


// ============================================================
// STON.fi v2.2
// ROOSTER / pTON

// NO send()
// NO sender.send()
// NO connector.sendTransaction()
// NO TONKEEPER APPROVAL
// NO BROADCAST
// NO STATE CHANGE
// ============================================================


// ============================================================
// ADDRESSES
// ============================================================

const USER = Address.parse(
    'EQDxn5MLbFyV649-OBviQrMp5KqWjGHNVwFM6GTeaLl8N0Ix'
);

const ROUTER_ADDRESS = Address.parse(
    'EQCS4UEa5UaJLzOyyKieqQOQ2P9M-7kXpkO5HnP3Bv250cN3'
);

const ROOSTER_MASTER = Address.parse(
    'EQCyuv-cYXFibA05bhiL3seM3Ur2B_bpsc364Zb8F3NMtrYx'
);

const PTON_MASTER = Address.parse(
    'EQBnGWMCf3-FZZq1W4IWcWiGAc3PHuZ0_H-7sad2oY00o83S'
);

const ROOSTER_USER_WALLET = Address.parse(
    'EQBzQOOJu9qQVli2R0o6p3pXvi_wj9O2ncMhv3UyAjvMiMOh'
);

const PTON_POOL_WALLET = Address.parse(
    'EQCSIMGBps_qzRG3uPYhON8bucyCtu0mYdL1-u4gSz77IBa3'

// 100 ROOSTER

const PTON_AMOUNT = 249908n;
// 0.000249908 pTON

// ============================================================

const ROOSTER_TX_VALUE = 300000000n;
// 0.300000000 TON

// 0.310249908 TON


// ============================================================

function sameAddress(
    a: Address,
    b: Address
): boolean {

    return (
        a.toRawString() ===
        b.toRawString()
    );
}


function section(
    title: string
): void {

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        '============================================================'
    );
    console.log(title);
    console.log(
        '============================================================'
    );
}


function nanoToTon(
    value: bigint
): string {

    const whole =
        value / 1000000000n;

    const fraction =
        (value % 1000000000n)
            .toString()
            .padStart(9, '0');

    return (
        whole.toString() +
        '.' +
        fraction
    );
}


function sha256Hex(
    data: Buffer
): string {

    const crypto =
        require('crypto');

    return crypto
        .createHash('sha256')
        .update(data)
        .digest('hex');
}


// ============================================================
// TONCONNECT STORAGE
// ============================================================

function createStorage(
    storagePath: string
) {

    return {

        async setItem(
            key: string,
            value: string
        ): Promise<void> {

            let obj: Record<string, string> = {};

            try {

                obj =
                    JSON.parse(
                        await fs.promises.readFile(
                            storagePath,
                            'utf8'
                        )
                    );

            } catch {

                obj = {};
            }

            obj[key] = value;

            await fs.promises.mkdir(
                path.dirname(storagePath),
                {
                    recursive: true
                }
            );

            await fs.promises.writeFile(
                storagePath,
                JSON.stringify(obj)
            );
        },


        async getItem(
            key: string
        ): Promise<string | null> {

            try {

                const obj =
                    JSON.parse(
                        await fs.promises.readFile(
                            storagePath,
                            'utf8'
                        )
                    );

                return (
                    obj[key] ??
                    null
                );

            } catch {

                return null;
            }
        },


        async removeItem(
            key: string
        ): Promise<void> {

            let obj: Record<string, string> = {};

            try {

                obj =
                    JSON.parse(
                        await fs.promises.readFile(
                            storagePath,
                            'utf8'
                        )
                    );

            } catch {

                return;
            }

            delete obj[key];

            await fs.promises.writeFile(
                storagePath,
                JSON.stringify(obj)
            );
        }

    };
}


// ============================================================
// MAIN
// ============================================================

export async function run(
    provider: NetworkProvider
) {

    section(
        'STON.fi v2.2 TWO-MESSAGE TONCONNECT BUILDER'
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        'MODE: BUILD ONLY'
    );
    console.log(
        'NETWORK: TESTNET'
    );
    console.log(
        'SEND: NO'
    );
    console.log(
        'APPROVAL: NO'
    );
    console.log(
        'BROADCAST: NO'
    );
    console.log(
        'STATE CHANGE: NO'
    );


    // ========================================================
    // 1. TONCONNECT
    // ========================================================

    section(
        '1. TONCONNECT CONNECTION'
    );


    const storagePath =
        path.join(
            process.cwd(),
            'temp',
            'testnet',
            'tonconnect.json'
        );


    console.log('');
    console.log(
        'Storage:',
        storagePath
    );


    if (
        !fs.existsSync(
            storagePath
        )
    ) {

        throw new Error(
            'STOP: TonConnect storage file does not exist'
        );
    }


    const connector =
        new TonConnect({

            storage:
                createStorage(
                    storagePath
                ),

            manifestUrl:
                'https://raw.githubusercontent.com/ton-org/blueprint/main/tonconnect/manifest.json'

        });


    await connector.restoreConnection();


    if (!connector.wallet) {

        throw new Error(
            'STOP: TonConnect wallet was not restored'
        );
    }


    const connectedWallet =
        Address.parse(
            connector.wallet.account.address
        );


    console.log('');
    console.log(
        'Connected wallet:',
        connectedWallet.toString({
            bounceable: true,
            testOnly: true
        })
    );


    console.log(
        'Expected wallet:',
        USER.toString({
            bounceable: true,
            testOnly: true
        })
    );


    if (
        !sameAddress(
            connectedWallet,
            USER
        )
    ) {

        throw new Error(
            'STOP: Connected wallet mismatch'
        );
    }


    console.log(
        'Wallet identity: PASS'
    );


    // ========================================================
    // 2. ROUTER
    // ========================================================

    section(
        '2. STON.fi v2.2 ROUTER'
    );


    const router =
        provider.open(
            DEX.v2_2.Router.CPI.create(
                ROUTER_ADDRESS
            )
        );


    const routerData =
        await router.getRouterData();


    const routerVersion =
        await router.getRouterVersion();


    console.log('');
    console.log(
        'Router:',
        ROUTER_ADDRESS.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'Router ID:',
        routerData.routerId.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'Version:',
        routerVersion
    );

    console.log(
        'Locked:',
        routerData.isLocked
    );


    if (
        routerVersion.major !== 2 ||
        routerVersion.minor !== 2
    ) {

        throw new Error(
            'STOP: Router is not v2.2'
        );
    }


    if (
        routerData.isLocked
    ) {

        throw new Error(
            'STOP: Router is locked'
        );
    }


    console.log(
        'Router verification: PASS'
    );


    // ========================================================
    // 3. POOL
    // ========================================================

    section(
        '3. POOL'
    );


    const pool =
        await router.getPool({

            token0:
                ROOSTER_MASTER,

            token1:
                PTON_MASTER

        });


    console.log('');
    console.log(
        'Resolved pool:',
        pool.address.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'Expected pool:',
        'EQD5ALaCuEqJ7Mi9qlXOA7BHMrTq8e2fZ5B_teWN17MZq-cf'
    );


    if (
        !sameAddress(
            pool.address,
            Address.parse(
                'EQD5ALaCuEqJ7Mi9qlXOA7BHMrTq8e2fZ5B_teWN17MZq-cf'
            )
        )
    ) {

        throw new Error(
            'STOP: Pool mismatch'
        );
    }


    console.log(
        'Pool identity: PASS'
    );


    // ========================================================
    // 4. FRESH DEADLINE
    // ========================================================

    section(
        '4. FRESH DEADLINE'
    );


    const now =
        Math.floor(
            Date.now() / 1000
        );


    const deadline = now + 600;


    console.log('');
    console.log(
        'Current Unix:',
        now
    );

    console.log(
        'Deadline:',
        deadline.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'Validity:',
        '600 seconds'
    );


    // ========================================================
    // 5. BUILD ROOSTER LEG
    // ========================================================

    section(
        '5. BUILD ROOSTER LEG'
    );


    const roosterTx =
        await router.getProvideLiquidityJettonTxParams({

            userWalletAddress:
                USER,

            sendTokenAddress:
                ROOSTER_MASTER,

            otherTokenAddress:
                PTON_MASTER,

            sendAmount:
                ROOSTER_AMOUNT,

            minLpOut:
                MIN_LP_OUT,

            receiverAddress:
                USER,

            refundAddress:
                USER,

            excessesAddress:
                USER,

            deadline

        });


    if (!roosterTx.body) {

        throw new Error(
            'STOP: ROOSTER body missing'
        );
    }


    console.log('');
    console.log(
        'ROOSTER TO:',
        roosterTx.to.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'ROOSTER VALUE:',
        nanoToTon(
            BigInt(roosterTx.value)
        ),
        'TON'
    );


    if (
        !sameAddress(
            roosterTx.to,
            ROOSTER_USER_WALLET
        )
    ) {

        throw new Error(
            'STOP: ROOSTER destination mismatch'
        );
    }


    if (
        BigInt(roosterTx.value) !==
        ROOSTER_TX_VALUE
    ) {

        throw new Error(
            'STOP: ROOSTER TX value mismatch'
        );
    }


    // ========================================================
    // 6. BUILD pTON LEG
    // ========================================================

    section(
        '6. BUILD pTON LEG'
    );


    const proxyTon =
        pTON.v2_1.create(
            PTON_MASTER
        );


    const ptonTx =
        await router.getProvideLiquidityTonTxParams({

            userWalletAddress:
                USER,

            otherTokenAddress:
                ROOSTER_MASTER,

            sendAmount:
                PTON_AMOUNT,

            minLpOut:
                MIN_LP_OUT,

            receiverAddress:
                USER,

            refundAddress:
                USER,

            excessesAddress:
                USER,

            proxyTon,

            deadline

        });


    if (!ptonTx.body) {

        throw new Error(
            'STOP: pTON body missing'
        );
    }


    console.log('');
    console.log(
        'pTON TO:',
        ptonTx.to.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'pTON VALUE:',
        nanoToTon(
            BigInt(ptonTx.value)
        ),
        'TON'
    );


    if (
        !sameAddress(
            ptonTx.to,
            PTON_POOL_WALLET
        )
    ) {

        throw new Error(
            'STOP: pTON destination mismatch'
        );
    }


    if (
        BigInt(ptonTx.value) !==
        PTON_TX_VALUE
    ) {

        throw new Error(
            'STOP: pTON TX value mismatch'
        );
    }


    // ========================================================
    // 7. SERIALIZE PAYLOADS
    // ========================================================

    section(
        '7. SERIALIZE TWO PAYLOADS'
    );


    const roosterBoc =
        roosterTx.body.toBoc({
            idx: false
        });


    const ptonBoc =
        ptonTx.body.toBoc({
            idx: false
        });


    const roosterPayload =
        roosterBoc.toString(
            'base64'
        );


    const ptonPayload =
        ptonBoc.toString(
            'base64'
        );


    console.log('');
    console.log(
        'ROOSTER BOC bytes:',
        roosterBoc.length
    );

    console.log(
        'ROOSTER SHA256:',
        sha256Hex(roosterBoc)
    );


    console.log('');
    console.log(
        'pTON BOC bytes:',
        ptonBoc.length
    );

    console.log(
        'pTON SHA256:',
        sha256Hex(ptonBoc)
    );


    // ========================================================
    // 8. BUILD TONCONNECT MESSAGE 1
    // ========================================================

    section(
        '8. TONCONNECT MESSAGE 1'
    );


    const message1 = {

        address:
            roosterTx.to.toString({
                bounceable: true,
                testOnly: true
            }),

        amount:
            BigInt(
                roosterTx.value
            ).toString(),

        payload:
            roosterPayload

    };


    console.log('');
    console.log(
        'Message 1 address:',
        message1.address
    );

    console.log(
        'Message 1 amount:',
        message1.amount
    );

    console.log(
        'Message 1 TON:',
        nanoToTon(
            BigInt(
                message1.amount
            )
        )
    );

    console.log(
        'Message 1 payload: PRESENT'
    );


    // ========================================================
    // 9. TONCONNECT MESSAGE 2
    // ========================================================

    section(
        '9. TONCONNECT MESSAGE 2'
    );


    const message2 = {

        address:
            ptonTx.to.toString({
                bounceable: true,
                testOnly: true
            }),

        amount:
            BigInt(
                ptonTx.value
            ).toString(),

        payload:
            ptonPayload

    };


    console.log('');
    console.log(
        'Message 2 address:',
        message2.address
    );

    console.log(
        'Message 2 amount:',
        message2.amount
    );

    console.log(
        'Message 2 TON:',
        nanoToTon(
            BigInt(
                message2.amount
            )
        )
    );

    console.log(
        'Message 2 payload: PRESENT'
    );


    // ========================================================
    // 10. BUILD TWO-MESSAGE REQUEST
    // ========================================================

    section(
        '10. BUILD TWO-MESSAGE SEND REQUEST'
    );


    const request: SendTransactionRequest = {

        validUntil:
            now + 600,

        network:
            CHAIN.TESTNET,

        messages: [

            message1,

            message2

        ]

    };


    console.log('');
    console.log(
        'Network:',
        request.network
    );

    console.log(
        'Valid until:',
        request.validUntil
    );

    console.log(
        'Message count:',
        request.messages.length
    );


    // ========================================================
    // 11. STRUCTURE VALIDATION
    // ========================================================

    section(
        '11. TWO-MESSAGE STRUCTURE VALIDATION'
    );


    if (
        request.messages.length !== 2
    ) {

        throw new Error(
            'STOP: Expected exactly 2 messages'
        );
    }


    if (
        request.messages.length > 4
    ) {

        throw new Error(
            'STOP: TonConnect maximum is 4 messages'
        );
    }


    if (
        request.messages[0].address !==
        message1.address
    ) {

        throw new Error(
            'STOP: Message 1 address changed'
        );
    }


    if (
        request.messages[1].address !==
        message2.address
    ) {

        throw new Error(
            'STOP: Message 2 address changed'
        );
    }


    if (
        request.messages[0].amount !==
        ROOSTER_TX_VALUE.toString()
    ) {

        throw new Error(
            'STOP: Message 1 amount mismatch'
        );
    }


    if (
        request.messages[1].amount !==
        PTON_TX_VALUE.toString()
    ) {

        throw new Error(
            'STOP: Message 2 amount mismatch'
        );
    }


    if (
        request.network !==
        CHAIN.TESTNET
    ) {

        throw new Error(
            'STOP: Network is not TESTNET'
        );
    }


    if (
        request.validUntil <= now
    ) {

        throw new Error(
            'STOP: validUntil is not in the future'
        );
    }


    console.log(
        'Message count = 2: PASS'
    );

    console.log(
        'Message 1 destination: PASS'
    );

    console.log(
        'Message 1 amount: PASS'
    );

    console.log(
        'Message 1 payload: PASS'
    );

    console.log(
        'Message 2 destination: PASS'
    );

    console.log(
        'Message 2 amount: PASS'
    );

    console.log(
        'Message 2 payload: PASS'
    );

    console.log(
        'Network TESTNET: PASS'
    );

    console.log(
        'validUntil: PASS'
    );


    // ========================================================
    // 12. FINAL SUMMARY
    // ========================================================

    section(
        '12. FINAL TWO-MESSAGE REQUEST'
    );


    console.log('');
    console.log(
        'MESSAGE 1'
    );

    console.log(
        '  TO:',
        message1.address
    );

    console.log(
        '  VALUE:',
        nanoToTon(
            BigInt(message1.amount)
        ),
        'TON'
    );


    console.log('');
    console.log(
        'MESSAGE 2'
    );

    console.log(
        '  TO:',
        message2.address
    );

    console.log(
        '  VALUE:',
        nanoToTon(
            BigInt(message2.amount)
        ),
        'TON'
    );


    console.log('');
    console.log(
        'TOTAL VALUE:',
        nanoToTon(
            BigInt(message1.amount) +
            BigInt(message2.amount)
        ),
        'TON'
    );


    console.log('');
    console.log(
        'TONCONNECT REQUEST: BUILT'
    );


    // ========================================================
    // 13. ABSOLUTE SAFETY
    // ========================================================

    section(
        '13. ABSOLUTE SAFETY'
    );


    console.log('');
    console.log(
        'connector.sendTransaction(): NOT CALLED'
    );

    console.log(
        'sender.send(): NOT CALLED'
    );

    console.log(
        'wallet.send(): NOT CALLED'
    );

    console.log(
        'Tonkeeper approval: NOT REQUESTED'
    );

    console.log(
        'Transaction broadcast: NO'
    );

    console.log(
        'Wallet state change: NO'
    );

    console.log(
        'Pool state change: NO'
    );


    console.log('');
    console.log(
        '============================================================'
    );

    console.log(
        ' STATUS: TWO-MESSAGE REQUEST BUILD PASSED'
    );

    console.log(
        '============================================================'
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        'IMPORTANT: The request was constructed only.'
    );

    console.log(
        'No wallet transaction was submitted.'
    );
}


export default run;

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\buildV22ProvideLpPayload.ts
====================================================================================================
import { beginCell, Address } from "@ton/core";
import * as fs from "fs";

const OP_PROVIDE_LP = 0x37c096df;

const routerWalletAddress = Address.parse(
  "EQCSIMGBps_qzRG3uPYhON8bucyCtu0mYdL1-u4gSz77IBa3"
);

const refundAddress = Address.parse(
  "0QDxn5MLbFyV649-OBviQrMp5KqWjGHNVwFM6GTeaLl8N6R-"
);

const excessesAddress = refundAddress;
const receiverAddress = refundAddress;

const minLpOut = 150109265n;
const bothPositive = true;

const deadline = Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000) + 3600;

const forwardPayload = beginCell()
  .storeUint(OP_PROVIDE_LP, 32)
  .storeAddress(routerWalletAddress)
  .storeAddress(refundAddress)
  .storeAddress(excessesAddress)
  .storeUint(deadline, 64)
  .storeRef(
    beginCell()
      .storeCoins(minLpOut)
      .storeAddress(receiverAddress)
      .storeUint(bothPositive ? 1 : 0, 1)
      .storeCoins(0n)
      .storeMaybeRef(null)
      .endCell()
  )
  .endCell();

const boc = forwardPayload.toBoc();

fs.writeFileSync(
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\scripts\\v22_provide_lp_payload.boc",
  boc
);

console.log("========================================");
console.log("STON.fi V2.2 PROVIDE_LP PAYLOAD");
console.log("READ ONLY / BUILD ONLY");
console.log("========================================");

console.log("Opcode:", "0x" + OP_PROVIDE_LP.toString(16));
console.log("minLpOut:", minLpOut.toString());
console.log("bothPositive:", bothPositive);
console.log("deadline:", deadline);

console.log("");
console.log("===== ROOT =====");
console.log("bits:", forwardPayload.bits.length);
console.log("refs:", forwardPayload.refs.length);
console.log("hash:", forwardPayload.hash().toString("hex"));

console.log("");
console.log("===== BOC =====");
console.log(boc.toString("base64"));

console.log("");
console.log("===== FILE =====");
console.log(
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\scripts\\v22_provide_lp_payload.boc"
);

console.log("");
console.log("===== REF =====");
const ref = forwardPayload.refs[0];
console.log("bits:", ref.bits.length);
console.log("refs:", ref.refs.length);
console.log("hash:", ref.hash().toString("hex"));

console.log("");
console.log("NO TRANSACTION SENT.");

export function run() {}

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\calculateCurrentV22Amounts.ts
====================================================================================================
import { Address } from '@ton/ton';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';
import { DEX } from '@ston-fi/sdk';

const POOL = Address.parse(
    'EQD5ALaCuEqJ7Mi9qlXOA7BHMrTq8e2fZ5B_teWN17MZq-cf'
);

const ROOSTER_AMOUNT = 100_000_000_000n;

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {
    console.log('='.repeat(70));
    console.log(' STON.FI v2.2 CURRENT LIQUIDITY CALCULATOR');
    console.log(' READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION');
    console.log('='.repeat(70));

    const pool = DEX.v2_2.Pool.CPI.create(POOL);
    const poolProvider = provider.provider(POOL);

    const data = await pool.getPoolData(poolProvider);

    const roosterReserve = data.reserve0;
    const ptonReserve = data.reserve1;
    const totalLp = data.totalSupplyLP;

    const proportionalPton =
        (ROOSTER_AMOUNT * ptonReserve) / roosterReserve;

    const lpFromRooster =
        (ROOSTER_AMOUNT * totalLp) / roosterReserve;

    const lpFromPton =
        (proportionalPton * totalLp) / ptonReserve;

    const expectedLp =
        lpFromRooster < lpFromPton
            ? lpFromRooster
            : lpFromPton;

    const minLp95 =
        (expectedLp * 95n) / 100n;

    console.log('');
    console.log('CURRENT POOL');
    console.log('ROOSTER RESERVE:', roosterReserve.toString());
    console.log('pTON RESERVE   :', ptonReserve.toString());
    console.log('TOTAL LP       :', totalLp.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log('FOR 100 ROOSTER');
    console.log('ROOSTER AMOUNT  :', ROOSTER_AMOUNT.toString());
    console.log('PROPORTIONAL pTON:', proportionalPton.toString());
    console.log(
        'PROPORTIONAL pTON TON:',
        Number(proportionalPton) / 1e9
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log('LP FROM ROOSTER:', lpFromRooster.toString());
    console.log('LP FROM pTON   :', lpFromPton.toString());
    console.log('EXPECTED LP    :', expectedLp.toString());
    console.log('95% MIN LP OUT :', minLp95.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log('='.repeat(70));
    console.log(' READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION SENT');
    console.log('='.repeat(70));
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\calculateInitialLpV22.ts
====================================================================================================
import { Address } from '@ton/core';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';
import { DEX } from '@ston-fi/sdk';

function integerSqrt(value: bigint): bigint {
    if (value < 0n) {
        throw new Error('Square root of negative number');
    }

    if (value < 2n) {
        return value;
    }

    let x0 = value;
    let x1 = (x0 + 1n) >> 1n;

    while (x1 < x0) {
        x0 = x1;
        x1 = (x1 + value / x1) >> 1n;
    }

    return x0;
}

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {
    const routerAddress = Address.parse(
        'EQCS4UEa5UaJLzOyyKieqQOQ2P9M-7kXpkO5HnP3Bv250cN3'
    );

    const roosterAddress = Address.parse(
        'EQCyuv-cYXFibA05bhiL3seM3Ur2B_bpsc364Zb8F3NMtrYx'
    );

    const ptonMasterAddress = Address.parse(
        'EQBnGWMCf3-FZZq1W4IWcWiGAc3PHuZ0_H-7sad2oY00o83S'
    );

    const router = DEX.v2_2.Router.CPI.create(routerAddress);

    const roosterAmount = 100_000n * 1_000_000_000n;
    const ptonAmount = 1n * 1_000_000_000n;

    const requiredMinLiquidity = 1001n;

    const product = roosterAmount * ptonAmount;
    const sqrtProduct = integerSqrt(product);

    const lpAfterMinimum =
        sqrtProduct > requiredMinLiquidity
            ? sqrtProduct - requiredMinLiquidity
            : 0n;

    const poolAddress = await router.getPoolAddress(
        provider.provider(routerAddress),
        {
            token0: roosterAddress,
            token1: ptonMasterAddress,
        }
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log('================================================');
    console.log('        ROOSTER INITIAL LP CALCULATION');
    console.log('================================================');
    console.log('');

    console.log('Router:');
    console.log(routerAddress.toString());
    console.log('');

    console.log('Pool:');
    console.log(poolAddress.toString());
    console.log('');

    console.log('INPUT AMOUNTS');
    console.log('-----------------------------------------------');
    console.log('ROOSTER:');
    console.log('100,000 ROOSTER');
    console.log('nano:', roosterAmount.toString());
    console.log('');

    console.log('pTON:');
    console.log('1 pTON');
    console.log('nano:', ptonAmount.toString());
    console.log('');

    console.log('CALCULATION');
    console.log('-----------------------------------------------');
    console.log('Product:');
    console.log(product.toString());
    console.log('');

    console.log('sqrt(product):');
    console.log(sqrtProduct.toString());
    console.log('');

    console.log('Required minimum liquidity:');
    console.log(requiredMinLiquidity.toString());
    console.log('');

    console.log('sqrt(product) - minimum liquidity:');
    console.log(lpAfterMinimum.toString());
    console.log('');

    console.log('IMPORTANT');
    console.log('-----------------------------------------------');
    console.log('The calculated value above is NOT yet confirmed');
    console.log('as the actual STON.fi initial LP mint amount.');
    console.log('');

    console.log('minLpOut is NOT being set for a real transaction.');
    console.log('No transaction will be sent.');
    console.log('');

    console.log('================================================');
    console.log('                 DRY RUN ONLY');
    console.log('================================================');
    console.log('');
}

FILE: scripts\calculatePtonDeployRequirement.ts

import { Address, beginCell, Cell } from '@ton/core';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';
import { pTON } from '@ston-fi/sdk';

const PTON_MASTER = Address.parse(
    'EQCM3B12QK1e4yZSf8GtBRT0aLMNyEsBc_DhVfRRtOEffLez'
);

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {
    console.log('');
    console.log('================================================');
    console.log(' pTON V1 DEPLOY REQUIREMENT ANALYSIS');
    console.log('================================================');
    console.log('');
    console.log('READ-ONLY MODE');
    console.log('NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT.');
    console.log('');

    const owner = provider.sender().address;

    if (!owner) {
        console.log('Wallet address unavailable.');
        return;
    }

    console.log('OWNER:');
    console.log(owner.toString());
    console.log('');

    console.log('PTON MASTER:');
    console.log(PTON_MASTER.toString());
    console.log('');

    const pton = pTON.v1.create(PTON_MASTER);
    const masterProvider = provider.provider(PTON_MASTER);

    /*
     * Build the exact V1 deploy-wallet body.
     * This does NOT send anything.
     */
    const deployTx = await pton.getDeployWalletTxParams(
        masterProvider,
        {
            ownerAddress: owner,
            gasAmount: 50_000_000n
        }
    );

    if (!deployTx.body) {
        console.log('Deploy body is missing.');
        return;
    }

    console.log('================================================');
    console.log('DEPLOY MESSAGE');
    console.log('================================================');
    console.log('');

    console.log('Destination:');
    console.log(deployTx.to.toString());
    console.log('');

    console.log('Deploy body BOC:');
    console.log(
        deployTx.body.toBoc().toString('base64')
    );
    console.log('');

    /*
     * Calculate the user pTON wallet address.
     */
    const walletAddress = await pton.getWalletAddress(
        masterProvider,
        owner
    );

    console.log('USER PTON WALLET:');
    console.log(walletAddress.toString());
    console.log('');

    /*
     * Check current wallet state.
     */
    const walletState = await provider.provider(walletAddress)
        .getState();

    console.log('PTON WALLET STATE:');
    console.log(walletState);
    console.log('');

    /*
     * Estimate the external/inbound fee of the deploy message.
     * This is read-only.
     */
    const api: any = provider.api();

    if (!api || !api.api) {
        console.log('Internal HttpApi unavailable.');
        return;
    }

    try {
        const fee = await api.api.estimateFee(
            PTON_MASTER,
            {
                body: deployTx.body,
                initCode: null,
                initData: null,
                ignoreSignature: true
            }
        );

        console.log('================================================');
        console.log('NETWORK FEE ESTIMATE');
        console.log('================================================');
        console.log('');

        console.log(JSON.stringify(fee, null, 2));
        console.log('');

        const source = fee.source_fees;

        const inboundForward =
            BigInt(source.in_fwd_fee ?? 0);

        const storage =
            BigInt(source.storage_fee ?? 0);

        const gas =
            BigInt(source.gas_fee ?? 0);

        const forward =
            BigInt(source.fwd_fee ?? 0);

        const total =
            inboundForward +
            storage +
            gas +
            forward;

        console.log('TOTAL ESTIMATED FEES:');
        console.log(total.toString(), 'nanoTON');
        console.log(
            Number(total) / 1_000_000_000,
            'TON'
        );
        console.log('');

        /*
         * Official pTON V1 source requirement:
         *
         * MSG_VALUE >
         * (FWD_FEE * 3)
         * + storage_fee::minter
         * + gas::minter::deploy_wallet
         * + storage_fee::wallet
         * + gas::wallet::internal_deploy
         *
         * From official pTON source:
         *
         * storage_fee::minter       = 0.01 TON
         * gas::minter::deploy_wallet = 0.01 TON
         * storage_fee::wallet       = 0.01 TON
         * gas::wallet::internal_deploy = 0.01 TON
         *
         * Fixed part = 0.04 TON
         */

        const fixed =
            40_000_000n;

        console.log('================================================');
        console.log('KNOWN CONTRACT REQUIREMENT');
        console.log('================================================');
        console.log('');

        console.log('Fixed contract requirement:');
        console.log('0.04 TON');
        console.log('');

        console.log('Formula:');
        console.log(
            'MSG_VALUE > (FWD_FEE × 3) + 0.04 TON'
        );
        console.log('');

        /*
         * Show conservative examples for different
         * possible internal forwarding fees.
         */
        const candidates = [
            1_000_000n,
            2_000_000n,
            3_000_000n,
            5_000_000n,
            10_000_000n
        ];

        console.log('================================================');
        console.log('SCENARIO ANALYSIS');
        console.log('================================================');
        console.log('');

        for (const fwd of candidates) {
            const minimum =
                fixed + (fwd * 3n);

            console.log(
                `If FWD_FEE = ${
                    Number(fwd) / 1_000_000_000
                } TON`
            );

            console.log(
                `Required MSG_VALUE > ${
                    Number(minimum) / 1_000_000_000
                } TON`
            );

            console.log('');
        }

        console.log('================================================');
        console.log('IMPORTANT');
        console.log('================================================');
        console.log('');

        console.log(
            'The 0.05 TON value used above was ONLY for analysis.'
        );

        console.log(
            'No transaction was created or sent.'
        );

        console.log(
            'We will NOT deploy the pTON wallet yet.'
        );

        console.log('');
    } catch (error) {
        console.log('Fee analysis failed.');
        console.log('');
        console.log(error);
        console.log('');
        console.log('NO TRANSACTION WAS SENT.');
    }

    console.log('================================================');
    console.log('END');
    console.log('================================================');
}

FILE: scripts\calculatePtonMinimumDeploy.ts

import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';
import { TonClient } from '@ton/ton';
import { Address } from '@ton/core';

const PTON_MASTER = Address.parse(
    'EQCM3B12QK1e4yZSf8GtBRT0aLMNyEsBc_DhVfRRtOEffLez'
);

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {
    console.log('');
    console.log('================================================');
    console.log(' pTON V1 MINIMUM DEPLOY GAS CALCULATION');
    console.log('================================================');
    console.log('');
    console.log('READ-ONLY MODE');
    console.log('NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT.');
    console.log('');

    console.log('pTON MASTER:');
    console.log(PTON_MASTER.toString());
    console.log('');

    const api = provider.api();

    console.log('API TYPE:');
    console.log(api.constructor.name);
    console.log('');

    if (!(api instanceof TonClient)) {
        console.log('Unexpected API type.');
        console.log('');
        console.log('NO TRANSACTION WAS SENT.');
        return;
    }

    /*
     * Read the pTON master account.
     * This is only a read operation.
     */
    const masterState = await api.getContractState(PTON_MASTER);

    console.log('pTON MASTER STATE:');
    console.log(masterState.state);
    console.log('');

    /*
     * Contract source:
     *
     * MSG_VALUE >
     *     (FWD_FEE * 3)
     *     + storage_fee::minter
     *     + gas::minter::deploy_wallet
     *     + storage_fee::wallet
     *     + gas::wallet::internal_deploy
     *
     * Known fixed values:
     *
     * storage_fee::minter       = 0.01 TON
     * deploy_wallet gas         = 0.01 TON
     * storage_fee::wallet       = 0.01 TON
     * internal_deploy gas       = 0.01 TON
     *
     * Total fixed requirement = 0.04 TON
     */

    const fixedRequirement = 40_000_000n;

    console.log('FIXED CONTRACT REQUIREMENT:');
    console.log('0.04 TON');
    console.log('');

    console.log('FORMULA:');
    console.log('MSG_VALUE > (FWD_FEE × 3) + 0.04 TON');
    console.log('');

    console.log('IMPORTANT:');
    console.log('FWD_FEE is calculated by the TON transaction engine.');
    console.log('It is not a fixed value in the pTON source code.');
    console.log('');

    console.log('================================================');
    console.log('KNOWN DEPLOY AMOUNTS');
    console.log('================================================');
    console.log('');

    const amounts = [
        0.045,
        0.050,
        0.055,
        0.060,
        0.065,
        0.070,
        0.080,
        0.100
    ];

    for (const amount of amounts) {
        const nano = BigInt(Math.round(amount * 1_000_000_000));

        console.log(
            `${amount.toFixed(3)} TON -> ${nano.toString()} nanoTON`
        );

        if (nano > fixedRequirement) {
            console.log('  Fixed requirement: PASSES');
        } else {
            console.log('  Fixed requirement: FAILS');
        }

        console.log('');
    }

    console.log('================================================');
    console.log('CONCLUSION');
    console.log('================================================');
    console.log('');
    console.log('0.04 TON is the fixed part of the requirement.');
    console.log('');
    console.log('The actual minimum is:');
    console.log('0.04 TON + 3 × FWD_FEE');
    console.log('');
    console.log('We still need the actual forwarding fee before');
    console.log('selecting the final deployment amount.');
    console.log('');
    console.log('NO TRANSACTION WAS SENT.');
    console.log('');
    console.log('================================================');
    console.log('END');
    console.log('================================================');
}

import { Address, beginCell } from "@ton/core";

const ROOSTER_MASTER =
  "EQCyuv-cYXFibA05bhiL3seM3Ur2B_bpsc364Zb8F3NMtrYx";

const PTON_MASTER =
  "EQBnGWMCf3-FZZq1W4IWcWiGAc3PHuZ0_H-7sad2oY00o83S";

const USER_WALLET =
  "EQDxn5MLbFyV649-OBviQrMp5KqWjGHNVwFM6GTeaLl8N0Ix";

const ROUTER =
  "EQCS4UEa5UaJLzOyyKieqQOQ2P9M-7kXpkO5HnP3Bv250cN3";

async function checkJettonWallet(
  provider: any,
  label: string,
  masterAddress: Address,
  ownerAddress: Address
) {
  console.log(`\n===== ${label} =====`);

  console.log("Master:");
  console.log(masterAddress.toString());

  console.log("Owner:");
  console.log(ownerAddress.toString());

  try {
    const result = await provider.provider(masterAddress).get(
      "get_wallet_address",
      [
        {
          type: "slice",
          cell: beginCell()
            .storeAddress(ownerAddress)
            .endCell(),
        },
      ]
    );

    const wallet = result.stack.readAddress();

    console.log("Derived Jetton Wallet:");
    console.log(wallet.toString());

    console.log("STATUS: PASS");

    return wallet;
  } catch (error: any) {
    console.log("STATUS: FAIL");

    if (error?.message) {
      console.log("Error:");
      console.log(error.message);
    } else {
      console.log(error);
    }

    return null;
  }
}

export async function run(provider: any) {
  console.log("==============================================");
  console.log(" STON.fi v2.2 JETTON WALLET DIAGNOSTIC");
  console.log(" READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT");
  console.log("==============================================");

  const roosterMaster = Address.parse(ROOSTER_MASTER);
  const ptonMaster = Address.parse(PTON_MASTER);
  const user = Address.parse(USER_WALLET);
  const router = Address.parse(ROUTER);

  const roosterWallet = await checkJettonWallet(
    provider,
    "1. ROOSTER → USER",
    roosterMaster,
    user
  );

  const ptonWallet = await checkJettonWallet(
    provider,
    "2. pTON → ROUTER",
    ptonMaster,
    router
  );

  console.log("\n===== 3. KNOWN pTON WALLET =====");

  console.log(
    "Expected known Router pTON Wallet:"
  );
  console.log(
    "EQCSIMGBps_qzRG3uPYhON8bucyCtu0mYdL1-u4gSz77IBa3"
  );

  if (ptonWallet) {
    const matches =
      ptonWallet.toString() ===
      "EQCSIMGBps_qzRG3uPYhON8bucyCtu0mYdL1-u4gSz77IBa3";

    console.log(
      "Derived pTON wallet matches known address:",
      matches ? "PASS" : "FAIL"
    );
  }

  console.log("\n===== FINAL =====");

  if (roosterWallet && ptonWallet) {
    console.log(
      "STATUS: PASS"
    );
    console.log(
      "Both Jetton wallet derivations succeeded."
    );
  } else {
    console.log(
      "STATUS: FAIL"
    );
    console.log(
      "At least one Jetton wallet derivation failed."
    );
  }

  console.log("NO TRANSACTION WAS SENT.");
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\checkCaller12287.ts
====================================================================================================
import fs from "fs";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

const TARGETS = new Set([87, 122]);
const IMPORTS = 83;

function uleb(pos: number) {
  let v = 0;
  let s = 0;

  while (true) {
    const b = wasm[pos++];
    v += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** s;

    if (!(b & 0x80))
      return { value: v, next: pos };

    s += 7;
  }
}

let p = 8;
let codeStart = -1;

while (p < wasm.length) {
  const id = wasm[p++];
  const sz = uleb(p);
  p = sz.next;

  if (id === 10) {
    codeStart = p;
    break;
  }

  p += sz.value;
}

if (codeStart < 0)
  throw new Error("CODE SECTION NOT FOUND");

let q = codeStart;
const count = uleb(q);
q = count.next;

console.log("FUNC 122 -> FUNC 87 DIRECT CHECK");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

for (let body = 0; body < count.value; body++) {

  const bs = uleb(q);
  q = bs.next;

  const start = q;
  const end = q + bs.value;
  const realFunc = IMPORTS + body;

  let calls87 = 0;
  let calls122 = 0;

  for (let i = start; i < end; i++) {

    if (wasm[i] !== 0x10)
      continue;

    const r = uleb(i + 1);

    if (r.value === 87) calls87++;
    if (r.value === 122) calls122++;
  }

  if (realFunc === 122 || calls87 || calls122) {

    if (realFunc === 122 || calls87 || calls122) {
      console.log(
        `FUNC ${realFunc} BODY ${body} ` +
        `CALL87=${calls87} CALL122=${calls122}`
      );
    }
  }

  q = end;
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("DONE");

FILE: scripts\checkCpiV22Pool.ts

import { Address } from '@ton/core';
import { DEX } from '@ston-fi/sdk';

const ROUTER =
    'EQALh-JBBIKK7gr0o4AVf9JZnEsFndqO0qTCyT-D-yBsWval';

export async function run(provider: any) {

    console.log('========================================');
    console.log('      STON.fi v2.2 CPI ROUTER DATA');
    console.log('========================================');
    console.log('NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT.');
    console.log('');

    const routerAddress = Address.parse(ROUTER);

    const routerContract =
        DEX.v2_2.Router.CPI.create(routerAddress);

    const router = provider.open(routerContract);

    console.log('Router version:', DEX.v2_2.Router.CPI.version);
    console.log('Router:', routerAddress.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log('===== GET ROUTER DATA =====');

    try {

        const data = await (router as any).getRouterData();

        console.log('');
        console.log('ROUTER DATA:');
        console.dir(data, { depth: 20 });

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\checkExactProportionV22.ts

export async function run() {
    const roosterReserve = 600000000000000n;
    const ptonReserve = 1499448684n;

    const roosterIn = 100000000000n;
    const ptonIn = 249908n;

    const roosterEquivalentFromPton =
        (ptonIn * roosterReserve) / ptonReserve;

    const ptonEquivalentFromRooster =
        (roosterIn * ptonReserve) / roosterReserve;

    const roosterDiff =
        roosterIn > roosterEquivalentFromPton
            ? roosterIn - roosterEquivalentFromPton
            : roosterEquivalentFromPton - roosterIn;

    const ptonDiff =
        ptonIn > ptonEquivalentFromRooster
            ? ptonIn - ptonEquivalentFromRooster
            : ptonEquivalentFromRooster - ptonIn;

    const roosterRatioNumerator = roosterIn * ptonReserve;
    const ptonRatioDenominator = ptonIn * roosterReserve;

    const ratioDifference =
        roosterRatioNumerator > ptonRatioDenominator
            ? roosterRatioNumerator - ptonRatioDenominator
            : ptonRatioDenominator - roosterRatioNumerator;

    const ratioPercent =
        Number(ratioDifference) /
        Number(ptonRatioDenominator) *
        100;

    console.log("================================================");
    console.log(" STON.fi v2.2 EXACT PROPORTION CHECK");
    console.log(" READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT");
    console.log("================================================");

    console.log("");
    console.log("===== LIVE RESERVES =====");
    console.log(`ROOSTER reserve: ${roosterReserve}`);
    console.log(`pTON reserve:    ${ptonReserve}`);

    console.log("");
    console.log("===== TEST INPUT =====");
    console.log(`ROOSTER input: ${roosterIn} nano = 100 ROOSTER`);
    console.log(`pTON input:    ${ptonIn} nano = 0.000249908 TON`);

    console.log("");
    console.log("===== EQUIVALENT AMOUNTS =====");

    console.log(
        `ROOSTER equivalent from pTON: ${roosterEquivalentFromPton} nano`
    );

    console.log(
        `ROOSTER difference:           ${roosterDiff} nano`
    );

    console.log(
        `pTON equivalent from ROOSTER: ${ptonEquivalentFromRooster} nano`
    );

    console.log(
        `pTON difference:              ${ptonDiff} nano`
    );

    console.log("");
    console.log("===== RATIO DIFFERENCE =====");

    console.log(`Absolute ratio difference: ${ratioDifference}`);
    console.log(`Relative difference: ${ratioPercent.toFixed(8)}%`);

    console.log("");

    if (ratioPercent < 0.001) {
        console.log("PROPORTIONALITY TOLERANCE: PASS");
    } else {
        console.log("PROPORTIONALITY TOLERANCE: CHECK");
    }

    console.log("");
    console.log("===== FINAL =====");
    console.log("READ ONLY");
    console.log("NO TRANSACTION SENT");
    console.log("================================================");
}

import { Address } from '@ton/core';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

async function getJettonWalletMaster(
    provider: NetworkProvider,
    walletAddress: Address
) {
    const result = await provider.provider(walletAddress).get(
        'get_wallet_data',
        []
    );

    const stack = result.stack;

    const balance = stack.readBigNumber();
    const owner = stack.readAddress();
    const jettonMaster = stack.readAddress();

    return {
        balance,
        owner,
        jettonMaster,
    };
}

import {
    LiteClient,
    LiteRoundRobinEngine,
    LiteSingleEngine,
    LiteEngine,
} from 'ton-lite-client';

const LIBRARY_HASHES = [
    '050ed2107f730a4493cfcddbc67bdb83854f1905b3486236530d5a50721d1817',
    '631cf98bbb3ee540dacc48256975d8e2c3080c634f39005ccd8e1593cab0035f',
    '7c1f3aa01265453058887378a358cf37314856350400804c169b648c3fdfbe4c',
    '1b63154f10acd98345a65890997094e9856aa6be4f1e37060865519fa42032fd',
    '5a34c333bcdcac70d41f9afca79aa6e782f1c80a5e4b2ef896ec5e20e9182c86',
];

const servers = [
    {
        host: 'tcp://168.119.95.207:27787',
        key: 'Y/QVf6G5VDiKTZOKitbFVm067WsuocTN8Vg036A4zGk=',
    },
    {
        host: 'tcp://168.119.82.221:51088',
        key: 'Sy5ghr3EahQd/1rDayzZXt5+inlfF+7kLfkZDJcU/ek=',
    },
];

export async function run() {

    console.log('========================================');
    console.log('    INTERNAL ROUTER LIBRARY HASH TEST');
    console.log('========================================');
    console.log('NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT.');
    console.log('');

    const engines: LiteEngine[] = [];

    for (const server of servers) {

        const key = Buffer.from(server.key, 'base64');

        engines.push(
            new LiteSingleEngine({
                host: server.host,
                publicKey: key,
                reconnectTimeout: 5000,
            })
        );
    }

    const engine =
        new LiteRoundRobinEngine(engines);

    const client = new LiteClient({
        engine,
        batchSize: 1,
    });

    try {

        const master =
            await client.getMasterchainInfo();

        console.log(
            'Masterchain seqno:',
            master.last.seqno
        );

        for (let i = 0; i < LIBRARY_HASHES.length; i++) {

            const hashHex = LIBRARY_HASHES[i];

            console.log('');
            console.log(`===== INTERNAL LIBRARY [${i}] =====`);
            console.log('Hash:', hashHex);

            try {

                const hash =
                    Buffer.from(hashHex, 'hex');

                const result =
                    await client.getLibraries([hash]);

                console.log('');
                console.log('RAW RESULT:');

                console.dir(result, {
                    depth: 20
                });

                console.log('');

                if (
                    result &&
                    Array.isArray(result.result)
                ) {

                    console.log(
                        'Entries:',
                        result.result.length
                    );

                    if (result.result.length > 0) {
                        console.log(
                            '>>> LIBRARY FOUND'
                        );
                    } else {
                        console.log(
                            '>>> LIBRARY NOT RETURNED'
                        );
                    }

                } else {

                    console.log(
                        'Unexpected result structure'
                    );
                }

            } catch (error) {

                console.log('ERROR:');
                console.error(error);
            }
        }

    } finally {

        console.log('');
        console.log('Closing engines...');

        for (const e of engines) {
            e.close();
        }
    }

    console.log('');
    console.log('========================================');
    console.log('              TEST FINISHED');
    console.log('              NO TRANSACTION');
    console.log('========================================');
}

FILE: scripts\checkLiquidityBalances.ts

import { Address, beginCell } from '@ton/core';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

const ROOSTER_MASTER = Address.parse(
    'EQCyuv-cYXFibA05bhiL3seM3Ur2B_bpsc364Zb8F3NMtrYx'
);

const PTON_MASTER = Address.parse(
    'EQCM3B12QK1e4yZSf8GtBRT0aLMNyEsBc_DhVfRRtOEffLez'
);

const USER_WALLET = Address.parse(
    '0QDxn5MLbFyV649-OBviQrMp5KqWjGHNVwFM6GTeaLl8N6R-'
);

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {
    console.log('');
    console.log('==========================================');
    console.log(' ROOSTER COIN - LIQUIDITY BALANCE CHECK');
    console.log(' READ-ONLY ONLY');
    console.log(' NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT');
    console.log('==========================================');
    console.log('');

    console.log('Wallet:');
    console.log(USER_WALLET.toString());
    console.log('');

    // --------------------------------------------------
    // TON account state
    // --------------------------------------------------

    const accountState = await provider
        .provider(USER_WALLET)
        .getState();

    console.log('===== TON =====');
    console.log('Account state:', accountState.state.type);
    console.log('');

    // --------------------------------------------------
    // Find Rooster Jetton Wallet
    // --------------------------------------------------

    const roosterResult = await provider
        .provider(ROOSTER_MASTER)
        .get('get_wallet_address', [
            {
                type: 'slice',
                cell: beginCell()
                    .storeAddress(USER_WALLET)
                    .endCell(),
            },
        ]);

    const roosterWallet = roosterResult.stack.readAddress();

    console.log('===== ROOSTER COIN =====');
    console.log('Master:');
    console.log(ROOSTER_MASTER.toString());
    console.log('');
    console.log('User Jetton Wallet:');
    console.log(roosterWallet.toString());
    console.log('');

    // Read Rooster Jetton wallet data
    const roosterData = await provider
        .provider(roosterWallet)
        .get('get_wallet_data', []);

    const roosterBalance = roosterData.stack.readBigNumber();

    console.log('Rooster Balance (raw):');
    console.log(roosterBalance.toString());
    console.log('');
    console.log('Rooster Balance:');
    console.log(formatJettonAmount(roosterBalance));
    console.log(' ROOSTER');
    console.log('');

    // --------------------------------------------------
    // Find pTON Jetton Wallet
    // --------------------------------------------------

    const ptonResult = await provider
        .provider(PTON_MASTER)
        .get('get_wallet_address', [
            {
                type: 'slice',
                cell: beginCell()
                    .storeAddress(USER_WALLET)
                    .endCell(),
            },
        ]);

    const ptonWallet = ptonResult.stack.readAddress();

    console.log('===== pTON =====');
    console.log('Master:');
    console.log(PTON_MASTER.toString());
    console.log('');
    console.log('User Jetton Wallet:');
    console.log(ptonWallet.toString());
    console.log('');

    // Read pTON Jetton wallet data
    const ptonData = await provider
        .provider(ptonWallet)
        .get('get_wallet_data', []);

    const ptonBalance = ptonData.stack.readBigNumber();

    console.log('pTON Balance (raw):');
    console.log(ptonBalance.toString());
    console.log('');
    console.log('pTON Balance:');
    console.log(formatJettonAmount(ptonBalance));
    console.log(' pTON');
    console.log('');

    // --------------------------------------------------
    // Final result
    // --------------------------------------------------

    console.log('==========================================');
    console.log(' BALANCE CHECK COMPLETED');
    console.log('==========================================');
    console.log('');
    console.log('Rooster:', formatJettonAmount(roosterBalance), 'ROOSTER');
    console.log('pTON:   ', formatJettonAmount(ptonBalance), 'pTON');
    console.log('');
    console.log('NO TRANSACTION SENT');
    console.log('==========================================');
}

function formatJettonAmount(amount: bigint): string {
    const decimals = 9n;
    const base = 10n ** decimals;

    const whole = amount / base;
    const fraction = amount % base;

    if (fraction === 0n) {
        return whole.toString();
    }

    const fractionText = fraction
        .toString()
        .padStart(Number(decimals), '0')
        .replace(/0+$/, '');

    return `${whole.toString()}.${fractionText}`;
}

FILE: scripts\checkLiquidityCalculationV22.ts
====================================================================================================
import { Address } from '@ton/core';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';
import { DEX } from '@ston-fi/sdk';

const ROUTER = Address.parse(
    'EQCS4UEa5UaJLzOyyKieqQOQ2P9M-7kXpkO5HnP3Bv250cN3'
);

const ROOSTER = Address.parse(
    'EQCyuv-cYXFibA05bhiL3seM3Ur2B_bpsc364Zb8F3NMtrYx'
);

const PTON = Address.parse(
    'EQBnGWMCf3-FZZq1W4IWcWiGAc3PHuZ0_H-7sad2oY00o83S'
);

const USER = Address.parse(
    'EQDxn5MLbFyV649-OBviQrMp5KqWjGHNVwFM6GTeaLl8N0Ix'
);

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {

    console.log('');
    console.log('================================================');
    console.log('       STON.fi V2.2 LP CALCULATION CHECK');
    console.log('================================================');
    console.log('');

    const router =
        DEX.v2_2.Router.CPI.create(ROUTER);

    const routerProvider =
        provider.provider(ROUTER);

    // ================================================
    // GET REAL POOL
    // ================================================

    const pool =
        await router.getPool(
            routerProvider,
            {
                token0: ROOSTER,
                token1: PTON,
            }
        );

    const poolAddress = pool.address;

    const poolProvider =
        provider.provider(poolAddress);

    console.log('Pool:');
    console.log(poolAddress.toString());
    console.log('');

    // ================================================
    // READ POOL DATA
    // ================================================

    const poolData =
        await pool.getPoolData(poolProvider);

    const reserveRooster =
        poolData.reserve0;

    const reservePton =
        poolData.reserve1;

    const totalSupplyLP =
        poolData.totalSupplyLP;

    // ================================================
    // AMOUNTS WE WANT TO ADD
    // ================================================

    const roosterAmount =
        100_000n * 1_000_000_000n;

    const ptonAmount =
        1n * 1_000_000_000n;

    // ================================================
    // CALCULATE LP FROM EACH SIDE
    // ================================================

    const lpFromRooster =
        roosterAmount *
        totalSupplyLP /
        reserveRooster;

    const lpFromPton =
        ptonAmount *
        totalSupplyLP /
        reservePton;

    // The smaller side limits LP creation.
    const expectedLp =
        lpFromRooster < lpFromPton
            ? lpFromRooster
            : lpFromPton;

    // 95% safety margin.
    const minLpOut =
        expectedLp * 95n / 100n;

    // ================================================
    // DISPLAY
    // ================================================

    console.log('===== CURRENT POOL =====');
    console.log('');

    console.log(
        'ROOSTER reserve:',
        Number(reserveRooster) / 1e9
    );

    console.log(
        'pTON reserve:',
        Number(reservePton) / 1e9
    );

    console.log(
        'Total LP:',
        totalSupplyLP.toString()
    );

    console.log('');

    console.log('===== PROPOSED DEPOSIT =====');
    console.log('');

    console.log(
        'ROOSTER:',
        Number(roosterAmount) / 1e9
    );

    console.log(
        'pTON:',
        Number(ptonAmount) / 1e9
    );

    console.log('');

    console.log('===== LP CALCULATION =====');
    console.log('');

    console.log(
        'LP from ROOSTER:',
        lpFromRooster.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'LP from pTON:',
        lpFromPton.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'Expected LP:',
        expectedLp.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'Minimum LP (95%):',
        minLpOut.toString()
    );

    console.log('');

    // ================================================
    // CHECK USER LP ACCOUNT
    // ================================================

    console.log('===== USER LP ACCOUNT =====');
    console.log('');

    console.log(
        'User:',
        USER.toString()
    );

    const lpAccountAddress =
        await pool.getLpAccount(
            poolProvider,
            {
                ownerAddress: USER,
            }
        );

    console.log('');

    console.log(
        'LP Account:',
        lpAccountAddress.address.toString()
    );

    console.log('');

    const lpDeployed =
        await provider.isContractDeployed(
            lpAccountAddress.address
        );

    console.log(
        'LP Account deployed:',
        lpDeployed
    );

    console.log('');

    // ================================================
    // IMPORTANT
    // ================================================

    console.log('================================================');
    console.log('              READ-ONLY CHECK');
    console.log('================================================');
    console.log('');

    console.log(
        'NO TRANSACTION WAS SENT.'
    );

    console.log('');
}

import {
    LiteClient,
    LiteRoundRobinEngine,
    LiteSingleEngine,
} from 'ton-lite-client';

async function main() {
    const servers = [
        {
            ip: -1468571697,
            port: 27787,
            key: 'Y/QVf6G5VDiKTZOKitbFVm067WsuocTN8Vg036A4zGk=',
        },
        {
            ip: -1468575011,
            port: 51088,
            key: 'Sy5ghr3EahQd/1rDayzZXt5+inlfF+7kLfkZDJcU/ek=',
        },
    ];

    const engines: LiteSingleEngine[] = [];

    for (const server of servers) {
        engines.push(
            new LiteSingleEngine({
                host: `tcp://${intToIP(server.ip)}:${server.port}`,
                publicKey: Buffer.from(server.key, 'base64'),
                reconnectTimeout: 3000,
            })
        );
    }

    const engine = new LiteRoundRobinEngine(engines);

    const client = new LiteClient({
        engine,
        batchSize: 1,
    });

    try {
        console.log('===== LITESERVER CAPABILITIES =====');

        console.log('');
        console.log('===== VERSION =====');

        const version = await client.getVersion();

        console.dir(version, {
            depth: 5,
        });

        console.log('');
        console.log('===== MASTERCHAIN INFO EXT =====');

        const info = await client.getMasterchainInfoExt();

        console.dir(info, {
            depth: 5,
        });

        console.log('');
        console.log('===== DONE =====');

    } finally {
        for (const e of engines) {
            e.close();
        }
    }
}

function intToIP(int: number) {
    const part1 = int & 255;
    const part2 = (int >> 8) & 255;
    const part3 = (int >> 16) & 255;
    const part4 = (int >> 24) & 255;

    return `${part4}.${part3}.${part2}.${part1}`;
}

main().catch((error) => {
    console.error('');
    console.error('===== ERROR =====');
    console.error(error);
    process.exit(1);
});

export async function run() {
    await main();
}

import { TonClient } from '@ton/ton';
import { Address } from '@ton/core';

async function main() {
    const endpoint = 'https://testnet.toncenter.com/api/v2/jsonRPC';

    const pton = Address.parse(
        'EQACS30DNoUQ7NfApPvzh7eBmSZ9L4ygJ-lkNWtba8TQT1h7'
    );

    const client = new TonClient({ endpoint });

    console.log('===== OFFICIAL pTON TESTNET CHECK =====');
    console.log('pTON:', pton.toString());
    console.log('');

    const state = await client.getContractState(pton);

    console.log('State:', state.state);
    console.log('Balance:', state.balance.toString());
    console.log('');

    const methods = [
        'get_jetton_data',
        'get_wallet_address',
    ];

    for (const method of methods) {
        try {
            console.log(`Calling ${method}...`);

            const result = await client.runMethod(
                pton,
                method
            );

            console.log('  exit_code:', result.exit_code);
            console.log('  stack:', result.stack.readNumber());
        } catch (e) {
            console.log('  ERROR:', e);
        }

        console.log('');
    }

    console.log('===== DONE =====');
}

main().catch((e) => {
    console.error('');
    console.error('===== ERROR =====');
    console.error(e);
    process.exit(1);
});

FILE: scripts\checkOfficialPtonLite.ts

import { LiteClient, LiteRoundRobinEngine, LiteSingleEngine } from 'ton-lite-client';
import { Address } from '@ton/core';

async function main() {
    const pton = Address.parse(
        'EQACS30DNoUQ7NfApPvzh7eBmSZ9L4ygJ-lkNWtba8TQT1h7'
    );

    const servers = [
        {
            host: 'tcp://49.12.147.32:27842',
            key: 'sU7QavX2F964iI9oToP9gffQpCQIoOLppeqL/pdPvpM=',
        },
        {
            host: 'tcp://168.119.95.207:27787',
            key: 'Y/QVf6G5VDiKTZOKitbFVm067WsuocTN8Vg036A4zGk=',
        },
        {
            host: 'tcp://168.119.82.221:51088',
            key: 'Sy5ghr3EahQd/1rDayzZXt5+inlfF+7kLfkZDJcU/ek=',
        },
    ];

    console.log('===== OFFICIAL pTON LITESERVER CHECK =====');
    console.log('pTON:', pton.toString());
    console.log('');

    const engines = servers.map(s =>
        new LiteSingleEngine({
            host: s.host,
            publicKey: Buffer.from(s.key, 'base64'),
        })
    );

    const engine = new LiteRoundRobinEngine(engines);

    const client = new LiteClient({
        engine,
    });

    console.log('Getting masterchain info...');
    const master = await client.getMasterchainInfo();

    console.log('Masterchain seqno:', master.last.seqno);
    console.log('');

    console.log('Getting pTON account state...');

    const result = await client.getAccountState(
        pton,
        master.last
    );

    console.log('Account state received.');
    console.log('');

    console.log('===== ACCOUNT INFORMATION =====');

    console.log(
        'State:',
        result.state === null ? 'UNINIT / NO ACCOUNT' : 'ACTIVE'
    );

    console.log(
        'Balance:',
        result.balance.coins.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'Balance TON:',
        Number(result.balance.coins) / 1e9
    );

    console.log(
        'Raw bytes:',
        result.raw.length
    );

    console.log(
        'Proof bytes:',
        result.proof.length
    );

    console.log('');

    if (result.lastTx) {
        console.log('Last TX LT:', result.lastTx.lt.toString());
        console.log('Last TX hash:', result.lastTx.hash.toString());
    } else {
        console.log('Last TX: none');
    }

    console.log('');
    console.log('===== DONE =====');
}

main().catch(e => {
    console.error('');
    console.error('===== ERROR =====');
    console.error(e);
    process.exit(1);
});

import { Address } from '@ton/core';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

const POOL_ADDRESS = Address.parse(
    'EQB2Ijt3RHMCfzYBUH44pPD04IYZOj8Ol4RsyNDgQhWwMvd_'
);

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {
    console.log('===== REAL POOL DATA =====');
    console.log('Pool:', POOL_ADDRESS.toString());

    const result = await provider.provider(
        POOL_ADDRESS
    ).get('get_pool_data', []);

    console.log('Stack size:', result.stack.remaining);

    console.log('');
    console.log('Reading pool data...');

    const isLocked = result.stack.readBoolean();
    const routerAddress = result.stack.readAddress();
    const totalSupplyLP = result.stack.readBigNumber();
    const reserve0 = result.stack.readBigNumber();
    const reserve1 = result.stack.readBigNumber();
    const token0WalletAddress = result.stack.readAddress();
    const token1WalletAddress = result.stack.readAddress();
    const lpFee = result.stack.readBigNumber();
    const protocolFee = result.stack.readBigNumber();

    console.log('isLocked:', isLocked);
    console.log('router:', routerAddress.toString());
    console.log('totalSupplyLP:', totalSupplyLP.toString());
    console.log('reserve0:', reserve0.toString());
    console.log('reserve1:', reserve1.toString());
    console.log('token0Wallet:', token0WalletAddress.toString());
    console.log('token1Wallet:', token1WalletAddress.toString());
    console.log('lpFee:', lpFee.toString());
    console.log('protocolFee:', protocolFee.toString());
}

);

    console.log('Pool:', POOL_ADDRESS.toString());

    const state = await provider.provider(POOL_ADDRESS).getState();

    console.log('State:', state.state);

    if (state.state.type === 'active') {
        console.log('Pool is ACTIVE');
        console.log('Balance:', state.balance.toString());
    } else if (state.state.type === 'uninit') {
        console.log('Pool is UNINITIALIZED');
        console.log('Balance:', state.balance.toString());
    } else {
        console.log('Pool is NONEXIST');
        console.log('Balance:', state.balance.toString());
    }
}
