FILE: scripts\checkPoolViaSdk.ts
====================================================================================================
import { Address, beginCell } from '@ton/core';
import { TonClient } from '@ton/ton';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {
    const client = new TonClient({
        endpoint: 'https://testnet.toncenter.com/api/v2/jsonRPC',
    });

    const router = Address.parse(
        'EQALh-JBBIKK7gr0o4AVf9JZnEsFndqO0qTCyT-D-yBsWval'
    );

    const rooster = Address.parse(
        'kQAmO8BvKx4ta9jkCCDx197VqrUrsaC42Dv6yhDAyKzFppYY'
    );

    const pton = Address.parse(
        'kQBbJjnahBMGbMUJwhAXLn8BiigcGXMJhSC0l7DBhdYABhG7'
    );

    const slice = (address: Address) => ({
        type: 'slice' as const,
        cell: beginCell()
            .storeAddress(address)
            .endCell(),
    });

    console.log('===== ROUTER =====');
    console.log(router.toString({
        urlSafe: true,
        bounceable: true,
        testOnly: true,
    }));

    // --------------------------------------------------
    // 1. Router data
    // --------------------------------------------------

    try {
        console.log('===== GET ROUTER DATA =====');

        const r = await client.runMethod(
            router,
            'get_router_data',
            []
        );

        console.log(r.stack);

    } catch (e) {
        console.log(
            'ROUTER DATA ERROR:',
            e instanceof Error ? e.message : String(e)
        );
    }

    // --------------------------------------------------
    // 2. Pool: ROOSTER -> pTON
    // --------------------------------------------------

    try {
        console.log('===== POOL ROOSTER -> PTON =====');

        const r = await client.runMethod(
            router,
            'get_pool_address',
            [
                slice(rooster),
                slice(pton),
            ]
        );

        console.log('POOL:',
            r.stack.readAddress().toString({
                urlSafe: true,
                bounceable: true,
                testOnly: true,
            })
        );

    } catch (e) {
        console.log(
            'ROOSTER -> PTON ERROR:',
            e instanceof Error ? e.message : String(e)
        );
    }

    // --------------------------------------------------
    // 3. Pool: pTON -> ROOSTER
    // --------------------------------------------------

    try {
        console.log('===== POOL PTON -> ROOSTER =====');

        const r = await client.runMethod(
            router,
            'get_pool_address',
            [
                slice(pton),
                slice(rooster),
            ]
        );

        console.log('POOL:',
            r.stack.readAddress().toString({
                urlSafe: true,
                bounceable: true,
                testOnly: true,
            })
        );

    } catch (e) {
        console.log(
            'PTON -> ROOSTER ERROR:',
            e instanceof Error ? e.message : String(e)
        );
    }
}

FILE: scripts\checkPtonBalance.ts
====================================================================================================
import { Address, beginCell } from '@ton/core';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

const PTON_MASTER = Address.parse(
    'EQBnGWMCf3-FZZq1W4IWcWiGAc3PHuZ0_H-7sad2oY00o83S'
);

const USER_WALLET = Address.parse(
    '0QDxn5MLbFyV649-OBviQrMp5KqWjGHNVwFM6GTeaLl8N6R-'
);

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {
    console.log('');
    console.log('==========================================');
    console.log(' ROOSTER COIN - PTON BALANCE CHECK');
    console.log(' READ-ONLY ONLY');
    console.log(' NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT');
    console.log('==========================================');
    console.log('');

    console.log('User wallet:');
    console.log(USER_WALLET.toString());
    console.log('');

    console.log('pTON v2.1 Master:');
    console.log(PTON_MASTER.toString());
    console.log('');

    // Calculate pTON Jetton Wallet address
    const masterProvider = provider.provider(PTON_MASTER);

    const result = await masterProvider.get('get_wallet_address', [
        {
            type: 'slice',
            cell: beginCell()
                .storeAddress(USER_WALLET)
                .endCell(),
        },
    ]);

    const ptonWallet = result.stack.readAddress();

    console.log('pTON Jetton Wallet:');
    console.log(ptonWallet.toString());
    console.log('');

    // Read pTON wallet data
    const walletProvider = provider.provider(ptonWallet);

    const walletData = await walletProvider.get('get_wallet_data', []);

    const balance = walletData.stack.readBigNumber();

    console.log('pTON balance:');
    console.log(balance.toString());
    console.log('');

    console.log('pTON balance in TON:');
    console.log(Number(balance) / 1e9);
    console.log('');

    console.log('==========================================');
    console.log(' PTON BALANCE CHECK COMPLETED');
    console.log(' NO TRANSACTION SENT');
    console.log('==========================================');
}

FILE: scripts\checkPtonTonTransfer.ts
====================================================================================================
import { Address, toNano } from '@ton/core';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';
import { pTON } from '@ston-fi/sdk';

const PTON_MASTER = Address.parse(
    'kQACS30DNoUQ7NfApPvzh7eBmSZ9L4ygJ-lkNWtba8TQT-Px'
);

const USER_WALLET = Address.parse(
    '0QDxn5MLbFyV649-OBviQrMp5KqWjGHNVwFM6GTeaLl8N6R-'
);

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {
    console.log('');
    console.log('==========================================');
    console.log(' ROOSTER COIN - PTON TON TRANSFER CHECK');
    console.log(' READ-ONLY ONLY');
    console.log(' NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT');
    console.log('==========================================');
    console.log('');

    const pton = pTON.v2_1.create(
        PTON_MASTER
    );

    console.log('pTON v2.1 Master:');
    console.log(pton.address.toString());
    console.log('');

    console.log('User wallet:');
    console.log(USER_WALLET.toString());
    console.log('');

    console.log('Test amount:');
    console.log('0.1 TON');
    console.log('');

    const contractProvider = provider.provider(pton.address);

    const txParams = await pton.getTonTransferTxParams(
        contractProvider,
        {
            tonAmount: toNano('0.1'),
            destinationAddress: USER_WALLET,
            refundAddress: USER_WALLET,
        }
    );

    console.log('Generated transaction parameters:');
    console.log('');

    console.log('Destination:');
    console.log(txParams.to.toString());
    console.log('');

    console.log('Value:');
    console.log(txParams.value.toString());
    console.log('');

    console.log('Value in TON:');
    console.log(Number(txParams.value) / 1e9);
    console.log('');

    if (txParams.body) {
        console.log('Body BOC:');
        console.log(txParams.body.toBoc().toString('base64'));
    } else {
        console.log('Body BOC:');
        console.log('No body returned.');
    }

    console.log('');

    console.log('==========================================');
    console.log(' CHECK COMPLETED');
    console.log(' NO TRANSACTION SENT');
    console.log('==========================================');
}

import { Address } from '@ton/core';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';
import { DEX } from '@ston-fi/sdk';

const POOL = Address.parse(
    'EQD5ALaCuEqJ7Mi9qlXOA7BHMrTq8e2fZ5B_teWN17MZq-cf'
);

const USER = Address.parse(
    'EQDxn5MLbFyV649-OBviQrMp5KqWjGHNVwFM6GTeaLl8N0Ix'
);

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {
    console.log('');
    console.log('==========================================');
    console.log(' LP ACCOUNT FINAL CHECK');
    console.log(' READ-ONLY ONLY');
    console.log(' NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT');
    console.log('==========================================');
    console.log('');

    const pool = provider.open(
        DEX.v2_2.Pool.CPI.create(POOL)
    );

    const lpAccount = await pool.getLpAccount({
        ownerAddress: USER
    });

    console.log('LP Account:');
    console.log(lpAccount.address.toString());
    console.log('');

    const contractProvider = provider.provider(
        lpAccount.address
    );

    const data = await lpAccount.getLpAccountData(
        contractProvider
    );

    console.log('===== LP ACCOUNT DATA =====');
    console.log('User:', data.userAddress.toString());
    console.log('Pool:', data.poolAddress.toString());
    console.log('Amount 0:', data.amount0.toString());
    console.log('Amount 1:', data.amount1.toString());
    console.log('');

    console.log('==========================================');
    console.log(' LP ACCOUNT CHECK COMPLETED');
    console.log(' NO TRANSACTION SENT');
    console.log('==========================================');
}

FILE: scripts\checkRealPoolV22.ts
====================================================================================================
import { Address } from '@ton/core';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {

    const poolAddress = Address.parse(
        'EQD5ALaCuEqJ7Mi9qlXOA7BHMrTq8e2fZ5B_teWN17MZq-cf'
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log('================================================');
    console.log('       REAL ROOSTER / pTON POOL v2.2');
    console.log('       DECODE POOL DATA');
    console.log('================================================');
    console.log('');

    console.log('Pool:');
    console.log(poolAddress.toString());
    console.log('');

    try {

        const result = await provider.provider(poolAddress).get(
            'get_pool_data',
            []
        );

        const stack = result.stack;

        console.log('get_pool_data: SUCCESS');
        console.log('');

        const locked = stack.readBigNumber();
        const routerCell = stack.readCell();
        const totalLp = stack.readBigNumber();
        const reserve0 = stack.readBigNumber();
        const reserve1 = stack.readBigNumber();
        const token0Cell = stack.readCell();
        const token1Cell = stack.readCell();
        const lpFee = stack.readBigNumber();
        const protocolFee = stack.readBigNumber();
        const extraCell = stack.readCell();
        const protocolFeeEnabled = stack.readBigNumber();
        const lastK = stack.readBigNumber();

        console.log('===== BASIC DATA =====');
        console.log('Locked:', locked.toString());
        console.log('Total LP:', totalLp.toString());
        console.log('Reserve 0:', reserve0.toString());
        console.log('Reserve 1:', reserve1.toString());
        console.log('LP Fee:', lpFee.toString());
        console.log('Protocol Fee:', protocolFee.toString());
        console.log('Protocol Fee Enabled:', protocolFeeEnabled.toString());
        console.log('Last K:', lastK.toString());
        console.log('');

        console.log('===== RAW CELLS =====');
        console.log('Router Cell:', routerCell.toString());
        console.log('Token 0 Cell:', token0Cell.toString());
        console.log('Token 1 Cell:', token1Cell.toString());
        console.log('');

        try {
            const routerSlice = routerCell.beginParse();
            const router = routerSlice.loadAddress();

            console.log('Router Address:');
            console.log(router?.toString());
        } catch (e) {
            console.log('Router decode failed');
        }

        console.log('');

        try {
            const token0Slice = token0Cell.beginParse();
            const token0 = token0Slice.loadAddress();

            console.log('Token 0 Wallet:');
            console.log(token0?.toString());
        } catch (e) {
            console.log('Token 0 decode failed');
        }

        console.log('');

        try {
            const token1Slice = token1Cell.beginParse();
            const token1 = token1Slice.loadAddress();

            console.log('Token 1 Wallet:');
            console.log(token1?.toString());
        } catch (e) {
            console.log('Token 1 decode failed');
        }

        console.log('');
        console.log('================================================');
        console.log('                 DECODE FINISHED');
        console.log('================================================');
        console.log('');

    } catch (error: any) {

        console.log('get_pool_data: FAILED');
        console.log('');
        console.log(error?.message ?? error);
        console.log('');

    }
}

import { Address } from '@ton/core';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';
import { RoosterCoin } from '../wrappers/RoosterCoin';

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {
    const ownerAddress = provider.sender().address;

    if (!ownerAddress) {
        throw new Error('Wallet address not available.');
    }

    const roosterCoinAddress = Address.parse(
        'EQCyuv-cYXFibA05bhiL3seM3Ur2B_bpsc364Zb8F3NMtrYx'
    );

    const roosterCoin = provider.open(
        RoosterCoin.createFromAddress(roosterCoinAddress)
    );

    const jettonWalletAddress =
        await roosterCoin.getWalletAddress(ownerAddress);

    const walletProvider = provider.provider(jettonWalletAddress);

    const result = await walletProvider.get('get_wallet_data', []);

    const balance = result.stack.readBigNumber();

    console.log('');
    console.log('========== ROOSTER COIN BALANCE ==========');
    console.log('');
    console.log('ROOSTER Jetton Wallet:');
    console.log(jettonWalletAddress.toString());
    console.log('');
    console.log('Owner Wallet:');
    console.log(ownerAddress.toString());
    console.log('');
    console.log('Rooster Coin Master:');
    console.log(roosterCoinAddress.toString());
    console.log('');
    console.log('Raw Balance:');
    console.log(balance.toString());
    console.log('');
    console.log('ROOSTER Balance:');
    console.log(Number(balance) / 1e9);
    console.log('');
    console.log('==========================================');
}

import { Address, toNano } from '@ton/core';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';
import { DEX, pTON } from '@ston-fi/sdk';

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {
    const userWalletAddress = provider.sender().address;

    if (!userWalletAddress) {
        throw new Error('Wallet address not available.');
    }

    const router = DEX.v2_1.Router.CPI.create(
        'kQALh-JBBIKK7gr0o4AVf9JZnEsFndqO0qTCyT-D-yBsWk0v'
    );

    const proxyTon = pTON.v2_1.create(
        'kQACS30DNoUQ7NfApPvzh7eBmSZ9L4ygJ-lkNWtba8TQT-Px'
    );

    const roosterAddress = Address.parse(
        'EQCyuv-cYXFibA05bhiL3seM3Ur2B_bpsc364Zb8F3NMtrYx'
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log('===== ROOSTER / TON LIQUIDITY TX CHECK =====');
    console.log('Wallet:', userWalletAddress.toString());
    console.log('Router:', router.address.toString());
    console.log('pTON:', proxyTon.address.toString());
    console.log('ROOSTER:', roosterAddress.toString());
    console.log('');
    console.log('Test TON amount: 1 TON');
    console.log('Test ROOSTER amount: 100,000,000 ROOSTER');
    console.log('Min LP out: 1');
    console.log('');

    try {
        const tx = await router.getProvideLiquidityTonTxParams(
            provider.provider(router.address),
            {
                userWalletAddress,
                proxyTon,
                otherTokenAddress: roosterAddress,
                sendAmount: toNano('1'),
                minLpOut: 1n,
            }
        );

        console.log('===== TRANSACTION GENERATED =====');
        console.log('To:', tx.to.toString());
        console.log('Value:', tx.value.toString());
        console.log('Value TON:', Number(tx.value) / 1e9);

        console.log('Body BOC:');

        if (tx.body) {
            console.log(
                tx.body.toBoc().toString('base64')
            );
        } else {
            console.log('Body: undefined');
        }

        console.log('');
        console.log('===== NO TRANSACTION SENT =====');
        console.log('This script only generated transaction parameters.');
        console.log('==============================================');
    } catch (error) {
        console.log('');
        console.log('===== LIQUIDITY TX GENERATION FAILED =====');
        console.error(error);
        console.log('===========================================');
    }
}

import { Address, toNano } from '@ton/core';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';
import { DEX, pTON } from '@ston-fi/sdk';

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {
    const userWalletAddress = provider.sender().address;

    if (!userWalletAddress) {
        throw new Error('Wallet address not available.');
    }

    // STON.fi CPI Router v2.1 Testnet
    const router = DEX.v2_1.Router.CPI.create(
        'kQALh-JBBIKK7gr0o4AVf9JZnEsFndqO0qTCyT-D-yBsWk0v'
    );

    // STON.fi pTON v2.1 Testnet
    const proxyTon = pTON.v2_1.create(
        'kQACS30DNoUQ7NfApPvzh7eBmSZ9L4ygJ-lkNWtba8TQT-Px'
    );

    // Rooster Coin Master
    const roosterAddress = Address.parse(
        'EQCyuv-cYXFibA05bhiL3seM3Ur2B_bpsc364Zb8F3NMtrYx'
    );

    // 100,000,000 ROOSTER with 9 decimals
    const roosterAmount = 100000000n * 1000000000n;

    // 1 TON
    const tonAmount = toNano('1');

    // Minimum LP output for test
    const minLpOut = 1n;

    console.log('');
    console.log('===== ROOSTER / TON LIQUIDITY CHECK =====');
    console.log('');
    console.log('Wallet:');
    console.log(userWalletAddress.toString());
    console.log('');
    console.log('Router:');
    console.log(router.address.toString());
    console.log('');
    console.log('pTON:');
    console.log(proxyTon.address.toString());
    console.log('');
    console.log('ROOSTER Master:');
    console.log(roosterAddress.toString());
    console.log('');
    console.log('ROOSTER amount:');
    console.log('100,000,000 ROOSTER');
    console.log('');
    console.log('TON amount:');
    console.log('1 TON');
    console.log('');
    console.log('Minimum LP out:');
    console.log(minLpOut.toString());
    console.log('');

    try {
        // ------------------------------------------------------------
        // LEG 1: ROOSTER -> Router
        // ------------------------------------------------------------

        const roosterTx =
            await router.getProvideLiquidityJettonTxParams(
                provider.provider(router.address),
                {
                    userWalletAddress,
                    sendTokenAddress: roosterAddress,
                    sendAmount: roosterAmount,
                    otherTokenAddress: proxyTon.address,
                    minLpOut,
                }
            );

        console.log('===== ROOSTER TRANSACTION =====');
        console.log('');
        console.log('To:');
        console.log(roosterTx.to.toString());
        console.log('');
        console.log('Value:');
        console.log(roosterTx.value.toString());
        console.log('Value TON:', Number(roosterTx.value) / 1e9);
        console.log('');
        console.log('Body BOC:');

        if (roosterTx.body) {
            console.log(
                roosterTx.body.toBoc().toString('base64')
            );
        } else {
            console.log('Body: undefined');
        }

        console.log('');

        // ------------------------------------------------------------
        // LEG 2: TON -> Router
        // ------------------------------------------------------------

        const tonTx =
            await router.getProvideLiquidityTonTxParams(
                provider.provider(router.address),
                {
                    userWalletAddress,
                    proxyTon,
                    otherTokenAddress: roosterAddress,
                    sendAmount: tonAmount,
                    minLpOut,
                }
            );

        console.log('===== TON TRANSACTION =====');
        console.log('');
        console.log('To:');
        console.log(tonTx.to.toString());
        console.log('');
        console.log('Value:');
        console.log(tonTx.value.toString());
        console.log('Value TON:', Number(tonTx.value) / 1e9);
        console.log('');
        console.log('Body BOC:');

        if (tonTx.body) {
            console.log(
                tonTx.body.toBoc().toString('base64')
            );
        } else {
            console.log('Body: undefined');
        }

        console.log('');
        console.log('==========================================');
        console.log('BOTH TRANSACTIONS GENERATED SUCCESSFULLY');
        console.log('==========================================');
        console.log('');
        console.log('NO TRANSACTION WAS SENT.');
        console.log('');
    } catch (error) {
        console.log('');
        console.log('===== LIQUIDITY GENERATION FAILED =====');
        console.error(error);
        console.log('=======================================');
    }
}

import { Address } from '@ton/core';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';
import { DEX } from '@ston-fi/sdk';

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {
    const routerAddress = Address.parse(
        'EQCS4UEa5UaJLzOyyKieqQOQ2P9M-7kXpkO5HnP3Bv250cN3'
    );

    const roosterAddress = Address.parse(
        'EQCyuv-cYXFibA05bhiL3seM3Ur2B_bpsc364Zb8F3NMtrYx'
    );

    const ptonAddress = Address.parse(
        'EQACS30DNoUQ7NfApPvzh7eBmSZ9L4ygJ-lkNWtba8TQT1h7'
    );

    console.log('===== ROOSTER / pTON POOL CHECK =====');
    console.log('Router v2.2:', routerAddress.toString());
    console.log('ROOSTER:', roosterAddress.toString());
    console.log('pTON v2.1:', ptonAddress.toString());

    const router = provider.open(
        DEX.v2_2.Router.CPI.create(routerAddress)
    );

    try {
        const routerData = await router.getRouterData();

        console.log('');
        console.log('===== ROUTER DATA =====');
        console.log('routerId:', routerData.routerId);
        console.log('dexType:', routerData.dexType);
        console.log('isLocked:', routerData.isLocked);
        console.log('version:', '2.2');
    } catch (e: any) {
        console.log('');
        console.log('Router data FAILED');
        console.log(e?.message || e);
        return;
    }

    try {
        const poolAddress = await router.getPoolAddressByJettonMinters({
            token0: roosterAddress,
            token1: ptonAddress,
        });

        console.log('');
        console.log('===== POOL ADDRESS =====');
        console.log(poolAddress.toString());

        const pool = provider.open(
            DEX.v2_2.Pool.CPI.create(poolAddress)
        );

        try {
            const poolData = await pool.getPoolData();

            console.log('');
            console.log('===== POOL DATA =====');
            console.log(poolData);
        } catch (e: any) {
            console.log('');
            console.log('Pool data FAILED');
            console.log(e?.message || e);
        }

    } catch (e: any) {
        console.log('');
        console.log('getPoolAddress FAILED');
        console.log(e?.message || e);
    }
}

import { Address } from '@ton/core';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';
import { DEX, pTON } from '@ston-fi/sdk';

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {
    const router = DEX.v2_1.Router.CPI.create(
        'kQALh-JBBIKK7gr0o4AVf9JZnEsFndqO0qTCyT-D-yBsWk0v'
    );

    const pton = pTON.v2_1.create(
        'kQACS30DNoUQ7NfApPvzh7eBmSZ9L4ygJ-lkNWtba8TQT-Px'
    );

    const roosterAddress = Address.parse(
        'EQCyuv-cYXFibA05bhiL3seM3Ur2B_bpsc364Zb8F3NMtrYx'
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log('===== ROOSTER / TON POOL ADDRESS CHECK =====');
    console.log('');

    console.log('Router:', router.address.toString());
    console.log('pTON:', pton.address.toString());
    console.log('ROOSTER:', roosterAddress.toString());

    try {
        // Router's ROOSTER Jetton Wallet
        const roosterRouterWallet =
            await provider
                .provider(roosterAddress)
                .open(
                    // The SDK's internal JettonMinter class is not exported
                    // from the package root, so we use the Router helper.
                    DEX.v2_1.Router.CPI.create(router.address)
                );

        console.log('');
        console.log('Router object created successfully.');
        console.log('ROOSTER Router Wallet calculation will be tested next.');

        // Use the Router's public helper.
        const poolAddress =
            await router.getPoolAddress(
                provider.provider(router.address),
                {
                    token0: roosterAddress,
                    token1: pton.address,
                }
            );

        console.log('');
        console.log('===== POOL ADDRESS =====');
        console.log(poolAddress.toString());
        console.log('========================');
    } catch (error) {
        console.log('');
        console.log('===== POOL ADDRESS LOOKUP FAILED =====');
        console.error(error);
        console.log('======================================');
    }
}

FILE: scripts\checkRoosterProject.ts

import { Address } from '@ton/core';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';
import { DEX } from '@ston-fi/sdk';
import { execSync } from 'child_process';
import * as fs from 'fs';
import * as path from 'path';

const PROJECT_ROOT =
    'G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin';

const MASTER =
    'EQCyuv-cYXFibA05bhiL3seM3Ur2B_bpsc364Zb8F3NMtrYx';

const USER_WALLET =
    '0QDxn5MLbFyV649-OBviQrMp5KqWjGHNVwFM6GTeaLl8N6R-';

const ROOSTER_WALLET =
    'EQBzQOOJu9qQVli2R0o6p3pXvi_wj9O2ncMhv3UyAjvMiMOh';

const ROUTER =
    'EQCS4UEa5UaJLzOyyKieqQOQ2P9M-7kXpkO5HnP3Bv250cN3';

const PTON_MASTER =
    'EQBnGWMCf3-FZZq1W4IWcWiGAc3PHuZ0_H-7sad2oY00o83S';

const PTON_ROUTER_WALLET =
    'EQCSIMGBps_qzRG3uPYhON8bucyCtu0mYdL1-u4gSz77IBa3';

const POOL =
    'EQB2Ijt3RHMCfzYBUH44pPD04IYZOj8Ol4RsyNDgQhWwMvd_';

let passed = 0;
let failed = 0;
let warnings = 0;

// ------------------------------------------------
// BIGINT-SAFE JSON
// ------------------------------------------------

function safeStringify(
    value: unknown
): string {

    return JSON.stringify(
        value,
        (_key, value) =>
            typeof value === 'bigint'
                ? value.toString()
                : value,
        2
    );
}

// ------------------------------------------------
// RESULT HELPERS
// ------------------------------------------------

function ok(
    label: string,
    message = ''
) {

    passed++;

    console.log(
        `  [OK] ${label}${message ? ` - ${message}` : ''}`
    );
}

function fail(
    label: string,
    message = ''
) {

    failed++;

    console.log(
        `  [FAIL] ${label}${message ? ` - ${message}` : ''}`
    );
}

function warn(
    label: string,
    message = ''
) {

    warnings++;

    console.log(
        `  [WARN] ${label}${message ? ` - ${message}` : ''}`
    );
}

function section(
    title: string
) {

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        '================================================'
    );
    console.log(title);
    console.log(
        '================================================'
    );
}

// ------------------------------------------------
// FILE CHECK
// ------------------------------------------------

function checkFileCandidates(
    label: string,
    candidates: string[]
) {

    for (
        const relativePath of candidates
    ) {

        const fullPath =
            path.join(
                PROJECT_ROOT,
                relativePath
            );

        if (
            fs.existsSync(fullPath)
        ) {

            ok(
                label,
                `Found: ${relativePath}`
            );

            return;
        }
    }

    warn(
        label,
        `Not found in expected locations`
    );
}

// ------------------------------------------------
// COMMAND CHECK
// ------------------------------------------------

function commandExists(
    command: string
): boolean {

    try {

        execSync(
            `where.exe ${command}`,
            {
                stdio: 'ignore',
                shell: 'powershell.exe'
            }
        );

        return true;

    } catch {

        return false;
    }
}

function getCommandVersion(
    command: string
): string {

    try {

        return execSync(
            `${command} --version`,
            {
                encoding: 'utf8',
                shell: 'powershell.exe'
            }
        ).trim();

    } catch {

        return 'Unavailable';
    }
}

// ------------------------------------------------
// ADDRESS CHECK
// ------------------------------------------------

function checkAddress(
    addressText: string,
    label: string
) {

    try {

        const address =
            Address.parse(
                addressText
            );

        ok(
            label,
            address.toString()
        );

    } catch {

        fail(
            label,
            'Invalid TON address'
        );
    }
}

// ------------------------------------------------
// HTTP CHECK
// ------------------------------------------------

async function checkHttp(
    url: string,
    label: string
) {

    try {

        const response =
            await fetch(url);

        if (
            response.ok
        ) {

            ok(
                label,
                `HTTP ${response.status}`
            );

        } else {

            warn(
                label,
                `HTTP ${response.status}`
            );
        }

    } catch (error) {

        fail(
            label,
            error instanceof Error
                ? error.message
                : String(error)
        );
    }
}

// ------------------------------------------------
// MAIN
// ------------------------------------------------

export async function run(
    provider: NetworkProvider
) {

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        '================================================'
    );
    console.log(
        '          ROOSTER COIN PROJECT CHECK'
    );
    console.log(
        '================================================'
    );
    console.log('');
    console.log(
        'READ-ONLY CHECK'
    );
    console.log(
        'NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT'
    );
    console.log('');

    // =================================================
    // 1. SOFTWARE ENVIRONMENT
    // =================================================

    section(
        '1. SOFTWARE ENVIRONMENT'
    );

    console.log('');

    if (
        commandExists('node')
    ) {

        ok(
            'Node.js',
            getCommandVersion('node')
        );

    } else {

        fail(
            'Node.js',
            'node command not found'
        );
    }

    if (
        commandExists('npm.cmd')
    ) {

        ok(
            'npm.cmd',
            getCommandVersion('npm.cmd')
        );

    } else {

        warn(
            'npm.cmd',
            'npm.cmd not found'
        );
    }

    if (
        commandExists('git')
    ) {

        ok(
            'Git',
            getCommandVersion('git')
        );

    } else {

        warn(
            'Git',
            'git command not found'
        );
    }

    if (
        commandExists('npx.cmd')
    ) {

        ok(
            'npx.cmd',
            'Available'
        );

    } else {

        fail(
            'npx.cmd',
            'Not found'
        );
    }

    // =================================================
    // 2. PROJECT FILES
    // =================================================

    section(
        '2. PROJECT FILES'
    );

    checkFileCandidates(
        'package.json',
        [
            'package.json'
        ]
    );

    checkFileCandidates(
        'tsconfig.json',
        [
            'tsconfig.json'
        ]
    );

        'blueprint configuration',
        [
            'blueprint.config.ts',
            'blueprint.config.tsx',
            'blueprint.config.js',
            'blueprint.config.mjs'
        ]
    );

        'Rooster Coin contract',
        [
            'contracts/RoosterCoin.tolk'
        ]
    );

    checkFileCandidates(
        'Rooster Coin wrapper',
        [
            'wrappers/RoosterCoin.ts'
        ]
    );

    checkFileCandidates(
        'metadata.json',
        [
            'metadata.json',
            'assets/metadata.json',
            'public/metadata.json'
        ]
    );

    checkFileCandidates(
        'Rooster Coin logo',
        [
            'rooster-coin.png',
            'assets/rooster-coin.png',
            'public/rooster-coin.png'
        ]
    );

    const scriptsToCheck = [

        'scripts/deployRoosterCoin.ts',

        'scripts/mintRoosterCoin.ts',

        'scripts/getWalletAddress.ts',

    ];

    ) {

        checkFileCandidates(
            script,
            [script]
        );
    }

    // 3. NODE MODULES
    // =================================================

        '3. NODE MODULES'
    );

        '@ton/core',

        '@ton/blueprint',

        '@ston-fi/sdk'
    ];

    for (
        const moduleName of modulesToCheck
    ) {

            path.join(
                PROJECT_ROOT,
                'node_modules',
                moduleName
            );

        if (
            fs.existsSync(
                modulePath
            )
        ) {

            ok(
                moduleName,
                'Installed'
            );

        } else {

            fail(
                moduleName,
                'Not installed'
            );
        }

    // =================================================

        '4. IMPORTANT VERSIONS'
    );

                        PROJECT_ROOT,

                    ),

                )
            );

        const deps = {

            ...(packageJson.devDependencies ?? {})

                ?? 'Not declared'
        );

            deps['@ton/core']
                ?? 'Not declared'
        );

                ?? 'Not declared'
        );

        console.log(
            'typescript:',
            deps['typescript']
                ?? 'Not declared'
        );

    );

        USER_WALLET,
        'User Wallet'
    );

        ROOSTER_WALLET,
        'ROOSTER Jetton Wallet'
    );

        ROUTER,
        'STON.fi Router v2.2'
    );

        PTON_MASTER,
        'pTON Master'
    );

            'STON.fi Router getters',
            'Router v2.2 responding'
        );

    } catch (error) {

        fail(
            'STON.fi Router getters',
            error instanceof Error
                ? error.message
                : String(error)
        );
    }

    // =================================================
    // 11. POOL ADDRESS
    // =================================================

    section(
        '11. POOL ADDRESS DERIVATION'
    );

    try {

        const router =
            DEX.v2_2.Router.CPI.create(
                Address.parse(
                    ROUTER
                )
            );

        const derivedPool =
            (
                await router.getPoolAddress(
                    provider.provider(
                        Address.parse(
                            ROUTER
                        )
                    ),
                    {
                        token0:
                            Address.parse(
                                MASTER
                            ),

                        token1:
                            Address.parse(
                                PTON_MASTER
                            )
                    }
                )
            ).toString();

        console.log('');

        console.log(
            'Expected Pool:'
        );

        console.log(
            POOL
        );

        console.log('');

        console.log(
            'Derived Pool:'
        );

        console.log(
            derivedPool
        );

        if (
            derivedPool === POOL
        ) {

            ok(
                'Pool address',
                'Matches expected address'
            );

        } else {

            fail(
                'Pool address',
                'Derived address differs'
            );
        }

    } catch (error) {

        fail(
            'Pool address derivation',
            error instanceof Error
                ? error.message
                : String(error)
        );
    }

    // =================================================
    // 12. POOL STATE
    // =================================================

    section(
        '12. POOL STATE'
    );

    try {

        const pool =
            provider.provider(
                Address.parse(
                    POOL
                )
            );

        const state =
            await pool.getState();

        console.log('');

        console.log(
            'Pool state:'
        );

        console.log(
            safeStringify(state)
        );

        const stateText =
            safeStringify(state);

        if (
            stateText.includes(
                '"active"'
            )
        ) {

            ok(
                'Pool contract state',
                'ACTIVE'
            );

            try {

                const data =
                    await pool.get(
                        'get_pool_data',
                        []
                    );

                console.log('');

                console.log(
                    'get_pool_data:'
                );

                console.log(
                    safeStringify(data)
                );

                ok(
                    'Pool getter',
                    'get_pool_data executed successfully'
                );

            } catch (error) {

                warn(
                    'Pool getter',
                    error instanceof Error
                        ? error.message
                        : String(error)
                );
            }

        } else if (
            stateText.includes(
                '"uninit"'
            )
        ) {

            warn(
                'Pool contract state',
                'UNINITIALIZED - pool has not been deployed/activated'
            );

        } else {

            warn(
                'Pool contract state',
                stateText
            );
        }

    } catch (error) {

        fail(
            'Pool state check',
            error instanceof Error
                ? error.message
                : String(error)
        );
    }

    // =================================================
    // FINAL RESULT
    // =================================================

    section(
        'FINAL RESULT'
    );

    console.log('');

    console.log(
        `PASSED  : ${passed}`
    );

    console.log(
        `WARNINGS: ${warnings}`
    );

    console.log(
        `FAILED  : ${failed}`
    );

    console.log('');

    if (
        failed === 0
    ) {

        console.log(
            'PROJECT CHECK: NO CRITICAL SOFTWARE ERROR FOUND'
        );

    } else {

        console.log(
            'PROJECT CHECK: SOME ITEMS REQUIRE ATTENTION'
        );
    }

    console.log('');

    console.log(
        'IMPORTANT:'
    );

    console.log(
        'This check is READ-ONLY.'
    );

    console.log(
        'No Tonkeeper approval was requested.'
    );

    console.log(
        'No blockchain transaction was sent.'
    );

    console.log('');

    console.log(
        '================================================'
    );

    console.log(
        '                 CHECK FINISHED'
    );

    console.log(
        '================================================'
    );

    console.log('');
}

import { Address } from '@ton/core';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';
import { DEX } from '@ston-fi/sdk';

const ROUTER = Address.parse(
    'EQCS4UEa5UaJLzOyyKieqQOQ2P9M-7kXpkO5HnP3Bv250cN3'
);

const ROOSTER = Address.parse(
    'EQCyuv-cYXFibA05bhiL3seM3Ur2B_bpsc364Zb8F3NMtrYx'
);

const PTON = Address.parse(
    'EQBnGWMCf3-FZZq1W4IWcWiGAc3PHuZ0_H-7sad2oY00o83S'
);

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {
    console.log('');
    console.log('==========================================');
    console.log(' ROOSTER / pTON POOL ADDRESS CHECK');
    console.log(' READ-ONLY ONLY');
    console.log(' NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT');
    console.log('==========================================');
    console.log('');

    console.log('Router v2.2:');
    console.log(ROUTER.toString());
    console.log('');

    console.log('ROOSTER Master:');
    console.log(ROOSTER.toString());
    console.log('');

    console.log('pTON Master:');
    console.log(PTON.toString());
    console.log('');

    const router = provider.open(
        DEX.v2_2.Router.CPI.create(ROUTER)
    );

    console.log('Router v2.2 object created.');
    console.log('');

    console.log('Calling getPoolAddress...');
    console.log('');

    const poolAddress = await router.getPoolAddress({
        token0: ROOSTER,
        token1: PTON
    });

    console.log('Deterministic Pool Address:');
    console.log(poolAddress.toString());
    console.log('');

    const poolProvider = provider.provider(poolAddress);
    const poolState = await poolProvider.getState();

    console.log('Pool state:');
    console.log(poolState.state.type);
    console.log('');

    console.log('Pool balance:');
    console.log(poolState.balance.toString());
    console.log('');

    console.log('==========================================');
    console.log(' POOL ADDRESS CHECK COMPLETED');
    console.log(' NO TRANSACTION SENT');
    console.log('==========================================');
}

import { StonApiClient } from '@ston-fi/api';

async function main() {
    const routerAddress =
        'EQALh-JBBIKK7gr0o4AVf9JZnEsFndqO0qTCyT-D-yBsWval';

    const client = new StonApiClient();

    console.log('===== STON API ROUTER CHECK =====');
    console.log('Router:', routerAddress);
    console.log('');

    try {
        const router = await client.getRouter(routerAddress);

        console.log('===== ROUTER INFO =====');
        console.log(JSON.stringify(router, null, 2));
    } catch (error) {
        console.log('===== API ERROR =====');
        console.log(error);
    }
}

main().catch((error) => {
    console.error('');
    console.error('===== FATAL ERROR =====');
    console.error(error);
});

    console.log('');

    console.log('Router:', router.toString());

        await provider.provider(router).getState();

    console.log('State type:', state.state.type);

    console.log('');

    try {

        const result =
            await provider.provider(router).get(
                'get_router_data',

            error?.message ?? error
        );
    }

    console.log('');

}

        console.log('===== ROUTER CHECK FAILED =====');

    }

    console.log('');

}

FILE: scripts\checkRouterLibrary.ts
====================================================================================================
import { LiteClient, LiteSingleEngine } from 'ton-lite-client';

function sleep(ms: number) {
    return new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, ms));
}

export async function run() {
    const libraryHash = Buffer.from(
        '5a34c333bcdcac70d41f9afca79aa6e782f1c80a5e4b2ef896ec5e20e9182c86',
        'hex'
    );

    console.log('===== ROUTER LIBRARY CHECK =====');
    console.log('Library hash:');
    console.log(libraryHash.toString('hex'));

    const servers = [
        {
            host: 'tcp://49.12.147.32:27842',
            key: 'sU7QavX2F964iI9oToP9gffQpCQIoOLppeqL/pdPvpM='
        },
        {
            host: 'tcp://168.119.95.207:27787',
            key: 'Y/QVf6G5VDiKTZOKitbFVm067WsuocTN8Vg036A4zGk='
        },
        {
            host: 'tcp://168.119.82.221:51088',
            key: 'Sy5ghr3EahQd/1rDayzZXt5+inlfF+7kLfkZDJcU/ek='
        }
    ];

    for (const server of servers) {
        console.log('\n--------------------------------');
        console.log('Server:', server.host);

        try {
            const engine = new LiteSingleEngine({
                host: server.host,
                publicKey: Buffer.from(server.key, 'base64'),
                reconnectTimeout: 3000
            });

            console.log('Connecting...');

            for (let i = 0; i < 20; i++) {
                if (engine.isReady()) {
                    break;
                }

                await sleep(500);

                console.log(
                    `Waiting... ${i + 1}/20 | closed=${engine.isClosed()} ready=${engine.isReady()}`
                );
            }

            if (!engine.isReady()) {
                console.log('ENGINE NOT READY');
                continue;
            }

            console.log('ENGINE READY');

            const client = new LiteClient({
                engine
            });

            const info = await client.getMasterchainInfo();

            console.log('Masterchain seqno:', info.last.seqno);

            const result = await client.getLibraries([
                libraryHash
            ]);

            console.log('Result count:', result.result.length);

            if (result.result.length === 0) {
                console.log('LIBRARY NOT FOUND');
                continue;
            }

            const lib = result.result[0];

            console.log('LIBRARY FOUND');
            console.log('Hash:', lib.hash.toString('hex'));
            console.log('Data bytes:', lib.data.length);

        } catch (e) {
            console.log('ERROR:');
            console.log(e instanceof Error ? e.message : String(e));
        }
    }
}

import { LiteClient, LiteRoundRobinEngine } from 'ton-lite-client';

async function main() {
    const libraryHash = Buffer.from(
        '5A34C333BCDCAC70D41F9AFCA79AA6E782F1C80A5E4B2EF896EC5E20E9182C86',
        'hex'
    );

    const engine = new LiteRoundRobinEngine([
        {
            ip: 822907680,
            port: 27842,
            key: 'sU7QavX2F964iI9oToP9gffQpCQIoOLppeqL/pdPvpM=',
        },
        {
            ip: -1468571697,
            port: 27787,
            key: 'Y/QVf6G5VDiKTZOKitbFVm067WsuocTN8Vg036A4zGk=',
        },
        {
            ip: -1468575011,
            port: 51088,
            key: 'Sy5ghr3EahQd/1rDayzZXt5+inlfF+7kLfkZDJcU/ek=',
        },
    ]);

    const client = new LiteClient({
        engine,

    const result = await client.getLibraries([libraryHash]);

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\checkRouterLibraryFromTestnetConfig.ts
====================================================================================================
import { LiteClient, LiteSingleEngine } from 'ton-lite-client';

function sleep(ms: number) {
    return new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, ms));
}

function ipToString(ip: number): string {
    const unsigned = ip >>> 0;

    return [
        (unsigned >>> 24) & 255,
        (unsigned >>> 16) & 255,
        (unsigned >>> 8) & 255,
        unsigned & 255,
    ].join('.');
}

export async function run() {
    const libraryHash = Buffer.from(
        '5a34c333bcdcac70d41f9afca79aa6e782f1c80a5e4b2ef896ec5e20e9182c86',
        'hex'
    );

    const configUrl =
        'https://ton-blockchain.github.io/testnet-global.config.json';

    console.log('========================================');
    console.log(' ROUTER LIBRARY TESTNET CONFIG CHECK');
    console.log('========================================');

    console.log('\nConfig:');
    console.log(configUrl);

    console.log('\nRouter Library ID:');
    console.log(libraryHash.toString('hex'));

    console.log('\nDownloading official Testnet config...');

    const response = await fetch(configUrl);

    if (!response.ok) {
        throw new Error(
            `Config download failed: HTTP ${response.status}`
        );
    }

    const config: any = await response.json();

    if (!Array.isArray(config.liteservers)) {
        throw new Error('No liteservers array found in config.');
    }

    console.log(
        '\nLiteServers in config:',
        config.liteservers.length
    );

    const servers = config.liteservers.slice(0, 8);

    for (let i = 0; i < servers.length; i++) {
        const item = servers[i];

        const host = `tcp://${ipToString(item.ip)}:${item.port}`;
        const key = item.id?.key;

        console.log('\n--------------------------------');
        console.log(`Server ${i + 1}/${servers.length}:`, host);

        if (!key) {
            console.log('Missing public key.');
            continue;
        }

        try {
            const engine = new LiteSingleEngine({
                host,
                publicKey: Buffer.from(key, 'base64'),
                reconnectTimeout: 3000,
            });

            console.log('Connecting...');

            for (let j = 0; j < 20; j++) {
                if (engine.isReady()) {
                    break;
                }

                await sleep(500);

                console.log(
                    `Waiting... ${j + 1}/20 | closed=${engine.isClosed()} ready=${engine.isReady()}`
                );
            }

            if (!engine.isReady()) {
                console.log('ENGINE NOT READY');
                continue;
            }

            console.log('ENGINE READY');

            const client = new LiteClient({
                engine,
            });

            const info = await client.getMasterchainInfo();

            console.log(
                'Masterchain seqno:',
                info.last.seqno
            );

            const result = await client.getLibraries([
                libraryHash,
            ]);

            console.log(
                'Result count:',
                result.result.length
            );

            if (result.result.length === 0) {
                console.log('LIBRARY NOT FOUND');
                continue;
            }

            const lib = result.result[0];

            console.log('*** LIBRARY FOUND ***');
            console.log(
                'Returned hash:',
                lib.hash.toString('hex')
            );
            console.log(
                'Data bytes:',
                lib.data.length
            );

            console.log(
                '\nLibrary hash matches:',
                lib.hash.equals(libraryHash)
            );

            console.log('\n===== SUCCESS =====');
            console.log(
                'Router Library is available from this Testnet LiteServer.'
            );

            return;
        } catch (e) {
            console.log('ERROR:');
            console.log(
                e instanceof Error ? e.message : String(e)
            );
        }
    }

    console.log('\n========================================');
    console.log(' FINAL RESULT');
    console.log('========================================');
    console.log(
        'Router Library was not returned by the tested Testnet LiteServers.'
    );
}

FILE: scripts\checkRouterLibraryPerServer.ts
====================================================================================================
import {
    LiteClient,
    LiteSingleEngine,
} from 'ton-lite-client';

function sleep(ms: number) {
    return new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, ms));
}

const LIBRARY_HASH = Buffer.from(
    '5a34c333bcdcac70d41f9afca79aa6e782f1c80a5e4b2ef896ec5e20e9182c86',
    'hex'
);

const SERVERS = [
    {
        name: 'Server 1',
        host: 'tcp://168.119.95.207:27787',
        key: 'Y/QVf6G5VDiKTZOKitbFVm067WsuocTN8Vg036A4zGk=',
    },
    {
        name: 'Server 2',
        host: 'tcp://168.119.82.221:51088',
        key: 'Sy5ghr3EahQd/1rDayzZXt5+inlfF+7kLfkZDJcU/ek=',
    },
];

export async function run() {
    console.log('========================================');
    console.log(' ROUTER LIBRARY PER-SERVER TEST');
    console.log('========================================');
    console.log('NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT.');
    console.log('');
    console.log('Library ID:');
    console.log(LIBRARY_HASH.toString('hex'));

    for (const server of SERVERS) {
        console.log('');
        console.log('----------------------------------------');
        console.log(server.name);
        console.log('Host:', server.host);

        let engine: LiteSingleEngine | undefined;

        try {
            engine = new LiteSingleEngine({
                host: server.host,
                publicKey: Buffer.from(server.key, 'base64'),
                reconnectTimeout: 5000,
            });

            for (let i = 0; i < 20; i++) {
                if (engine.isReady()) {
                    break;
                }

                await sleep(500);
            }

            if (!engine.isReady()) {
                console.log('ENGINE NOT READY');
                continue;
            }

            console.log('ENGINE READY');

            const client = new LiteClient({
                engine,
                batchSize: 1,
            });

            const master = await client.getMasterchainInfo();

            console.log(
                'Masterchain seqno:',
                master.last.seqno
            );

            const result = await client.getLibraries([
                LIBRARY_HASH,
            ]);

            console.log(
                'Result count:',
                result.result.length
            );

            if (result.result.length === 0) {
                console.log('LIBRARY NOT RETURNED');
                continue;
            }

            const lib = result.result[0];

            console.log('LIBRARY FOUND');
            console.log(
                'Returned hash:',
                lib.hash.toString('hex')
            );
            console.log(
                'Data bytes:',
                lib.data.length
            );

            console.log(
                'Hash matches:',
                lib.hash.equals(LIBRARY_HASH)
            );

        } catch (error) {
            console.log('ERROR:');
            console.log(
                error instanceof Error
                    ? error.message
                    : String(error)
            );
        } finally {
            if (engine) {
                engine.close();
            }
        }
    }

    console.log('');
    console.log('========================================');
    console.log(' TEST FINISHED');
    console.log(' NO TRANSACTION');
    console.log('========================================');
}

import { DEX } from '@ston-fi/sdk';

export async function run() {
    console.log('===== STON SDK ROUTER INSPECTION =====');

    console.log('\n===== DEX.v2_1.Router =====');
    console.log(Object.keys(DEX.v2_1.Router));

    for (const [name, RouterClass] of Object.entries(DEX.v2_1.Router)) {
        try {
            console.log(`\n--- ${name} ---`);

            const R: any = RouterClass;

            console.log('Static properties:',
                Object.getOwnPropertyNames(R)
            );

            if (typeof R.create === 'function') {
                console.log('create(): available');

                try {
                    const router = R.create();

                    console.log('Created router:');
                    console.log(router);

                    if (router.address) {
                        console.log(
                            'Address:',
                            router.address.toString({
                                urlSafe: true,
                                bounceable: true,
                                testOnly: true,
                            })
                        );
                    }
                } catch (e) {
                    console.log(
                        'create() ERROR:',
                        e instanceof Error ? e.message : String(e)
                    );
                }
            } else {
                console.log('create(): NOT available');
            }

        } catch (e) {
            console.log(
                'ERROR:',
                e instanceof Error ? e.message : String(e)
            );
        }
    }
}

import { Address } from '@ton/core';
import { TonClient } from '@ton/ton';

const ROUTER = Address.parse(
    'EQALh-JBBIKK7gr0o4AVf9JZnEsFndqO0qTCyT-D-yBsWval'
);

async function main() {
    const client = new TonClient({
        endpoint: 'https://testnet.toncenter.com/api/v2/jsonRPC',
    });

    console.log('===== RAW ROUTER VERSION =====');
    console.log('Router:', ROUTER.toString());

    try {
        const result = await client.runMethod(
            ROUTER,
            'get_router_version'
        );

        console.log('Gas used:', result.gas_used);
        console.log('Stack:', result.stack);

        console.log('\n===== DECODED =====');

        const major = result.stack.readNumber();
        const minor = result.stack.readNumber();
        const development = result.stack.readString();

        console.log('Major:', major);
        console.log('Minor:', minor);
        console.log('Development:', development);

    } catch (e) {
        console.error('\n===== ERROR =====');
        console.error(e);
    }
}

main().catch(console.error);

FILE: scripts\checkRouterWallets.ts

import { Address, beginCell } from '@ton/core';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {

    const routerAddress = Address.parse(
        'kQALh-JBBIKK7gr0o4AVf9JZnEsFndqO0qTCyT-D-yBsWk0v'
    );

    const roosterAddress = Address.parse(
        'EQCyuv-cYXFibA05bhiL3seM3Ur2B_bpsc364Zb8F3NMtrYx'
    );

    const ptonAddress = Address.parse(
        'EQCM3B12QK1e4yZSf8GtBRT0aLMNyEsBc_DhVfRRtOEffLez'
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log('===== JETTON WALLET ADDRESS CHECK =====');
    console.log('Router :', routerAddress.toString());
    console.log('');

    async function getWalletAddress(
        master: Address,
        owner: Address
    ): Promise<Address> {

        const contract = provider.provider(master);

        const ownerCell = beginCell()
            .storeAddress(owner)
            .endCell();

        const result = await contract.get('get_wallet_address', [
            {
                type: 'slice',
                cell: ownerCell
            }
        ]);

        return result.stack.readAddress();
    }

    console.log('Getting ROOSTER wallet of Router...');

    const roosterWallet = await getWalletAddress(
        roosterAddress,
        routerAddress
    );

    console.log(
        'ROOSTER Router Wallet:',
        roosterWallet.toString()
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log('Getting pTON wallet of Router...');

    const ptonWallet = await getWalletAddress(
        ptonAddress,
        routerAddress
    );

    console.log(
        'pTON Router Wallet:',
        ptonWallet.toString()
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log('===== DONE =====');
}

import { Address } from '@ton/core';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';
import { DEX } from '@ston-fi/sdk';

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {

    const routerAddress = Address.parse(
        'kQALh-JBBIKK7gr0o4AVf9JZnEsFndqO0qTCyT-D-yBsWk0v'
    );

    const roosterAddress = Address.parse(
        'EQCyuv-cYXFibA05bhiL3seM3Ur2B_bpsc364Zb8F3NMtrYx'
    );

    const ptonAddress = Address.parse(
        'EQCM3B12QK1e4yZSf8GtBRT0aLMNyEsBc_DhVfRRtOEffLez'
    );

    const router = provider.open(
        DEX.v2_1.Router.CPI.create(routerAddress)
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log('===== SDK POOL CHECK =====');
    console.log('Router :', routerAddress.toString());
    console.log('ROOSTER:', roosterAddress.toString());
    console.log('pTON   :', ptonAddress.toString());
    console.log('');

    const roosterRouterWallet =
        await provider.open(
            DEX.v2_1.Router.CPI.create(routerAddress)
        ).getPoolAddressByJettonMinters({
            token0: roosterAddress,
            token1: ptonAddress
        });

    console.log('POOL:', roosterRouterWallet.toString());
}

import { Address, beginCell } from '@ton/core';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';
import { DEX } from '@ston-fi/sdk';

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {
    const router = DEX.v2_1.Router.CPI.create(
        Address.parse(
            'kQALh-JBBIKK7gr0o4AVf9JZnEsFndqO0qTCyT-D-yBsWk0v'
        )
    );

    const rooster = Address.parse(
        'EQCyuv-cYXFibA05bhiL3seM3Ur2B_bpsc364Zb8F3NMtrYx'
    );

    const pton = Address.parse(
        'EQCM3B12QK1e4yZSf8GtBRT0aLMNyEsBc_DhVfRRtOEffLez'
    );

    console.log('===== SDK POOL ADDRESS CHECK =====');
    console.log('Router:', router.address.toString());
    console.log('ROOSTER:', rooster.toString());
    console.log('pTON:', pton.toString());

    const routerProvider = provider.provider(router.address);

    try {
        const roosterWallet =
            await provider.provider(rooster).get('get_wallet_address', [
                {
                    type: 'slice',
                    cell: beginCell()
                        .storeAddress(router.address)
                        .endCell()
                }
            ]);

        console.log(
            'ROOSTER router wallet:',
            roosterWallet.stack.readAddress().toString()
        );
    } catch (e) {
        console.log('ROOSTER wallet ERROR:', e);
    }

    try {
        const poolAddress = await router.getPoolAddressByJettonMinters(
            routerProvider,
            {
                token0: rooster,
                token1: pton
            }
        );

        console.log('POOL:', poolAddress.toString());
    } catch (e) {
        console.log('POOL ERROR:', e);
    }
}

import { Address } from '@ton/core';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';
import { DEX } from '@ston-fi/sdk';

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {
    if (provider.network() !== 'testnet') {
        throw new Error('STOP: Testnet only.');
    }

    const routerAddress = Address.parse(
        'kQALh-JBBIKK7gr0o4AVf9JZnEsFndqO0qTCyT-D-yBsWk0v'
    );

    const router = new DEX.v2_1.Router.CPI(routerAddress);
    const routerProvider = provider.provider(routerAddress);

    const rooster = Address.parse(
        'EQCyuv-cYXFibA05bhiL3seM3Ur2B_bpsc364Zb8F3NMtrYx'
    );

    const pton = Address.parse(
        'kQACS30DNoUQ7NfApPvzh7eBmSZ9L4ygJ-lkNWtba8TQT-Px'
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log('===== STON TESTNET POOL CHECK =====');
    console.log('Router:', routerAddress.toString());
    console.log('ROOSTER:', rooster.toString());
    console.log('pTON:', pton.toString());
    console.log('');

    const poolAddress = await router.getPoolAddress(routerProvider, {
        token0: rooster,
        token1: pton,
    });

    console.log('Calculated Pool:', poolAddress.toString());
    console.log('');

    const poolState = await provider
        .provider(poolAddress)
        .getState();

    console.log('Pool state:', poolState.state.type);

    if (poolState.state.type === 'active') {
        console.log('POOL IS ACTIVE.');
    } else {
        console.log('POOL IS NOT ACTIVE YET.');
    }

    console.log('');
    console.log('NO TRANSACTION WAS SENT.');
    console.log('===================================');
}

FILE: scripts\checkStonReferencePool.ts
====================================================================================================
import { Address, beginCell } from '@ton/core';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {
    const routerAddress = Address.parse(
        'kQALh-JBBIKK7gr0o4AVf9JZnEsFndqO0qTCyT-D-yBsWk0v'
    );

    const tesReed = Address.parse(
        'kQDLvsZol3juZyOAVG8tWsJntOxeEZWEaWCbbSjYakQpuYN5'
    );

    const testBlue = Address.parse(
        'kQB_TOJSB7q3-Jm1O8s0jKFtqLElZDPjATs5uJGsujcjznq3'
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log('===== STON.FI TESTNET REFERENCE PAIR =====');
    console.log('Router:', routerAddress.toString());
    console.log('TesREED:', tesReed.toString());
    console.log('TestBlue:', testBlue.toString());
    console.log('');

    const routerProvider = provider.provider(routerAddress);

    // --------------------------------------------------
    // 1. TesREED wallet owned by Router
    // --------------------------------------------------
    console.log('1. Getting TesREED Router wallet...');

    const tesReedResult = await provider.provider(tesReed).get(
        'get_wallet_address',
        [
            {
                type: 'slice',
                cell: beginCell()
                    .storeAddress(routerAddress)
                    .endCell(),
            },
        ]
    );

    const tesReedWallet = tesReedResult.stack.readAddress();

    console.log(
        'TesREED Router wallet:',
        tesReedWallet.toString()
    );

    // --------------------------------------------------
    // 2. TestBlue wallet owned by Router
    // --------------------------------------------------
    console.log('');
    console.log('2. Getting TestBlue Router wallet...');

    const testBlueResult = await provider.provider(testBlue).get(
        'get_wallet_address',
        [
            {
                type: 'slice',
                cell: beginCell()
                    .storeAddress(routerAddress)
                    .endCell(),
            },
        ]
    );

    const testBlueWallet = testBlueResult.stack.readAddress();

    console.log(
        'TestBlue Router wallet:',
        testBlueWallet.toString()
    );

    // --------------------------------------------------
    // 3. Ask Router for pool address
    // --------------------------------------------------
    console.log('');
    console.log('3. Calling Router get_pool_address...');

    try {
        const poolResult = await routerProvider.get(
            'get_pool_address',
            [
                {
                    type: 'slice',
                    cell: beginCell()
                        .storeAddress(tesReedWallet)
                        .endCell(),
                },
                {
                    type: 'slice',
                    cell: beginCell()
                        .storeAddress(testBlueWallet)
                        .endCell(),
                },
            ]
        );

        const poolAddress = poolResult.stack.readAddress();

        console.log('');
        console.log('===== SUCCESS =====');
        console.log('Reference pool:', poolAddress.toString());
        console.log('');
        console.log('Router getter works for the official testnet pair.');
        console.log('No transaction was sent.');

    } catch (error) {
        console.log('');
        console.log('===== FAILED =====');
        console.log('Router get_pool_address failed.');
        console.log('');

        if (error instanceof Error) {
            console.log(error.message);
        } else {
            console.log(error);
        }

        console.log('');
        console.log('No transaction was sent.');
    }
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\checkStonRouter.ts
====================================================================================================
import { Address } from '@ton/core';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {
    if (provider.network() !== 'testnet') {
        throw new Error('STOP: Testnet only.');
    }

    const routerAddress = Address.parse(
        'kQALh-JBBIKK7gr0o4AVf9JZnEsFndqO0qTCyT-D-yBsWk0v'
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log('===== STON TESTNET ROUTER GETTERS =====');
    console.log('Router:', routerAddress.toString());
    console.log('');

    const routerProvider = provider.provider(routerAddress);

    const getters = [
        'get_router_version',
        'get_router_data',
        'get_pool_address',
    ];

    for (const getter of getters) {
        console.log('Testing:', getter);

        try {
            if (getter === 'get_pool_address') {
                console.log('Skipped: requires token arguments.');
                console.log('');
                continue;
            }

            const result = await routerProvider.get(getter, []);

            console.log('SUCCESS');
            console.log(result.stack);
        } catch (error) {
            console.log('FAILED');
            console.log(String(error));
        }

        console.log('');
    }

    console.log('NO TRANSACTION WAS SENT.');
    console.log('=======================================');
}

import { Address, beginCell } from '@ton/core';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {
    if (provider.network() !== 'testnet') {
        throw new Error('STOP: This script is only for TON Testnet.');
    }

    const roosterMaster = Address.parse(
        'EQCyuv-cYXFibA05bhiL3seM3Ur2B_bpsc364Zb8F3NMtrYx'
    );

    const ptonMaster = Address.parse(
        'EQACS30DNoUQ7NfApPvzh7eBmSZ9L4ygJ-lkNWtba8TQT1h7'
    );

    const routerAddress = Address.parse(
        'kQALh-JBBIKK7gr0o4AVf9JZnEsFndqO0qTCyT-D-yBsWk0v'
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log('===== STON TESTNET JETTON WALLETS =====');
    console.log('Router:', routerAddress.toString());
    console.log('');

    const roosterProvider = provider.provider(roosterMaster);
    const ptonProvider = provider.provider(ptonMaster);

    const roosterResult = await roosterProvider.get('get_wallet_address', [
        {
            type: 'slice',
            cell: beginCell()
                .storeAddress(routerAddress)
                .endCell(),
        },
    ]);

    const ptonResult = await ptonProvider.get('get_wallet_address', [
        {
            type: 'slice',
            cell: beginCell()
                .storeAddress(routerAddress)
                .endCell(),
        },
    ]);

    const roosterWallet = roosterResult.stack.readAddress();
    const ptonWallet = ptonResult.stack.readAddress();

    console.log('Rooster Wallet:', roosterWallet.toString());
    console.log('pTON Wallet:', ptonWallet.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log('NO TRANSACTION WAS SENT.');
    console.log('=======================================');
}

import { Address, TonClient4, fromNano } from '@ton/ton';

const USER = Address.parse(
    'EQDxn5MLbFyV649-OBviQrMp5KqWjGHNVwFM6GTeaLl8N0Ix'
);

const RPC_ENDPOINT = 'https://sandbox-v4.tonhubapi.com';

export async function run() {
    console.log('='.repeat(70));
    console.log(' STON.FI v2.2 USER TON BALANCE CHECK');
    console.log(' READ ONLY - RPC ONLY - NO TRANSACTION');
    console.log('='.repeat(70));

    console.log('\n===== 1. USER =====');
    console.log(USER.toString());

    console.log('\n===== 2. RPC =====');

    const client = new TonClient4({
        endpoint: RPC_ENDPOINT,
    });

    const last = await client.getLastBlock();

    console.log('RPC: PASS');
    console.log('Masterchain seqno:', last.last.seqno);

    console.log('\n===== 3. ACCOUNT =====');

    const result = await client.getAccount(
        last.last.seqno,
        USER
    );

    const account = result.account;
    const balanceNano = BigInt(account.balance.coins);

    console.log('State:', account.state.type);
    console.log('Balance nanoTON:', balanceNano.toString());
    console.log('Balance TON:', fromNano(balanceNano));

    console.log('\n===== 4. LIQUIDITY MESSAGE VALUES =====');

    const roosterValue = 300_000_000n;
    const ptonValue = 810_000_000n;
    const combined = roosterValue + ptonValue;

    console.log('ROOSTER message:', fromNano(roosterValue), 'TON');
    console.log('pTON message:', fromNano(ptonValue), 'TON');
    console.log('Combined:', fromNano(combined), 'TON');

    console.log('\n===== 5. BALANCE VS MESSAGE VALUE =====');

    if (balanceNano > combined) {
        console.log(
            'PASS | Balance is greater than combined message values'
        );
    } else {
        console.log(
            'FAIL | Balance is NOT greater than combined message values'
        );
    }

    console.log('\n===== 6. CONSERVATIVE CHECK =====');

    const conservativeTarget = 1_300_000_000n;

    console.log(
        'Conservative target:',
        fromNano(conservativeTarget),
        'TON'
    );

    if (balanceNano >= conservativeTarget) {
        console.log(
            'PASS | Balance >= 1.30 TON conservative target'
        );
    } else {
        console.log(
            'WARNING | Balance < 1.30 TON conservative target'
        );
    }

    console.log('\n===== FINAL =====');

    console.log('READ ONLY: YES');
    console.log('RPC USED: YES');
    console.log('TRANSACTION SENT: NO');

    console.log('='.repeat(70));
}
