====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\classify25100C.ts
====================================================================================================
import fs from "fs";

const file =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const b = fs.readFileSync(file);

const TARGET = 0x25100c;
const hits = [
  0x000112ac,
  0x0001130f,
  0x00038491,
  0x000384d8,
  0x0006f756,
  0x0006f773,
  0x0006f780,
  0x0006f78e,
  0x0006f7cd,
  0x000fdc9b,
  0x0023a03b,
  0x0023a18b,
  0x0023a1a8,
  0x0023a1d7,
  0x0023a201,
];

function readULEB(p: number) {
  let v = 0;
  let s = 0;
  while (true) {
    const x = b[p++];
    v |= (x & 0x7f) << s;
    if (!(x & 0x80)) return { value: v >>> 0, next: p };
    s += 7;
  }
}

function readName(p: number) {
  const r = readULEB(p);
  return { name: "", next: r.next + r.value };
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Parse CODE section and associate body ranges with FUNC indexes
// ------------------------------------------------------------

let p = 8;
let codeStart = -1;
let codeEnd = -1;

while (p < b.length) {
  const id = b[p++];
  const s = readULEB(p);
  const size = s.value;
  p = s.next;

  const start = p;
  const end = p + size;

  if (id === 10) {
    codeStart = start;
    codeEnd = end;
    break;
  }

  p = end;
}

if (codeStart < 0) {
  throw new Error("CODE section not found");
}

let q = codeStart;
const count = readULEB(q);
q = count.next;

const bodies: {
  func: number;
  start: number;
  end: number;
}[] = [];

const importedCount = 83;

for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {
  const sz = readULEB(q);
  q = sz.next;

  const bodyStart = q;
  const bodyEnd = q + sz.value;

  bodies.push({
    func: importedCount + i,
    start: bodyStart,
    end: bodyEnd,
  });

  q = bodyEnd;
}

function hex(x: number) {
  return "0x" + x.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

console.log("FUNC 25100C CLASSIFICATION");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`TARGET : ${hex(TARGET)}`);
console.log(`HITS   : ${hits.length}`);
console.log("============================================================");

for (const hit of hits) {
  const body = bodies.find(x => hit >= x.start && hit < x.end);

  console.log(`\nHIT ${hex(hit)}`);

  if (!body) {
    console.log("FUNC : NOT FOUND");
    continue;
  }

  console.log(
    `FUNC : ${body.func}`
  );
  console.log(
    `BODY : ${hex(body.start)} .. ${hex(body.end)}`
  );

  const from = Math.max(body.start, hit - 20);
  const to = Math.min(body.end, hit + 40);

  const bytes = Array.from(b.subarray(from, to))
    .map(x => x.toString(16).padStart(2, "0"))
    .join(" ");

  console.log(`RAW  : ${bytes}`);

  // Look for store/load following the target constant.
  const rel = hit - body.start;
  const scanEnd = Math.min(body.end, hit + 35);

  let foundStore = false;
  let foundLoad = false;

  for (let x = hit; x < scanEnd; x++) {
    // i32.load
    if (b[x] === 0x28) foundLoad = true;

    // i32.store
    if (b[x] === 0x36) foundStore = true;
  }

  if (foundStore) {
    console.log("CLASS : WRITER-CANDIDATE (i32.store found)");
  } else if (foundLoad) {
    console.log("CLASS : READER-CANDIDATE (i32.load found)");
  } else {
    console.log("CLASS : INDIRECT / NEEDS DECODING");
  }
}

console.log("\n============================================================");
console.log("DONE");

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\classifyDispatchRefs.ts
====================================================================================================
import fs from "fs";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

const TARGETS = new Set([
  0x195348,
  0x19534c,
  0x195350,
  0x195354,
  0x195358,
  0x195360,
  0x195364,
]);

function uleb(pos: number) {
  let v = 0, shift = 0, p = pos;
  while (p < wasm.length) {
    const b = wasm[p++];
    v += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;
    if (!(b & 0x80)) break;
    shift += 7;
  }
  return { value: v >>> 0, next: p };
}

function sectionBodies() {
  const bodies: { func: number, start: number, end: number }[] = [];

  let p = 8;
  let imported = 0;

  // First get imported function count.
  while (p < wasm.length) {
    const id = wasm[p++];
    const s = uleb(p);
    p = s.next;
    const end = p + s.value;

    if (id === 2) {
      let q = p;
      const n = uleb(q);
      q = n.next;

      for (let i = 0; i < n.value; i++) {
        const ml = uleb(q); q = ml.next + ml.value;
        const nl = uleb(q); q = nl.next + nl.value;
        const kind = wasm[q++];

        if (kind === 0) {
          const x = uleb(q);
          q = x.next;
          imported++;
        } else if (kind === 1) {
          q++;
          const x = uleb(q); q = x.next;
          const y = uleb(q); q = y.next;
          if (x.value !== 0x70) {}
        } else if (kind === 2) {
          const flags = uleb(q); q = flags.next;
          const min = uleb(q); q = min.next;
          if (flags.value & 1) {
            const max = uleb(q); q = max.next;
          }
        } else if (kind === 3) {
          q += 2;
        }
      }
    }

    p = end;
  }

  // Locate code section.
  p = 8;

  while (p < wasm.length) {
    const id = wasm[p++];
    const s = uleb(p);
    p = s.next;
    const end = p + s.value;

    if (id === 10) {
      let q = p;
      const n = uleb(q);
      q = n.next;

      for (let i = 0; i < n.value; i++) {
        const bs = uleb(q);
        q = bs.next;

        const bodyStart = q;
        const bodyEnd = q + bs.value;

        bodies.push({
          func: imported + i,
          start: bodyStart,
          end: bodyEnd
        });

        q = bodyEnd;
      }

      break;
    }

    p = end;
  }

  return bodies;
}

function funcAt(pos: number, bodies: ReturnType<typeof sectionBodies>) {
  return bodies.find(x => pos >= x.start && pos < x.end);
}

const bodies = sectionBodies();

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("CLASSIFY DISPATCH TABLE REFERENCES");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`FUNCTION BODIES : ${bodies.length}`);
console.log("");

let hits = 0;

for (const body of bodies) {
  for (let p = body.start; p < body.end - 2; p++) {

    if (wasm[p] !== 0x41) continue;

    const c = uleb(p + 1);

    if (!TARGETS.has(c.value)) continue;

    const next = c.next;
    const op = wasm[next];

    let kind = "UNKNOWN";

    if (op >= 0x28 && op <= 0x2b) {
      kind = "LOAD";
    } else if (op >= 0x36 && op <= 0x3e) {
      kind = "STORE";
    } else if (op === 0x6a || op === 0x6b || op === 0x6c) {
      kind = "ADDRESS_ARITHMETIC";
    } else if (op === 0x20 || op === 0x21 || op === 0x22) {
      kind = "LOCAL_OPERATION";
    } else if (op === 0x10) {
      kind = "CALL";
    }

    console.log(
      `FUNC ${body.func.toString().padStart(5)} ` +
      `CONST 0x${p.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")} ` +
      `ADDR 0x${c.value.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")} ` +
      `NEXT=0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")} ` +
      `${kind}`
    );

    // Short raw context.
    const from = Math.max(body.start, p - 4);
    const to = Math.min(body.end, next + 8);

    let raw = "";
    for (let q = from; q < to; q++) {
      raw += wasm[q].toString(16).padStart(2, "0") + " ";
    }

    console.log(`  RAW: ${raw}`);

    hits++;
  }
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`TOTAL BODY-AWARE REFERENCES : ${hits}`);
console.log("============================================================");

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\compareDispatchFuncs.ts

import fs from "fs";

const WASM = "emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const TARGETS = [91, 119, 656];

const buf = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

function uleb(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = buf[pos++];
        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return { value, next: pos };
        }

        shift += 7;
    }
}

function readSections() {
    let p = 8;
    const sections: {
        id: number;
        start: number;
        end: number;
    }[] = [];

    while (p < buf.length) {
        const id = buf[p++];
        const s = uleb(p);
        p = s.next;

        const start = p;
        const end = p + s.value;

        sections.push({ id, start, end });

        p = end;
    }

    return sections;
}

const sections = readSections();

const funcSec = sections.find(x => x.id === 3);
const codeSec = sections.find(x => x.id === 10);

if (!funcSec || !codeSec) {
    throw new Error("Function/code section not found");
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Function section: defined function type indices
// ------------------------------------------------------------

let p = funcSec.start;
const fc = uleb(p);
p = fc.next;

const definedType: number[] = [];

for (let i = 0; i < fc.value; i++) {
    const x = uleb(p);
    p = x.next;
    definedType.push(x.value);
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Code section: function body ranges
// ------------------------------------------------------------

p = codeSec.start;

const cc = uleb(p);
p = cc.next;

const bodies: {
    globalFunc: number;
    ordinal: number;
    bodyStart: number;
    bodyEnd: number;
}[] = [];

for (let ordinal = 0; ordinal < cc.value; ordinal++) {
    const bs = uleb(p);
    p = bs.next;

    const bodyStart = p;
    const bodyEnd = p + bs.value;

    bodies.push({
        globalFunc: 83 + ordinal,
        ordinal,
        bodyStart,
        bodyEnd,
    });

    p = bodyEnd;
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Print target functions
// ------------------------------------------------------------

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("COMPARE DISPATCH FUNCTIONS");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`TARGETS = ${TARGETS.join(", ")}`);
console.log("TARGET ADDRESS = 0x00195354");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

for (const target of TARGETS) {
    const fn = bodies.find(x => x.globalFunc === target);

    if (!fn) {
        console.log(`FUNC ${target} NOT FOUND`);
        continue;
    }

    console.log();
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(`FUNC ${target}`);
    console.log(`ORDINAL = ${fn.ordinal}`);
    console.log(
        `BODY    = 0x${fn.bodyStart.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")} .. 0x${fn.bodyEnd.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}`
    );
    console.log(`SIZE    = ${fn.bodyEnd - fn.bodyStart}`);
    console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

    const start = Math.max(codeSec.start, fn.bodyStart);
    const end = Math.min(codeSec.end, fn.bodyEnd);

    for (let off = start; off < end; off += 16) {
        const e = Math.min(off + 16, end);

        const bytes: string[] = [];

        for (let i = off; i < e; i++) {
            bytes.push(buf[i].toString(16).padStart(2, "0"));
        }

        console.log(
            `0x${off.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  ${bytes.join(" ")}`
        );
    }

    console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // Find exact 0x195354 constant inside this function
    // --------------------------------------------------------

    const pattern = [0x41, 0xd4, 0xa6, 0xe5, 0x00];

    for (let off = start; off <= end - pattern.length; off++) {
        let ok = true;

        for (let j = 0; j < pattern.length; j++) {
            if (buf[off + j] !== pattern[j]) {
                ok = false;
                break;
            }
        }

        if (!ok) continue;

        console.log(
            `DISPATCH LOAD @ 0x${off.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}`
        );

        const contextStart = Math.max(start, off - 24);
        const contextEnd = Math.min(end, off + 64);

        for (let c = contextStart; c < contextEnd; c += 16) {
            const ce = Math.min(c + 16, contextEnd);

            const bytes: string[] = [];

            for (let i = c; i < ce; i++) {
                bytes.push(buf[i].toString(16).padStart(2, "0"));
            }

            console.log(
                `  0x${c.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  ${bytes.join(" ")}`
            );
        }
    }

    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // Find direct calls to FUNC 83 and FUNC 91
    // --------------------------------------------------------

    console.log();
    console.log("DIRECT CALL REFERENCES");

    for (let off = start; off < end; off++) {
        if (buf[off] !== 0x10) continue;

        const x = uleb(off + 1);

        if (x.value === 83 || x.value === 91) {
            console.log(
                `  call FUNC ${x.value} @ 0x${off.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}`
            );
        }
    }

    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // call_indirect
    // --------------------------------------------------------

    console.log();
    console.log("CALL_INDIRECT REFERENCES");

    for (let off = start; off < end; off++) {
        if (buf[off] !== 0x11) continue;

        const type = uleb(off + 1);

        console.log(
            `  call_indirect @ 0x${off.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")} | type=${type.value}`
        );
    }
}

console.log();
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("END");
console.log("NO WASM EXECUTION");
console.log("============================================================");

FILE: scripts\compareFunc2088Func3403.mjs

import fs from "fs";

const WASM =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const b = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

const TARGETS = [2088, 3403];
const IMPORTED = 83;

function uleb(p) {
  let v = 0, s = 0;
  while (true) {
    const x = b[p++];
    v += (x & 0x7f) * 2 ** s;
    if (!(x & 0x80)) return { value: v, next: p };
    s += 7;
  }
}

function sleb(p) {
  let v = 0, s = 0, x;
  do {
    x = b[p++];
    v |= (x & 0x7f) << s;
    s += 7;
  } while (x & 0x80);

  if (s < 32 && (x & 0x40))
    v |= (~0 << s);

  return { value: v, next: p };
}

/* ---------------------------------------------------------- */
/* Sections                                                   */
/* ---------------------------------------------------------- */

let p = 8;
let functionSection = null;
let codeSection = null;

while (p < b.length) {
  const id = b[p++];

  const z = uleb(p);
  const size = z.value;
  p = z.next;

  if (id === 3)
    functionSection = { start: p, end: p + size };

  if (id === 10)
    codeSection = { start: p, end: p + size };

  p += size;
}

function getBody(target) {

  const ordinal = target - IMPORTED;

  let fp = functionSection.start;
  const fc = uleb(fp);
  fp = fc.next;

  let typeIndex = null;

  for (let i = 0; i <= ordinal; i++) {
    const x = uleb(fp);
    fp = x.next;

    if (i === ordinal)
      typeIndex = x.value;
  }

  let cp = codeSection.start;
  const cc = uleb(cp);
  cp = cc.next;

  for (let i = 0; i < cc.value; i++) {

    const z = uleb(cp);
    const size = z.value;
    cp = z.next;

    const start = cp;
    const end = cp + size;

    if (i === ordinal)
      return { start, end, typeIndex };

    cp = end;
  }

  return null;
}

/* ---------------------------------------------------------- */
/* Decoder                                                    */
/* ---------------------------------------------------------- */

function decode(p) {

  const addr = p;
  const op = b[p++];

  const result = (next, text) => ({
    next,
    text:
      `0x${addr.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  ${text}`
  });

  switch (op) {

    case 0x00:
      return result(p, "UNREACHABLE");

    case 0x01:
      return result(p, "NOP");

    case 0x02:
      return result(p + 1, `BLOCK type=0x${b[p].toString(16)}`);

    case 0x03:
      return result(p + 1, `LOOP type=0x${b[p].toString(16)}`);

    case 0x04:
      return result(p + 1, `IF type=0x${b[p].toString(16)}`);

    case 0x05:
      return result(p, "ELSE");

    case 0x0b:
      return result(p, "END");

    case 0x0c: {
      const x = uleb(p);
      return result(x.next, `BR ${x.value}`);
    }

    case 0x0d: {
      const x = uleb(p);
      return result(x.next, `BR_IF ${x.value}`);
    }

    case 0x0f:
      return result(p, "RETURN");

    case 0x10: {
      const x = uleb(p);
      return result(x.next, `CALL FUNC ${x.value}`);
    }

    case 0x11: {
      const t = uleb(p);
      p = t.next;

      const tb = uleb(p);
      p = tb.next;

      return result(
        tb.next,
        `CALL_INDIRECT type=${t.value} table=${tb.value}`
      );
    }

    case 0x1a:
      return result(p, "DROP");

    case 0x1b:
      return result(p, "SELECT");

    case 0x20: {
      const x = uleb(p);
      return result(x.next, `LOCAL.GET ${x.value}`);
    }

    case 0x21: {
      const x = uleb(p);
      return result(x.next, `LOCAL.SET ${x.value}`);
    }

    case 0x22: {
      const x = uleb(p);
      return result(x.next, `LOCAL.TEE ${x.value}`);
    }

    case 0x23: {
      const x = uleb(p);
      return result(x.next, `GLOBAL.GET ${x.value}`);
    }

    case 0x24: {
      const x = uleb(p);
      return result(x.next, `GLOBAL.SET ${x.value}`);
    }

    case 0x28: {
      const a = uleb(p);
      p = a.next;

      const o = uleb(p);
      p = o.next;

      return result(
        p,
        `I32.LOAD align=${a.value} offset=${o.value}`
      );
    }

    case 0x29: {
      const a = uleb(p);
      p = a.next;

      const o = uleb(p);
      p = o.next;

      return result(
        p,
        `I64.LOAD align=${a.value} offset=${o.value}`
      );
    }

    case 0x36: {
      const a = uleb(p);
      p = a.next;

      const o = uleb(p);
      p = o.next;

      return result(
        p,
        `I32.STORE align=${a.value} offset=${o.value}`
      );
    }

    case 0x41: {
      const x = sleb(p);
      return result(
        x.next,
        `I32.CONST ${x.value} (0x${(x.value >>> 0).toString(16)})`
      );
    }

    case 0x42: {
      const x = sleb(p);
      return result(
        x.next,
        `I64.CONST ${x.value}`
      );
    }

    case 0x45:
      return result(p, "I32.EQZ");

    case 0x46:
      return result(p, "I32.EQ");

    case 0x47:
      return result(p, "I32.NE");

    case 0x48:
      return result(p, "I32.LT_S");

    case 0x49:
      return result(p, "I32.LT_U");

    case 0x4a:
      return result(p, "I32.GT_S");

    case 0x4c:
      return result(p, "I32.LE_S");

    case 0x6a:
      return result(p, "I32.ADD");

    case 0x6b:
      return result(p, "I32.SUB");

    case 0x71:
      return result(p, "I32.AND");

    case 0x72:
      return result(p, "I32.OR");

    case 0x73:
      return result(p, "I32.XOR");

    case 0x74:
      return result(p, "I32.SHL");

    case 0x75:
      return result(p, "I32.SHR_S");

    case 0x76:
      return result(p, "I32.SHR_U");

    default:
      return result(
        p,
        `OPCODE 0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`
      );
  }
}

/* ---------------------------------------------------------- */
/* Main                                                        */
/* ---------------------------------------------------------- */

for (const target of TARGETS) {

  const body = getBody(target);

  console.log("");
  console.log("============================================================");
  console.log(`FUNC ${target} EXACT BODY`);
  console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
  console.log("============================================================");
  console.log(`FUNCTION INDEX : ${target}`);
  console.log(`TYPE INDEX     : ${body.typeIndex}`);
  console.log(
    `BODY           : 0x${body.start.toString(16)} .. 0x${body.end.toString(16)}`
  );
  console.log(`SIZE           : ${body.end - body.start}`);
  console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

  let q = body.start;

  while (q < body.end) {
    const x = decode(q);
    console.log(x.text);
    q = x.next;
  }

  console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
  console.log("IMPORTANT CONSTANTS / CALLS");

  q = body.start;

  while (q < body.end) {

    const addr = q;
    const op = b[q++];

    if (op === 0x41) {
      const x = sleb(q);

      if (
        x.value === 4589 ||
        x.value === 4588 ||
        x.value === 0x19534c ||
        x.value === 0x250fb8 ||
        x.value === 0x121e7b4
      ) {
        console.log(
          `0x${addr.toString(16)} I32.CONST ${x.value} ` +
          `(0x${(x.value >>> 0).toString(16)})`
        );
      }

      q = x.next;
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x10) {
      const x = uleb(q);
      console.log(
        `0x${addr.toString(16)} CALL FUNC ${x.value}`
      );
      q = x.next;
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x11) {
      const t = uleb(q);
      q = t.next;

      const tb = uleb(q);
      q = tb.next;

      console.log(
        `0x${addr.toString(16)} CALL_INDIRECT type=${t.value} table=${tb.value}`
      );
      continue;
    }

    if (
      op === 0x20 ||
      op === 0x21 ||
      op === 0x22 ||
      op === 0x23 ||
      op === 0x24 ||
      op === 0x0c ||
      op === 0x0d
    ) {
      q = uleb(q).next;
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x28 || op === 0x29 || op === 0x36) {
      q = uleb(q).next;
      q = uleb(q).next;
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x02 || op === 0x03 || op === 0x04) {
      q++;
    }
  }
}

console.log("");

console.log("DONE");

import fs from "fs";

const WASM =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const b = fs.readFileSync(WASM);
const TARGETS = [7171, 7172];
const IMPORTED = 83;

function uleb(p) {
  let v = 0, s = 0;
  while (true) {
    const x = b[p++];
    v += (x & 0x7f) * 2 ** s;
    if (!(x & 0x80)) return { value: v, next: p };
    s += 7;
  }
}

function sleb(p) {
  let v = 0, s = 0, x;
  do {
    x = b[p++];
    v |= (x & 0x7f) << s;
    s += 7;
  } while (x & 0x80);

  if (s < 32 && (x & 0x40))
    v |= (~0 << s);

  return { value: v, next: p };
}

/* sections */
let p = 8;
let funcSec = null;
let codeSec = null;

while (p < b.length) {
  const id = b[p++];

  const z = uleb(p);
  const size = z.value;
  p = z.next;

  if (id === 3)
    funcSec = { start: p, end: p + size };

  if (id === 10)
    codeSec = { start: p, end: p + size };

  p += size;
}

function bodyOf(target) {

  const ordinal = target - IMPORTED;

  let p = funcSec.start;
  const fc = uleb(p);
  p = fc.next;

  let typeIndex = null;

  for (let i = 0; i <= ordinal; i++) {
    const x = uleb(p);
    p = x.next;

    if (i === ordinal)
      typeIndex = x.value;
  }

  let cp = codeSec.start;
  const cc = uleb(cp);
  cp = cc.next;

  for (let i = 0; i < cc.value; i++) {

    const z = uleb(cp);
    const size = z.value;
    cp = z.next;

    const start = cp;
    const end = cp + size;

    if (i === ordinal)
      return { start, end, typeIndex };

    cp = end;
  }

  return null;
}

function decode(p) {

  const addr = p;
  const op = b[p++];

  const R = (next, s) => ({
    next,
    text: `0x${addr.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  ${s}`
  });

  switch (op) {

    case 0x00: return R(p, "UNREACHABLE");
    case 0x01: return R(p, "NOP");
    case 0x05: return R(p, "ELSE");
    case 0x0b: return R(p, "END");
    case 0x0f: return R(p, "RETURN");
    case 0x1a: return R(p, "DROP");
    case 0x1b: return R(p, "SELECT");

    case 0x02:
      return R(p + 1, `BLOCK type=0x${b[p].toString(16)}`);

    case 0x03:
      return R(p + 1, `LOOP type=0x${b[p].toString(16)}`);

    case 0x04:
      return R(p + 1, `IF type=0x${b[p].toString(16)}`);

    case 0x0c: {
      const x = uleb(p);
      return R(x.next, `BR ${x.value}`);
    }

    case 0x0d: {
      const x = uleb(p);
      return R(x.next, `BR_IF ${x.value}`);
    }

    case 0x10: {
      const x = uleb(p);
      return R(x.next, `CALL FUNC ${x.value}`);
    }

    case 0x11: {
      const t = uleb(p);
      p = t.next;
      const tb = uleb(p);
      p = tb.next;

      return R(
        p,
        `CALL_INDIRECT type=${t.value} table=${tb.value}`
      );
    }

    case 0x20: {
      const x = uleb(p);
      return R(x.next, `LOCAL.GET ${x.value}`);
    }

    case 0x21: {
      const x = uleb(p);
      return R(x.next, `LOCAL.SET ${x.value}`);
    }

    case 0x22: {
      const x = uleb(p);
      return R(x.next, `LOCAL.TEE ${x.value}`);
    }

    case 0x23: {
      const x = uleb(p);
      return R(x.next, `GLOBAL.GET ${x.value}`);
    }

    case 0x24: {
      const x = uleb(p);
      return R(x.next, `GLOBAL.SET ${x.value}`);
    }

    case 0x28: {
      const a = uleb(p);
      p = a.next;
      const o = uleb(p);
      p = o.next;

      return R(
        p,
        `I32.LOAD align=${a.value} offset=${o.value}`
      );
    }

    case 0x29: {
      const a = uleb(p);
      p = a.next;
      const o = uleb(p);
      p = o.next;

      return R(
        p,
        `I64.LOAD align=${a.value} offset=${o.value}`
      );
    }

    case 0x36: {
      const a = uleb(p);
      p = a.next;
      const o = uleb(p);
      p = o.next;

      return R(
        p,
        `I32.STORE align=${a.value} offset=${o.value}`
      );
    }

    case 0x41: {
      const x = sleb(p);

      return R(
        x.next,
        `I32.CONST ${x.value} (0x${(x.value >>> 0).toString(16)})`
      );
    }

    case 0x42: {
      const x = sleb(p);

      return R(
        x.next,
        `I64.CONST ${x.value}`
      );
    }

    case 0x45: return R(p, "I32.EQZ");
    case 0x46: return R(p, "I32.EQ");
    case 0x47: return R(p, "I32.NE");
    case 0x48: return R(p, "I32.LT_S");
    case 0x49: return R(p, "I32.LT_U");
    case 0x4a: return R(p, "I32.GT_S");
    case 0x4b: return R(p, "I32.GT_U");
    case 0x4c: return R(p, "I32.LE_S");

    case 0x6a: return R(p, "I32.ADD");
    case 0x6b: return R(p, "I32.SUB");
    case 0x71: return R(p, "I32.AND");
    case 0x72: return R(p, "I32.OR");
    case 0x73: return R(p, "I32.XOR");
    case 0x74: return R(p, "I32.SHL");
    case 0x75: return R(p, "I32.SHR_S");
    case 0x76: return R(p, "I32.SHR_U");

    default:
      return R(
        p,
        `OPCODE 0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`
      );
  }
}

        console.log(
          `0x${addr.toString(16)} CONST ${x.value} ` +
          `(0x${(x.value >>> 0).toString(16)})`
        );
      }

      q = x.next;
      continue;
    }

    if (
      op === 0x20 ||
      op === 0x21 ||
      op === 0x22 ||
      op === 0x23 ||
      op === 0x24 ||
      op === 0x0c ||
      op === 0x0d
    ) {
      q = uleb(q).next;
      continue;
    }

    if (
      op === 0x28 ||
      op === 0x29 ||
      op === 0x36
    ) {
      q = uleb(q).next;
      q = uleb(q).next;
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x02 || op === 0x03 || op === 0x04)
      q++;
  }
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("DONE");
console.log("============================================================");

import { Cell } from '@ton/core';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

const TX1_BOC =
    'te6cckEBAgEAgQABcVDGplQAAAAAAAAAAFF0h26AAECPJ8UQwA8Z+TC2xcleuPfjgb4kKzKeSqloxhzVcBTOhk3mi5fDdAEAhYAeM/JhbYuSvXHvxwN8SFZlPJVS0Yw5quApnQybzRcvhvADxn5MLbFyV649+OBviQrMp5KqWjGHNVwFM6GTeaLl8N6B+o0s';

const TX2_BOC =
    'te6cckEBAgEAgwABdVDGplQAAAAAAAAAAGtfdACC7EBUXtAar2DADxn5MLbFyV649+OBviQrMp5KqWjGHNVwFM6GTeaLl8N0AQCFgB4z8mFti5K9ce/HA3xIVmU8lVLRjDmq4CmdDJvNFy+G8APGfkwtsXJXrj344G+JCsynkqpaMYc1XAUzoZN5ouXw3rRLvn0=';

const CURRENT_AMOUNT0 =
    200173535827652n;

const CURRENT_AMOUNT1 =
    0n;

function decode(name: string, boc: string) {
    const cell = Cell.fromBase64(boc);
    const s = cell.beginParse();

    const opcode = s.loadUint(32);
    const queryId = s.loadUintBig(64);

    const amount0 = s.loadCoins();
    const amount1 = s.loadCoins();
    const minLpOut = s.loadCoins();

    const fwdAmount = s.loadCoins();
    const bothPositive = s.loadBit();

    const toAddress = s.loadAddress();

    console.log('');
    console.log('================================================');
    console.log(name);
    console.log('================================================');

    console.log('ROOT BITS:', cell.bits.length);
    console.log('ROOT REFS:', cell.refs.length);

    console.log(
        'OPCODE:',
        '0x' + opcode.toString(16).padStart(8, '0')
    );

    console.log(
        'QUERY ID:',
        queryId.toString()
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log('AMOUNT 0 RAW:', amount0.toString());
    console.log(
        'AMOUNT 0 DISPLAY:',
        Number(amount0) / 1e9,
        'ROOSTER'
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log('AMOUNT 1 RAW:', amount1.toString());
    console.log(
        'AMOUNT 1 DISPLAY:',
        Number(amount1) / 1e6,
        'pTON'
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log('MIN LP OUT:', minLpOut.toString());

    console.log(
        'FWD AMOUNT:',
        Number(fwdAmount) / 1e9,
        'TON'
    );

    console.log(
        'BOTH POSITIVE:',
        bothPositive ? 'YES' : 'NO'
    );

    console.log(
        'TO ADDRESS:',
        toAddress?.toString()
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log('REMAINING BITS:', s.remainingBits);
    console.log('REMAINING REFS:', s.remainingRefs);

    return {
        amount0,
        amount1,
        minLpOut,
        bothPositive
    };
}

import { Address, TonClient } from '@ton/ton';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';
import { Cell } from '@ton/core';
import crypto from 'crypto';

const DESTINATION = Address.parse(
    'EQCEwfJtb6ZmPJdNw387eJwed2N51K6dFFbH82jTxe_cf31e'
);

const KNOWN_LP_WALLET = Address.parse(
    'EQCNnXhynq5G7RZ8glhXSNg6eXby1kzqD-p8HNxu6PN5SIgd'
);

const TONCENTER =
    'https://testnet.toncenter.com/api/v2/jsonRPC';

function sha256Hex(data: Buffer): string {
    return crypto
        .createHash('sha256')
        .update(data)
        .digest('hex');
}

async function inspect(
    client: TonClient,
    address: Address,
    name: string
) {
    console.log('');
    console.log(
        '============================================================'
    );
    console.log(name);
    console.log(
        '============================================================'
    );

    console.log(
        `Address: ${address.toString()}`
    );

    const state =
        await client.getContractState(address);

    console.log(
        `state: ${state.state}`
    );

    console.log(
        `balance: ${state.balance}`
    );

    console.log(
        `balance TON: ${(Number(state.balance) / 1e9).toFixed(9)}`
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        `code bytes: ${state.code?.length ?? 0}`
    );

    console.log(
        `data bytes: ${state.data?.length ?? 0}`
    );

    if (state.code && state.code.length > 0) {
        console.log('');
        console.log('CODE SHA256:');
        console.log(
            sha256Hex(state.code)
        );

        try {
            const codeCell =
                Cell.fromBoc(state.code)[0];

            console.log('');
            console.log(
                `CODE BITS: ${codeCell.bits.length}`
            );

            console.log(
                `CODE REFS: ${codeCell.refs.length}`
            );

            console.log('');
            console.log('CODE BOC(base64):');

            console.log(
                codeCell
                    .toBoc()
                    .toString('base64')
            );
        } catch (e) {
            console.log(
                `code Cell decode failed: ${e}`
            );
        }
    }

    if (state.data && state.data.length > 0) {
        console.log('');
        console.log('DATA SHA256:');
        console.log(
            sha256Hex(state.data)
        );

        try {
            const dataCell =
                Cell.fromBoc(state.data)[0];

            console.log('');
            console.log(
                `DATA BITS: ${dataCell.bits.length}`
            );

            console.log(
                `DATA REFS: ${dataCell.refs.length}`
            );

            console.log('');
            console.log('DATA BOC(base64):');

            console.log(
                dataCell
                    .toBoc()
                    .toString('base64')
            );
        } catch (e) {
            console.log(
                `data Cell decode failed: ${e}`
            );
        }
    }
}

async function main() {
    console.log('');
    console.log('================================================================');
    console.log(' STON.FI v2.2 LP DESTINATION CODE COMPARATOR');
    console.log(' READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION');
    console.log('================================================================');

    const client = new TonClient({
        endpoint: TONCENTER,
    });

    await inspect(
        client,
        DESTINATION,
        'POOL OUTGOING DESTINATION'
    );

    await inspect(
        client,
        KNOWN_LP_WALLET,
        'KNOWN LP WALLET'
    );

    const a =
        await client.getContractState(
            DESTINATION
        );

    const b =
        await client.getContractState(
            KNOWN_LP_WALLET
        );

    console.log('');
    console.log('================================================================');
    console.log(' COMPARISON');
    console.log('================================================================');

    if (
        a.code &&
        b.code &&
        a.code.length > 0 &&
        b.code.length > 0
    ) {
        const hashA = sha256Hex(a.code);
        const hashB = sha256Hex(b.code);

        console.log(
            `Destination code hash: ${hashA}`
        );

        console.log(
            `Known LP wallet hash:  ${hashB}`
        );

        console.log('');

        if (hashA === hashB) {
            console.log(
                'RESULT: CODE HASHES MATCH'
            );
        } else {
            console.log(
                'RESULT: CODE HASHES DIFFER'
            );
        }
    } else {
        console.log(
            'Could not compare code hashes.'
        );
    }

    console.log('');
    console.log('================================================================');
    console.log(' COMPLETE');
    console.log(' NO TRANSACTION SENT');
    console.log('================================================================');
}

export async function run(
    provider: NetworkProvider
) {
    await main();
}

FILE: scripts\compareLpMathV22.ts
====================================================================================================
import { Address } from '@ton/core';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

function integerSqrt(value: bigint): bigint {
    if (value < 0n) {
        throw new Error('Square root of negative number');
    }

    if (value < 2n) {
        return value;
    }

    let x0 = value;
    let x1 = (x0 + 1n) >> 1n;

    while (x1 < x0) {
        x0 = x1;
        x1 = (x0 + value / x0) >> 1n;
    }

    return x0;
}

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {
    // =================================================
    // REAL v2.2 POOL
    // =================================================

    const poolAddress = Address.parse(
        'EQD5ALaCuEqJ7Mi9qlXOA7BHMrTq8e2fZ5B_teWN17MZq-cf'
    );

    // =================================================
    // CURRENT REAL POOL STATE
    // Obtained previously with get_pool_data
    // =================================================

    const reserveRooster = 600000000000000n;
    const reservePton = 1499448684n;
    const totalLp = 948058954033n;

    // =================================================
    // CURRENT BOC INPUT
    // =================================================

    const ptonAmount = 500000000n; // 0.5 TON

    // For a balanced proportional deposit, calculate
    // the ROOSTER amount corresponding to the pTON input.

    const roosterAmount =
        (ptonAmount * reserveRooster) / reservePton;

    // =================================================
    // METHOD 1
    // PROPORTIONAL LP FROM ROOSTER
    // =================================================

    const lpFromRooster =
        (roosterAmount * totalLp) / reserveRooster;

    // =================================================
    // METHOD 2
    // PROPORTIONAL LP FROM pTON
    // =================================================

    const lpFromPton =
        (ptonAmount * totalLp) / reservePton;

    // =================================================
    // INTEGER RESULT
    // =================================================

    const expectedLp =
        lpFromRooster < lpFromPton
            ? lpFromRooster
            : lpFromPton;

    // =================================================
    // BOC minLpOut
    // =================================================

    const minLpOut = 300329052918n;

    // =================================================
    // EXPECTED 95% MINIMUM
    // =================================================

    const expectedMinLpOut =
        (expectedLp * 95n) / 100n;

    // =================================================
    // DIFFERENCE
    // =================================================

    const minDifference =
        expectedMinLpOut - minLpOut;

    const lpDifference =
        lpFromRooster > lpFromPton
            ? lpFromRooster - lpFromPton
            : lpFromPton - lpFromRooster;

    // =================================================
    // OLD INITIAL-POOL FORMULA
    // Shown only for comparison.
    // NOT used for current pool.
    // =================================================

    const product =
        roosterAmount * ptonAmount;

    const sqrtProduct =
        integerSqrt(product);

    const oldInitialLp =
        sqrtProduct > 1001n
            ? sqrtProduct - 1001n
            : 0n;

    // =================================================
    // OUTPUT
    // =================================================

    console.log('');
    console.log('================================================');
    console.log('       ROOSTER / pTON V2.2 LP MATH');
    console.log('       REAL POOL / CURRENT BOC INPUT');
    console.log('================================================');
    console.log('');

    console.log('POOL');
    console.log('-----------------------------------------------');
    console.log('Pool:');
    console.log(poolAddress.toString());
    console.log('');

    console.log('CURRENT POOL');
    console.log('-----------------------------------------------');

    console.log('ROOSTER reserve:');
    console.log(reserveRooster.toString());
    console.log(
        'ROOSTER:',
        Number(reserveRooster) / 1e9
    );
    console.log('');

    console.log('pTON reserve:');
    console.log(reservePton.toString());
    console.log(
        'pTON:',
        Number(reservePton) / 1e9
    );
    console.log('');

    console.log('Total LP:');
    console.log(totalLp.toString());
    console.log('');

    console.log('BOC INPUT');
    console.log('-----------------------------------------------');

    console.log('pTON amount:');
    console.log(ptonAmount.toString());
    console.log(
        'pTON:',
        Number(ptonAmount) / 1e9
    );
    console.log('');

    console.log('PROPORTIONAL ROOSTER');
    console.log('-----------------------------------------------');

    console.log('Calculated ROOSTER amount:');
    console.log(roosterAmount.toString());
    console.log(
        'ROOSTER:',
        Number(roosterAmount) / 1e9
    );
    console.log('');

    console.log('LP CALCULATION');
    console.log('-----------------------------------------------');

    console.log('LP from ROOSTER:');
    console.log(lpFromRooster.toString());
    console.log('');

    console.log('LP from pTON:');
    console.log(lpFromPton.toString());
    console.log('');

    console.log('Difference between sides:');
    console.log(lpDifference.toString());
    console.log('');

    console.log('Expected LP:');
    console.log(expectedLp.toString());
    console.log('');

    console.log('BOC minLpOut:');
    console.log(minLpOut.toString());
    console.log('');

    console.log('Expected 95% minLpOut:');
    console.log(expectedMinLpOut.toString());
    console.log('');

    console.log('Difference: expected 95% - BOC minLpOut');
    console.log(minDifference.toString());
    console.log('');

    console.log('OLD INITIAL FORMULA');
    console.log('-----------------------------------------------');

    console.log('sqrt(product):');
    console.log(sqrtProduct.toString());
    console.log('');

    console.log('sqrt(product) - 1001:');
    console.log(oldInitialLp.toString());
    console.log('');

    console.log('FINAL CHECK');
    console.log('-----------------------------------------------');

    console.log(
        'Pool address:',
        poolAddress.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'Expected LP:',
        expectedLp.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'BOC minLpOut:',
        minLpOut.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'Expected 95%:',
        expectedMinLpOut.toString()
    );

    console.log('');

    if (lpDifference === 0n) {
        console.log(
            'OK   ROOSTER/pTON proportional LP values match'
        );
    } else {
        console.log(
            'INFO LP sides differ by:',
            lpDifference.toString()
        );
    }

    if (minLpOut === expectedMinLpOut) {
        console.log(
            'OK   BOC minLpOut matches 95% of expected LP'
        );
    } else {
        console.log(
            'WARNING BOC minLpOut differs from calculated 95%'
        );
    }

    console.log('');

    console.log('READ-ONLY');
    console.log('NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT');
    console.log('');

    console.log('================================================');
    console.log('                 DRY RUN ONLY');
    console.log('================================================');
    console.log('');
}

import { Address, beginCell } from '@ton/core';

const ENDPOINT =
    'https://sandbox-v4.tonhubapi.com';

const ROUTER =
    'EQBQ_UBQvR9ryUjKDwijtoiyyga2Wl-yJm6Y8gl0k-HDh_5x';

const ROOSTER_MASTER =
    'EQCyuv-cYXFibA05bhiL3seM3Ur2B_bpsc364Zb8F3NMtrYx';

    'EQBnGWMCf3-FZZq1W4IWcWiGAc3PHuZ0_H-7sad2oY00o83S';

const ROOSTER_ROUTER_WALLET =
    'EQA-oZKiTGR0Aes0yV7uck9-OLrLRJCwRMdwDnUgLU5EJYu0';

const CALCULATED_POOL =

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {
    console.log('');

    console.log(' STON.FI get_pool_address INPUT COMPARISON');
    console.log(' READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT');

    const latest =

    console.log('');

    console.log('');
    console.log('KNOWN ACTIVE POOL:');

    console.log('');
    console.log('PREVIOUS CALCULATED POOL:');
    console.log(CALCULATED_POOL);

    const router = Address.parse(ROUTER);

    const tests = [
        {
            name: '1. ROOSTER Router Wallet -> pTON Router Wallet',
            token0: ROOSTER_ROUTER_WALLET,
            token1: PTON_ROUTER_WALLET,
        },
        {
            name: '2. pTON Router Wallet -> ROOSTER Router Wallet',
            token0: PTON_ROUTER_WALLET,
            token1: ROOSTER_ROUTER_WALLET,
        },
        {
            name: '3. ROOSTER Master -> pTON Master',
            token0: ROOSTER_MASTER,
            token1: PTON_MASTER,
        },
        {
            name: '4. pTON Master -> ROOSTER Master',

    ];

    for (const test of tests) {

        console.log('============================================================');

        console.log(`TOKEN0: ${test.token0}`);
        console.log(`TOKEN1: ${test.token1}`);

        try {
            const token0 = Address.parse(test.token0);
            const token1 = Address.parse(test.token1);

            const result =
                await client.runMethod(
                    latest.last.seqno,
                    router,
                    'get_pool_address',
                    [
                        {
                            type: 'slice',
                            cell: beginCell()
                                .storeAddress(token0)
                                .endCell(),
                        },
                        {
                            type: 'slice',
                            cell: beginCell()
                                .storeAddress(token1)
                                .endCell(),
                        },
                    ],
                );

            console.log('');
            console.log(`EXIT CODE: ${result.exitCode}`);

            if (result.exitCode === 0) {
                try {
                    const pool =
                        result.reader.readAddress();

                    console.log(
                        `POOL: ${pool.toString()}`,
                    );

                    console.log(
                        `RAW : ${pool.toRawString()}`,
                    );

                    console.log('');

                    if (
                        pool.toString() ===
                        KNOWN_POOL
                    ) {
                        console.log(
                            'MATCH: KNOWN ACTIVE POOL',
                        );
                    } else if (
                        pool.toString() ===
                        CALCULATED_POOL
                    ) {
                        console.log(
                            'MATCH: PREVIOUS CALCULATED POOL',
                        );
                    } else {
                        console.log(
                            'MATCH: DIFFERENT ADDRESS',
                        );
                    }
                } catch (e) {
                    console.log('');
                    console.log(
                        'ADDRESS DECODE FAILED:',
                    );

                    console.log(
                        e instanceof Error
                            ? e.message
                            : String(e),
                    );

                    console.log('');
                    console.log(
                        'RESULT RAW:',
                    );

                    console.log(
                        result.resultRaw,
                    );
                }
            } else {
                console.log(
                    'GET_POOL_ADDRESS FAILED',
                );

                console.log('');
                console.log('RESULT RAW:');
                console.log(result.resultRaw);
            }
        } catch (e) {
            console.log('');
            console.log('CALL ERROR:');

            console.log(
                e instanceof Error
                    ? e.message
                    : String(e),
            );
        }
    }

    console.log('');
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log(' FINISHED - READ ONLY');
    console.log('============================================================');
}

import { Address, fromNano, toNano } from '@ton/core';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

const PTON_MASTER = Address.parse(
    'EQCM3B12QK1e4yZSf8GtBRT0aLMNyEsBc_DhVfRRtOEffLez'
);

const USER_WALLET = Address.parse(
    '0QDxn5MLbFyV649-OBviQrMp5KqWjGHNVwFM6GTeaLl8N6R-'
);

const TEST_AMOUNTS = [
    '0.05',
    '0.07',
    '0.10',
    '0.15',
    '0.20',
];

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {

    console.log(' pTON V1 DEPLOY GAS COMPARISON');

    const ptonProvider = provider.provider(PTON_MASTER);

    console.log('');

        fromNano(pton.gasConstants.deployWallet),
        'TON'
    );

    console.log('');

            ptonProvider,

                tx.body.toBoc().toString('base64')

    console.log('================================================');

    console.log('================================================');

    console.log('storage_fee::minter        = 0.01 TON');
    console.log('storage_fee::wallet        = 0.01 TON');
    console.log('gas::wallet::internal_deploy = 0.01 TON');

    console.log('');

    console.log('MSG_VALUE > (FWD_FEE * 3) + 0.04 TON');

    console.log('');

    console.log('It is NOT proven to be the contract minimum.');

    console.log('');
    console.log('================================================');
    console.log('END - NO TRANSACTION SENT');
    console.log('================================================');
    console.log('');
}

import { Address } from '@ton/core';
import {
    LiteClient,
    LiteRoundRobinEngine,
    LiteSingleEngine,
} from 'ton-lite-client';
import { Functions } from 'ton-lite-client/dist/schema';

const ROUTER = Address.parse(
    'EQALh-JBBIKK7gr0o4AVf9JZnEsFndqO0qTCyT-D-yBsWval'
);

const METHOD_ID = 128085;

const engines = [
    new LiteSingleEngine({
        host: 'tcp://168.119.95.207:27787',
        publicKey: Buffer.from(
            'Y/QVf6G5VDiKTZOKitbFVm067WsuocTN8Vg036A4zGk=',
            'base64'
        ),
    }),
    new LiteSingleEngine({
        host: 'tcp://168.119.82.221:51088',
        publicKey: Buffer.from(
            'Sy5ghr3EahQd/1rDayzZXt5+inlfF+7kLfkZDJcU/ek=',
            'base64'
        ),
    }),
];

async function main() {
    console.log('ROUTER LIBRARY CONTEXT TEST');
    console.log('READ ONLY');
    console.log('');

    const client = new LiteClient({
        engine: new LiteRoundRobinEngine(engines),
        batchSize: 1,
    });

    const master = await client.getMasterchainInfo();

    console.log('Masterchain seqno:', master.last.seqno);

    for (const mode of [4, 20]) {
        const result = await client.engine.query(
            Functions.liteServer_runSmcMethod,
            {
                kind: 'liteServer.runSmcMethod',
                mode,
                id: master.last,
                account: {
                    kind: 'liteServer.accountId',
                    workchain: ROUTER.workChain,
                    id: ROUTER.hash,
                },
                methodId: METHOD_ID.toString(),
                params: Buffer.alloc(0),
            }
        );

        console.log('');
        console.log('MODE:', mode);
        console.log('Exit code:', result.exitCode);
        console.log('Result bytes:', result.result?.length ?? 0);
        console.log('libExtras bytes:', result.libExtras?.length ?? 0);
    }

    await client.close();
}

main().catch(console.error);

import { Address } from '@ton/core';
import {
    LiteClient,
    LiteRoundRobinEngine,
    LiteSingleEngine,
} from 'ton-lite-client';

const ROUTER = Address.parse(
    'EQALh-JBBIKK7gr0o4AVf9JZnEsFndqO0qTCyT-D-yBsWval'
);

const ROOSTER_MASTER = Address.parse(
    'EQCyuv-cYXFibA05bhiL3seM3Ur2B_bpsc364Zb8F3NMtrYx'
);

async function main() {
    console.log('========================================');
    console.log(' ROUTER vs JETTON GETTER TEST');
    console.log(' READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION');
    console.log('========================================');

    const engines = [
        new LiteSingleEngine({
            host: 'tcp://168.119.95.207:27787',
            publicKey: Buffer.from(
                'Y/QVf6G5VDiKTZOKitbFVm067WsuocTN8Vg036A4zGk=',
                'base64'
            ),
        }),

        new LiteSingleEngine({
            host: 'tcp://168.119.82.221:51088',
            publicKey: Buffer.from(
                'Sy5ghr3EahQd/1rDayzZXt5+inlfF+7kLfkZDJcU/ek=',
                'base64'
            ),
        }),
    ];

        engine: new LiteRoundRobinEngine(engines),

            ROOSTER_MASTER,
            'get_jetton_data',
            Buffer.alloc(0),

        console.log(
            'exit code:',
            jettonResult.exitCode
        );

        if (jettonResult.result) {
            console.log(
                'result length:',
                jettonResult.result.length
            );

            console.log(
                'result BOC:',
                jettonResult.result
            );
        }
    } catch (error) {
        console.error('\nJETTON ERROR');
        console.error(error);
    }

    // ============================================================
    // TEST 2 — STON ROUTER
    // ============================================================

    console.log('\n========================================');
    console.log('TEST 2: STON ROUTER');
    console.log('========================================');

    console.log('address:', ROUTER.toString());
    console.log('getter: get_router_data');

        const routerResult = await client.runMethod(
            ROUTER,
            'get_router_data',
            Buffer.alloc(0),
            master.last
        );

        console.dir(routerResult, { depth: 5 });

        console.log(

            routerResult.exitCode
        );

        if (routerResult.result) {
            console.log(
                'result length:',
                routerResult.result.length
            );

            console.log(
                'result BOC:',
                routerResult.result
            );

FILE: scripts\compareSdkBodyV22.ts
====================================================================================================
import { Address, Cell, beginCell } from "@ton/core";
import { DEX } from "@ston-fi/sdk";
import * as fs from "fs";
import * as crypto from "crypto";

const ROOSTER_MASTER =
  "EQCyuv-cYXFibA05bhiL3seM3Ur2B_bpsc364Zb8F3NMtrYx";

const PTON_MASTER =
  "EQBnGWMCf3-FZZq1W4IWcWiGAc3PHuZ0_H-7sad2oY00o83S";

const ROUTER =
  "EQCS4UEa5UaJLzOyyKieqQOQ2P9M-7kXpkO5HnP3Bv250cN3";

const USER =
  "EQDxn5MLbFyV649-OBviQrMp5KqWjGHNVwFM6GTeaLl8N0Ix";

const EXPECTED_ROOSTER_WALLET =
  "EQBzQOOJu9qQVli2R0o6p3pXvi_wj9O2ncMhv3UyAjvMiMOh";

const ROOSTER_AMOUNT = 200073535827652n;
const MIN_LP_OUT = 300329052918n;

const GAS_AMOUNT = 300000000n;
const FORWARD_GAS_AMOUNT = 240000000n;

const BOC_FILE =
  "./final_v22_rooster_liquidity.boc";

function sha256(buffer: Buffer): string {
  return crypto.createHash("sha256").update(buffer).digest("hex");
}

export async function run(provider: any) {
  console.log("==============================================");
  console.log(" STON.fi v2.2 SDK BODY EXACT COMPARISON");
  console.log(" READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT");
  console.log("==============================================");
  console.log();

  // --------------------------------------------------
  // 1. Load prepared BOC
  // --------------------------------------------------

  console.log("===== 1. PREPARED BOC =====");

  if (!fs.existsSync(BOC_FILE)) {
    throw new Error(`BOC file not found: ${BOC_FILE}`);
  }

  const base64 = fs.readFileSync(BOC_FILE, "utf8").trim();

  console.log("File:", BOC_FILE);
  console.log("Base64 chars:", base64.length);

  const preparedBytes = Buffer.from(base64, "base64");

  console.log("BOC bytes:", preparedBytes.length);
  console.log("SHA-256:", sha256(preparedBytes));

  const decoded = Cell.fromBoc(preparedBytes);

  if (decoded.length !== 1) {
    throw new Error(`Expected exactly 1 root cell, got ${decoded.length}`);
  }

  const preparedBody = decoded[0];

  console.log("Prepared root cell: PASS");
  console.log();

  // --------------------------------------------------
  // 2. Create SDK router
  // --------------------------------------------------

  console.log("===== 2. SDK ROUTER =====");

  const routerAddress = Address.parse(ROUTER);
  const userAddress = Address.parse(USER);
  const roosterMaster = Address.parse(ROOSTER_MASTER);
  const ptonMaster = Address.parse(PTON_MASTER);

  const routerContract =
    DEX.v2_2.Router.CPI.create(routerAddress);

  console.log("Router class: PASS");
  console.log("Router:", routerAddress.toString());
  console.log();

  // --------------------------------------------------
  // 3. Derive user ROOSTER wallet independently
  // --------------------------------------------------

  console.log("===== 3. USER ROOSTER JETTON WALLET =====");

  const roosterWalletResult =
    await provider.provider(roosterMaster).get(
      "get_wallet_address",
      [
        {
          type: "slice",
          cell: beginCell()
  .storeAddress(userAddress)
  .endCell(),
        },
      ],
    );

  const roosterWallet =
    roosterWalletResult.stack.readAddress();

  console.log("Derived:", roosterWallet.toString());
  console.log(
    "Expected:",
    EXPECTED_ROOSTER_WALLET,
  );

  const walletMatch =
    roosterWallet.toString() === EXPECTED_ROOSTER_WALLET;

  console.log(
    "Wallet match:",
    walletMatch ? "PASS" : "FAIL",
  );

  if (!walletMatch) {
    throw new Error("User ROOSTER Jetton Wallet mismatch");
  }

  console.log();

  // --------------------------------------------------
  // 4. Ask the real SDK to build transaction params
  // --------------------------------------------------

  console.log("===== 4. SDK METHOD =====");

  const params =
    await routerContract.getProvideLiquidityJettonTxParams(
      provider.provider(routerAddress),
      {
        userWalletAddress: userAddress,
        sendTokenAddress: roosterMaster,
        otherTokenAddress: ptonMaster,
        sendAmount: ROOSTER_AMOUNT,
        minLpOut: MIN_LP_OUT,
        gasAmount: GAS_AMOUNT,
        forwardGasAmount: FORWARD_GAS_AMOUNT,
        transferExcessAddress: userAddress,
      },
    );

  console.log("SDK method: PASS");
  console.log();

  // --------------------------------------------------
  // 5. Transaction-level comparison
  // --------------------------------------------------

  console.log("===== 5. SDK TX PARAMETERS =====");

  console.log("SDK to:", params.to.toString());
  console.log("Expected to:", EXPECTED_ROOSTER_WALLET);

  const toMatch =
    params.to.toString() === EXPECTED_ROOSTER_WALLET;

  console.log(
    "TX destination:",
    toMatch ? "PASS" : "FAIL",
  );

  console.log("SDK value:", params.value.toString());
  console.log("Expected value:", GAS_AMOUNT.toString());

  const valueMatch =
    BigInt(params.value) === GAS_AMOUNT;

  console.log(
    "TX gas:",
    valueMatch ? "PASS" : "FAIL",
  );

  if (!toMatch || !valueMatch) {
    throw new Error(
      "SDK transaction parameter mismatch",
    );
  }

  console.log();

  // --------------------------------------------------
  // 6. Extract exact SDK body
  // --------------------------------------------------

  console.log("===== 6. SDK BODY =====");

  const sdkBody = params.body;

if (!sdkBody) {
  throw new Error("SDK returned empty transaction body");
}

const sdkBodyBytes = sdkBody.toBoc({
  idx: false,
});

  const sdkBodyBase64 =
    sdkBodyBytes.toString("base64");

  console.log("SDK body bytes:", sdkBodyBytes.length);
  console.log("SDK body Base64 chars:", sdkBodyBase64.length);
  console.log("SDK body SHA-256:", sha256(sdkBodyBytes));

  console.log();

  // --------------------------------------------------
  // 7. Exact byte comparison
  // --------------------------------------------------

  console.log("===== 7. EXACT BODY COMPARISON =====");

  const preparedBodyBytes =
    preparedBody.toBoc({
      idx: false,
    });

  console.log(
    "Prepared body bytes:",
    preparedBodyBytes.length,
  );

  console.log(
    "Prepared body SHA-256:",
    sha256(preparedBodyBytes),
  );

  const byteExact =
    sdkBodyBytes.equals(preparedBodyBytes);

  console.log(
    "BYTE-EXACT MATCH:",
    byteExact ? "PASS" : "FAIL",
  );

  console.log();

  // --------------------------------------------------
  // 8. Base64 comparison
  // --------------------------------------------------

  console.log("===== 8. BASE64 COMPARISON =====");

  const base64Exact =
    sdkBodyBase64 === base64;

  console.log(
    "BASE64-EXACT MATCH:",
    base64Exact ? "PASS" : "FAIL",
  );

  console.log();

  // --------------------------------------------------
  // 9. Final result
  // --------------------------------------------------

  console.log("==============================================");

  if (byteExact && base64Exact) {
    console.log(" FINAL STATUS: PASS");
    console.log();
    console.log(
      "The prepared BOC body is byte-for-byte identical",
    );
    console.log(
      "to the body generated by the installed",
    );
    console.log(
      "STON.fi v2.2 SDK.",
    );
    console.log();
    console.log(
      "SDK TX destination: USER ROOSTER JETTON WALLET",
    );
    console.log(
      "SDK gas value: 0.3 TON",
    );
    console.log();
    console.log("NO TRANSACTION WAS SENT.");
  } else {
    console.log(" FINAL STATUS: FAIL");
    console.log();

    if (!byteExact) {
      console.log(
        "Reason: SDK body and prepared BOC differ.",
      );
    }

    if (!base64Exact) {
      console.log(
        "Reason: Base64 representations differ.",
      );
    }

    console.log();
    console.log(
      "NO TRANSACTION WAS SENT.",
    );
  }

  console.log("==============================================");
}

import { Address, beginCell, Cell } from "@ton/core";
import { readFileSync } from "fs";

const PTON_WALLET = Address.parse(
  "EQCSIMGBps_qzRG3uPYhON8bucyCtu0mYdL1-u4gSz77IBa3"
);

const REFUND = Address.parse(
  "EQDxn5MLbFyV649-OBviQrMp5KqWjGHNVwFM6GTeaLl8N0Ix"
);

const MIN_LP_OUT = 150109265n;
const DEADLINE = 1790531314;
const OP_PROVIDE_LP = 0x37c096df;

const payload = beginCell()
  .storeUint(OP_PROVIDE_LP, 32)
  .storeAddress(PTON_WALLET)
  .storeAddress(REFUND)
  .storeAddress(REFUND)
  .storeUint(DEADLINE, 64)
  .storeRef(
    beginCell()
      .storeCoins(MIN_LP_OUT)
      .storeAddress(REFUND)
      .storeUint(1, 1)
      .storeCoins(0n)
      .storeMaybeRef(null)
      .endCell()
  )
  .endCell();

const path =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\scripts\\v22_provide_lp_payload.boc";

const existing = Cell.fromBoc(readFileSync(path))[0];

const payloadBoc = payload.toBoc();
const existingBoc = existing.toBoc();

console.log("========================================");
console.log(" PROVIDE_LP PAYLOAD COMPARISON");
console.log("========================================");
console.log("NO TRANSACTION SENT.");
console.log("");

console.log("===== GENERATED PAYLOAD =====");
console.log("bits:", payload.bits.length);
console.log("refs:", payload.refs.length);
console.log("hash:", payload.hash().toString("hex"));
console.log("BOC:", payloadBoc.toString("base64"));
console.log("");

console.log("===== EXISTING BOC FILE =====");
console.log("bits:", existing.bits.length);
console.log("refs:", existing.refs.length);
console.log("hash:", existing.hash().toString("hex"));
console.log("BOC:", existingBoc.toString("base64"));
console.log("");

console.log("===== COMPARISON =====");

console.log(
  "Root hash match:",
  payload.hash().equals(existing.hash()) ? "YES" : "NO"
);

console.log(
  "BOC match:",
  payloadBoc.equals(existingBoc) ? "YES" : "NO"
);

console.log("");
console.log("NO TRANSACTION SENT.");

FILE: scripts\compareSelectorFunctions.ts
====================================================================================================
import fs from "fs";

const wasmPath = "emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";
const buf = fs.readFileSync(wasmPath);

const TARGETS = [122, 656, 91, 787];

function readULEB(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = buf[pos++];
        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return {
                value: value >>> 0,
                next: pos
            };
        }

        shift += 7;
    }
}

function hex(n: number) {
    return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function opName(op: number): string {
    switch (op) {
        case 0x10: return "call";
        case 0x11: return "call_indirect";
        case 0x20: return "local.get";
        case 0x21: return "local.set";
        case 0x22: return "local.tee";
        case 0x23: return "global.get";
        case 0x24: return "global.set";
        case 0x28: return "i32.load";
        case 0x29: return "i64.load";
        case 0x36: return "i32.store";
        case 0x37: return "i64.store";
        case 0x3a: return "i32.store8";
        case 0x3b: return "i32.store16";
        case 0x41: return "i32.const";
        case 0x42: return "i64.const";
        case 0x45: return "i32.eqz";
        case 0x46: return "i32.eq";
        case 0x47: return "i32.ne";
        case 0x48: return "i32.lt_s";
        case 0x49: return "i32.lt_u";
        case 0x4a: return "i32.gt_s";
        case 0x4b: return "i32.gt_u";
        case 0x4e: return "i32.ge_s";
        case 0x4f: return "i32.ge_u";
        case 0x6a: return "i32.add";
        case 0x6b: return "i32.sub";
        case 0x71: return "i32.and";
        case 0x72: return "i32.or";
        case 0x73: return "i32.xor";
        case 0x74: return "i32.shl";
        case 0x75: return "i32.shr_s";
        case 0x76: return "i32.shr_u";
        case 0x7c: return "i32.rotl";
        case 0x0b: return "end";
        default: return `opcode 0x${op.toString(16)}`;
    }
}

//
// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Locate sections
// ------------------------------------------------------------
//

const sections: {
    id: number;
    start: number;
    end: number;
}[] = [];

let p = 8;

while (p < buf.length) {

    const id = buf[p++];

    const s = readULEB(p);
    const size = s.value;
    p = s.next;

    sections.push({
        id,
        start: p,
        end: p + size
    });

    p += size;
}

function getSection(id: number) {
    return sections.find(x => x.id === id);
}

//
// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Function section
// ------------------------------------------------------------
//

const funcSec = getSection(3);

if (!funcSec)
    throw new Error("FUNCTION SECTION NOT FOUND");

let f = funcSec.start;

const fc = readULEB(f);
f = fc.next;

const functionTypes: number[] = [];

for (let i = 0; i < fc.value; i++) {

    const r = readULEB(f);

    functionTypes.push(r.value);

    f = r.next;
}

//
// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Type section
// ------------------------------------------------------------
//

const typeSec = getSection(1);

if (!typeSec)
    throw new Error("TYPE SECTION NOT FOUND");

let t = typeSec.start;

const tc = readULEB(t);
t = tc.next;

const types: {
    params: number[];
    results: number[];
}[] = [];

for (let i = 0; i < tc.value; i++) {

    const form = buf[t++];

    if (form !== 0x60)
        throw new Error("Unexpected type form");

    const pc = readULEB(t);
    t = pc.next;

    const params: number[] = [];

    for (let j = 0; j < pc.value; j++)
        params.push(buf[t++]);

    const rc = readULEB(t);
    t = rc.next;

    const results: number[] = [];

    for (let j = 0; j < rc.value; j++)
        results.push(buf[t++]);

    types.push({
        params,
        results
    });
}

function vt(v: number): string {

    switch (v) {
        case 0x7f: return "i32";
        case 0x7e: return "i64";
        case 0x7d: return "f32";
        case 0x7c: return "f64";
        default: return hex(v);
    }
}

//
// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Code section
// ------------------------------------------------------------
//

const codeSec = getSection(10);

if (!codeSec)
    throw new Error("CODE SECTION NOT FOUND");

let c = codeSec.start;

const cc = readULEB(c);
const codeCount = cc.value;
c = cc.next;

const bodies = new Map<
    number,
    {
        start: number;
        end: number;
        instrStart: number;
    }
>();

for (let ordinal = 0; ordinal < codeCount; ordinal++) {

    const bs = readULEB(c);
    const size = bs.value;
    c = bs.next;

    const start = c;
    const end = c + size;

    let x = start;

    const groups = readULEB(x);
    x = groups.next;

    for (let i = 0; i < groups.value; i++) {
        const n = readULEB(x);
        x = n.next;
        x++;
    }

    bodies.set(ordinal, {
        start,
        end,
        instrStart: x
    });

    c = end;
}

//
// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Analyze target functions
// ------------------------------------------------------------
//

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("SELECTOR FUNCTION COMPARISON");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

for (const globalFunc of TARGETS) {

    //
    // 83 imported functions
    //
    if (globalFunc < 83) {
        console.log(`FUNC ${globalFunc} = IMPORTED`);
        continue;
    }

    const ordinal = globalFunc - 83;

    const body = bodies.get(ordinal);

    if (!body) {
        console.log(`FUNC ${globalFunc} = BODY NOT FOUND`);
        continue;
    }

    const typeIndex = functionTypes[ordinal];
    const ft = types[typeIndex];

    console.log("");
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(`GLOBAL FUNC ${globalFunc}`);
    console.log(`CODE ORDINAL = ${ordinal}`);
    console.log(`TYPE INDEX   = ${typeIndex}`);
    console.log(
        `SIGNATURE    = (${ft.params.map(vt).join(",")}) -> (${ft.results.map(vt).join(",")})`
    );
    console.log(`BODY         = ${hex(body.start)} .. ${hex(body.end)}`);
    console.log(`INSTRUCTIONS = ${hex(body.instrStart)}`);
    console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

    let x = body.instrStart;

    let instructionCount = 0;
    let callCount = 0;
    let indirectCount = 0;
    let loadCount = 0;
    let storeCount = 0;
    let globalCount = 0;
    let target1953Count = 0;

    const calls: number[] = [];
    const indirectTypes: number[] = [];

    while (x < body.end) {

        const pos = x;
        const op = buf[x++];

        instructionCount++;

        if (op === 0x0b) {
            break;
        }

        if (op === 0x10) {

            const r = readULEB(x);
            x = r.next;

            callCount++;
            calls.push(r.value);

            continue;
        }

        if (op === 0x11) {

            const r = readULEB(x);
            x = r.next;

            //
            // table index
            //
            const table = readULEB(x);
            x = table.next;

            indirectCount++;
            indirectTypes.push(r.value);

            continue;
        }

        if (op === 0x20 || op === 0x21 || op === 0x22) {

            const r = readULEB(x);
            x = r.next;

            continue;
        }

        if (op === 0x23 || op === 0x24) {

            const r = readULEB(x);
            x = r.next;

            globalCount++;

            continue;
        }

        if (
            op === 0x28 ||
            op === 0x29
        ) {

            const a = readULEB(x);
            x = a.next;

            const o = readULEB(x);
            x = o.next;

            loadCount++;

            continue;
        }

        if (
            op === 0x36 ||
            op === 0x37 ||
            op === 0x3a ||
            op === 0x3b
        ) {

            const a = readULEB(x);
            x = a.next;

            const o = readULEB(x);
            x = o.next;

            storeCount++;

            continue;
        }

        if (op === 0x41) {

            const r = readULEB(x);
            x = r.next;

            //
            // Absolute selector block addresses.
            //
            if (
                r.value === 0x195348 ||
                r.value === 0x19534c ||
                r.value === 0x195350 ||
                r.value === 0x195354 ||
                r.value === 0x195358 ||
                r.value === 0x19535c
            ) {
                target1953Count++;

                console.log(
                    `  TARGET ADDRESS @ ${hex(pos)} = ${hex(r.value)}`
                );
            }

            continue;
        }

        if (op === 0x42) {

            const r = readULEB(x);
            x = r.next;

            continue;
        }

        //
        // Structured control instructions
        //
        if (
            op === 0x02 ||
            op === 0x03 ||
            op === 0x04
        ) {
            x++;
            continue;
        }

        if (
            op === 0x0c ||
            op === 0x0d
        ) {
            const r = readULEB(x);
            x = r.next;
            continue;
        }

        //
        // Memory.size / grow
        //
        if (op === 0x3f || op === 0x40) {
            x++;
            continue;
        }
    }

    console.log(`INSTRUCTION COUNT = ${instructionCount}`);
    console.log(`LOADS             = ${loadCount}`);
    console.log(`STORES            = ${storeCount}`);
    console.log(`DIRECT CALLS      = ${callCount}`);
    console.log(`CALL_INDIRECT     = ${indirectCount}`);
    console.log(`GLOBAL ACCESS     = ${globalCount}`);
    console.log(`TARGET 0x1953xx   = ${target1953Count}`);

    if (calls.length) {
        console.log(
            `CALL TARGETS      = ${calls.join(", ")}`
        );
    }

    if (indirectTypes.length) {
        console.log(
            `INDIRECT TYPES    = ${indirectTypes.join(", ")}`
        );
    }
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("END");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("NO WASM EXECUTION");
console.log("============================================================");

====================================================================================================

import { Address, TonClient } from '@ton/ton';

export async function run() {
const client = new TonClient({
endpoint: 'https://testnet.toncenter.com/api/v2/jsonRPC',
});

const routerAddress = Address.parse(
    'kQALh-JBBIKK7gr0o4AVf9JZnEsFndqO0qTCyT-D-yBsWk0v'
);

console.log('');
console.log('===== STON ROUTER CODE CHECK =====');
console.log('Router:', routerAddress.toString());
console.log('');

const state = await client.getContractState(routerAddress);

if (state.state !== 'active' || !state.code) {
    console.log('Router is not active or code is unavailable.');
    return;
}

const code = state.code.toString('hex');

console.log('Code bytes:', state.code.length);
console.log('');
console.log('Code HEX:');
console.log(code);
console.log('');

console.log('Known compact-code prefix check:');

if (code.startsWith('b5ee9c72')) {
    console.log('Valid TON BOC header detected.');
} else {
    console.log('Unexpected BOC header.');
}

console.log('');
console.log('Testing standard Router getter:');

try {
    const result = await client.runMethod(
        routerAddress,
        'get_router_version',
        []
    );

    console.log('GETTER SUCCESS');
    console.log(result.stack);
} catch (error) {
    console.log('GETTER FAILED');
    console.log(String(error));
}

console.log('');
console.log('NO TRANSACTION WAS SENT.');
console.log('=================================');

}
