import { Address, toNano } from '@ton/core';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';
import { pTON } from '@ston-fi/sdk';

const PTON_MASTER = Address.parse(
    'kQACS30DNoUQ7NfApPvzh7eBmSZ9L4ygJ-lkNWtba8TQT-Px'
);

const USER_WALLET = Address.parse(
    '0QDxn5MLbFyV649-OBviQrMp5KqWjGHNVwFM6GTeaLl8N6R-'
);

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {
    console.log('');
    console.log('==========================================');
    console.log(' ROOSTER COIN - TON TO pTON');
    console.log('==========================================');
    console.log('');

    const pton = pTON.v2_1.create(
        PTON_MASTER
    );

    console.log('pTON v2.1 Master:');
    console.log(pton.address.toString());
    console.log('');

    console.log('User wallet:');
    console.log(USER_WALLET.toString());
    console.log('');

    console.log('Amount to convert:');
    console.log('0.1 TON');
    console.log('');

    console.log('SDK gas amount:');
    console.log('0.01 TON');
    console.log('');

    console.log('Total transaction value:');
    console.log('0.11 TON');
    console.log('');

    console.log('Sending transaction to Tonkeeper...');
    console.log('Please confirm ONLY if the transaction details are correct.');
    console.log('');

    const contractProvider = provider.provider(pton.address);

    await pton.sendTonTransfer(
        contractProvider,
        provider.sender(),
        {
            tonAmount: toNano('0.1'),
            destinationAddress: USER_WALLET,
            refundAddress: USER_WALLET,
        }
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log('TON -> pTON transaction submitted.');
    console.log('Wait for the transaction to be confirmed before checking balance.');
    console.log('');
}

import { Address, toNano } from '@ton/core';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';
import { DEX } from '@ston-fi/sdk';

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {
    const owner = provider.sender().address;

    if (!owner) {
        throw new Error('Tonkeeper wallet address not available');
    }

    const pton = DEX.v2_2.pTON.create(
        Address.parse(
            'EQBnGWMCf3-FZZq1W4IWcWiGAc3PHuZ0_H-7sad2oY00o83S'
        )
    );

    const destinationWallet = Address.parse(
        'EQCFe1J8epgixmnGvSWBsB6O7gEABl1BiY8NJkL2pjqv1I6S'
    );

    console.log('pTON Master:', pton.address.toString());
    console.log('pTON Wallet:', destinationWallet.toString());
    console.log('Amount:', '0.5 TON');
    console.log('Gas:', '0.01 TON');

    await pton.sendTonTransfer(
        provider.provider(pton.address),
        provider.sender(),
        {
            tonAmount: toNano('0.5'),
            destinationAddress: destinationWallet,
            destinationWalletAddress: destinationWallet,
            refundAddress: owner,
        }
    );

    console.log('0.5 TON -> pTON transaction sent.');
}


====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\crossCheckLiquidityV22.ts

import { Cell, Slice, Address } from '@ton/core';
import * as fs from 'fs';

const ROOSTER_BOC_FILE =
    'G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\rooster_v22_generated.boc';

const PTON_BOC_FILE =
    'G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\pton_v22_generated.boc';

const EXPECTED_ROUTER = Address.parse(
    'EQCS4UEa5UaJLzOyyKieqQOQ2P9M-7kXpkO5HnP3Bv250cN3'
);

const EXPECTED_USER = Address.parse(
    'EQDxn5MLbFyV649-OBviQrMp5KqWjGHNVwFM6GTeaLl8N0Ix'
);

const EXPECTED_ROUTER_ROOSTER_WALLET = Address.parse(
    'EQCePy3CW0qsWj03tvCwBxpavtYcu27LURfEy7H6tRhTbwsq'
);

const EXPECTED_ROUTER_PTON_WALLET = Address.parse(
    'EQCSIMGBps_qzRG3uPYhON8bucyCtu0mYdL1-u4gSz77IBa3'
);

const EXPECTED_USER_ROOSTER_WALLET = Address.parse(
    'EQBzQOOJu9qQVli2R0o6p3pXvi_wj9O2ncMhv3UyAjvMiMOh'
);

const JETTON_TRANSFER_OPCODE = 0x0f8a7ea5;
const TON_TRANSFER_OPCODE = 0x01f3835d;
const PROVIDE_LP_OPCODE = 0x37c096df;

function loadBocFile(file: string): Cell {
    let data = fs.readFileSync(file);

    if (data.subarray(0, 4).toString('hex') !== 'b5ee9c72') {
        const text = data.toString('utf8').trim();
        data = Buffer.from(text, 'base64');
    }

    if (data.subarray(0, 4).toString('hex') !== 'b5ee9c72') {
        throw new Error(`INVALID BOC: ${file}`);
    }

    const roots = Cell.fromBoc(data);

    if (roots.length !== 1) {
        throw new Error(
            `EXPECTED 1 ROOT CELL, GOT ${roots.length}: ${file}`
        );
    }

    return roots[0];
}

function readAddress(slice: Slice): Address {
    const address = slice.loadAddress();

    if (address === null) {
        throw new Error('Expected non-null address');
    }

    return address;
}

function assertEqual(
    name: string,
    actual: string | number | bigint | boolean,
    expected: string | number | bigint | boolean
) {
    if (actual !== expected) {
        throw new Error(
            `FAIL | ${name} | actual=${actual} | expected=${expected}`
        );
    }

    console.log(
        `PASS | ${name} | ${actual}`
    );
}

function assertAddress(
    name: string,
    actual: Address,
    expected: Address
) {
    if (!actual.equals(expected)) {
        throw new Error(
            `FAIL | ${name} | actual=${actual.toString()} | expected=${expected.toString()}`
        );
    }

    console.log(
        `PASS | ${name} | ${actual.toString()}`
    );
}

function decodeRooster(root: Cell) {
    const s = root.beginParse();

    const opcode = s.loadUint(32);

    assertEqual(
        'ROOSTER JETTON_TRANSFER opcode',
        opcode,
        JETTON_TRANSFER_OPCODE
    );

    const queryId = s.loadUintBig(64);
    const amount = s.loadCoins();

    const destination = readAddress(s);
    const responseDestination = readAddress(s);

    const hasCustomPayload = s.loadBit();

    if (hasCustomPayload) {
        s.loadRef();
    }

    const forwardTonAmount = s.loadCoins();
    const hasForwardPayload = s.loadBit();

    if (!hasForwardPayload) {
        throw new Error(
            'FAIL | ROOSTER forward payload missing'
        );
    }

    const forwardPayload = s.loadRef();

    const p = forwardPayload.beginParse();

    const provideLpOpcode = p.loadUint(32);

    assertEqual(
        'ROOSTER PROVIDE_LP opcode',
        provideLpOpcode,
        PROVIDE_LP_OPCODE
    );

    const routerPtonWallet =
        readAddress(p);

    const refundAddress =
        readAddress(p);

    const excessesAddress =
        readAddress(p);

    const deadline =
        p.loadUintBig(64);

    const inner =
        p.loadRef();

    const i =
        inner.beginParse();

    const minLpOut =
        i.loadCoins();

    const receiverAddress =
        readAddress(i);

    const bothPositive =
        i.loadBit();

    const customForwardGas =
        i.loadCoins();

    const hasDexCustomPayload =
        i.loadMaybeRef() !== null;

    return {
        queryId,
        amount,
        destination,
        responseDestination,
        hasCustomPayload,
        forwardTonAmount,
        routerPtonWallet,
        refundAddress,
        excessesAddress,
        deadline,
        minLpOut,
        receiverAddress,
        bothPositive,
        customForwardGas,
        hasDexCustomPayload,
    };
}

function decodePton(root: Cell) {
    const s = root.beginParse();

    const opcode = s.loadUint(32);

    assertEqual(
        'pTON TON_TRANSFER opcode',
        opcode,
        TON_TRANSFER_OPCODE
    );

    const queryId = s.loadUintBig(64);
    const tonAmount = s.loadCoins();

    const refundAddress =
        readAddress(s);

    const hasForwardPayload =
        s.loadBit();

    if (!hasForwardPayload) {
        throw new Error(
            'FAIL | pTON forward payload missing'
        );
    }

    const forwardPayload =
        s.loadRef();

    const p =
        forwardPayload.beginParse();

    const provideLpOpcode =
        p.loadUint(32);

    assertEqual(
        'pTON PROVIDE_LP opcode',
        provideLpOpcode,
        PROVIDE_LP_OPCODE
    );

    const routerRoosterWallet =
        readAddress(p);

    const lpRefundAddress =
        readAddress(p);

    const excessesAddress =
        readAddress(p);

    const deadline =
        p.loadUintBig(64);

    const inner =
        p.loadRef();

    const i =
        inner.beginParse();

    const minLpOut =
        i.loadCoins();

    const receiverAddress =
        readAddress(i);

    const bothPositive =
        i.loadBit();

    const customForwardGas =
        i.loadCoins();

    const hasDexCustomPayload =
        i.loadMaybeRef() !== null;

    return {
        queryId,
        tonAmount,
        refundAddress,
        routerRoosterWallet,
        lpRefundAddress,
        excessesAddress,
        deadline,
        minLpOut,
        receiverAddress,
        bothPositive,
        customForwardGas,
        hasDexCustomPayload,
    };
}

export async function run() {
    console.log(
        '======================================================================'
    );
    console.log(
        ' STON.FI v2.2 LIQUIDITY BOC CROSS-CHECK'
    );
    console.log(
        ' READ ONLY - TWO BOC FILES - NO RPC - NO TRANSACTION'
    );
    console.log(
        '======================================================================'
    );

    console.log('\n===== 1. LOAD BOC FILES =====');

    console.log(
        'ROOSTER BOC:',
        ROOSTER_BOC_FILE
    );

    console.log(
        'pTON BOC:',
        PTON_BOC_FILE
    );

    const roosterRoot =
        loadBocFile(ROOSTER_BOC_FILE);

    const ptonRoot =
        loadBocFile(PTON_BOC_FILE);

    console.log(
        'ROOSTER ROOT: PASS'
    );

    console.log(
        'pTON ROOT: PASS'
    );

    console.log('\n===== 2. DECODE BOTH BOCS =====');

    const rooster =
        decodeRooster(roosterRoot);

    const pton =
        decodePton(ptonRoot);

    console.log(
        'ROOSTER DECODE: PASS'
    );

    console.log(
        'pTON DECODE: PASS'
    );

    console.log('\n===== 3. ROOSTER INTERNAL CHECKS =====');

    assertAddress(
        'ROOSTER destination = USER ROOSTER wallet',
        rooster.destination,
        EXPECTED_ROUTER
    );

    assertAddress(
        'ROOSTER responseDestination = USER',
        rooster.responseDestination,
        EXPECTED_USER
    );

    assertAddress(
        'ROOSTER router pTON wallet',
        rooster.routerPtonWallet,
        EXPECTED_ROUTER_PTON_WALLET
    );

    assertAddress(
        'ROOSTER refundAddress = USER',
        rooster.refundAddress,
        EXPECTED_USER
    );

    assertAddress(
        'ROOSTER excessesAddress = USER',
        rooster.excessesAddress,
        EXPECTED_USER
    );

    assertAddress(
        'ROOSTER receiverAddress = USER',
        rooster.receiverAddress,
        EXPECTED_USER
    );

    assertEqual(
        'ROOSTER bothPositive',
        rooster.bothPositive,
        true
    );

    assertEqual(
        'ROOSTER custom forward gas',
        rooster.customForwardGas,
        0n
    );

    assertEqual(
        'ROOSTER no DEX custom payload',
        rooster.hasDexCustomPayload,
        false
    );

    console.log(
        '\n===== 4. pTON INTERNAL CHECKS ====='
    );

    assertAddress(
        'pTON refundAddress = USER',
        pton.refundAddress,
        EXPECTED_USER
    );

    assertAddress(
        'pTON router ROOSTER wallet',
        pton.routerRoosterWallet,
        EXPECTED_ROUTER_ROOSTER_WALLET
    );

    assertAddress(
        'pTON LP refundAddress = USER',
        pton.lpRefundAddress,
        EXPECTED_USER
    );

    assertAddress(
        'pTON excessesAddress = USER',
        pton.excessesAddress,
        EXPECTED_USER
    );

    assertAddress(
        'pTON receiverAddress = USER',
        pton.receiverAddress,
        EXPECTED_USER
    );

    assertEqual(
        'pTON bothPositive',
        pton.bothPositive,
        true
    );

    assertEqual(
        'pTON custom forward gas',
        pton.customForwardGas,
        0n
    );

    assertEqual(
        'pTON no DEX custom payload',
        pton.hasDexCustomPayload,
        false
    );

    console.log(
        '\n===== 5. CROSS-CHECK COMMON PARAMETERS ====='
    );

    assertEqual(
        'deadline identical',
        rooster.deadline,
        pton.deadline
    );

    assertEqual(
        'minLpOut identical',
        rooster.minLpOut,
        pton.minLpOut
    );

    assertEqual(
        'bothPositive identical',
        rooster.bothPositive,
        pton.bothPositive
    );

    console.log(
        '\n===== 6. CROSS-CHECK OPPOSITE ROUTER WALLETS ====='
    );

    assertAddress(
        'ROOSTER -> Router pTON wallet',
        rooster.routerPtonWallet,
        EXPECTED_ROUTER_PTON_WALLET
    );

    assertAddress(
        'pTON -> Router ROOSTER wallet',
        pton.routerRoosterWallet,
        EXPECTED_ROUTER_ROOSTER_WALLET
    );

    console.log(
        '\n===== 7. CROSS-CHECK ROUTER CONSISTENCY ====='
    );

    assertAddress(
        'ROOSTER destination = expected Router',
        rooster.destination,
        EXPECTED_ROUTER
    );

    console.log(
        '\n===== 8. FINAL CROSS-CHECK SUMMARY ====='
    );

    console.log(
        'ROOSTER amount:',
        rooster.amount.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'pTON amount:',
        pton.tonAmount.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'Shared minLpOut:',
        rooster.minLpOut.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'Shared deadline:',
        rooster.deadline.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'ROOSTER -> Router pTON wallet:',
        rooster.routerPtonWallet.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'pTON -> Router ROOSTER wallet:',
        pton.routerRoosterWallet.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        '\n======================================================================'
    );

    console.log(
        ' ALL TWO-BOC CROSS-CHECKS PASSED'
    );

    console.log(
        '======================================================================'
    );

    console.log(
        'READ ONLY: NO RPC USED.'
    );

    console.log(
        'NO TRANSACTION WAS SENT.'
    );
}

FILE: scripts\debugAddress.ts

import { beginCell, contractAddress, toNano } from '@ton/core';
import { compile } from '@ton/blueprint';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {
    const adminAddress = provider.sender().address;

    if (!adminAddress) {
        throw new Error('Admin address not available');
    }

    const content = beginCell()
    .storeUint(2, 32)
    .endCell();

    const jettonWalletCode = await compile('JettonWallet');
    const minterCode = await compile('RoosterCoin');

    const data = beginCell()
        .storeCoins(0n)
        .storeAddress(adminAddress)
        .storeRef(content)
        .storeRef(jettonWalletCode)
        .storeBit(false)
        .endCell();

    const address = contractAddress(0, {
        code: minterCode,
        data,
    });

    console.log('Admin:', adminAddress.toString());
    console.log('RoosterCoin code hash:', minterCode.hash().toString('hex'));
    console.log('JettonWallet code hash:', jettonWalletCode.hash().toString('hex'));
    console.log('Data hash:', data.hash().toString('hex'));
    console.log('Calculated NEW address:', address.toString());
}

const LIBRARY_HASH =
    '5a34c333bcdcac70d41f9afca79aa6e782f1c80a5e4b2ef896ec5e20e9182c86';

async function main() {
    console.log('========================================');
    console.log(' DEBUG TON LITE getLibraries');
    console.log('========================================');
    console.log('NO WALLET');
    console.log('NO TRANSACTION');
    console.log('');

    const engine1 = new LiteSingleEngine({
        host: 'tcp://168.119.95.207:27787',
        publicKey: Buffer.from(
            'Y/QVf6G5VDiKTZOKitbFVm067WsuocTN8Vg036A4zGk=',
            'base64'
        ),
    });

    const engine2 = new LiteSingleEngine({
        host: 'tcp://168.119.82.221:51088',
        publicKey: Buffer.from(
            'Sy5ghr3EahQd/1rDayzZXt5+inlfF+7kLfkZDJcU/ek=',
            'base64'
        ),
    });

    const engine = new LiteRoundRobinEngine([
        engine1,
        engine2,
    ]);

    const client = new LiteClient({
        engine,
        batchSize: 1,
    });

    try {
        console.log('LIBRARY HASH');
        console.log(LIBRARY_HASH);
        console.log('');

        const hash = Buffer.from(LIBRARY_HASH, 'hex');

        console.log('BUFFER');
        console.log('Length:', hash.length);
        console.log('Hex:', hash.toString('hex'));
        console.log('');

        console.log('ENGINE STATUS');
        console.log('Engine 1 ready:', engine1.isReady());
        console.log('Engine 2 ready:', engine2.isReady());
        console.log('RoundRobin ready:', engine.isReady());
        console.log('');

        console.log('MASTERCHAIN');

        const master = await client.getMasterchainInfo();

        console.log('Seqno:', master.last.seqno);
        console.log(
            'Root hash:',
            Buffer.from(master.last.rootHash).toString('hex')
        );
        console.log('');

        console.log('ENGINE STATUS AFTER QUERY');
        console.log('Engine 1 ready:', engine1.isReady());
        console.log('Engine 2 ready:', engine2.isReady());
        console.log('RoundRobin ready:', engine.isReady());
        console.log('');

        console.log('CALLING getLibraries...');
        console.log('');

        const result = await client.getLibraries([hash]);

        console.log('RAW RESULT');
        console.dir(result, { depth: 10 });
        console.log('');

        console.log('RESULT TYPE');
        console.log(typeof result);
        console.log('');

        console.log('RESULT KEYS');
        console.log(Object.keys(result));
        console.log('');

        console.log('RESULT ARRAY');
        console.log(Array.isArray(result.result));
        console.log('');

        console.log('RESULT COUNT');
        console.log(result.result.length);
        console.log('');

        if (result.result.length > 0) {
            console.log('LIBRARY WAS RETURNED');

            for (let i = 0; i < result.result.length; i++) {
                const item = result.result[i];

                console.log('');
                console.log('ITEM', i);
                console.log('Type:', typeof item);

                if (Buffer.isBuffer(item)) {
                    console.log('Buffer length:', item.length);
                    console.log('Buffer hex:', item.toString('hex'));
                } else {
                    console.dir(item, { depth: 10 });
                }
            }
        } else {
            console.log('LIBRARY RESULT IS EMPTY');
        }

        console.log('');
        console.log('========================================');
        console.log(' TEST FINISHED');
        console.log(' NO TRANSACTION WAS SENT');
        console.log('========================================');

    } finally {
        console.log('');
        console.log('Closing engines...');

        engine.close();

        console.log('Done.');
    }
}

main().catch((error) => {
    console.error('');
    console.error('ERROR');
    console.error(error);
    process.exit(1);
});

FILE: scripts\debugMintPayload.ts

import { Address, beginCell } from '@ton/core';

export async function run() {
    const recipient = Address.parse(
        'EQDxn5MLbFyV649-OBviQrMp5KqWjGHNVwFM6GTeaLl8N0Ix'
    );

    const queryId = 0n;

    const jettonAmount = 1000000000000000000n;

    const internalTransferMsg = beginCell()
        .storeUint(0x178d4519, 32)
        .storeUint(queryId, 64)
        .storeCoins(jettonAmount)
        .storeBit(false)
        .storeBit(false)
        .storeCoins(0n)
        .endCell();

    const body = beginCell()
        .storeUint(0x00000015, 32)
        .storeUint(queryId, 64)
        .storeAddress(recipient)
        .storeCoins(100000000n)
        .storeRef(internalTransferMsg)
        .endCell();

    console.log('Recipient:');
    console.log(recipient.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log('Body BOC Base64:');
    console.log(body.toBoc().toString('base64'));

    console.log('');
    console.log('Body BOC Hex:');
    console.log(body.toBoc().toString('hex'));

    console.log('');
    console.log('Body bits:', body.bits.length);
    console.log('Body refs:', body.refs.length);

    console.log('');
    console.log('Internal transfer BOC Base64:');
    console.log(internalTransferMsg.toBoc().toString('base64'));

    console.log('');
    console.log('SUCCESS: Payload created locally.');
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\debugPushcontOperandV22.ts
====================================================================================================
import { Cell } from '@ton/core';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

const tactCore: any =
    require('@tact-lang/opcode/node_modules/@ton/core');

const LIBRARY_HASH =
    'C95A2ED22AB516F77F9D4898DC4578E72F18A2448E8F6832334B0B4BF501BC79';

const TARGET_HASH =
    'DB8B6488B1D9B9051532EE339C86B249DF8F923B6E29975E8E474009157F601A';

const LITESERVER_HOST =
    'tcp://49.12.147.32:27842';

const LITESERVER_PUBLIC_KEY =
    'sU7QavX2F964iI9oToP9gffQpCQIoOLppeqL/pdPvpM=';

const PUSHCONT_OFFSET = 120;
const PUSHCONT_PREFIX_BITS = 7;
const R_BITS = 2;
const XX_BITS = 7;

async function getTargetCell(): Promise<any> {
    const {
        LiteClient,
        LiteSingleEngine,
    } = require('ton-lite-client');

    const engine = new LiteSingleEngine({
        host: LITESERVER_HOST,
        publicKey: Buffer.from(
            LITESERVER_PUBLIC_KEY,
            'base64'
        ),
    });

    try {
        const started = Date.now();

        while (!engine.isReady()) {
            if (Date.now() - started > 15000) {
                throw new Error(
                    'LiteServer READY timeout'
                );
            }

            await new Promise(resolve =>
                setTimeout(resolve, 250)
            );
        }

        const client = new LiteClient({
            engine,
        });

        const result =
            await client.getLibraries([
                Buffer.from(
                    LIBRARY_HASH,
                    'hex'
                ),
            ]);

        if (!result.result?.length) {
            throw new Error(
                'Library was not returned'
            );
        }

        const root =
            Cell.fromBoc(
                Buffer.from(
                    result.result[0].data
                )
            )[0];

        // Important:
        // Rebuild through the nested @ton/core used
        // by @tact-lang/opcode.
        const rebuilt =
            tactCore.Cell.fromBoc(
                root.toBoc()
            )[0];

        function find(
            cell: any,
            wanted: string
        ): any | null {
            const hash =
                cell.hash()
                    .toString('hex')
                    .toUpperCase();

            if (hash === wanted) {
                return cell;
            }

            for (const ref of cell.refs) {
                const found =
                    find(ref, wanted);

                if (found) {
                    return found;
                }
            }

            return null;
        }

        const target =
            find(
                rebuilt,
                TARGET_HASH
            );

        if (!target) {
            throw new Error(
                'Target Cell not found'
            );
        }

        return target;
    } finally {
        engine.close();
    }
}

function loadBitsHex(
    slice: any,
    count: number
): string {
    return slice
        .loadBits(count)
        .toString()
        .replace(/^x\{/, '')
        .replace(/\}$/, '')
        .toUpperCase();
}

export async function run(
    _provider: NetworkProvider
) {
    console.log(
        '========================================================================'
    );
    console.log(
        ' STON.FI v2.2 PUSHCONT OPERAND DEBUGGER'
    );
    console.log(
        ' READ ONLY - EXACT SUBSLICE RECONSTRUCTION'
    );
    console.log(
        '========================================================================'
    );
    console.log();

    const target =
        await getTargetCell();

    console.log(
        `Target hash: ${target.hash()
            .toString('hex')
            .toUpperCase()}`
    );

    console.log(
        `Target bits: ${target.bits.length}`
    );

    console.log(
        `Target refs: ${target.refs.length}`
    );

    console.log();

    /*
     * ------------------------------------------------------------
     * STEP 1
     * Locate PUSHCONT
     * ------------------------------------------------------------
     */

    const slice =
        target.beginParse();

    slice.skip(
        PUSHCONT_OFFSET
    );

    console.log(
        '========================================================================'
    );
    console.log(
        ' STEP 1 - PUSHCONT HEADER'
    );
    console.log(
        '========================================================================'
    );
    console.log();

    console.log(
        `Start offset: ${slice.offsetBits}`
    );

    const prefix =
        loadBitsHex(
            slice,
            PUSHCONT_PREFIX_BITS
        );

    console.log(
        `Prefix (${PUSHCONT_PREFIX_BITS} bits): ${prefix}`
    );

    const r =
        slice.loadUint(R_BITS);

    console.log(
        `r (${R_BITS} bits): ${r}`
    );

    const xx =
        slice.loadUint(XX_BITS);

    console.log(
        `xx (${XX_BITS} bits): ${xx}`
    );

    console.log(
        `Offset after header: ${slice.offsetBits}`
    );

    const bitLength =
        xx * 8;

    const refLength =
        r;

    console.log();
    console.log(
        `bitLength = ${xx} * 8 = ${bitLength}`
    );

    console.log(
        `refLength = ${r}`
    );

    /*
     * ------------------------------------------------------------
     * STEP 2
     * Read the exact continuation bits
     * ------------------------------------------------------------
     */

    console.log();
    console.log(
        '========================================================================'
    );
    console.log(
        ' STEP 2 - LOAD CONTINUATION BITS'
    );
    console.log(
        '========================================================================'
    );
    console.log();

    const offsetBits =
        slice.offsetBits;

    console.log(
        `Continuation start: ${offsetBits}`
    );

    console.log(
        `Requested bits: ${bitLength}`
    );

    const continuationBits =
        slice.loadBits(
            bitLength
        );

    console.log(
        `Continuation end: ${slice.offsetBits}`
    );

    console.log(
        `Remaining bits in source Cell: ${slice.remainingBits}`
    );

    const continuationHex =
        continuationBits
            .toString()
            .replace(/^x\{/, '')
            .replace(/\}$/, '')
            .toUpperCase();

    console.log();
    console.log(
        `Continuation hex length: ${continuationHex.length} hex chars`
    );

    console.log(
        `Expected hex chars: ${bitLength / 4}`
    );

    console.log();

    console.log(
        'Continuation HEX:'
    );

    console.log(
        continuationHex
    );

    /*
     * ------------------------------------------------------------
     * STEP 3
     * Load the refs
     * ------------------------------------------------------------
     */

    console.log();
    console.log(
        '========================================================================'
    );
    console.log(
        ' STEP 3 - LOAD PUSHCONT REFS'
    );
    console.log(
        '========================================================================'
    );
    console.log();

    const refs: any[] = [];

    for (
        let i = 0;
        i < refLength;
        i++
    ) {
        console.log(
            `Loading ref ${i}...`
        );

        const ref =
            slice.loadRef();

        refs.push(ref);

        console.log(
            `ref[${i}] hash: ${
                ref.hash()
                    .toString('hex')
                    .toUpperCase()
            }`
        );

        console.log(
            `ref[${i}] bits: ${ref.bits.length}`
        );

        console.log(
            `ref[${i}] refs: ${ref.refs.length}`
        );

        console.log();
    }

    console.log(
        `Source slice offset after refs: ${slice.offsetBits}`
    );

    console.log(
        `Source slice remaining refs: ${slice.remainingRefs}`
    );

    /*
     * ------------------------------------------------------------
     * STEP 4
     * Rebuild exactly like operand-loader.js
     * ------------------------------------------------------------
     */

    console.log();
    console.log(
        '========================================================================'
    );
    console.log(
        ' STEP 4 - REBUILD SUBSLICE CELL'
    );
    console.log(
        '========================================================================'
    );
    console.log();

    console.log(
        'Creating Builder...'
    );

    const builder =
        new tactCore.Builder();

    console.log(
        'Builder created.'
    );

    console.log(
        `Storing ${bitLength} continuation bits...`
    );

    builder.storeBits(
        continuationBits
    );

    console.log(
        'Continuation bits stored.'
    );

    for (
        let i = 0;
        i < refs.length;
        i++
    ) {
        console.log(
            `Storing ref ${i}...`
        );

        builder.storeRef(
            refs[i]
        );

        console.log(
            `Ref ${i} stored.`
        );
    }

    console.log();
    console.log(
        'Calling builder.endCell()...'
    );

    let rebuiltCell: any;

    try {
        rebuiltCell =
            builder.endCell();

        console.log(
            'builder.endCell(): SUCCESS'
        );
    } catch (err: any) {
        console.log(
            'builder.endCell(): FAILED'
        );

        console.log(
            err?.stack ??
            err?.message ??
            String(err)
        );

        throw err;
    }

    /*
     * ------------------------------------------------------------
     * STEP 5
     * Validate reconstructed Cell
     * ------------------------------------------------------------
     */

    console.log();
    console.log(
        '========================================================================'
    );
    console.log(
        ' STEP 5 - RECONSTRUCTED CELL'
    );
    console.log(
        '========================================================================'
    );
    console.log();

    console.log(
        `Bits: ${rebuiltCell.bits.length}`
    );

    console.log(
        `Refs: ${rebuiltCell.refs.length}`
    );

    console.log(
        `Hash: ${
            rebuiltCell.hash()
                .toString('hex')
                .toUpperCase()
        }`
    );

    console.log();

    /*
     * Compare reconstructed refs.
     */

    for (
        let i = 0;
        i < rebuiltCell.refs.length;
        i++
    ) {
        console.log(
            `rebuilt ref[${i}] hash: ${
                rebuiltCell.refs[i]
                    .hash()
                    .toString('hex')
                    .toUpperCase()
            }`
        );
    }

    /*
     * ------------------------------------------------------------
     * STEP 6
     * Compare source continuation with reconstructed Cell
     * ------------------------------------------------------------
     */

    console.log();
    console.log(
        '========================================================================'
    );
    console.log(
        ' STEP 6 - VALIDATION'
    );
    console.log(
        '========================================================================'
    );
    console.log();

    const rebuiltBits =
        rebuiltCell.bits
            .toString()
            .replace(/^x\{/, '')
            .replace(/\}$/, '')
            .toUpperCase();

    const bitsMatch =
        rebuiltBits === continuationHex;

    console.log(
        `Continuation bits match: ${bitsMatch}`
    );

    console.log(
        `Bit count match: ${
            rebuiltCell.bits.length === bitLength
        }`
    );

    console.log(
        `Ref count match: ${
            rebuiltCell.refs.length === refLength
        }`
    );

    console.log();

    console.log(
        '========================================================================'
    );
    console.log(
        ' DEBUG COMPLETE'
    );
    console.log(
        '========================================================================'
    );
    console.log(
        'No blockchain transaction was sent.'
    );
}

import { Address, beginCell } from '@ton/core';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

const ROUTER = Address.parse(
    'EQALh-JBBIKK7gr0o4AVf9JZnEsFndqO0qTCyT-D-yBsWval'
);

const ROOSTER_MASTER = Address.parse(
    'EQCyuv-cYXFibA05bhiL3seM3Ur2B_bpsc364Zb8F3NMtrYx'
);

const PTON_MASTER = Address.parse(
    'EQACS30DNoUQ7NfApPvzh7eBmSZ9L4ygJ-lkNWtba8TQT1h7'
);

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {
    console.log('');
    console.log('==============================================');
    console.log(' ROUTER JETTON WALLET DEBUG');
    console.log(' READ-ONLY');
    console.log(' NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT');
    console.log('==============================================');

    console.log('');
    console.log('ROUTER:');
    console.log(ROUTER.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log('ROOSTER MASTER:');
    console.log(ROOSTER_MASTER.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log('pTON MASTER:');
    console.log(PTON_MASTER.toString());

    // ------------------------------------------

import { Address, beginCell } from "@ton/core";
import { TonClient4 } from "@ton/ton";

const client = new TonClient4({
  endpoint: "https://testnet-v4.tonhubapi.com",
});

const router = Address.parse(
  "EQALh-JBBIKK7gr0o4AVf9JZnEsFndqO0qTCyT-D-yBsWval"
);

const roosterMaster = Address.parse(
  "EQCyuv-cYXFibA05bhiL3seM3Ur2B_bpsc364Zb8F3NMtrYx"
);

const ptonMaster = Address.parse(
  "EQCM3B12QK1e4yZSf8GtBRT0aLMNyEsBc_DhVfRRtOEffLez"
);

async function getJettonWallet(
  master: Address,
  name: string
) {
  const result = await client.runMethod(
    master,
    "get_wallet_address",
    [
      {
        type: "slice",
        cell: beginCell()
          .storeAddress(router)
          .endCell(),
      },
    ]
  );

  const wallet = result.stack.readAddress();

  console.log(`\n===== ${name} ROUTER WALLET =====`);
  console.log("Master :", master.toString());
  console.log("Router :", router.toString());
  console.log("Wallet :", wallet.toString());

  return wallet;
}

async function main() {
  console.log("===== STON.fi TESTNET ROUTER WALLET TEST =====");

  const roosterWallet = await getJettonWallet(
    roosterMaster,
    "ROOSTER"
  );

  const ptonWallet = await getJettonWallet(
    ptonMaster,
    "pTON"
  );

  console.log("\n===== RESULT =====");
  console.log("ROOSTER Router Wallet:");
  console.log(roosterWallet.toString());

  console.log("\npTON Router Wallet:");
  console.log(ptonWallet.toString());

  console.log("\n===== NEXT STEP =====");
  console.log("These two addresses must be passed to get_pool_address.");
}

main().catch((err) => {

  console.error(err);
  process.exit(1);

import fs from "fs";

const b = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

function u(p) {
  let v = 0, s = 0;
  while (true) {
    const x = b[p++];
    v += (x & 127) * 2 ** s;
    if (!(x & 128)) return [v >>> 0, p];
    s += 7;
  }
}

const start = 0xeec8;
const end   = 0xef43;

function op(p) {
  const x = b[p];

  if (x === 0x20) {
    const [v,q] = u(p+1);
    return [`local.get L${v}`,q];
  }

  if (x === 0x21) {
    const [v,q] = u(p+1);
    return [`local.set L${v}`,q];
  }

  if (x === 0x22) {
    const [v,q] = u(p+1);
    return [`local.tee L${v}`,q];
  }

  if (x === 0x41) {
    const [v,q] = u(p+1);
    return [`i32.const ${v} (0x${v.toString(16)})`,q];
  }

  if (x === 0x28) {
    const [a,q1] = u(p+1);
    const [o,q2] = u(q1);
    return [`i32.load align=${a} offset=${o}`,q2];
  }

  if (x === 0x04) return ["IF",p+1];
  if (x === 0x05) return ["ELSE",p+1];
  if (x === 0x0b) return ["END",p+1];

  if (x === 0x02) return ["BLOCK",p+1];
  if (x === 0x03) return ["LOOP",p+1];

  if (x === 0x0c) {
    const [v,q] = u(p+1);
    return [`BR ${v}`,q];
  }

  if (x === 0x0d) {
    const [v,q] = u(p+1);
    return [`BR_IF ${v}`,q];
  }

  if (x === 0x0f) return ["RETURN",p+1];

  if (x === 0x10) {
    const [v,q] = u(p+1);
    return [`CALL ${v}`,q];
  }

  if (x === 0x11) {
    const [t,q1] = u(p+1);
    const [tab,q2] = u(q1);
    return [`CALL_INDIRECT type=${t} table=${tab}`,q2];
  }

  const cmp = {
    0x45:"i32.eqz",
    0x46:"i32.eq",
    0x47:"i32.ne",
    0x48:"i32.lt_s",
    0x49:"i32.lt_u",
    0x4a:"i32.gt_s",
    0x4b:"i32.gt_u",
    0x4c:"i32.le_s",
    0x4d:"i32.le_u",
    0x4e:"i32.ge_s",
    0x4f:"i32.ge_u"
  };

  if (cmp[x]) return [cmp[x],p+1];

  return [`opcode=0x${x.toString(16).padStart(2,"0")}`,p+1];
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FUNC 122 EXACT LOGIC");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("");

let p=start;
let depth=0;

while(p<end) {

  const pos=p;
  const [text,q]=op(p);

  if(text==="END")
    depth=Math.max(0,depth-1);

  console.log(
    `${"  ".repeat(depth)}0x${pos.toString(16).padStart(6,"0")}  ${text}`
  );

  if(
    text==="BLOCK" ||
    text==="LOOP" ||
    text==="IF"
  )
    depth++;

  p=q;
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");

FILE: scripts\decode14470_14530V22.ts

import fs from "fs";

const WASM = "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

function uleb(buf: Buffer, pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (pos < buf.length) {
        const b = buf[pos++];
        value += (b & 0x7f) * Math.pow(2, shift);
        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return { value, next: pos };
        }
        shift += 7;
    }

    throw new Error("Invalid ULEB128");
}

function hex(buf: Buffer, start: number, end: number): string {
    return Array.from(buf.subarray(start, end))
        .map(b => b.toString(16).padStart(2, "0"))
        .join(" ");
}

function decodeOps(buf: Buffer, start: number, end: number) {
    let p = start;

    while (p < end) {
        const op = buf[p];

        let name = "";
        let extra = "";

        switch (op) {
            case 0x41: {
                const r = uleb(buf, p + 1);
                name = "i32.const";
                extra = ` ${r.value} (0x${r.value.toString(16)})`;
                p = r.next;
                break;
            }

            case 0x20:
                name = "local.get";
                extra = ` ${buf[p + 1]}`;
                p += 2;
                break;

            case 0x21:
                name = "local.set";
                extra = ` ${buf[p + 1]}`;
                p += 2;
                break;

            case 0x22:
                name = "local.tee";
                extra = ` ${buf[p + 1]}`;
                p += 2;
                break;

            case 0x28: {
                const align = uleb(buf, p + 1);
                const off = uleb(buf, align.next);
                name = "i32.load";
                extra =
                    ` align=${align.value}` +
                    ` offset=${off.value} (0x${off.value.toString(16)})`;
                p = off.next;
                break;
            }

            case 0x36: {
                const align = uleb(buf, p + 1);
                const off = uleb(buf, align.next);
                name = "i32.store";
                extra =
                    ` align=${align.value}` +
                    ` offset=${off.value} (0x${off.value.toString(16)})`;
                p = off.next;
                break;
            }

            case 0x6a:
                name = "i32.add";
                p++;
                break;

            case 0x6b:
                name = "i32.sub";
                p++;
                break;

            case 0x71:
                name = "i32.and";
                p++;
                break;

            case 0x72:
                name = "i32.or";
                p++;
                break;

            case 0x73:
                name = "i32.xor";
                p++;
                break;

            case 0x74:
                name = "i32.shr_u";
                p++;
                break;

            case 0x75:
                name = "i32.shr_s";
                p++;
                break;

            case 0x76:
                name = "i32.rotl";
                p++;
                break;

            case 0x77:
                name = "i32.rotr";
                p++;
                break;

            case 0x45:
                name = "i32.eqz";
                p++;
                break;

            case 0x46:
                name = "i32.eq";
                p++;
                break;

            case 0x47:
                name = "i32.ne";
                p++;
                break;

            case 0x48:
                name = "i32.lt_s";
                p++;
                break;

            case 0x49:
                name = "i32.lt_u";
                p++;
                break;

            case 0x4a:
                name = "i32.gt_s";
                p++;
                break;

            case 0x4b:
                name = "i32.gt_u";
                p++;
                break;

            default:
                name = `op 0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`;
                p++;
                break;
        }

        console.log(
            `0x${(p - (name === "i32.const" || name.includes("load") || name.includes("store") ? 0 : 1))
                .toString(16)
                .padStart(8, "0")}  ${name}${extra}`
        );
    }
}

function main() {
    if (!fs.existsSync(WASM)) {
        throw new Error(`WASM not found:\n${WASM}`);
    }

    const buf = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

    const start = 0x14470;
    const end = 0x14530;

    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(" EXACT WASM WINDOW 0x14470 - 0x14530");
    console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(`WASM size : ${buf.length.toLocaleString()}`);
    console.log(`START     : 0x${start.toString(16)}`);
    console.log(`END       : 0x${end.toString(16)}`);
    console.log("");

    console.log("RAW BYTES:");
    console.log(hex(buf, start, end));
    console.log("");

    console.log("BYTE OFFSETS:");
    for (let p = start; p < end; p += 16) {
        const e = Math.min(p + 16, end);
        console.log(
            `0x${p.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  ${hex(buf, p, e)}`
        );
    }

    console.log("");
    console.log("DECODE:");
    console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

    decodeOps(buf, start, end);
}

main();

FILE: scripts\decode2057.ts

import fs from "fs";

const file = "emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";
const wasm = fs.readFileSync(file);

// Known exact locations
const CODE_START = 0x093e1b;
const CODE_END   = 0x093f30;

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FUNC 2057 DATAFLOW");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`CODE : 0x${CODE_START.toString(16)} .. 0x${CODE_END.toString(16)}`);
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

function uleb(pos: number) {
  let v = 0, shift = 0, p = pos;
  while (true) {
    const b = wasm[p++];
    v |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
    if (!(b & 0x80)) return { value: v >>> 0, next: p };
    shift += 7;
  }
}

function sleb(pos: number) {
  let v = 0, shift = 0, p = pos, b = 0;
  while (true) {
    b = wasm[p++];
    v |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
    shift += 7;
    if (!(b & 0x80)) break;
  }
  if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40)) v |= (~0 << shift);
  return { value: v | 0, next: p };
}

function hex(n: number) {
  return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

let p = CODE_START;
let depth = 0;

while (p < CODE_END) {
  const at = p;
  const op = wasm[p++];

  let text = "";

  switch (op) {
    case 0x00: text = "unreachable"; break;
    case 0x01: text = "nop"; break;

    case 0x02:
      text = "block";
      depth++;
      if (wasm[p] === 0x40) p++;
      else p++;
      break;

    case 0x03:
      text = "loop";
      depth++;
      if (wasm[p] === 0x40) p++;
      else p++;
      break;

    case 0x04:
      text = "if";
      depth++;
      if (wasm[p] === 0x40) p++;
      else p++;
      break;

    case 0x05:
      text = "else";
      break;

    case 0x0b:
      text = "end";
      depth--;
      break;

    case 0x0c: {
      const x = uleb(p); p = x.next;
      text = `br ${x.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x0d: {
      const x = uleb(p); p = x.next;
      text = `br_if ${x.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x0f: text = "return"; break;
    case 0x10: {
      const x = uleb(p); p = x.next;
      text = `call ${x.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x11: {
      const t = uleb(p); p = t.next;
      const tab = uleb(p); p = tab.next;
      text = `call_indirect type=${t.value} table=${tab.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x1a: text = "drop"; break;

    case 0x20: {
      const x = uleb(p); p = x.next;
      text = `local.get ${x.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x21: {
      const x = uleb(p); p = x.next;
      text = `local.set ${x.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x22: {
      const x = uleb(p); p = x.next;
      text = `local.tee ${x.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x23: {
      const x = uleb(p); p = x.next;
      text = `global.get ${x.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x24: {
      const x = uleb(p); p = x.next;
      text = `global.set ${x.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x28: {
      const a = uleb(p); p = a.next;
      const o = uleb(p); p = o.next;
      text = `i32.load align=${a.value} offset=${o.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x29: {
      const a = uleb(p); p = a.next;
      const o = uleb(p); p = o.next;
      text = `i64.load align=${a.value} offset=${o.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x2d: {
      const a = uleb(p); p = a.next;
      const o = uleb(p); p = o.next;
      text = `i32.load8_u align=${a.value} offset=${o.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x36: {
      const a = uleb(p); p = a.next;
      const o = uleb(p); p = o.next;
      text = `i32.store align=${a.value} offset=${o.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x37: {
      const a = uleb(p); p = a.next;
      const o = uleb(p); p = o.next;
      text = `i64.store align=${a.value} offset=${o.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x41: {
      const x = sleb(p); p = x.next;
      text = `i32.const ${x.value} (${hex(x.value >>> 0)})`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x42: {
      const x = sleb(p); p = x.next;
      text = `i64.const ${x.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x45: text = "i32.eqz"; break;
    case 0x46: text = "i32.eq"; break;
    case 0x47: text = "i32.ne"; break;
    case 0x4a: text = "i32.gt_s"; break;
    case 0x4b: text = "i32.gt_u"; break;
    case 0x4c: text = "i32.lt_s"; break;
    case 0x4d: text = "i32.lt_u"; break;

    case 0x6a: text = "i32.add"; break;
    case 0x6b: text = "i32.sub"; break;
    case 0x72: text = "i32.rotr"; break;
    case 0x74: text = "i32.shl"; break;
    case 0x76: text = "i32.shr_u"; break;

    case 0x7c: text = "i64.add"; break;

    case 0xa7: text = "i32.wrap_i64"; break;
    case 0xad: text = "i64.extend_i32_u"; break;

    case 0xfc: {
      const x = uleb(p); p = x.next;
      text = `EXT-FC subop=${x.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0xfd: {
      const x = uleb(p); p = x.next;
      text = `EXT-FD subop=${x.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0xfe: {
      const x = uleb(p); p = x.next;
      text = `EXT-FE subop=${x.value}`;
      break;
    }

    default:
      text = `UNKNOWN 0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`;
  }

  console.log(`${hex(at)}  ${text}`);
}

FILE: scripts\decode2057Callers.mjs

import fs from "fs";

const b = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

function u(p) {
  let v = 0, s = 0;
  while (true) {
    const x = b[p++];
    v += (x & 127) * 2 ** s;
    if (!(x & 128)) return [v >>> 0, p];
    s += 7;
  }
}

let p = 8, code = null;

while (p < b.length) {
  const id = b[p++];
  const [n, q] = u(p);
  p = q;
  if (id === 10) code = [p, p + n];
  p += n;
}

p = code[0];
const [count, q0] = u(p);
p = q0;

const targets = new Set([3328, 3331, 3332]);

for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
  const [size, q] = u(p);
  p = q;

  const func = 83 + i;
  const start = p;
  const end = p + size;

  if (!targets.has(func)) {
    p = end;
    continue;
  }

  console.log("\n============================================================");
  console.log(`FUNC ${func}`);
  console.log("============================================================");

  for (let x = start; x < end; x++) {
    if (b[x] !== 0x10) continue;

    const [target, q2] = u(x + 1);

    if (target !== 2057) continue;

    console.log(`CALL2057 @ 0x${x.toString(16)}`);
    console.log("PRECEDING:");

    const from = Math.max(start, x - 45);

    console.log(
      [...b.subarray(from, x + 3)]
        .map(v => v.toString(16).padStart(2, "0"))
        .join(" ")
    );
    console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
  }

  p = end;
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\decode2057Callers.ts
====================================================================================================
import fs from "fs";

const WASM =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const TARGETS = new Set([3328, 3331, 3332]);
const IMPORTS = 83;

const buf = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

function u32(p: number) {
  let v = 0, s = 0;
  while (true) {
    const b = buf[p++];
    v += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** s;
    if (!(b & 0x80)) return { value: v, next: p };
    s += 7;
  }
}

function s32(p: number) {
  let v = 0, s = 0;
  while (true) {
    const b = buf[p++];
    v += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** s;
    if (!(b & 0x80)) {
      if (s < 32 && (b & 0x40)) v -= 2 ** (s + 7);
      return { value: v, next: p };
    }
    s += 7;
  }
}

function hex(n: number) {
  return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(6, "0");
}

function opcodeName(op: number) {
  switch (op) {
    case 0x20: return "local.get";
    case 0x21: return "local.set";
    case 0x22: return "local.tee";
    case 0x23: return "global.get";
    case 0x24: return "global.set";
    case 0x28: return "i32.load";
    case 0x36: return "i32.store";
    case 0x41: return "i32.const";
    case 0x42: return "i64.const";
    case 0x45: return "i32.eqz";
    case 0x47: return "i32.ne";
    case 0x4b: return "i32.gt_u";
    case 0x6a: return "i32.add";
    case 0x6b: return "i32.sub";
    case 0x71: return "i32.and";
    case 0x72: return "i32.or";
    case 0x74: return "i32.shl";
    case 0x76: return "i32.shr_u";
    case 0x7c: return "i64.shl";
    case 0x7f: return "i64.shr_s";
    case 0x0c: return "br";
    case 0x0d: return "br_if";
    case 0x0e: return "br_table";
    case 0x0f: return "return";
    case 0x10: return "call";
    case 0x1a: return "drop";
    case 0x02: return "block";
    case 0x03: return "loop";
    case 0x04: return "if";
    case 0x05: return "else";
    case 0x0b: return "end";
    default: return `OP_0x${op.toString(16)}`;
  }
}

let p = 8;
let found = 0;

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("DECODE CALLERS OF FUNC 2057");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

while (p < buf.length) {

  const id = buf[p++];

  const si = u32(p);
  p = si.next;

  const sectionEnd = p + si.value;

  if (id !== 10) {
    p = sectionEnd;
    continue;
  }

  const ci = u32(p);
  const bodyCount = ci.value;
  p = ci.next;

  for (let body = 0; body < bodyCount; body++) {

    const bi = u32(p);
    const bodySize = bi.value;
    p = bi.next;

    const bodyStart = p;
    const bodyEnd = p + bodySize;

    const li = u32(p);
    const localDecls = li.value;
    p = li.next;

    for (let i = 0; i < localDecls; i++) {
      p = u32(p).next;
      p++;
    }

    const codeStart = p;
    const realFunc = body + IMPORTS;

    if (!TARGETS.has(realFunc)) {
      p = bodyEnd;
      continue;
    }

    found++;

    console.log("");
    console.log("############################################################");
    console.log(`FUNC ${realFunc}`);
    console.log("############################################################");
    console.log(`BODY START : ${hex(bodyStart)}`);
    console.log(`CODE START : ${hex(codeStart)}`);
    console.log(`BODY END   : ${hex(bodyEnd)}`);
    console.log("");

    let q = codeStart;

    while (q < bodyEnd) {

      const at = q;
      const op = buf[q++];

      let text = opcodeName(op);

      if (
        op === 0x20 ||
        op === 0x21 ||
        op === 0x22 ||
        op === 0x23 ||
        op === 0x24 ||
        op === 0x0c ||
        op === 0x0d ||
        op === 0x10
      ) {
        const x = u32(q);
        q = x.next;

        if (op === 0x10)
          text += ` FUNC ${x.value}`;
        else
          text += ` ${x.value}`;

      } else if (op === 0x41) {

        const x = s32(q);
        q = x.next;

        text += ` ${x.value} (${hex(x.value >>> 0)})`;

      } else if (op === 0x42) {

        const x = s32(q);
        q = x.next;

        text += ` ${x.value}`;

      } else if (op === 0x28 || op === 0x36) {

        const a = u32(q);
        q = a.next;

        const o = u32(q);
        q = o.next;

        text += ` align=${a.value} offset=${o.value}`;
      }

      console.log(`${hex(at)}  ${text}`);
    }
  }

  p = sectionEnd;
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`TARGET CALLERS DECODED : ${found}`);
console.log("============================================================");

import fs from "fs";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

const START = 0x000000; // پیدا کردن BODY از روی function index
const TARGET = 2654;

function uleb(pos:number) {
  let v=0, s=0, p=pos;
  while(true) {
    const b=wasm[p++];
    v |= (b & 0x7f) << s;
    if(!(b&0x80)) return {value:v>>>0,next:p};
    s+=7;
  }
}

let p=8;

// skip wasm sections and locate:
// section 3 = function type indices
let funcTypes:number[]=[];
let codeStart=0, codeSize=0;

while(p<wasm.length) {
  const id=wasm[p++];
  const s=uleb(p); const size=s.value; p=s.next;
  const end=p+size;

  if(id===3) {
    const n=uleb(p); p=n.next;
    for(let i=0;i<n.value;i++) {
      const x=uleb(p); p=x.next;
      funcTypes.push(x.value);
    }
  }

  if(id===10) {
    codeStart=p;
    codeSize=size;
    break;
  }

  p=end;
}

const bodyIndex=TARGET-83;

let q=codeStart;
const count=uleb(q); q=count.next;

for(let i=0;i<count.value;i++) {
  const sz=uleb(q); q=sz.next;
  const bs=q;
  const be=q+sz.value;

  if(i===bodyIndex) {
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log("FUNC 2654 EXACT BODY");
    console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(`REAL FUNC     : ${TARGET}`);
    console.log(`BODY INDEX    : ${bodyIndex}`);
    console.log(`TYPE INDEX    : ${funcTypes[bodyIndex]}`);
    console.log(`BODY START    : 0x${bs.toString(16)}`);
    console.log(`BODY END      : 0x${be.toString(16)}`);
    console.log(`BODY SIZE     : ${sz.value}`);
    console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

    let r=bs;

    while(r<be) {
      const at=r;
      const op=wasm[r++];

      if(op===0x0b) {
        console.log(`0x${at.toString(16).padStart(8,"0")} end`);
        continue;
      }

      if(op===0x20 || op===0x21 || op===0x22) {
        const x=uleb(r); r=x.next;
        console.log(`0x${at.toString(16).padStart(8,"0")} local.${op===0x20?"get":op===0x21?"set":"tee"} ${x.value}`);
        continue;
      }

      if(op===0x41) {
        const x=uleb(r); r=x.next;
        console.log(`0x${at.toString(16).padStart(8,"0")} i32.const ${x.value} (0x${x.value.toString(16)})`);
        continue;
      }

      if(op===0x42) {
        const x=uleb(r); r=x.next;
        console.log(`0x${at.toString(16).padStart(8,"0")} i64.const ${x.value}`);
        continue;
      }

      if(op===0x28 || op===0x29 || op===0x2d) {
        const a=uleb(r); r=a.next;
        const o=uleb(r); r=o.next;
        console.log(`0x${at.toString(16).padStart(8,"0")} ${op===0x28?"i32.load":op===0x29?"i64.load":"i32.load8_u"} align=${a.value} offset=${o.value}`);
        continue;
      }

      if(op===0x36 || op===0x37 || op===0x3a) {
        const a=uleb(r); r=a.next;
        const o=uleb(r); r=o.next;
        console.log(`0x${at.toString(16).padStart(8,"0")} store opcode=0x${op.toString(16)} align=${a.value} offset=${o.value}`);
        continue;
      }

      if(op===0x10) {
        const x=uleb(r); r=x.next;
        console.log(`0x${at.toString(16).padStart(8,"0")} call ${x.value}`);
        continue;
      }

      if(op===0x0c || op===0x0d) {
        const x=uleb(r); r=x.next;
        console.log(`0x${at.toString(16).padStart(8,"0")} ${op===0x0c?"br":"br_if"} ${x.value}`);
        continue;
      }

      const names:any = {
        0x02:"block",0x03:"loop",0x04:"if",0x05:"else",
        0x0f:"return",0x1a:"drop",
        0x45:"i32.eqz",0x46:"i32.eq",0x47:"i32.ne",
        0x4a:"i32.gt_s",0x4b:"i32.gt_u",
        0x6a:"i32.add",0x6b:"i32.sub",
        0x71:"i32.and",0x72:"i32.rotr",
        0x74:"i32.shl",0x76:"i32.shr_u",
        0x7c:"i64.add",0xa7:"i32.wrap_i64",
        0xad:"i64.extend_i32_u"
      };

      console.log(
        `0x${at.toString(16).padStart(8,"0")} ${names[op] ?? "UNKNOWN 0x"+op.toString(16)}`
      );
    }

    console.log("============================================================");
    break;
  }

  q=be;
}

FILE: scripts\decode3632GaWindowV43.ts

import fs from "fs";

const wasmPath =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(wasmPath);

const START = 0x1242e0;
const END   = 0x124340;

function readULEB(pos: number): { value: number; next: number; bytes: number[] } {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let p = pos;
    const bytes: number[] = [];

    while (p < wasm.length) {
        const b = wasm[p++];
        bytes.push(b);
        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return { value: value >>> 0, next: p, bytes };
        }

        shift += 7;

        if (shift > 35) {
            throw new Error("ULEB too long");
        }
    }

    throw new Error("Unexpected EOF in ULEB");
}

function readSLEB64(pos: number): { value: bigint; next: number; bytes: number[] } {
    let value = 0n;
    let shift = 0n;
    let p = pos;
    const bytes: number[] = [];
    let b = 0;

    while (p < wasm.length) {
        b = wasm[p++];
        bytes.push(b);

        value |= BigInt(b & 0x7f) << shift;
        shift += 7n;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            if (shift < 64n && (b & 0x40)) {
                value |= (-1n) << shift;
            }

            return {
                value,
                next: p,
                bytes
            };
        }

        if (shift > 70n) {
            throw new Error("SLEB too long");
        }
    }

    throw new Error("Unexpected EOF in SLEB64");
}

function hex(v: number, width = 8): string {
    return "0x" + v.toString(16).padStart(width, "0");
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" FUNCTION 3632 a.ga WINDOW v43");
console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log();
console.log("WASM size :", wasm.length.toLocaleString());
console.log("Window    :", `${hex(START)} .. ${hex(END)}`);
console.log();

let p = START;

while (p < END) {
    const addr = p;
    const op = wasm[p++];

    process.stdout.write(`${hex(addr)}  op=0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")} `);

    try {
        switch (op) {

            case 0x20: {
                const r = readULEB(p);
                p = r.next;
                console.log(`local.get ${r.value}`);
                break;
            }

            case 0x21: {
                const r = readULEB(p);
                p = r.next;
                console.log(`local.set ${r.value}`);
                break;
            }

            case 0x22: {
                const r = readULEB(p);
                p = r.next;
                console.log(`local.tee ${r.value}`);
                break;
            }

            case 0x10: {
                const r = readULEB(p);
                p = r.next;
                console.log(
                    `call ${r.value}` +
                    (r.value === 60 ? "  <<< IMPORT #60 = a.ga >>>" : "")
                );
                break;
            }

            case 0x11: {
                const type = readULEB(p);
                p = type.next;

                const table = readULEB(p);
                p = table.next;

                console.log(
                    `call_indirect type=${type.value} table=${table.value}`
                );
                break;
            }

            case 0x28: {
                const align = readULEB(p);
                p = align.next;

                const offset = readULEB(p);
                p = offset.next;

                console.log(
                    `i32.load align=${align.value} offset=${offset.value}`
                );
                break;
            }

            case 0x29: {
                const align = readULEB(p);
                p = align.next;

                const offset = readULEB(p);
                p = offset.next;

                console.log(
                    `i64.load align=${align.value} offset=${offset.value}`
                );
                break;
            }

            case 0x2d: {
                const align = readULEB(p);
                p = align.next;

                const offset = readULEB(p);
                p = offset.next;

                console.log(
                    `i32.load8_u align=${align.value} offset=${offset.value}`
                );
                break;
            }

            case 0x36: {
                const align = readULEB(p);
                p = align.next;

                const offset = readULEB(p);
                p = offset.next;

                console.log(
                    `i32.store align=${align.value} offset=${offset.value}`
                );
                break;
            }

            case 0x37: {
                const align = readULEB(p);
                p = align.next;

                const offset = readULEB(p);
                p = offset.next;

                console.log(
                    `i64.store align=${align.value} offset=${offset.value}`
                );
                break;
            }

            case 0x41: {
                const r = readSLEB64(p);
                p = r.next;

                console.log(`i32.const ${r.value}`);
                break;
            }

            case 0x42: {
                const r = readSLEB64(p);
                p = r.next;

                console.log(`i64.const ${r.value}`);
                break;
            }

            case 0x6a:
                console.log("i32.add");
                break;

            case 0x6b:
                console.log("i32.sub");
                break;

            case 0x6c:
                console.log("i32.mul");
                break;

            case 0x71:
                console.log("i32.and");
                break;

            case 0x72:
                console.log("i32.or");
                break;

            case 0x73:
                console.log("i32.xor");
                break;

            case 0x74:
                console.log("i32.shl");
                break;

            case 0x75:
                console.log("i32.shr_s");
                break;

            case 0x76:
                console.log("i32.shr_u");
                break;

            case 0x83:
                console.log("i64.and");
                break;

            case 0x88:
                console.log("i64.add");
                break;

            case 0xa7:
                console.log("i32.wrap_i64");
                break;

            case 0xad:
                console.log("i64.extend_i32_s");
                break;

            case 0x0b:
                console.log("end");
                break;

            case 0x0f:
                console.log("return");
                break;

            case 0x00:
                console.log("unreachable");
                break;

            case 0x01:
                console.log("nop");
                break;

            default:
                console.log("UNKNOWN");
                break;
        }
    } catch (e) {
        console.log("DECODER ERROR:", e);
        break;
    }
}

    const b = Math.min(a + 16, END);
    const bytes: string[] = [];

    for (let i = a; i < b; i++) {
        bytes.push(wasm[i].toString(16).padStart(2, "0"));
    }

    console.log(`${hex(a)}  ${bytes.join(" ")}`);
}

FILE: scripts\decode3632TargetV42.ts

import fs from "fs";

const WASM =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const bytes = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

const START = 0x124303;
const END = 0x124333;

function readU32Leb(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = bytes[pos++];

        value += (b & 0x7f) * Math.pow(2, shift);

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return {
                value: value >>> 0,
                next: pos,
            };
        }

        shift += 7;
    }
}

function readS32Leb(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let b = 0;

    while (true) {
        b = bytes[pos++];

        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
        shift += 7;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            break;
        }
    }

    if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40)) {
        value |= (-1 << shift);
    }

    return {
        value: value | 0,
        next: pos,
    };
}

function readS64Leb(pos: number): { value: bigint; next: number } {
    let value = 0n;
    let shift = 0n;
    let b = 0;

    while (true) {
        b = bytes[pos++];

        value |= BigInt(b & 0x7f) << shift;
        shift += 7n;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            break;
        }
    }

    if (shift < 64n && (b & 0x40)) {
        value |= (-1n) << shift;
    }

    return {
        value,
        next: pos,
    };
}

function hexByte(b: number): string {
    return b.toString(16).padStart(2, "0");
}

console.log("============================================================");

console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");

    `Region: 0x${START.toString(16)} .. 0x${(END - 1).toString(16)}`
);
console.log();

let p = START;

while (p < END) {
    const offset = p;
    const op = bytes[p++];

    console.log(
        `0x${offset.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  op=0x${hexByte(op)}`

    switch (op) {

FILE: scripts\decode6618Prologue.js

const fs = require("fs");

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm"
);

const START = 0x0022ccb5;
const END   = 0x0022ccdc;

function u32(p) {
  let v = 0, s = 0;
  while (true) {
    const b = wasm[p++];
    v |= (b & 0x7f) << s;
    if (!(b & 0x80))
      return { value: v >>> 0, next: p };
    s += 7;
  }
}

function i32(p) {
  let v = 0, s = 0;
  while (true) {
    const b = wasm[p++];
    v |= (b & 0x7f) << s;
    if (!(b & 0x80)) {
      s += 7;
      if (s < 32 && (b & 0x40))
        v |= (~0 << s);
      return { value: v | 0, next: p };
    }
    s += 7;
  }
}

function hx(x) {
  return "0x" + x.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("EXACT PROLOGUE OF FUNC 6618");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

let p = START;
const stack = [];

function push(x) {
  stack.push(x);
}

function pop() {
  return stack.length ? stack.pop() : "<?>"; 
}

while (p < END) {

  const off = p;
  const op = wasm[p++];

  // block
  if (op === 0x02) {
    const type = wasm[p++];
    console.log(
      `${hx(off)} block type=0x${type.toString(16)}`
    );
    continue;
  }

  // loop
  if (op === 0x03) {
    const type = wasm[p++];
    console.log(
      `${hx(off)} loop type=0x${type.toString(16)}`
    );
    continue;
  }

  // if
  if (op === 0x04) {
    const type = wasm[p++];
    console.log(
      `${hx(off)} if type=0x${type.toString(16)}`
    );
    continue;
  }

  // else
  if (op === 0x05) {
    console.log(`${hx(off)} else`);
    continue;
  }

  // end
  if (op === 0x0b) {
    console.log(`${hx(off)} end`);
    continue;
  }

  // br
  if (op === 0x0c) {
    const x = u32(p);
    p = x.next;
    console.log(`${hx(off)} br ${x.value}`);
    continue;
  }

  // br_if
  if (op === 0x0d) {
    const x = u32(p);
    p = x.next;
    const cond = pop();
    console.log(`${hx(off)} br_if ${x.value} [${cond}]`);
    continue;
  }

  // return
  if (op === 0x0f) {
    console.log(`${hx(off)} return`);
    continue;
  }

  // global.get
  if (op === 0x23) {
    const x = u32(p);
    p = x.next;

    const e = `GLOBAL[${x.value}]`;

    push(e);

    console.log(
      `${hx(off)} global.get ${x.value} -> ${e}`
    );

    continue;
  }

  // global.set
  if (op === 0x24) {
    const x = u32(p);
    p = x.next;

    const e = pop();

    console.log(
      `${hx(off)} global.set ${x.value} = ${e}`
    );

    continue;
  }

  // local.get
  if (op === 0x20) {
    const x = u32(p);
    p = x.next;

    const e = `local${x.value}`;

    push(e);

    console.log(
      `${hx(off)} local.get ${x.value}`
    );

    continue;
  }

  // local.set
  if (op === 0x21) {
    const x = u32(p);
    p = x.next;

    const e = pop();

    console.log(
      `${hx(off)} local.set ${x.value} = ${e}`
    );

    continue;
  }

  // local.tee
  if (op === 0x22) {
    const x = u32(p);
    p = x.next;

    const e = pop();

    push(e);

    console.log(
      `${hx(off)} local.tee ${x.value} = ${e}`
    );

    continue;
  }

  // i32.const
  if (op === 0x41) {
    const x = i32(p);
    p = x.next;

    push(String(x.value));

    console.log(
      `${hx(off)} i32.const ${x.value}`
    );

    continue;
  }

  // i32.eqz
  if (op === 0x45) {
    const a = pop();
    const e = `(${a} == 0)`;

    push(e);

    console.log(
      `${hx(off)} i32.eqz -> ${e}`
    );

    continue;
  }

  // i32.sub
  if (op === 0x6b) {
    const b = pop();
    const a = pop();

    const e = `(${a} - ${b})`;

    push(e);

    console.log(
      `${hx(off)} i32.sub -> ${e}`
    );

    continue;
  }

  // unknown
  console.log(
    `${hx(off)} UNKNOWN 0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2,"0")}`
  );
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FINAL STACK");
console.log("============================================================");

stack.forEach((x, i) => {
  console.log(`[${i}] ${x}`);
});

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("END");
console.log("============================================================");

import { Cell } from '@ton/core';
import { readFileSync } from 'fs';

const SOURCE_FILE =
    './scripts/decodePoolOutJettonV22.ts';

function extractBoc(text: string): string {
    const match = text.match(
        /const\s+BOC\s*=\s*'([^']+)'/
    );

    if (!match) {
        throw new Error(
            'Could not find const BOC in source file'
        );
    }

    return match[1].replace(/\s+/g, '');
}

function main() {
    console.log('================================================');
    console.log(' STON.fi v2.2 ADD_LIQUIDITY DECODER');
    console.log(' BOC SOURCE: KNOWN-GOOD OFFLINE DECODER');
    console.log(' READ ONLY - NO NETWORK');
    console.log('================================================');
    console.log('');

    const source = readFileSync(
        SOURCE_FILE,
        'utf8'
    );

    const boc = extractBoc(source);

    console.log(
        'BOC LENGTH:',
        boc.length
    );

    const cell = Cell.fromBase64(boc);

    console.log(
        'ROOT BITS:',
        cell.bits.length
    );

    console.log(
        'ROOT REFS:',
        cell.refs.length
    );

    const s = cell.beginParse();

    const opcode = s.loadUint(32);

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        'OPCODE:',
        '0x' +
            opcode
                .toString(16)
                .padStart(8, '0')
    );

    const queryId = s.loadUintBig(64);

    console.log(
        'QUERY ID:',
        queryId.toString()
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log('========== DECODE ==========');

    const amount0 = s.loadCoins();

    console.log(
        'AMOUNT0 RAW:',
        amount0.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'AMOUNT0 / 1e9:',
        Number(amount0) / 1e9
    );

    const amount1 = s.loadCoins();

    console.log(
        'AMOUNT1 RAW:',
        amount1.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'AMOUNT1 / 1e9:',
        Number(amount1) / 1e9
    );

    const minLpOut = s.loadCoins();

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        'MIN LP OUT RAW:',
        minLpOut.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'MIN LP OUT / 1e9:',
        Number(minLpOut) / 1e9
    );

    const fwdAmount = s.loadCoins();

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        'FWD AMOUNT RAW:',
        fwdAmount.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'FWD AMOUNT TON:',
        Number(fwdAmount) / 1e9
    );

    const bothPositive = s.loadBit();

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        'BOTH POSITIVE:',
        bothPositive
    );

    try {
        const toAddress = s.loadAddress();

        console.log(
            'TO ADDRESS:',
            toAddress?.toString()
        );
    } catch (e) {
        console.log(
            'TO ADDRESS: decode failed'
        );
    }

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        'REMAINING BITS:',
        s.remainingBits
    );

    console.log(
        'REMAINING REFS:',
        s.remainingRefs
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log('================================================');
    console.log(' END - READ ONLY');
    console.log('================================================');
}

main();
