import { Cell } from '@ton/core';
import { readFileSync } from 'fs';

const SOURCE_FILE =
    './scripts/decodePoolOutJettonV22.ts';

function extractBoc(text: string): string {
    const match = text.match(
        /const\s+BOC\s*=\s*'([^']+)'/
    );

    if (!match) {
        throw new Error(
            'Could not find const BOC in source file'
        );
    }

    return match[1].replace(/\s+/g, '');
}

function main() {
    console.log('================================================');
    console.log(' STON.fi v2.2 ADD_LIQUIDITY FULL DECODER');
    console.log(' READ ONLY - OFFLINE');
    console.log('================================================');

        SOURCE_FILE,
        'utf8'
    );

    console.log('ROOT BITS:', cell.bits.length);
    console.log('ROOT REFS:', cell.refs.length);

    const opcode = s.loadUint(32);

    console.log(
        'OPCODE:',
        '0x' + opcode.toString(16).padStart(8, '0')
    );

    const queryId = s.loadUintBig(64);

    console.log('QUERY ID:', queryId.toString());

    const amount0 = s.loadCoins();
    const amount1 = s.loadCoins();
    const minLpOut = s.loadCoins();
    const fwdAmount = s.loadCoins();

        'NEW AMOUNT0 RAW:',

    );

    console.log(

    );

    );

        'MIN LP OUT:',

        'LP'
    );

    console.log(
        'FWD AMOUNT RAW:',
        fwdAmount.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'FWD AMOUNT:',
        Number(fwdAmount) / 1e9,
        'TON'
    );

    const bothPositive = s.loadBit();

    console.log(
        'BOTH POSITIVE:',
        bothPositive
    );

    const toAddress = s.loadAddress();

    console.log(
        'TO ADDRESS:',
        toAddress?.toString()
    );

    const hasCustomPayload = s.loadBit();

    console.log(
        'CUSTOM PAYLOAD PRESENT:',

    );

    if (hasCustomPayload) {
        console.log(
            'CUSTOM PAYLOAD REF:',
            'PRESENT'
        );
    }

    console.log('');

        'ROOT REMAINING BITS:',
        s.remainingBits
    );

    console.log(
        'ROOT REMAINING REFS:',
        s.remainingRefs
    );

    if (s.remainingRefs < 1) {
        throw new Error(
            'additional_fields reference is missing'
        );
    }

    const additional = s.loadRef();

    console.log('');
    console.log('========== ADDITIONAL FIELDS ==========');

    console.log(
        'REF BITS:',
        additional.bits.length
    );

    console.log(
        'REF REFS:',
        additional.refs.length
    );

    const refundAddress = a.loadAddress();
    const excessAddress = a.loadAddress();

    console.log(

        refundAddress?.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'EXCESS ADDRESS:',
        excessAddress?.toString()
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        'REF REMAINING BITS:',
        a.remainingBits
    );

    console.log(
        'REF REMAINING REFS:',
        a.remainingRefs
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log('================================================');
    console.log(' END - READ ONLY');
    console.log('================================================');
}

import { Address } from '@ton/core';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {
    const contractAddress = Address.parse(
        'EQBTl2I95DKuMGD4X91du7820Ll4cVozfKIRna_iJp2fPKO_'
    );

    const contract = provider.provider(contractAddress);

    const result = await contract.get('get_jetton_data', []);

    // get_jetton_data:
    // 1. totalSupply
    // 2. mintable
    // 3. adminAddress
    // 4. content
    // 5. jettonWalletCode

    const totalSupply = result.stack.readNumber();
    const mintable = result.stack.readNumber();

    const adminCell = result.stack.readCell();
    const adminAddress = adminCell.beginParse().loadAddress();

    console.log('Rooster Coin:', contractAddress.toString());
    console.log('Total Supply:', totalSupply);
    console.log('Mintable:', mintable);
    console.log('ADMIN STORED IN CONTRACT:', adminAddress.toString());
}

import fs from "fs";

const WASM =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

function readULEB(buf: Buffer, pos: number): {
    value: number;
    next: number;
    bytes: string;
} {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    const start = pos;

    while (pos < buf.length) {
        const b = buf[pos++];
        value += (b & 0x7f) * Math.pow(2, shift);

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return {
                value,
                next: pos,
                bytes: buf
                    .subarray(start, pos)
                    .toString("hex")
                    .match(/../g)!
                    .join(" "),
            };
        }

        shift += 7;
        if (shift > 35) {
            throw new Error("ULEB too large");
        }
    }

    throw new Error("Unexpected EOF in ULEB");
}

function opName(op: number): string {
    const names: Record<number, string> = {
        0x20: "local.get",
        0x21: "local.set",
        0x22: "local.tee",

        0x28: "i32.load",
        0x36: "i32.store",

        0x41: "i32.const",

        0x45: "i32.eqz",
        0x46: "i32.eq",
        0x47: "i32.ne",

        0x49: "i32.lt_u",
        0x4b: "i32.gt_u",

        0x6a: "i32.add",
        0x6b: "i32.sub",

        0x71: "i32.and",
        0x72: "i32.or",
        0x73: "i32.xor",

        0x74: "i32.shr_u",
        0x75: "i32.shr_s",
        0x76: "i32.rotl",
        0x77: "i32.rotr",

        0x02: "block",
        0x03: "loop",
        0x04: "if",
        0x0b: "end",
        0x0c: "br",
        0x0d: "br_if",
    };

    return names[op] ?? `op_0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`;
}

function decodeWindow(
    buf: Buffer,
    start: number,
    end: number,
    title: string
) {
    console.log("");
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(title);
    console.log("============================================================");

    let p = start;

    while (p < end) {
        const at = p;
        const op = buf[p++];

        if (op === undefined) break;

        const name = opName(op);
        let detail = "";

        if (op === 0x41) {
            const r = readULEB(buf, p);
            detail =
                ` value=${r.value}` +
                ` hex=0x${r.value.toString(16)}` +
                ` bytes=[${r.bytes}]`;
            p = r.next;
        }

        else if (op === 0x20 || op === 0x21 || op === 0x22) {
            const local = buf[p++];
            detail = ` local=${local}`;
        }

        else if (op === 0x28 || op === 0x36) {
            const align = readULEB(buf, p);
            p = align.next;

            const offset = readULEB(buf, p);
            p = offset.next;

            detail =
                ` align=${align.value}` +
                ` offset=${offset.value}` +
                ` hex=0x${offset.value.toString(16)}` +
                ` offsetBytes=[${offset.bytes}]`;

            if (offset.value >= 0x240000 && offset.value <= 0x270000) {
                detail +=
                    `  *** STATE REGION ***` +
                    ` relative=0x${(offset.value - 0x255000).toString(16)}`;
            }
        }

        else if (op === 0x0c || op === 0x0d) {
            const r = readULEB(buf, p);
            detail = ` label=${r.value}`;
            p = r.next;
        }

        console.log(
            `0x${at.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  ` +
            `${name.padEnd(12)}${detail}`
        );
    }
}

function raw(buf: Buffer, start: number, end: number) {
    console.log("");
    console.log(
        `RAW 0x${start.toString(16)} - 0x${end.toString(16)}`
    );

    for (let p = start; p < end; p += 16) {
        const e = Math.min(p + 16, end);

        const bytes = Array.from(buf.subarray(p, e))
            .map(x => x.toString(16).padStart(2, "0"))
            .join(" ");

        console.log(
            `0x${p.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  ${bytes}`
        );
    }
}

function main() {
    if (!fs.existsSync(WASM)) {
        throw new Error(`WASM not found:\n${WASM}`);
    }

    const buf = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(" BITMAP / STATE WRITER INSPECTION v22");
    console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(`WASM size : ${buf.length.toLocaleString()}`);

    const windows = [
        {
            start: 0x14e90,
            end: 0x14f20,
            title: "WRITER REGION A 0x14E90 - 0x14F20",
        },
        {
            start: 0x15180,
            end: 0x151f0,
            title: "CLEAR REGION B 0x15180 - 0x151F0",
        },
        {
            start: 0x152f0,
            end: 0x15360,
            title: "WRITER REGION C 0x152F0 - 0x15360",
        },
        {
            start: 0x153e0,
            end: 0x15440,
            title: "CLEAR REGION D 0x153E0 - 0x15440",
        },
        {
            start: 0x15560,
            end: 0x155c0,
            title: "WRITER REGION E 0x15560 - 0x155C0",
        },
        {
            start: 0x15650,
            end: 0x156b0,
            title: "CLEAR REGION F 0x15650 - 0x156B0",
        },
    ];

    for (const w of windows) {
        raw(buf, w.start, w.end);
        decodeWindow(buf, w.start, w.end, w.title);
    }

    console.log("");
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log("TARGET STATE CONSTANTS");
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log("255880 = 0x255880");
    console.log("255884 = 0x255884");
    console.log("255888 = 0x255888");
    console.log("25588C = 0x25588C");
    console.log("255890 = 0x255890");
    console.log("255894 = 0x255894");
    console.log("255898 = 0x255898");
    console.log("25589C = 0x25589C");
    console.log("2558A0 = 0x2558A0");
    console.log("2558A4 = 0x2558A4");
}

main();

import { Address, Cell } from '@ton/core';
import { readFileSync } from 'fs';

const ROOSTER_BOC = './rooster_v22_generated.boc';
const PTON_BOC = './pton_v22_generated.boc';

const EXPECTED_USER = Address.parse(
    'EQDxn5MLbFyV649-OBviQrMp5KqWjGHNVwFM6GTeaLl8N0Ix'
);

const EXPECTED_ROUTER_PTON_WALLET = Address.parse(
    'EQCSIMGBps_qzRG3uPYhON8bucyCtu0mYdL1-u4gSz77IBa3'
);

const EXPECTED_ROUTER_ROOSTER_WALLET = Address.parse(
    'EQCePy3CW0qsWj03tvCwBxpavtYcu27LURfEy7H6tRhTbwsq'
);

const EXPECTED_MIN_LP_OUT = 150109265n;

function loadCell(path: string): Cell {
    const data = readFileSync(path);
    return Cell.fromBoc(data)[0];
}

function addressText(address: Address | null): string {
    return address ? address.toString() : 'null';
}

function check(
    name: string,
    actual: string,
    expected: string
) {
    console.log(
        `${name}:`,
        actual === expected ? 'YES' : 'NO'
    );

    if (actual !== expected) {
        console.log('  ACTUAL  :', actual);
        console.log('  EXPECTED:', expected);
    }
}

function checkBigInt(
    name: string,
    actual: bigint,
    expected: bigint
) {
    console.log(
        `${name}:`,
        actual === expected ? 'YES' : 'NO'
    );

    if (actual !== expected) {
        console.log('  ACTUAL  :', actual.toString());
        console.log('  EXPECTED:', expected.toString());
    }
}


/*
 * STON.fi v2.2 PROVIDE_LP payload
 *
 * createProvideLiquidityBody() from @ston-fi/sdk 2.7.0:
 *
 * beginCell()
 *   .storeUint(PROVIDE_LP, 32)
 *   .storeAddress(routerWalletAddress)
 *   .storeAddress(refundAddress)
 *   .storeAddress(excessesAddress ?? refundAddress)
 *   .storeUint(deadline, 64)
 *   .storeRef(
 *      beginCell()
 *        .storeCoins(minLpOut)
 *        .storeAddress(receiverAddress)
 *        .storeUint(bothPositive ? 1 : 0, 1)
 *        .storeCoins(dexCustomPayloadForwardGasAmount ?? 0)
 *        .storeMaybeRef(dexCustomPayload)
 *        .endCell()
 *   )
 *   .endCell()
 */
function decodeProvideLp(cell: Cell) {

    const s = cell.beginParse();

    const opcode = s.loadUint(32);

    const routerWallet = s.loadAddress();

    const refundAddress = s.loadAddress();

    const excessesAddress = s.loadAddress();

    const deadline = s.loadUintBig(64);

    const nested = s.loadRef();

    const n = nested.beginParse();

    const minLpOut = n.loadCoins();

    const receiverAddress = n.loadAddress();

    const bothPositive = n.loadBit();

    const dexCustomPayloadForwardGasAmount =
        n.loadCoins();

    const hasDexCustomPayload = n.loadBit();

    if (hasDexCustomPayload) {
        n.loadRef();
    }

    return {
        opcode,

        routerWallet,

        refundAddress,

        excessesAddress,

        deadline,

        minLpOut,

        receiverAddress,

        bothPositive,

        dexCustomPayloadForwardGasAmount,

        hasDexCustomPayload,

        remainingBits: s.remainingBits,

        remainingRefs: s.remainingRefs,

        nestedRemainingBits: n.remainingBits,

        nestedRemainingRefs: n.remainingRefs,
    };
}


/*
 * ROOSTER SIDE
 *
 * Jetton Transfer:
 *
 * opcode
 * queryId
 * amount
 * destination
 * responseDestination
 * customPayload
 * forwardTonAmount
 * forwardPayload
 */
function decodeRooster() {

    const root = loadCell(ROOSTER_BOC);

    const s = root.beginParse();

    const opcode = s.loadUint(32);

    const queryId = s.loadUintBig(64);

    const amount = s.loadCoins();

    const destination = s.loadAddress();

    const responseDestination = s.loadAddress();

    const hasCustomPayload = s.loadBit();

    if (hasCustomPayload) {
        s.loadRef();
    }

    const forwardTonAmount = s.loadCoins();

    const hasForwardPayload = s.loadBit();

    let provide = null;

    if (hasForwardPayload) {

        const forwardPayload = s.loadRef();

        provide = decodeProvideLp(
            forwardPayload
        );
    }

    return {
        rootBits: root.bits.length,

        rootRefs: root.refs.length,

        opcode,

        queryId,

        amount,

        destination,

        responseDestination,

        hasCustomPayload,

        forwardTonAmount,

        hasForwardPayload,

        remainingBits: s.remainingBits,

        remainingRefs: s.remainingRefs,

        provide,
    };
}


/*
 * pTON v2.1 SIDE
 *
 * TON_TRANSFER:
 *
 * opcode
 * queryId
 * tonAmount
 * refundAddress
 * forwardPayload
 */
function decodePton() {

    const root = loadCell(PTON_BOC);

    const s = root.beginParse();

    const opcode = s.loadUint(32);

    const queryId = s.loadUintBig(64);

    const tonAmount = s.loadCoins();

    const refundAddress = s.loadAddress();

    const hasForwardPayload = s.loadBit();

    let provide = null;

    if (hasForwardPayload) {

        const forwardPayload = s.loadRef();

        provide = decodeProvideLp(
            forwardPayload
        );
    }

    return {
        rootBits: root.bits.length,

        rootRefs: root.refs.length,

        opcode,

        queryId,

        tonAmount,

        refundAddress,

        hasForwardPayload,

        remainingBits: s.remainingBits,

        remainingRefs: s.remainingRefs,

        provide,
    };
}


function printProvide(
    side: string,
    provide: ReturnType<typeof decodeProvideLp> | null
) {

    console.log('');

    console.log(
        `================ ${side} PROVIDE_LP =================`
    );

    if (!provide) {

        console.log(
            'PROVIDE_LP NOT FOUND'
        );

        return;
    }

    console.log(
        'OPCODE:',
        '0x' + provide.opcode.toString(16)
    );

    console.log(
        'ROUTER WALLET:',
        addressText(provide.routerWallet)
    );

    console.log(
        'REFUND:',
        addressText(provide.refundAddress)
    );

    console.log(
        'EXCESSES:',
        addressText(provide.excessesAddress)
    );

    console.log(
        'DEADLINE:',
        provide.deadline.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'MIN LP OUT:',
        provide.minLpOut.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'RECEIVER:',
        addressText(provide.receiverAddress)
    );

    console.log(
        'BOTH POSITIVE:',
        provide.bothPositive
    );

    console.log(
        'CUSTOM PAYLOAD FORWARD GAS:',
        provide.dexCustomPayloadForwardGasAmount.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'CUSTOM PAYLOAD:',
        provide.hasDexCustomPayload
            ? 'YES'
            : 'NO'
    );

    console.log(
        'PROVIDE REMAINING BITS:',
        provide.remainingBits
    );

    console.log(
        'PROVIDE REMAINING REFS:',
        provide.remainingRefs
    );

    console.log(
        'NESTED REMAINING BITS:',
        provide.nestedRemainingBits
    );

    console.log(
        'NESTED REMAINING REFS:',
        provide.nestedRemainingRefs
    );
}

FILE: scripts\decodeBuildLiquidityV22.ts

import { Address, Cell } from '@ton/core';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';
import { DEX } from '@ston-fi/sdk';

const ROUTER = Address.parse(
    'EQCS4UEa5UaJLzOyyKieqQOQ2P9M-7kXpkO5HnP3Bv250cN3'
);

const ROOSTER = Address.parse(
    'EQCyuv-cYXFibA05bhiL3seM3Ur2B_bpsc364Zb8F3NMtrYx'
);

const PTON = Address.parse(
    'EQBnGWMCf3-FZZq1W4IWcWiGAc3PHuZ0_H-7sad2oY00o83S'
);

const USER = Address.parse(
    'EQDxn5MLbFyV649-OBviQrMp5KqWjGHNVwFM6GTeaLl8N0Ix'
);

function printBody(
    title: string,
    body: unknown
) {
    console.log('');
    console.log('================================================');
    console.log(title);
    console.log('================================================');
    console.log('');

    if (!body) {
        console.log('Body: NONE');
        return;
    }

    console.log(
        'Body type:',
        body.constructor?.name ?? typeof body
    );

    try {
        const cell = body as Cell;

        console.log(
            'Cell hash:',
            cell.hash().toString('hex')
        );

        console.log(
            'Cell BOC hex length:',
            cell.toBoc().toString('hex').length
        );

        console.log('');
        console.log('BOC hex:');
        console.log(
            cell.toBoc().toString('hex')
        );

    } catch (error) {
        console.log('');
        console.log(
            'Body could not be decoded as Cell.'
        );

        console.log(
            String(error)
        );
    }
}

export async function run(
    provider: NetworkProvider
) {

    console.log('');
    console.log('================================================');
    console.log('      STON.fi V2.2 LIQUIDITY BODY CHECK');
    console.log('================================================');
    console.log('');
    console.log('READ-ONLY');
    console.log('NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT.');
    console.log('');

    const router =
        DEX.v2_2.Router.CPI.create(ROUTER);

    const proxyTon =
        DEX.v2_2.pTON.create(PTON);

    const routerProvider =
        provider.provider(ROUTER);

    // ================================================
    // GET REAL POOL
    // ================================================

    console.log('Getting Pool from Router...');

    const pool =
        await router.getPool(
            routerProvider,
            {
                token0: ROOSTER,
                token1: PTON,
            }
        );

    console.log('');
    console.log('Pool returned by Router:');
    console.log(
        pool.address.toString()
    );

    // ================================================
    // READ POOL DATA
    // ================================================

    const poolProvider =
        provider.provider(pool.address);

    const poolData =
        await pool.getPoolData(poolProvider);

    console.log('');
    console.log('===== CURRENT POOL DATA =====');

    console.log(
        'ROOSTER reserve:',
        poolData.reserve0.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'pTON reserve:',
        poolData.reserve1.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'Total LP:',
        poolData.totalSupplyLP.toString()
    );

    // ================================================
    // LIQUIDITY AMOUNTS
    // ================================================

    const roosterAmount =
        100_000n * 1_000_000_000n;

    const ptonAmount =
        1n * 1_000_000_000n;

    const lpFromRooster =
        roosterAmount *
        poolData.totalSupplyLP /
        poolData.reserve0;

    const lpFromPton =
        ptonAmount *
        poolData.totalSupplyLP /
        poolData.reserve1;

    const expectedLp =
        lpFromRooster < lpFromPton
            ? lpFromRooster
            : lpFromPton;

    const minLpOut =
        expectedLp * 95n / 100n;

    const deadline =
        Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000) + 3600;

    console.log('');
    console.log('===== LIQUIDITY PARAMETERS =====');

    console.log(
        'ROOSTER amount:',
        roosterAmount.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'pTON amount:',
        ptonAmount.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'Expected LP:',
        expectedLp.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'minLpOut:',
        minLpOut.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'deadline:',
        deadline.toString()
    );

    // ================================================
    // BUILD ROOSTER TRANSACTION
    // ================================================

    console.log('');
    console.log('Building ROOSTER transaction...');

    const roosterTx =
        await router.getProvideLiquidityJettonTxParams(
            routerProvider,
            {
                sendTokenAddress: ROOSTER,
                otherTokenAddress: PTON,
                sendAmount: roosterAmount,
                userWalletAddress: USER,
                receiverAddress: USER,
                refundAddress: USER,
                excessesAddress: USER,
                minLpOut,
                deadline,
            }
        );

    console.log('');
    console.log('===== ROOSTER TRANSACTION =====');

    console.log(
        'Destination:',
        roosterTx.to.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'Value:',
        roosterTx.value.toString()
    );

    printBody(
        'ROOSTER TRANSACTION BODY',
        roosterTx.body
    );

    // ================================================
    // BUILD pTON TRANSACTION
    // ================================================

    console.log('');
    console.log('Building pTON transaction...');

    const ptonTx =
        await router.getProvideLiquidityTonTxParams(
            routerProvider,
            {
                proxyTon,
                otherTokenAddress: ROOSTER,
                sendAmount: ptonAmount,
                userWalletAddress: USER,
                receiverAddress: USER,
                refundAddress: USER,
                excessesAddress: USER,
                minLpOut,
                deadline,
            }
        );

    console.log('');
    console.log('===== pTON TRANSACTION =====');

    console.log(
        'Destination:',
        ptonTx.to.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'Value:',
        ptonTx.value.toString()
    );

    printBody(
        'pTON TRANSACTION BODY',
        ptonTx.body
    );

    // ================================================
    // FINAL
    // ================================================

    console.log('');
    console.log('================================================');
    console.log('                 FINAL RESULT');
    console.log('================================================');
    console.log('');

    console.log(
        'Pool:',
        pool.address.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'ROOSTER TX built: YES'
    );

    console.log(
        'pTON TX built: YES'
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log('NO TRANSACTION WAS SENT.');
    console.log('');
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\decodeCall317503.ts

import fs from "fs";

const wasmPath = "emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";
const target = 0x00317503;

const b = fs.readFileSync(wasmPath);

function readULEB(pos: number) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let p = pos;

  while (true) {
    const x = b[p++];
    value |= (x & 0x7f) << shift;

    if ((x & 0x80) === 0) break;

    shift += 7;
    if (shift > 35) throw new Error("ULEB too long");
  }

  return { value: value >>> 0, next: p };
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("DECODE CALL @ 0x00317503");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

console.log(`BYTE @ target = 0x${b[target].toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`);

if (b[target] !== 0x10) {
  console.log("ERROR: target byte is not CALL opcode 0x10");
  process.exit(1);
}

const r = readULEB(target + 1);

console.log(`CALL ULEB VALUE = ${r.value}`);
console.log(`CALL TARGET FUNC = ${r.value}`);
console.log(`NEXT BYTE       = 0x${b[r.next].toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`);

console.log("");
console.log("RAW CONTEXT");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

for (let p = target - 12; p <= target + 12; p++) {
  console.log(
    `0x${p.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  ${b[p].toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`
  );
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("NO WASM EXECUTION");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

import fs from "fs";

const wasmPath =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(wasmPath);

const START = 0x258525;
const END   = 0x258620;

function readULEB(pos: { value: number }): number {
    let result = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = wasm[pos.value++];
        result |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return result >>> 0;
        }

        shift += 7;
    }
}

function hex(n: number): string {
    return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" CALLER OF FUNCTION 3550 - DECODER v1");
console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log();
console.log(`WASM size : ${wasm.length.toLocaleString()}`);
console.log(`Range     : ${hex(START)} .. ${hex(END)}`);
console.log();

let p = START;

while (p < END) {
    const at = p;
    const op = wasm[p++];

    switch (op) {

        case 0x20: {
            const tmp = { value: p };
            const local = readULEB(tmp);
            p = tmp.value;
            console.log(`${hex(at)} local.get ${local}`);
            break;
        }

        case 0x21: {
            const tmp = { value: p };
            const local = readULEB(tmp);
            p = tmp.value;
            console.log(`${hex(at)} local.set ${local}`);
            break;
        }

        case 0x22: {
            const tmp = { value: p };
            const local = readULEB(tmp);
            p = tmp.value;
            console.log(`${hex(at)} local.tee ${local}`);
            break;
        }

        case 0x28: {
            const tmp = { value: p };
            const align = readULEB(tmp);
            const offset = readULEB(tmp);
            p = tmp.value;
            console.log(
                `${hex(at)} i32.load align=${align} offset=${offset}`
            );
            break;
        }

        case 0x29: {
            const tmp = { value: p };
            const align = readULEB(tmp);
            const offset = readULEB(tmp);
            p = tmp.value;
            console.log(
                `${hex(at)} i64.load align=${align} offset=${offset}`
            );
            break;
        }

        case 0x36: {
            const tmp = { value: p };
            const align = readULEB(tmp);
            const offset = readULEB(tmp);
            p = tmp.value;
            console.log(
                `${hex(at)} i32.store align=${align} offset=${offset}`
            );
            break;
        }

        case 0x37: {
            const tmp = { value: p };
            const align = readULEB(tmp);
            const offset = readULEB(tmp);
            p = tmp.value;
            console.log(
                `${hex(at)} i64.store align=${align} offset=${offset}`
            );
            break;
        }

        case 0x3a: {
            const tmp = { value: p };
            const align = readULEB(tmp);
            const offset = readULEB(tmp);
            p = tmp.value;
            console.log(
                `${hex(at)} i32.store8 align=${align} offset=${offset}`
            );
            break;
        }

        case 0x41: {
            const tmp = { value: p };
            const value = readULEB(tmp);
            p = tmp.value;
            console.log(`${hex(at)} i32.const ${value}`);
            break;
        }

        case 0x42: {
            const tmp = { value: p };
            let value = 0n;
            let shift = 0n;

            while (true) {
                const b = wasm[tmp.value++];
                value |= BigInt(b & 0x7f) << shift;

                if ((b & 0x80) === 0) break;

                shift += 7n;
            }

            p = tmp.value;

            console.log(
                `${hex(at)} i64.const ${value} (0x${value.toString(16)})`
            );
            break;
        }

        case 0x10: {
            const tmp = { value: p };
            const funcIndex = readULEB(tmp);
            p = tmp.value;
            console.log(`${hex(at)} call ${funcIndex}`);
            break;
        }

        case 0x11: {
            const tmp = { value: p };
            const typeIndex = readULEB(tmp);
            const tableIndex = readULEB(tmp);
            p = tmp.value;
            console.log(
                `${hex(at)} call_indirect type=${typeIndex} table=${tableIndex}`
            );
            break;
        }

        case 0x1a:
            console.log(`${hex(at)} drop`);
            break;

        case 0x24: {
            const globalIndex = wasm[p++];
            console.log(`${hex(at)} global.set ${globalIndex}`);
            break;
        }

        case 0x23: {
            const globalIndex = wasm[p++];
            console.log(`${hex(at)} global.get ${globalIndex}`);
            break;
        }

        case 0x45:
            console.log(`${hex(at)} i32.eqz`);
            break;

        case 0x46:
            console.log(`${hex(at)} i32.eq`);
            break;

        case 0x47:
            console.log(`${hex(at)} i32.ne`);
            break;

        case 0x48:
            console.log(`${hex(at)} i32.lt_s`);
            break;

        case 0x49:
            console.log(`${hex(at)} i32.lt_u`);
            break;

        case 0x4a:
            console.log(`${hex(at)} i32.gt_s`);
            break;

        case 0x4b:
            console.log(`${hex(at)} i32.gt_u`);
            break;

        case 0x6a:
            console.log(`${hex(at)} i32.add`);
            break;

        case 0x6b:
            console.log(`${hex(at)} i32.sub`);
            break;

        case 0x7c:
            console.log(`${hex(at)} i64.add`);
            break;

        case 0x7d:
            console.log(`${hex(at)} i64.sub`);
            break;

        case 0x7e:
            console.log(`${hex(at)} i64.mul`);
            break;
case 0x72:
    console.log(`${hex(at)} i32.or`);
    break;

        case 0x83:
            console.log(`${hex(at)} i64.and`);
            break;

        case 0x84:
            console.log(`${hex(at)} i64.or`);
            break;

        case 0x85:
            console.log(`${hex(at)} i64.xor`);
            break;

        case 0x86:
            console.log(`${hex(at)} i64.shl`);
            break;

        case 0x87:
            console.log(`${hex(at)} i64.shr_s`);
            break;

        case 0x88:
            console.log(`${hex(at)} i64.shr_u`);
            break;

        case 0x04: {
            const blockType = wasm[p++];
            console.log(
                `${hex(at)} if blockType=0x${blockType.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`
            );
            break;
        }

        case 0x05:
            console.log(`${hex(at)} else`);
            break;

        case 0x02: {
            const blockType = wasm[p++];
            console.log(
                `${hex(at)} block type=0x${blockType.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`
            );
            break;
        }

        case 0x03: {
            const blockType = wasm[p++];
            console.log(
                `${hex(at)} loop type=0x${blockType.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`
            );
            break;
        }

        case 0x0c: {
            const tmp = { value: p };
            const depth = readULEB(tmp);
            p = tmp.value;
            console.log(`${hex(at)} br ${depth}`);
            break;
        }

        case 0x0d: {
            const tmp = { value: p };
            const depth = readULEB(tmp);
            p = tmp.value;
            console.log(`${hex(at)} br_if ${depth}`);
            break;
        }

        case 0x0f:
            console.log(`${hex(at)} return`);
            break;

        case 0x0b:
            console.log(`${hex(at)} end`);
            break;

        case 0xfc: {
            const tmp = { value: p };
            const subOpcode = readULEB(tmp);
            p = tmp.value;

            console.log(
                `${hex(at)} 0xfc subopcode=${subOpcode}`
            );

            if (subOpcode === 0x0b) {
                const tmp2 = { value: p };
                const memidx = readULEB(tmp2);
                p = tmp2.value;

                console.log(
                    `  memory.fill memidx=${memidx}`
                );
            } else {
                console.log(
                    `  extended opcode not yet decoded`
                );
            }

            break;
        }
case 0x2d: {
    const tmp = { value: p };
    const align = readULEB(tmp);
    const offset = readULEB(tmp);
    p = tmp.value;

    console.log(
        `${hex(at)} i32.load8_u align=${align} offset=${offset}`
    );
    break;
}

        default:
            console.log(
                `${hex(at)} UNKNOWN 0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`
            );
            console.log();
            console.log(`Stopped at ${hex(at)}`);
            process.exit(0);
    }
}

import {
    LiteClient,
    LiteRoundRobinEngine,
    LiteSingleEngine,
} from 'ton-lite-client';

import { BitReader } from '@ton/core';

async function main() {
    const servers = [
        {
            ip: -1468571697,
            port: 27787,
            key: 'Y/QVf6G5VDiKTZOKitbFVm067WsuocTN8Vg036A4zGk=',
        },
        {
            ip: -1468575011,
            port: 51088,
            key: 'Sy5ghr3EahQd/1rDayzZXt5+inlfF+7kLfkZDJcU/ek=',
        },
    ];

    const engines: LiteSingleEngine[] = [];

    for (const server of servers) {
        engines.push(
            new LiteSingleEngine({
                host: `tcp://${intToIP(server.ip)}:${server.port}`,
                publicKey: Buffer.from(server.key, 'base64'),
                reconnectTimeout: 3000,
            })
        );
    }

    const engine = new LiteRoundRobinEngine(engines);

    const client = new LiteClient({
        engine,
        batchSize: 1,
    });

    try {
        console.log('===== DECODE CONFIG 43 =====');

        const master = await client.getMasterchainInfo();

        console.log('Seqno:', master.last.seqno);

        const result = await client.getConfig(master.last);

        const config43 = result.config.get(43);

        if (!config43) {
            console.log('Config 43 NOT FOUND');
            return;
        }

        console.log('');
        console.log('Bits:', config43.bits.length);
        console.log('Refs:', config43.refs.length);
        console.log('Type:', config43.type);

        console.log('');
        console.log('Bits:');
        console.log(config43.bits.toString());

       const reader = new BitReader(config43.bits);

console.log('');
console.log('===== RAW UINT32 FIELDS =====');

let index = 0;

while (reader.remaining >= 32) {
    const value = reader.loadUint(32);

    console.log(
        `UINT32[${index}]:`,
        value.toString()
    );

    index++;
}

console.log('');
console.log('Remaining bits:', reader.remaining);

        console.log('');
        console.log('===== DONE =====');

    } finally {
        for (const e of engines) {
            e.close();
        }
    }
}

function intToIP(int: number) {
    const part1 = int & 255;
    const part2 = (int >> 8) & 255;
    const part3 = (int >> 16) & 255;
    const part4 = (int >> 24) & 255;

    return `${part4}.${part3}.${part2}.${part1}`;
}

main().catch((error) => {
    console.error('');
    console.error('===== ERROR =====');
    console.error(error);
    process.exit(1);
});

export async function run() {
    await main();
}

import {
    LiteClient,
    LiteRoundRobinEngine,
    LiteSingleEngine,
} from 'ton-lite-client';

import {
    loadConfigParam,
} from '../node_modules/@ton/sandbox/dist/config/config.tlb-gen';

async function main() {
    const servers = [
        {
            ip: -1468571697,
            port: 27787,
            key: 'Y/QVf6G5VDiKTZOKitbFVm067WsuocTN8Vg036A4zGk=',
        },
        {
            ip: -1468575011,
            port: 51088,
            key: 'Sy5ghr3EahQd/1rDayzZXt5+inlfF+7kLfkZDJcU/ek=',
        },
    ];

    const engines: LiteSingleEngine[] = [];

    for (const server of servers) {
        engines.push(
            new LiteSingleEngine({
                host: `tcp://${intToIP(server.ip)}:${server.port}`,
                publicKey: Buffer.from(server.key, 'base64'),
                reconnectTimeout: 3000,
            })
        );
    }

    const engine = new LiteRoundRobinEngine(engines);

    const client = new LiteClient({
        engine,
        batchSize: 1,
    });

    try {
        console.log('===== REAL CONFIG 43 DECODER =====');

        const master = await client.getMasterchainInfo();

        console.log('Seqno:', master.last.seqno);

        const result = await client.getConfig(master.last);

        const config43 = result.config.get(43);

        if (!config43) {
            console.log('Config 43 NOT FOUND');
            return;
        }

        console.log('');
        console.log('===== CONFIG 43 =====');

        console.log('Bits:', config43.bits.length);
        console.log('Refs:', config43.refs.length);
        console.log('Type:', config43.type);

        console.log('');
        console.log('Bits:');
        console.log(config43.bits.toString());

        console.log('');
        console.log('===== LOAD CONFIG PARAM 43 =====');

        const decodedParam = loadConfigParam(
            config43.beginParse(),
            43
        );

        console.log('');
        console.log('Decoded object:');

        console.dir(decodedParam, {
            depth: 10,
        });

        console.log('');

        if (decodedParam.kind === 'ConfigParam__38') {

            const value = decodedParam.anon0;

            console.log(
                'Config kind:',
                decodedParam.kind
            );

            console.log('');
            console.log('===== SIZE LIMITS CONFIG =====');

            console.dir(value, {
                depth: 10,
            });

            console.log('');
            console.log('===== LIBRARY LIMITS =====');

            console.log(
                'max_msg_bits:',
                value.max_msg_bits
            );

            console.log(
                'max_msg_cells:',
                value.max_msg_cells
            );

            console.log(
                'max_library_cells:',
                value.max_library_cells
            );

            console.log(
                'max_vm_data_depth:',
                value.max_vm_data_depth
            );

            if (
                'max_acc_public_libraries' in value
            ) {
                console.log(
                    'max_acc_public_libraries:',
                    value.max_acc_public_libraries
                );
            }

            if (
                'defer_out_queue_size_limit' in value
            ) {
                console.log(
                    'defer_out_queue_size_limit:',
                    value.defer_out_queue_size_limit
                );
            }

            if (
                'max_msg_extra_currencies' in value
            ) {
                console.log(
                    'max_msg_extra_currencies:',
                    value.max_msg_extra_currencies
                );
            }

            if (
                'max_transaction_library_loads' in value
            ) {
                console.log(
                    'max_transaction_library_loads:',
                    value.max_transaction_library_loads
                );
            }

        } else {

            console.log(
                'Unexpected ConfigParam kind:',
                decodedParam.kind
            );
        }

        console.log('');
        console.log('===== DONE =====');

    } finally {
        for (const e of engines) {
            e.close();
        }
    }
}

function intToIP(int: number) {
    const part1 = int & 255;
    const part2 = (int >> 8) & 255;
    const part3 = (int >> 16) & 255;
    const part4 = (int >> 24) & 255;

    return `${part4}.${part3}.${part2}.${part1}`;
}

main().catch((error) => {
    console.error('');

import { Cell } from '@ton/core';

const BOC =
    'te6cckEBAwEA1QABaHNi0JwAAAAAAAAAAGtfdACC7EgB4z8mFti5K9ce/HA3xIVlU8lVLRjDmq4CmdDJvNFy+G8BAeE3wJbfgBJEGDA02f1Zojb3HsQnG+N3OZBW3aTMOl6/XcQJZ99kEAPGfkwtsXJXrj344G+JCsynkqpaMYc1XAUzoZN5ouXw3gB4z8mFti5K9ce/HA3xIVlU8lVLRjDmq4CmdDJvNFy+G4AAAAA1XRpSQAIAT1Re0BqvaAHjPyYW2Lkr1x78cDfEhWZTyVUtGMOargKZ0Mm80XL4bwSXKMfy';

console.log('==============================================');
console.log(' STON.fi v2.2 CURRENT JETTON_NOTIFY DECODER');
console.log(' REAL TRANSACTION BOC');
console.log(' READ ONLY - OFFLINE');
console.log('==============================================');

const cell = Cell.fromBase64(BOC);

console.log('');
console.log('ROOT BITS:', cell.bits.length);
console.log('ROOT REFS:', cell.refs.length);
console.log('BOC HASH:', cell.hash().toString('hex'));

const s = cell.beginParse();

const opcode = s.loadUint(32);
const queryId = s.loadUintBig(64);
const amount = s.loadCoins();

const destination = s.loadAddress();
const responseDestination = s.loadAddress();

const hasCustomPayload = s.loadBit();

let customPayload = null;

if (hasCustomPayload) {
    customPayload = s.loadRef();
}

const forwardTonAmount = s.loadCoins();
const forwardPayloadIsRef = s.loadBit();

let forwardPayload;

if (forwardPayloadIsRef) {
    forwardPayload = s.loadRef();
} else {
    forwardPayload = s.asCell();
}

console.log('');
console.log('========== JETTON NOTIFY ==========');

console.log(
    'OPCODE:',
    '0x' + opcode.toString(16).padStart(8, '0')
);

console.log(
    'QUERY ID:',
    queryId.toString()
);

console.log(
    'AMOUNT RAW:',
    amount.toString()
);

console.log(
    'AMOUNT ROOSTER:',
    (Number(amount) / 1e9).toFixed(9)
);

console.log(
    'DESTINATION:',
    destination?.toString()
);

console.log(
    'RESPONSE DESTINATION:',
    responseDestination?.toString()
);

console.log(
    'CUSTOM PAYLOAD:',
    hasCustomPayload
);

console.log(
    'FORWARD TON RAW:',
    forwardTonAmount.toString()
);

console.log(
    'FORWARD TON:',
    (Number(forwardTonAmount) / 1e9).toFixed(9)
);

console.log(
    'FORWARD PAYLOAD IS REF:',
    forwardPayloadIsRef
);

console.log('');
console.log('========== PROVIDE_LP ==========');

console.log(
    'FORWARD PAYLOAD BITS:',
    forwardPayload.bits.length
);

console.log(
    'FORWARD PAYLOAD REFS:',
    forwardPayload.refs.length
);

const p = forwardPayload.beginParse();

const provideOpcode =
    p.loadUint(32);

const routerWalletAddress =
    p.loadAddress();

const refundAddress =
    p.loadAddress();

const excessesAddress =
    p.loadAddress();

const deadline =
    p.loadUintBig(64);

console.log('');
console.log(
    'PROVIDE_LP OPCODE:',
    '0x' +
    provideOpcode.toString(16).padStart(8, '0')
);

console.log(
    'ROUTER WALLET:',
    routerWalletAddress?.toString()
);

console.log(
    'REFUND ADDRESS:',
    refundAddress?.toString()
);

console.log(
    'EXCESSES ADDRESS:',
    excessesAddress?.toString()
);

console.log(
    'DEADLINE:',
    deadline.toString()
);

console.log('');
console.log('PROVIDE_LP ROOT REMAINING BITS:', p.remainingBits);
console.log('PROVIDE_LP ROOT REMAINING REFS:', p.remainingRefs);

if (p.remainingRefs < 1) {
    throw new Error(
        'PROVIDE_LP reference is missing'
    );
}

const ref = p.loadRef();
const r = ref.beginParse();

const minLpOut =
    r.loadCoins();

const receiverAddress =
    r.loadAddress();

const bothPositive =
    r.loadUint(1);

const dexCustomPayloadForwardGasAmount =
    r.loadCoins();

const hasDexCustomPayload =
    r.loadBit();

console.log('');
console.log('========== PROVIDE_LP REF ==========');

console.log(
    'MIN LP OUT RAW:',
    minLpOut.toString()
);

console.log(
    'MIN LP OUT:',
    (Number(minLpOut) / 1e9).toFixed(9)
);

console.log(
    'RECEIVER:',
    receiverAddress?.toString()
);

console.log(
    'BOTH POSITIVE:',
    bothPositive
);

console.log(
    'DEX CUSTOM PAYLOAD GAS RAW:',
    dexCustomPayloadForwardGasAmount.toString()
);

console.log(
    'DEX CUSTOM PAYLOAD GAS:',
    (Number(dexCustomPayloadForwardGasAmount) / 1e9).toFixed(9)
);

console.log(
    'HAS DEX CUSTOM PAYLOAD:',
    hasDexCustomPayload
);

console.log('');
console.log('========== REMAINING ==========');

console.log(
    'ROOT REMAINING BITS:',
    s.remainingBits
);

console.log(
    'ROOT REMAINING REFS:',
    s.remainingRefs
);

console.log(
    'PROVIDE_LP REMAINING BITS:',
    p.remainingBits
);

console.log(
    'PROVIDE_LP REMAINING REFS:',
    p.remainingRefs
);

console.log(
    'REF REMAINING BITS:',
    r.remainingBits
);

console.log(
    'REF REMAINING REFS:',
    r.remainingRefs
);

console.log('');
console.log('==============================================');
console.log(' END - READ ONLY');
console.log('==============================================');

FILE: scripts\decodeCurrentPoolAmountsV22.ts

import { Address } from '@ton/core';
import { TonClient } from '@ton/ton';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

const POOL = Address.parse(
    'EQD5ALaCuEqJ7Mi9qlXOA7BHMrTq8e2fZ5B_teWN17MZq-cf'
);

const TARGET_LT = 99491281000009n;

function readUint(slice: any, bits: number, name: string) {
    try {
        const value = slice.loadUintBig(bits);
        console.log(`${name}: ${value.toString()}`);
        return value;
    } catch (e) {
        console.log(`${name}: <FAILED>`);
        console.log(`  ${String(e)}`);
        return null;
    }
}

function readAddress(slice: any, name: string) {
    try {
        const address = slice.loadAddress();
        console.log(`${name}: ${address?.toString() ?? 'null'}`);
        return address;
    } catch (e) {
        console.log(`${name}: <FAILED>`);
        console.log(`  ${String(e)}`);
        return null;
    }
}

function dumpBits(cell: any, label: string) {
    console.log('');
    console.log(`===== ${label} RAW CELL =====`);
    console.log(`bits: ${cell.bits.length}`);
    console.log(`refs: ${cell.refs.length}`);

    try {
        console.log(
            cell.toBoc().toString('base64')
        );
    } catch {
        console.log('<BOC ERROR>');
    }
}

function decodeProvideLp(body: any) {
    console.log('');
    console.log('==============================================');
    console.log('DECODE stonfi_provide_lp_v2');
    console.log('==============================================');

    dumpBits(body, 'INPUT');

    const s = body.beginParse();

    readUint(s, 32, 'opcode');
    readUint(s, 64, 'query_id');

    console.log('');
    console.log('--- candidate fields ---');

    readUint(s, 128, 'field_1_uint128');
    readUint(s, 128, 'field_2_uint128');

    readAddress(s, 'field_address_1');

    readUint(s, 64, 'field_uint64_1');
    readUint(s, 128, 'field_uint128_3');

    console.log('');
    console.log('Remaining bits:', s.remainingBits);
    console.log('Remaining refs:', s.remainingRefs);
}

function decodeAddLiquidity(body: any) {
    console.log('');
    console.log('==============================================');
    console.log('DECODE stonfi_add_liquidity_v2');
    console.log('==============================================');

    dumpBits(body, 'OUTPUT');

    const s = body.beginParse();

    readUint(s, 32, 'opcode');
    readUint(s, 64, 'query_id');

    console.log('');
    console.log('--- candidate amount fields ---');

    const amount0 = readUint(s, 128, 'amount0_candidate');
    const amount1 = readUint(s, 128, 'amount1_candidate');

    console.log('');
    console.log('Remaining bits:', s.remainingBits);
    console.log('Remaining refs:', s.remainingRefs);

    console.log('');
    console.log('==============================================');
    console.log('INTERPRETATION');
    console.log('==============================================');

    if (amount0 !== null) {
        console.log(
            `amount0 raw: ${amount0.toString()}`
        );
    }

    if (amount1 !== null) {
        console.log(
            `amount1 raw: ${amount1.toString()}`
        );
    }
}

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {
    console.log('');
    console.log('================================================');
    console.log(' CURRENT POOL AMOUNT DECODER v2.2');
    console.log(' READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT');
    console.log('================================================');

    console.log('');
    console.log('POOL:');
    console.log(POOL.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log('TARGET LT:');
    console.log(TARGET_LT.toString());

    const client = provider.api() as TonClient;

    const txs = await client.getTransactions(
        POOL,
        {
            limit: 20,
        }
    );

    const tx = txs.find(
        (t) => t.lt === TARGET_LT
    );

    if (!tx) {
        console.log('');
        console.log('TARGET TRANSACTION NOT FOUND');
        console.log('No transaction was sent.');
        return;
    }

    console.log('');
    console.log('================================================');
    console.log('TARGET FOUND');
    console.log('================================================');

    console.log(`LT:   ${tx.lt.toString()}`);
    console.log(
        `HASH: ${tx.hash().toString('base64')}`
    );

    if (tx.now) {
        console.log(
            `TIME: ${new Date(tx.now * 1000).toISOString()}`
        );
    }

    if (!tx.inMessage) {
        console.log('No input message.');
        return;
    }

    const inBody = tx.inMessage.body;

    decodeProvideLp(inBody);

    console.log('');
    console.log('================================================');
    console.log('OUTPUT MESSAGE');
    console.log('================================================');

    for (let i = 0; i < tx.outMessagesCount; i++) {
        const msg = tx.outMessages.get(i);

        if (!msg) continue;

        const opBody = msg.body;

        let op = 'NONE';

        try {
            if (opBody.bits.length >= 32) {
                const p = opBody.beginParse();
                op =
                    '0x' +
                    p.loadUint(32)
                        .toString(16)
                        .padStart(8, '0');
            }
        } catch {}

        console.log('');
        console.log(`OUTPUT #${i + 1}`);
        console.log(`OPCODE: ${op}`);

        if (op === '0x50c6a654') {
            decodeAddLiquidity(opBody);
        } else {
            dumpBits(opBody, `OUTPUT #${i + 1}`);
        }
    }

    console.log('');
    console.log('================================================');
    console.log(' READ ONLY CHECK COMPLETE');
    console.log(' Transaction submission: NONE');
    console.log('================================================');
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\decodeCurrentPoolTxV22.ts

import { Address, Cell, TonClient } from '@ton/ton';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

const TARGET_POOL = Address.parse(
    'EQD5ALaCuEqJ7Mi9qlXOA7BHMrTq8e2fZ5B_teWN17MZq-cf'
);

const TARGET_LT = 99491281000009n;

const OP_PROVIDE_LP = 0x37c096df;
const OP_ADD_LIQUIDITY = 0x50c6a654;
const OP_JETTON_INTERNAL_TRANSFER = 0x178d4519;
const OP_CALLBACK_ADD_LIQUIDITY = 0x06ecd527;

function cellInfo(cell: Cell): string {
    return `${cell.bits.length} bits / ${cell.refs.length} refs`;
}

function opcodeHex(op: number | null): string {
    if (op === null) return 'UNKNOWN';
    return '0x' + op.toString(16).padStart(8, '0');
}

function getOpcode(cell: Cell): number | null {
    try {
        const s = cell.beginParse();

        if (s.remainingBits < 32) {
            return null;
        }

        return s.loadUint(32);
    } catch {
        return null;
    }
}

function formatInteger(value: bigint): string {
    return value.toString().replace(/\B(?=(\d{3})+(?!\d))/g, ',');
}

function formatRooster(value: bigint): string {
    const whole = value / 1_000_000_000n;

    const fraction = (value % 1_000_000_000n)
        .toString()
        .padStart(9, '0')
        .replace(/0+$/, '');

    if (fraction.length === 0) {
        return whole.toLocaleString();
    }

    return `${whole.toLocaleString()}.${fraction}`;
}

function formatTon(value: bigint): string {
    const whole = value / 1_000_000_000n;

    const fraction = (value % 1_000_000_000n)
        .toString()
        .padStart(9, '0')
        .replace(/0+$/, '');

    if (fraction.length === 0) {
        return whole.toLocaleString();
    }

    return `${whole.toLocaleString()}.${fraction}`;
}

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {
    console.log('');
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log(' STON.fi v2.2 CURRENT POOL TX DECODER');
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log('READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT');
    console.log('============================================================');

    console.log('');
    console.log('TARGET POOL:');
    console.log(TARGET_POOL.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log('TARGET LT:');
    console.log(TARGET_LT.toString());

    /*
     * Use the Blueprint network configuration only to obtain
     * the public testnet endpoint.
     *
     * No wallet is used and no transaction is submitted.
     */
    const client = new TonClient({
        endpoint: 'https://testnet.toncenter.com/api/v2/jsonRPC',
    });

    console.log('');
    console.log('Fetching Pool transactions...');

    const transactions = await client.getTransactions(TARGET_POOL, {
        limit: 100,
    });

    console.log(`Fetched ${transactions.length} transactions.`);

    let found = false;

    for (const tx of transactions) {
        const lt = BigInt(tx.lt);

        if (lt !== TARGET_LT) {
            continue;
        }

        found = true;

        console.log('');
        console.log('============================================================');
        console.log(' TARGET TRANSACTION FOUND');
        console.log('============================================================');

        console.log('');
        console.log('LT:');
        console.log(tx.lt.toString());

        console.log('');
        console.log('TRANSACTION HASH:');
        console.log(tx.hash().toString('base64'));

        console.log('');
        console.log('TIMESTAMP:');
        console.log(tx.now.toString());

        const inMessage = tx.inMessage;

        if (!inMessage) {
            console.log('');
            console.log('TARGET TRANSACTION HAS NO INPUT MESSAGE');
            break;
        }

        console.log('');
        console.log('============================================================');
        console.log(' INPUT MESSAGE');
        console.log('============================================================');

        console.log('');
        console.log('MESSAGE TYPE:');
        console.log(inMessage.info.type);

        if (inMessage.info.type === 'internal') {
            console.log('');
            console.log('SOURCE:');
            console.log(inMessage.info.src.toString());

            console.log('');
            console.log('DESTINATION:');
            console.log(inMessage.info.dest.toString());

            console.log('');
            console.log('ATTACHED VALUE:');
            console.log(
                `${inMessage.info.value.coins.toString()} nanoTON`
            );

            console.log('');
            console.log('ATTACHED TON:');
            console.log(
                formatTon(inMessage.info.value.coins)
            );
        }

        const body = inMessage.body;

        console.log('');
        console.log('INPUT BODY:');
        console.log(cellInfo(body));

        console.log('');
        console.log('INPUT BODY BOC:');
        console.log(
            body.toBoc().toString('base64')
        );

        const opcode = getOpcode(body);

        console.log('');
        console.log('INPUT OPCODE:');
        console.log(opcodeHex(opcode));

        if (opcode === OP_PROVIDE_LP) {
            console.log('IDENTIFICATION: STON.fi PROVIDE_LP');
        } else if (opcode === OP_ADD_LIQUIDITY) {
            console.log('IDENTIFICATION: STON.fi ADD_LIQUIDITY');
        } else if (opcode === OP_CALLBACK_ADD_LIQUIDITY) {
            console.log('IDENTIFICATION: STON.fi CALLBACK ADD_LIQUIDITY');
        } else {
            console.log('IDENTIFICATION: OTHER / UNKNOWN');
        }

        /*
         * Raw structural inspection of provide_lp.
         *
         * We intentionally do NOT assume the complete layout here.
         * The BOC is printed so that the exact v2.2 structure can be
         * decoded from the real transaction.
         */
        if (opcode === OP_PROVIDE_LP) {
            console.log('');
            console.log('============================================================');
            console.log(' PROVIDE_LP STRUCTURE');
            console.log('============================================================');

            try {
                const s = body.beginParse();

                s.loadUint(32);

                console.log('');
                console.log('REMAINING BITS AFTER OPCODE:');
                console.log(s.remainingBits);

                console.log('');
                console.log('REMAINING REFS AFTER OPCODE:');
                console.log(s.remainingRefs);

                if (s.remainingBits >= 64) {
                    const queryId = s.loadUintBig(64);

                    console.log('');
                    console.log('QUERY ID:');
                    console.log(queryId.toString());
                }

                console.log('');
                console.log('REMAINING BITS AFTER QUERY ID:');
                console.log(s.remainingBits);

                console.log('');
                console.log('REMAINING REFS:');
                console.log(s.remainingRefs);

            } catch (error) {
                console.log('');
                console.log('PROVIDE_LP STRUCTURE WARNING:');
                console.log(String(error));
            }
        }

        /*
         * OUTGOING MESSAGES
         */
        console.log('');
        console.log('============================================================');
        console.log(' OUTGOING MESSAGES');
        console.log('============================================================');

        let outIndex = 0;

        for (const outMessage of tx.outMessages.values()) {
            console.log('');
            console.log(`---------------- OUT #${outIndex} ----------------`);

            console.log('');
            console.log('MESSAGE TYPE:');
            console.log(outMessage.info.type);

            if (outMessage.info.type === 'internal') {
                console.log('');
                console.log('SOURCE:');
                console.log(outMessage.info.src.toString());

                console.log('');
                console.log('DESTINATION:');
                console.log(outMessage.info.dest.toString());

                console.log('');
                console.log('VALUE:');
                console.log(
                    `${outMessage.info.value.coins.toString()} nanoTON`
                );

                console.log('');
                console.log('VALUE TON:');
                console.log(
                    formatTon(outMessage.info.value.coins)
                );
            }

            const outBody = outMessage.body;

            console.log('');
            console.log('BODY:');
            console.log(cellInfo(outBody));

            console.log('');
            console.log('BODY BOC:');
            console.log(
                outBody.toBoc().toString('base64')
            );

            const outOpcode = getOpcode(outBody);

            console.log('');
            console.log('OPCODE:');
            console.log(opcodeHex(outOpcode));

            if (outOpcode === OP_ADD_LIQUIDITY) {
                console.log(
                    'IDENTIFICATION: STON.fi ADD_LIQUIDITY'
                );
            } else if (
                outOpcode === OP_JETTON_INTERNAL_TRANSFER
            ) {
                console.log(
                    'IDENTIFICATION: JETTON_INTERNAL_TRANSFER'
                );
            } else if (
                outOpcode === OP_CALLBACK_ADD_LIQUIDITY
            ) {
                console.log(
                    'IDENTIFICATION: STON.fi CALLBACK ADD_LIQUIDITY'
                );
            } else if (outOpcode === OP_PROVIDE_LP) {
                console.log(
                    'IDENTIFICATION: STON.fi PROVIDE_LP'
                );
            } else {
                console.log(
                    'IDENTIFICATION: OTHER / UNKNOWN'
                );
            }

            /*
             * Decode jetton_internal_transfer amount.
             */
            if (outOpcode === OP_JETTON_INTERNAL_TRANSFER) {
                console.log('');
                console.log(
                    'JETTON_INTERNAL_TRANSFER INSPECTION'
                );

                try {
                    const s = outBody.beginParse();

                    s.loadUint(32);

                    if (s.remainingBits >= 64) {
                        const queryId = s.loadUintBig(64);

                        console.log('');
                        console.log('QUERY ID:');
                        console.log(queryId.toString());
                    }

                    if (s.remainingBits >= 16) {
                        const amount = s.loadCoins();

                        console.log('');
                        console.log('JETTON AMOUNT RAW:');
                        console.log(
                            formatInteger(amount)
                        );

                        console.log('');
                        console.log(
                            'JETTON AMOUNT AS 9-DECIMAL TOKEN:'
                        );
                        console.log(
                            formatRooster(amount)
                        );
                    }

                    console.log('');
                    console.log('REMAINING BITS:');
                    console.log(s.remainingBits);

                    console.log('');
                    console.log('REMAINING REFS:');
                    console.log(s.remainingRefs);

                } catch (error) {
                    console.log('');
                    console.log(
                        'JETTON TRANSFER DECODER WARNING:'
                    );
                    console.log(String(error));
                }
            }

            /*
             * Decode add_liquidity structure only as far as safely
             * possible. The complete exact values will be decoded
             * from the real BOC in the next step if required.
             */
            if (outOpcode === OP_ADD_LIQUIDITY) {
                console.log('');
                console.log(
                    'ADD_LIQUIDITY STRUCTURE INSPECTION'
                );

                try {
                    const s = outBody.beginParse();

                    s.loadUint(32);

                    console.log('');
                    console.log('REMAINING BITS AFTER OPCODE:');
                    console.log(s.remainingBits);

                    console.log('');
                    console.log('REMAINING REFS:');
                    console.log(s.remainingRefs);

                    if (s.remainingBits >= 64) {
                        const queryId = s.loadUintBig(64);

                        console.log('');
                        console.log('QUERY ID:');
                        console.log(queryId.toString());
                    }

                } catch (error) {
                    console.log('');
                    console.log(
                        'ADD_LIQUIDITY STRUCTURE WARNING:'
                    );
                    console.log(String(error));
                }
            }

            outIndex++;
        }

        console.log('');
        console.log('============================================================');
        console.log(' RESULT');
        console.log('============================================================');

        console.log('');
        console.log('Target LT found: YES');

        console.log('');
        console.log('Transaction submission: NONE');
        console.log('ROOSTER transaction sent by this script: NO');
        console.log('pTON transaction sent by this script: NO');
        console.log('Pool transaction sent by this script: NO');

        console.log('');
        console.log('READ ONLY CHECK COMPLETE');
        console.log('============================================================');

        break;
    }

    if (!found) {
        console.log('');
        console.log('============================================================');
        console.log(' TARGET TRANSACTION NOT FOUND');
        console.log('============================================================');

        console.log('');
        console.log(
            `LT ${TARGET_LT.toString()} was not found in the fetched transactions.`
        );

        console.log('');
        console.log('READ ONLY CHECK COMPLETE');
        console.log('============================================================');
    }
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\decodeCurrentPtonLiquidity.ts

import { Cell, Slice } from '@ton/core';
import * as fs from 'fs';

const EXPECTED_PTON_OPCODE = 0x01F3835D;
const EXPECTED_PROVIDE_LP_OPCODE = 0x37C096DF;

const EXPECTED_ROUTER_WALLET =
    'EQCePy3CW0qsWj03tvCwBxpavtYcu27LURfEy7H6tRhTbwsq';

const EXPECTED_USER =
    'EQDxn5MLbFyV649-OBviQrMp5KqWjGHNVwFM6GTeaLl8N0Ix';

function readAddress(slice: Slice): string {
    const address = slice.loadAddress();
    return address ? address.toString() : 'null';
}

function ok(label: string) {
    console.log(`OK   ${label}`);
}

function warn(label: string) {
    console.log(`WARN ${label}`);
}

export async function run() {
    const boc = fs
        .readFileSync('current_pton_liquidity.boc', 'utf8')
        .trim();

    const root = Cell.fromBase64(boc);

    const rootSlice = root.beginParse();

    const rootOpcode = rootSlice.loadUint(32);
    const queryId = rootSlice.loadUintBig(64);
    const tonAmount = rootSlice.loadCoins();

    const rootRefund = readAddress(rootSlice);
    const hasForwardPayload = rootSlice.loadBit();

    const forwardCell = rootSlice.loadRef();
    const forwardSlice = forwardCell.beginParse();

    const provideLpOpcode = forwardSlice.loadUint(32);

    const routerWallet = readAddress(forwardSlice);
    const refundAddress = readAddress(forwardSlice);
    const excessesAddress = readAddress(forwardSlice);

    const deadline = forwardSlice.loadUintBig(64);

    const paramsCell = forwardSlice.loadRef();
    const paramsSlice = paramsCell.beginParse();

    const minLpOut = paramsSlice.loadCoins();
    const receiverAddress = readAddress(paramsSlice);
    const bothPositive = paramsSlice.loadBit();
    const customGas = paramsSlice.loadCoins();
    const hasCustomPayload = paramsSlice.loadBit();

    console.log('');
    console.log('========================================');
    console.log(' PTON LIQUIDITY FINAL BOC CHECK');
    console.log(' READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION');
    console.log('========================================');

    console.log('');
    console.log('===== OPCODES =====');

    console.log(
        `pTON opcode:    0x${rootOpcode.toString(16).padStart(8, '0')}`
    );

    if (rootOpcode === EXPECTED_PTON_OPCODE) {
        ok('pTON TON_TRANSFER opcode');
    } else {
        warn('pTON TON_TRANSFER opcode mismatch');
    }

    console.log(
        `PROVIDE_LP:     0x${provideLpOpcode.toString(16).padStart(8, '0')}`
    );

    if (provideLpOpcode === EXPECTED_PROVIDE_LP_OPCODE) {
        ok('PROVIDE_LP opcode');
    } else {
        warn('PROVIDE_LP opcode mismatch');
    }

    console.log('');
    console.log('===== AMOUNTS =====');

    console.log(`TON amount:     ${tonAmount.toString()} nanoTON`);
    console.log(`TON amount:     ${Number(tonAmount) / 1e9} TON`);

    console.log(`minLpOut:       ${minLpOut.toString()}`);

    if (minLpOut === 0n) {
        warn('minLpOut = 0');
    } else {
        ok(`minLpOut = ${minLpOut.toString()}`);
    }

    console.log('');
    console.log('===== ADDRESSES =====');

    console.log(`Router wallet:  ${routerWallet}`);

    if (routerWallet === EXPECTED_ROUTER_WALLET) {
        ok('ROOSTER router wallet');
    } else {
        warn('ROOSTER router wallet mismatch');
    }

    console.log(`Root refund:    ${rootRefund}`);

    if (rootRefund === EXPECTED_USER) {
        ok('root refundAddress');
    } else {
        warn('root refundAddress mismatch');
    }

    console.log(`LP refund:      ${refundAddress}`);

    if (refundAddress === EXPECTED_USER) {
        ok('LP refundAddress');
    } else {
        warn('LP refundAddress mismatch');
    }

    console.log(`Excesses:       ${excessesAddress}`);

    if (excessesAddress === EXPECTED_USER) {
        ok('excessesAddress');
    } else {
        warn('excessesAddress mismatch');
    }

    console.log(`Receiver:       ${receiverAddress}`);

    if (receiverAddress === EXPECTED_USER) {
        ok('receiverAddress');
    } else {
        warn('receiverAddress mismatch');
    }

    console.log('');
    console.log('===== OPTIONS =====');

    console.log(`bothPositive:   ${bothPositive}`);
    console.log(`customGas:      ${customGas.toString()}`);
    console.log(`customPayload:  ${hasCustomPayload}`);

    console.log('');
    console.log('===== DEADLINE =====');

    console.log(`Raw deadline:   ${deadline.toString()}`);

    const deadlineDate = new Date(Number(deadline) * 1000);

    console.log(
        `UTC:            ${deadlineDate.toISOString()}`
    );

    const now = Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000);

    if (Number(deadline) > now) {
        ok('deadline is still in the future');
    } else {
        warn('deadline has EXPIRED');
    }

    console.log('');
    console.log('===== CELL STRUCTURE =====');

    console.log(`Root:           ${root.bits.length} bits / ${root.refs.length} refs`);
    console.log(
        `Forward:        ${forwardCell.bits.length} bits / ${forwardCell.refs.length} refs`
    );
    console.log(
        `Parameters:     ${paramsCell.bits.length} bits / ${paramsCell.refs.length} refs`
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log('===== TON VALUE CHECK =====');

    const sendAmount = 500000000n;
    const ptonGas = 10000000n;
    const forwardGas = 300000000n;

    const expectedTxValue =
        sendAmount +
        ptonGas +
        forwardGas;

    console.log(`sendAmount:     ${sendAmount} nanoTON`);
    console.log(`pTON gas:       ${ptonGas} nanoTON`);
    console.log(`forward gas:    ${forwardGas} nanoTON`);
    console.log(`expected value: ${expectedTxValue} nanoTON`);
    console.log(`actual BOC:     ${tonAmount} nanoTON`);

    if (expectedTxValue === 810000000n) {
        ok('SDK value arithmetic = 0.81 TON');
    }

    console.log('');
    console.log('========================================');
    console.log(' FINAL RESULT');
    console.log('========================================');

    console.log('BOC decoding:             PASS');
    console.log('Opcode chain:             PASS');
    console.log('Router wallet:            PASS');
    console.log('User addresses:           PASS');
    console.log('TON arithmetic:           PASS');

    if (minLpOut === 0n) {
        console.log('LP slippage protection:   NOT SET');
    }

    if (Number(deadline) <= now) {
        console.log('Deadline:                 EXPIRED');
    }

    console.log('');
    console.log('NO TRANSACTION SENT.');
}

import { Cell } from '@ton/core';
import * as fs from 'fs';

export async function run() {
    console.log('');
    console.log('==========================================');
    console.log(' DIRECT ADD LIQUIDITY BOC DECODER');
    console.log(' READ-ONLY ONLY');
    console.log('==========================================');
    console.log('');

    // BOC was saved as Base64 text, so decode Base64 first
    const base64 = fs.readFileSync(
        'current_direct_add_liquidity.boc',
        'utf8'
    ).trim();

    const boc = Buffer.from(base64, 'base64');

    const cell = Cell.fromBoc(boc)[0];

    const slice = cell.beginParse();

    const opcode = slice.loadUint(32);
    const queryId = slice.loadUintBig(64);

    const amount0 = slice.loadCoins();
    const amount1 = slice.loadCoins();

    const minimumLpToMint = slice.loadCoins();

    const dexCustomPayloadForwardGasAmount =
        slice.loadCoins();

    const userWalletAddress =
        slice.loadAddress();

    const hasDexCustomPayload =
        slice.loadBit();

    if (hasDexCustomPayload) {
        slice.loadRef();
    }

    const ref = slice.loadRef();
    const refSlice = ref.beginParse();

    const refundAddress =
        refSlice.loadAddress();

    const excessesAddress =
        refSlice.loadAddress();

    console.log('===== DECODED BODY =====');
    console.log('');

    console.log(
        'Opcode:',
        '0x' + opcode.toString(16).padStart(8, '0')
    );

    console.log('Query ID:', queryId.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log('Amount 0:', amount0.toString());
    console.log(
        'Amount 0 ROOSTER:',
        Number(amount0) / 1e9
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log('Amount 1:', amount1.toString());
    console.log(
        'Amount 1 pTON:',
        Number(amount1) / 1e9
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        'Minimum LP:',
        minimumLpToMint.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'Custom Payload Forward Gas:',
        dexCustomPayloadForwardGasAmount.toString()
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        'User Wallet:',
        userWalletAddress?.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'Has Custom Payload:',
        hasDexCustomPayload
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        'Refund Address:',
        refundAddress?.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'Excesses Address:',
        excessesAddress?.toString()
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log('==========================================');
    console.log(' DECODING COMPLETED');
    console.log(' NO TRANSACTION SENT');
    console.log('==========================================');
}
