FILE: scripts\decodeElement2052.js
====================================================================================================
const fs = require("fs");

const WASM = "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const TARGET_FUNC = 8952;
const TARGET_POS  = 2052;

const buf = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("DECODE ELEMENT SEGMENT AROUND 2052");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("WASM SIZE :", buf.length);
console.log("TARGET FUNC:", TARGET_FUNC);
console.log("TARGET POS :", TARGET_POS);
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

function readU32LE(pos) {
    return (
        buf[pos] |
        (buf[pos + 1] << 8) |
        (buf[pos + 2] << 16) |
        (buf[pos + 3] << 24)
    ) >>> 0;
}

function readULEB(pos) {
    let result = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let p = pos;

    while (true) {
        const b = buf[p++];
        result += (b & 0x7f) * Math.pow(2, shift);

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) break;

        shift += 7;

        if (shift > 35) {
            throw new Error("ULEB too large at 0x" + pos.toString(16));
        }
    }

    return {
        value: result,
        next: p
    };
}

function readName(pos) {
    const r = readULEB(pos);
    const end = r.next + r.value;

    return {
        value: buf.subarray(r.next, end).toString("utf8"),
        next: end
    };
}

function sectionName(id) {
    const names = {
        1: "TYPE",
        2: "IMPORT",
        3: "FUNCTION",
        4: "TABLE",
        5: "MEMORY",
        6: "GLOBAL",
        7: "EXPORT",
        8: "START",
        9: "ELEMENT",
        10: "CODE",
        11: "DATA"
    };

    return names[id] || "UNKNOWN";
}

let p = 8;
let elementSection = null;

while (p < buf.length) {
    const sectionStart = p;

    const id = buf[p++];

    const sizeInfo = readULEB(p);
    const size = sizeInfo.value;
    p = sizeInfo.next;

    const payloadStart = p;
    const payloadEnd = p + size;

    console.log(
        `SECTION ${id} ${sectionName(id)} START 0x${sectionStart.toString(16)} SIZE ${size}`
    );

    if (id === 9) {
        elementSection = {
            sectionStart,
            payloadStart,
            payloadEnd,
            size
        };
        break;
    }

    p = payloadEnd;
}

if (!elementSection) {
    throw new Error("ELEMENT SECTION NOT FOUND");
}

console.log("");
console.log(">>> ELEMENT SECTION FOUND");
console.log(
    "PAYLOAD : 0x" +
    elementSection.payloadStart.toString(16) +
    " .. 0x" +
    elementSection.payloadEnd.toString(16)
);

let q = elementSection.payloadStart;

const countInfo = readULEB(q);
const segmentCount = countInfo.value;
q = countInfo.next;

console.log("ELEMENT COUNT :", segmentCount);
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

for (let seg = 0; seg < segmentCount; seg++) {

    const segStart = q;

    console.log("");
    console.log("ELEMENT SEGMENT", seg);
    console.log("START :", "0x" + segStart.toString(16));

    const flagInfo = readULEB(q);
    const flags = flagInfo.value;
    q = flagInfo.next;

    console.log("FLAGS :", flags, "0x" + flags.toString(16));

    let tableIndex = 0;
    let elemKind = null;
    let offsetExpr = null;

    /*
     * MVP / active element segment:
     *
     * flags 0:
     *   offset expr
     *   elemkind 0x00
     *   vec(funcidx)
     *
     * flags 1:
     *   elemkind
     *   vec(funcidx)
     *
     * flags 2:
     *   tableidx
     *   offset expr
     *   elemkind
     *   vec(funcidx)
     */

    if (flags === 0) {

        console.log("MODE  : active");
        console.log("TABLE : 0");

        const opcode = buf[q++];

        if (opcode !== 0x41) {
            console.log(
                "OFFSET EXPR FIRST OPCODE : 0x" +
                opcode.toString(16)
            );
        }

        let offsetValue = null;

        if (opcode === 0x41) {
            const oi = readULEB(q);
            offsetValue = oi.value;
            q = oi.next;

            const endOpcode = buf[q++];

            offsetExpr = {
                opcode,
                value: offsetValue,
                endOpcode
            };
        } else {
            throw new Error(
                "Unexpected offset expression opcode at 0x" +
                (q - 1).toString(16)
            );
        }

        console.log(
            "OFFSET VALUE :",
            offsetExpr.value
        );

        console.log(
            "OFFSET END   : 0x" +
            offsetExpr.endOpcode.toString(16)
        );

    } else if (flags === 1) {

        console.log("MODE  : passive");

        elemKind = buf[q++];

        console.log(
            "ELEM KIND : 0x" +
            elemKind.toString(16)
        );

    } else if (flags === 2) {

        console.log("MODE  : active + explicit table");

        const ti = readULEB(q);
        tableIndex = ti.value;
        q = ti.next;

        console.log("TABLE INDEX :", tableIndex);

        const opcode = buf[q++];

        if (opcode !== 0x41) {
            throw new Error(
                "Unexpected offset opcode 0x" +
                opcode.toString(16)
            );
        }

        const oi = readULEB(q);
        q = oi.next;

        const endOpcode = buf[q++];

        offsetExpr = {
            value: oi.value,
            endOpcode
        };

        console.log("OFFSET VALUE :", offsetExpr.value);

        elemKind = buf[q++];

        console.log(
            "ELEM KIND : 0x" +
            elemKind.toString(16)
        );

    } else {
        console.log(
            "UNHANDLED FLAGS:",
            flags,
            "0x" + flags.toString(16)
        );

        break;
    }

    const vecInfo = readULEB(q);
    const funcCount = vecInfo.value;
    q = vecInfo.next;

    console.log("FUNCTION COUNT :", funcCount);
    console.log(
        "FUNCTION VECTOR START : 0x" +
        q.toString(16)
    );

    const interestingStart = Math.max(0, TARGET_POS - 8);
    const interestingEnd = Math.min(
        funcCount - 1,
        TARGET_POS + 8
    );

    console.log("");
    console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
    console.log(
        "VECTOR ENTRIES AROUND POSITION " + TARGET_POS
    );
    console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

    let targetFound = false;

    for (let i = 0; i < funcCount; i++) {

        const fi = readULEB(q);
        const funcIdx = fi.value;
        q = fi.next;

        if (i >= interestingStart && i <= interestingEnd) {

            const tableIndexActual =
                (offsetExpr ? offsetExpr.value : 0) + i;

            console.log(
                String(i).padStart(6),
                "TABLE[" +
                String(tableIndexActual).padStart(5) +
                "]",
                "FUNC",
                String(funcIdx).padStart(5),
                "OFFSET",
                "0x" + (fi.next - 1).toString(16)
            );
        }

        if (i === TARGET_POS) {

            targetFound = true;

            console.log("");
            console.log(">>> TARGET VECTOR POSITION HIT");
            console.log("VECTOR POSITION :", i);

            console.log(
                "CALCULATED TABLE INDEX :",
                (offsetExpr ? offsetExpr.value : 0) + i
            );

            console.log("FUNCTION INDEX :", funcIdx);

            if (funcIdx === TARGET_FUNC) {
                console.log("STATUS : *** EXACT MATCH ***");
            } else {
                console.log(
                    "STATUS : MISMATCH - expected",
                    TARGET_FUNC,
                    "got",
                    funcIdx
                );
            }
        }
    }

    console.log("");
    console.log("SEGMENT END : 0x" + q.toString(16));

    if (!targetFound) {
        console.log(
            "TARGET POSITION",
            TARGET_POS,
            "IS OUTSIDE VECTOR"
        );
    }
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FINAL VERIFICATION");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(
    "ELEMENT PAYLOAD END : 0x" +
    elementSection.payloadEnd.toString(16)
);
console.log(
    "DECODER POSITION     : 0x" +
    q.toString(16)
);

if (q === elementSection.payloadEnd) {
    console.log("SECTION CONSUMPTION : EXACT");
} else {
    console.log(
        "SECTION CONSUMPTION : MISMATCH",
        "remaining =",
        elementSection.payloadEnd - q,
        "bytes"
    );
}

console.log("============================================================");

FILE: scripts\decodeElement5079V44.ts

import * as fs from "fs";

const file =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const b = fs.readFileSync(file);

const TARGET = 5079;

function uleb(p: number) {
  let v = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (true) {
    const x = b[p++];

    v += (x & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;

    if (!(x & 0x80))
      return { v, p };

    shift += 7;
  }
}

function hex(n: number) {
  return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

let p = 8;

let elementStart = -1;
let elementEnd = -1;

while (p < b.length) {
  const id = b[p++];

  const z = uleb(p);
  p = z.p;

  const start = p;
  const end = p + z.v;

  if (id === 9) {
    elementStart = start;
    elementEnd = end;
    break;
  }

  p = end;
}

if (elementStart < 0)
  throw new Error("Element section not found");

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("ELEMENT SEGMENT DECODER FOR FUNCTION 5079 v44");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

console.log(`Element section : ${hex(elementStart)} .. ${hex(elementEnd)}`);
console.log(`Target function : ${TARGET}`);
console.log("");

let q = elementStart;

const segmentCount = uleb(q);
q = segmentCount.p;

console.log(`Element segments : ${segmentCount.v}`);
console.log("");

let found = 0;

for (let seg = 0; seg < segmentCount.v; seg++) {

  const segStart = q;

  const flagsR = uleb(q);
  const flags = flagsR.v;
  q = flagsR.p;

  console.log(`SEGMENT #${seg}`);
  console.log(`  start : ${hex(segStart)}`);
  console.log(`  flags : ${flags}`);

  /*
     Handle common active function-table forms.

     flags 0:
       offset expr + vec(funcidx)

     flags 2:
       table index + offset expr + vec(funcidx)

     Other forms are reported but not decoded as function-index vectors.
  */

  if (flags === 0 || flags === 2) {

    if (flags === 2) {
      const tableR = uleb(q);
      q = tableR.p;
      console.log(`  table : ${tableR.v}`);
    }

    // offset expression
    const opcode = b[q++];

    console.log(
      `  offset expr opcode : ${hex(opcode)}`
    );

    // i32.const
    if (opcode === 0x41) {
      const off = uleb(q);
      q = off.p;
      console.log(`  offset : ${off.v}`);
    } else {
      console.log("  offset : non-i32.const expression");
    }

    // end
    if (b[q] === 0x0b)
      q++;

    const nR = uleb(q);
    const n = nR.v;
    q = nR.p;

    console.log(`  function count : ${n}`);

    for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {

      const pos = q;

      const fR = uleb(q);
      const f = fR.v;
      q = fR.p;

      if (f === TARGET) {

        found++;

        console.log("");
        console.log("  >>> TARGET FOUND <<<");
        console.log(`  function index : ${f}`);
        console.log(`  element index  : ${i}`);
        console.log(`  byte offset    : ${hex(pos)}`);
        console.log(`  segment        : ${seg}`);
        console.log(
          `  table slot     : ${i}`
        );
        console.log("");
      }
    }

  } else {

    console.log(
      "  unsupported element encoding; skipping segment payload"
    );

    /*
       For unsupported encodings we cannot safely advance without
       implementing that exact encoding, so stop rather than guess.
    */

    break;
  }

  console.log("");
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`TOTAL TARGET OCCURRENCES : ${found}`);
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("END V44");
console.log("============================================================");

const fs = require("fs");

const WASM =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const TARGET = 8952;

const buf = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("EXACT ELEMENT -> TABLE SLOT FOR FUNC 8952");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("WASM SIZE :", buf.length);
console.log("TARGET    :", TARGET);
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

function readULEB(pos) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = buf[pos++];

        value += (b & 0x7f) * Math.pow(2, shift);

        if (!(b & 0x80)) {
            return { value, next: pos };
        }

        shift += 7;
    }
}

function readSLEB(pos) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let byte;

    while (true) {
        byte = buf[pos++];

        value |= (byte & 0x7f) << shift;
        shift += 7;

        if (!(byte & 0x80)) break;
    }

    if (shift < 32 && (byte & 0x40)) {
        value |= (~0 << shift);
    }

    return {
        value: value | 0,
        next: pos
    };
}

function hex(v) {
    return "0x" + v.toString(16).padStart(6, "0");
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Locate Element Section
// ------------------------------------------------------------

let pos = 8;

let elementStart = -1;
let elementPayload = -1;
let elementSize = -1;

while (pos < buf.length) {

    const sectionStart = pos;

    const sectionType = buf[pos++];

    const sizeInfo = readULEB(pos);
    const size = sizeInfo.value;
    pos = sizeInfo.next;

    const payloadStart = pos;
    const payloadEnd = payloadStart + size;

    if (sectionType === 9) {
        elementStart = sectionStart;
        elementPayload = payloadStart;
        elementSize = size;
        break;
    }

    pos = payloadEnd;
}

if (elementStart < 0) {
    throw new Error("ELEMENT SECTION NOT FOUND");
}

console.log("ELEMENT SECTION START :", hex(elementStart));
console.log("PAYLOAD START         :", hex(elementPayload));
console.log("SIZE                  :", elementSize);
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

pos = elementPayload;

// segment count
let r = readULEB(pos);
const segmentCount = r.value;
pos = r.next;

console.log("SEGMENT COUNT :", segmentCount);
console.log();

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Decode segments
// ------------------------------------------------------------

for (let seg = 0; seg < segmentCount; seg++) {

    const segStart = pos;

    r = readULEB(pos);
    const flags = r.value;
    pos = r.next;

    console.log(
        `SEGMENT ${seg} START ${hex(segStart)} FLAGS ${flags}`
    );

    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // flags = 0
    //
    // active segment
    // implicit table 0
    // offset expression
    // vector of function indices
    // --------------------------------------------------------

    if (flags === 0) {

        console.log("  MODE : active / table 0");

        // offset expression
        const opcode = buf[pos++];

        console.log(
            "  OFFSET OPCODE :",
            "0x" + opcode.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")
        );

        let offsetValue = null;

        if (opcode === 0x41) {
            r = readSLEB(pos);
            offsetValue = r.value;
            pos = r.next;

            console.log("  OFFSET VALUE  :", offsetValue);
        }
        else if (opcode === 0x42) {
            // i64.const
            r = readSLEB(pos);
            offsetValue = r.value;
            pos = r.next;

            console.log("  OFFSET VALUE  :", offsetValue);
        }
        else if (opcode === 0x23) {
            // global.get
            r = readULEB(pos);
            const globalIndex = r.value;
            pos = r.next;

            console.log("  OFFSET GLOBAL :", globalIndex);
        }
        else {
            console.log(
                "  UNKNOWN OFFSET OPCODE"
            );
        }

        // end opcode
        if (buf[pos] === 0x0b) {
            pos++;
        }

        // vector count
        r = readULEB(pos);
        const count = r.value;
        pos = r.next;

        console.log("  FUNCTION COUNT :", count);
        console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

        let hits = 0;

        for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {

            const funcPos = pos;

            r = readULEB(pos);
            const funcIndex = r.value;
            pos = r.next;

            if (funcIndex === TARGET) {

                hits++;

                console.log("******** EXACT TARGET HIT ********");
                console.log("SEGMENT       :", seg);
                console.log("ELEMENT INDEX :", i);
                console.log("TABLE SLOT    :", offsetValue + i);
                console.log("FUNC INDEX    :", funcIndex);
                console.log("BYTE OFFSET   :", hex(funcPos));

                const a = Math.max(elementPayload, funcPos - 16);
                const b = Math.min(pos + 16, elementPayload + elementSize);

                console.log(
                    "CONTEXT BYTES :",
                    [...buf.subarray(a, b)]
                        .map(x => x.toString(16).padStart(2, "0"))
                        .join(" ")
                );

                console.log("**********************************");
                console.log();
            }
        }

        console.log(
            "SEGMENT DECODE END :",
            hex(pos)
        );

        console.log(
            "TARGET HITS        :",
            hits
        );

    } else {

        console.log(
            "UNSUPPORTED FLAGS FOR THIS PASS:",
            flags
        );

        break;
    }

    console.log("============================================================");
}

console.log("DONE");
console.log("============================================================");

FILE: scripts\decodeElementTable.ts

import * as fs from "fs";

const WASM =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

function readULEB(
    buf: Buffer,
    p: number
): { value: number; next: number } {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = buf[p++];

        value += (b & 0x7f) * Math.pow(2, shift);

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return { value, next: p };
        }

        shift += 7;

        if (shift > 35) {
            throw new Error("ULEB overflow");
        }
    }
}

function hex(n: number): string {
    return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function parseSections(buf: Buffer) {
    const sections: any[] = [];

    let p = 8;

    while (p < buf.length) {
        const id = buf[p++];

        const size = readULEB(buf, p);
        p = size.next;

        const payloadStart = p;
        const payloadEnd = p + size.value;

        sections.push({
            id,
            payloadStart,
            payloadEnd,
            size: size.value
        });

        p = payloadEnd;
    }

    return sections;
}

export async function run() {
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(" ELEMENT → FUNCTION TABLE DECODER");
    console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
    console.log("============================================================");

    const buf = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

    const sections = parseSections(buf);

    const sec = sections.find((x) => x.id === 9);

    if (!sec) {
        throw new Error("Element section not found");
    }

    let p = sec.payloadStart;

    const segmentCount = readULEB(buf, p);
    p = segmentCount.next;

    console.log("");
    console.log(
        "Element segments :",
        segmentCount.value
    );

    const flags = readULEB(buf, p);
    p = flags.next;

    console.log(
        "Flags            :",
        flags.value
    );

    if (flags.value !== 0) {
        throw new Error(
            `Expected flags=0, got ${flags.value}`
        );
    }

    /*
     * offset expression
     */

    const opcode = buf[p++];

    console.log(
        "Offset opcode    :",
        hex(opcode)
    );

    if (opcode !== 0x41) {
        throw new Error(
            `Expected i32.const, got ${hex(opcode)}`
        );
    }

    const offsetInfo = readULEB(buf, p);
    p = offsetInfo.next;

    const endOpcode = buf[p++];

    if (endOpcode !== 0x0b) {
        throw new Error(
            `Expected end opcode, got ${hex(endOpcode)}`
        );
    }

    const tableOffset = offsetInfo.value;

    console.log(
        "Table offset     :",
        tableOffset
    );

    /*
     * IMPORTANT:
     *
     * flags=0 modern encoding:
     *
     *   offset expression
     *   vec(funcidx)
     *
     * There is NO elemkind byte here.
     */

    const countInfo = readULEB(buf, p);
    p = countInfo.next;

    const count = countInfo.value;

    console.log(
        "Function count   :",
        count
    );

    const table = new Map<number, number>();

    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
        const fnInfo = readULEB(buf, p);
        p = fnInfo.next;

        const tableIndex = tableOffset + i;

        table.set(tableIndex, fnInfo.value);
    }

    console.log("");
    console.log("Final element cursor :", hex(p));
    console.log("Expected section end :", hex(sec.payloadEnd));

    if (p !== sec.payloadEnd) {
        console.log(
            "WARNING: cursor != section end, remaining =",
            sec.payloadEnd - p
        );
    }

    console.log("");
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(" TARGET TABLE LOOKUP");
    console.log("============================================================");

   const targets = [
    0x0A6E,
    0x0CDD,
    0x2163,
    0x21D2,
    0x12B4, // 4788
    0x12B6  // 4790
];

    for (const idx of targets) {
        const fn = table.get(idx);

        if (fn === undefined) {
            console.log(
                `table[${hex(idx)}] -> NOT PRESENT`
            );
        } else {
            console.log(
                `table[${hex(idx)}] -> function ${fn}`
            );
        }
    }

    console.log("");
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(" SEGMENT 413 CANDIDATE RANGE");
    console.log("============================================================");

    let hits = 0;

    for (let idx = 0; idx < count; idx++) {
        const tableIndex = tableOffset + idx;
        const fn = table.get(tableIndex)!;

        if (fn >= 4057 && fn <= 4188) {
            console.log(
                `table[${hex(tableIndex)}] -> function ${fn}`
            );

            hits++;
        }
    }

    console.log("");
    console.log(
        "Candidate function mappings :",
        hits
    );

    console.log("");
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(" TABLE WINDOW 0x80..0xA0");
    console.log("============================================================");

    for (
        let idx = 0x80;
        idx <= 0xa0;
        idx++
    ) {
        const fn = table.get(idx);

        if (fn === undefined) {
            console.log(
                `table[${hex(idx)}] -> undefined`
            );
        } else {
            console.log(
                `table[${hex(idx)}] -> function ${fn}`
            );
        }
    }

    console.log("");
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(" TABLE WINDOW 0xFC0..0xFDF");
    console.log("============================================================");

    for (
        let idx = 0xfc0;
        idx <= 0xfdf;
        idx++
    ) {
        const fn = table.get(idx);

        if (fn === undefined) {
            console.log(
                `table[${hex(idx)}] -> undefined`
            );
        } else {
            console.log(
                `table[${hex(idx)}] -> function ${fn}`
            );
        }
    }

    console.log("");
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(" END");
    console.log("============================================================");
}
run().catch((error) => {
    console.error(error);
    process.exitCode = 1;
});

import { Address, Cell, TonClient } from '@ton/ton';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

const POOL = Address.parse(
    'EQD5ALaCuEqJ7Mi9qlXOA7BHMrTq8e2fZ5B_teWN17MZq-cf'
);

const TARGET_LT = 99491281000009n;

const PROVIDE_LP = 0x37c096df;
const ADD_LIQUIDITY = 0x50c6a654;
const JETTON_INTERNAL_TRANSFER = 0x178d4519;

function opHex(op: number): string {
    return '0x' + op.toString(16).padStart(8, '0');
}

function fmt(value: bigint): string {
    return value.toString().replace(/\B(?=(\d{3})+(?!\d))/g, ',');
}

function rooster(value: bigint): string {
    const whole = value / 1_000_000_000n;
    const fraction = (value % 1_000_000_000n)
        .toString()
        .padStart(9, '0')
        .replace(/0+$/, '');

    return fraction
        ? `${whole.toLocaleString()}.${fraction}`
        : whole.toLocaleString();
}

function ton(value: bigint): string {
    const whole = value / 1_000_000_000n;
    const fraction = (value % 1_000_000_000n)
        .toString()
        .padStart(9, '0')
        .replace(/0+$/, '');

    return fraction
        ? `${whole.toLocaleString()}.${fraction}`
        : whole.toLocaleString();
}

function opcode(cell: Cell): number | null {
    try {
        const s = cell.beginParse();

        if (s.remainingBits < 32) {
            return null;
        }

        return s.loadUint(32);
    } catch {
        return null;
    }
}

function printAddress(label: string, address: Address | null | undefined) {
    console.log('');
    console.log(`${label}:`);

    if (!address) {
        console.log('NULL');
    } else {
        console.log(address.toString());
    }
}

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {
    console.log('');
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log(' EXACT STON.fi v2.2 POOL LIQUIDITY DECODER');
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log('READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT');
    console.log('============================================================');

    console.log('');
    console.log('POOL:');
    console.log(POOL.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log('TARGET LT:');
    console.log(TARGET_LT.toString());

    const client = new TonClient({
        endpoint: 'https://testnet.toncenter.com/api/v2/jsonRPC',
    });

    console.log('');
    console.log('Fetching Pool transactions...');

    const transactions = await client.getTransactions(POOL, {
        limit: 100,
    });

    console.log(`Fetched ${transactions.length} transactions.`);

    const tx = transactions.find(
        (item) => BigInt(item.lt) === TARGET_LT
    );

    if (!tx) {
        console.log('');
        console.log('TARGET TRANSACTION NOT FOUND');
        console.log('READ ONLY CHECK COMPLETE');
        return;
    }

    console.log('');
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log(' TARGET TRANSACTION');
    console.log('============================================================');

    console.log('');
    console.log('LT:');
    console.log(tx.lt.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log('HASH:');
    console.log(tx.hash().toString('base64'));

    console.log('');
    console.log('TIMESTAMP:');
    console.log(tx.now.toString());

    const inMessage = tx.inMessage;

    if (!inMessage) {
        console.log('');
        console.log('NO INPUT MESSAGE');
        return;
    }

    console.log('');
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log(' INPUT MESSAGE');
    console.log('============================================================');

    if (inMessage.info.type === 'internal') {
        printAddress('SOURCE', inMessage.info.src);
        printAddress('DESTINATION', inMessage.info.dest);

        console.log('');
        console.log('ATTACHED TON:');
        console.log(ton(inMessage.info.value.coins));
    }

    const inBody = inMessage.body;
    const inOp = opcode(inBody);

    console.log('');
    console.log('INPUT OPCODE:');
    console.log(inOp === null ? 'UNKNOWN' : opHex(inOp));

    console.log('');
    console.log('INPUT BODY:');
    console.log(
        `${inBody.bits.length} bits / ${inBody.refs.length} refs`
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log('INPUT BOC:');
    console.log(
        inBody.toBoc().toString('base64')
    );

    /*
     * ==========================================================
     * PROVIDE_LP
     * ==========================================================
     */

    if (inOp === PROVIDE_LP) {
        console.log('');
        console.log('============================================================');
        console.log(' PROVIDE_LP EXACT FIELD DECODING');
        console.log('============================================================');

        try {
            const s = inBody.beginParse();

            const op = s.loadUint(32);

            console.log('');
            console.log('OPCODE:');
            console.log(opHex(op));

            const queryId = s.loadUintBig(64);

            console.log('');
            console.log('QUERY ID:');
            console.log(queryId.toString());

            const fromUser = s.loadAddress();

            printAddress('FROM USER', fromUser);

            /*
             * These are coins encoded with TON's variable-length
             * coins representation.
             */
            const leftAmount = s.loadCoins();
            const rightAmount = s.loadCoins();

            console.log('');
            console.log('LEFT AMOUNT RAW:');
            console.log(fmt(leftAmount));

            console.log('');
            console.log('RIGHT AMOUNT RAW:');
            console.log(fmt(rightAmount));

            console.log('');
            console.log('LEFT AMOUNT AS 9-DECIMAL TOKEN:');
            console.log(rooster(leftAmount));

            console.log('');
            console.log('RIGHT AMOUNT AS 9-DECIMAL TOKEN:');
            console.log(rooster(rightAmount));

            console.log('');
            console.log('REMAINING BITS:');
            console.log(s.remainingBits);

            console.log('');
            console.log('REMAINING REFS:');
            console.log(s.remainingRefs);

            /*
             * If the next field is an address, inspect it.
             */
            if (s.remainingBits >= 2) {
                try {
                    const tokenWallet1 = s.loadAddress();

                    printAddress(
                        'TOKEN WALLET 1',
                        tokenWallet1
                    );
                } catch (e) {
                    console.log('');
                    console.log('TOKEN WALLET 1 DECODE WARNING:');
                    console.log(String(e));
                }
            }

            console.log('');
            console.log('REMAINING BITS AFTER TOKEN WALLET 1:');
            console.log(s.remainingBits);

            console.log('');
            console.log('REMAINING REFS AFTER TOKEN WALLET 1:');
            console.log(s.remainingRefs);

        } catch (e) {
            console.log('');
            console.log('PROVIDE_LP EXACT DECODER ERROR:');
            console.log(String(e));
        }
    }

    /*
     * ==========================================================
     * OUTGOING MESSAGES
     * ==========================================================
     */

    console.log('');
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log(' OUTGOING MESSAGE DECODING');
    console.log('============================================================');

    let index = 0;

    for (const message of tx.outMessages.values()) {
        console.log('');
        console.log(`================ OUT #${index} =================`);

        if (message.info.type === 'internal') {
            printAddress('SOURCE', message.info.src);
            printAddress('DESTINATION', message.info.dest);

            console.log('');
            console.log('ATTACHED TON:');
            console.log(
                ton(message.info.value.coins)
            );
        }

        const body = message.body;
        const op = opcode(body);

        console.log('');
        console.log('OPCODE:');
        console.log(
            op === null ? 'UNKNOWN' : opHex(op)
        );

        console.log('');
        console.log('BODY:');
        console.log(
            `${body.bits.length} bits / ${body.refs.length} refs`
        );

        console.log('');
        console.log('BOC:');
        console.log(
            body.toBoc().toString('base64')
        );

        /*
         * ======================================================
         * ADD_LIQUIDITY
         * ======================================================
         */

        if (op === ADD_LIQUIDITY) {
            console.log('');
            console.log('============================================================');
            console.log(' ADD_LIQUIDITY EXACT FIELD DECODING');
            console.log('============================================================');

            try {
                const s = body.beginParse();

                const addOp = s.loadUint(32);

                console.log('');
                console.log('OPCODE:');
                console.log(opHex(addOp));

                const queryId = s.loadUintBig(64);

                console.log('');
                console.log('QUERY ID:');
                console.log(queryId.toString());

                console.log('');
                console.log('REMAINING BITS BEFORE AMOUNTS:');
                console.log(s.remainingBits);

                console.log('');
                console.log('REMAINING REFS BEFORE AMOUNTS:');
                console.log(s.remainingRefs);

                /*
                 * Try the amount fields.
                 */
                const amount0 = s.loadCoins();
                const amount1 = s.loadCoins();

                console.log('');
                console.log('AMOUNT 0 RAW:');
                console.log(fmt(amount0));

                console.log('');
                console.log('AMOUNT 1 RAW:');
                console.log(fmt(amount1));

                console.log('');
                console.log('AMOUNT 0 AS 9-DECIMAL:');
                console.log(rooster(amount0));

                console.log('');
                console.log('AMOUNT 1 AS 9-DECIMAL:');
                console.log(rooster(amount1));

                console.log('');
                console.log('REMAINING BITS:');
                console.log(s.remainingBits);

                console.log('');
                console.log('REMAINING REFS:');
                console.log(s.remainingRefs);

            } catch (e) {
                console.log('');
                console.log('ADD_LIQUIDITY EXACT DECODER ERROR:');
                console.log(String(e));
            }
        }

        /*
         * ======================================================
         * JETTON INTERNAL TRANSFER
         * ======================================================
         */

        if (op === JETTON_INTERNAL_TRANSFER) {
            console.log('');
            console.log('============================================================');
            console.log(' JETTON INTERNAL TRANSFER');
            console.log('============================================================');

            try {
                const s = body.beginParse();

                s.loadUint(32);

                if (s.remainingBits >= 64) {
                    const queryId = s.loadUintBig(64);

                    console.log('');
                    console.log('QUERY ID:');
                    console.log(queryId.toString());
                }

                if (s.remainingBits >= 16) {
                    const amount = s.loadCoins();

                    console.log('');
                    console.log('AMOUNT RAW:');
                    console.log(fmt(amount));

                    console.log('');
                    console.log('AMOUNT AS 9-DECIMAL:');
                    console.log(rooster(amount));
                }

                console.log('');
                console.log('REMAINING BITS:');
                console.log(s.remainingBits);

                console.log('');
                console.log('REMAINING REFS:');
                console.log(s.remainingRefs);

            } catch (e) {
                console.log('');
                console.log('JETTON TRANSFER DECODER ERROR:');
                console.log(String(e));
            }
        }

        index++;
    }

    console.log('');
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log(' FINAL STATUS');
    console.log('============================================================');

    console.log('');
    console.log('Target transaction found: YES');

    console.log('');
    console.log('Transaction submission: NONE');
    console.log('ROOSTER transaction sent by this script: NO');
    console.log('pTON transaction sent by this script: NO');
    console.log('Pool transaction sent by this script: NO');

    console.log('');
    console.log('READ ONLY CHECK COMPLETE');
    console.log('============================================================');
}

FILE: scripts\decodeExactPoolTxV22.ts
====================================================================================================
import { Cell } from '@ton/core';

const RPC = 'https://104.26.1.179/api/v2/jsonRPC';

const POOL =
    'EQD5ALaCuEqJ7Mi9qlXOA7BHMrTq8e2fZ5B_teWN17MZq-cf';

const TARGET_LT = 99491281000009;

async function rpc(method: string, params: any) {
    const controller = new AbortController();
    const timer = setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), 15000);

    try {
        const response = await fetch(RPC, {
            method: 'POST',
            headers: {
                'Content-Type': 'application/json',
                'Host': 'testnet.toncenter.com',
            },
            body: JSON.stringify({
                jsonrpc: '2.0',
                id: 1,
                method,
                params,
            }),
            signal: controller.signal,
        });

        const text = await response.text();

        if (!response.ok) {
            throw new Error(
                `HTTP ${response.status}: ${text}`
            );
        }

        const json = JSON.parse(text);

        if (json.error) {
            throw new Error(
                `RPC ERROR: ${JSON.stringify(json.error)}`
            );
        }

        return json.result;
    } finally {
        clearTimeout(timer);
    }
}

function addr(msg: any): string {
    return (
        msg?.address ||
        msg?.destination ||
        msg?.source ||
        '<none>'
    );
}

function decodeBody(body?: string) {
    if (!body) {
        console.log('BODY: NONE');
        return;
    }

    console.log('BODY BOC:');
    console.log(body);

    try {
        const cell = Cell.fromBase64(body);
        const slice = cell.beginParse();

        console.log(
            `BODY ROOT: bits=${cell.bits.length} refs=${cell.refs.length}`
        );

        if (slice.remainingBits >= 32) {
            const opcode = slice.loadUint(32);

            console.log(
                `OPCODE: 0x${opcode.toString(16).padStart(8, '0')}`
            );

            console.log(
                `OPCODE DECIMAL: ${opcode}`
            );
        }

        console.log(
            `ROOT REMAINING BITS: ${slice.remainingBits}`
        );
        console.log(
            `ROOT REMAINING REFS: ${slice.remainingRefs}`
        );
    } catch (e) {
        console.log(
            'BODY DECODE ERROR:',
            e instanceof Error ? e.message : e
        );
    }
}

async function main() {
    console.log('================================================');
    console.log(' STON.fi v2.2 EXACT POOL TRANSACTION DECODER');
    console.log(' READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT');
    console.log('================================================');
    console.log('');
    console.log('POOL:');
    console.log(POOL);
    console.log('');
    console.log('TARGET LT:');
    console.log(TARGET_LT);
    console.log('');
    console.log('RPC:');
    console.log(RPC);
    console.log('');

    const result = await rpc('getTransactions', {
        address: POOL,
        limit: 1,
        lt: String(TARGET_LT),
    });

    console.log('TRANSACTION RESULT RECEIVED');
    console.log('');

    if (!result || result.length === 0) {
        console.log('NO TRANSACTION FOUND');
        return;
    }

    const tx = result[0];

    console.log('===== TRANSACTION =====');

    console.log('LT:', tx.lt);
    console.log('HASH:', tx.hash);
    console.log('UTIME:', tx.utime);

    if (tx.utime) {
        console.log(
            'DATE:',
            new Date(Number(tx.utime) * 1000).toISOString()
        );
    }

    console.log('');
    console.log('===== IN MESSAGE =====');

    const inMsg = tx.in_msg;

    if (!inMsg) {
        console.log('NO IN MESSAGE');
    } else {
        console.log('SOURCE:', inMsg.source);
        console.log('DESTINATION:', inMsg.destination);
        console.log('VALUE:', inMsg.value);
        console.log(
            'VALUE TON:',
            Number(inMsg.value || 0) / 1e9
        );

        decodeBody(inMsg.message_content?.body);
    }

    console.log('');
    console.log('===== OUT MESSAGES =====');

    const outMsgs = tx.out_msgs || [];

    console.log('COUNT:', outMsgs.length);

    for (let i = 0; i < outMsgs.length; i++) {
        const msg = outMsgs[i];

        console.log('');
        console.log(`--- OUT MESSAGE ${i + 1} ---`);

        console.log('SOURCE:', msg.source);
        console.log('DESTINATION:', msg.destination);
        console.log('VALUE:', msg.value);
        console.log(
            'VALUE TON:',
            Number(msg.value || 0) / 1e9
        );

        decodeBody(msg.message_content?.body);
    }

    console.log('');
    console.log('================================================');
    console.log(' END - READ ONLY');
    console.log('================================================');
}

main().catch((e) => {
    console.log('');
    console.log('ERROR:');

    if (e instanceof Error) {
        console.log(e.stack || e.message);
    } else {
        console.log(e);
    }
});

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\decodeExactSdkBodyV22.ts
====================================================================================================
import { Address, Cell } from "@ton/core";
import * as fs from "fs";

const BOC_FILE = "./sdk_generated_v22_rooster_liquidity.boc";

function printCell(label: string, cell: Cell) {
  console.log(`\n===== ${label} =====`);
  console.log("bits:", cell.bits.length);
  console.log("refs:", cell.refs.length);
  console.log("hash:", cell.hash().toString("hex"));
}

function formatAddress(address: Address | null) {
  return address ? address.toString() : "null";
}

export async function run() {
  console.log("=================================================");
  console.log(" STON.fi v2.2 EXACT SDK BODY DECODER");
  console.log(" READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT");
  console.log("=================================================");

  if (!fs.existsSync(BOC_FILE)) {
    throw new Error(`BOC file not found: ${BOC_FILE}`);
  }

  const base64 = fs.readFileSync(BOC_FILE, "utf8").trim();

  const body = Cell.fromBoc(
    Buffer.from(base64, "base64")
  )[0];

  printCell("ROOT BODY", body);

  const root = body.beginParse();

  console.log("\n===== JETTON TRANSFER =====");

  const opcode = root.loadUint(32);
  const queryId = root.loadUintBig(64);
  const amount = root.loadCoins();

  const destination = root.loadAddress();
  const responseDestination = root.loadAddress();

  const hasCustomPayload = root.loadBit();

  let customPayload: Cell | null = null;

  if (hasCustomPayload) {
    customPayload = root.loadRef();
  }

  const forwardTonAmount = root.loadCoins();

  const hasForwardPayload = root.loadBit();

  let forwardPayload: Cell | null = null;

  if (hasForwardPayload) {
    forwardPayload = root.loadRef();
  } else {
    throw new Error("SDK body has no forward payload.");
  }

  console.log("opcode:", "0x" + opcode.toString(16));
  console.log("queryId:", queryId.toString());
  console.log("amount:", amount.toString());

  console.log(
    "destination:",
    formatAddress(destination)
  );

  console.log(
    "responseDestination:",
    formatAddress(responseDestination)
  );

  console.log(
    "customPayload:",
    customPayload ? "REFERENCE" : "none"
  );

  console.log(
    "forwardTonAmount:",
    forwardTonAmount.toString()
  );

  console.log(
    "forwardPayload:",
    "REFERENCE"
  );

  console.log("\n===== FORWARD PAYLOAD =====");

  printCell("PROVIDE_LP ROOT", forwardPayload);

  const provide = forwardPayload.beginParse();

  const provideOpcode = provide.loadUint(32);

  console.log(
    "PROVIDE_LP opcode:",
    "0x" + provideOpcode.toString(16)
  );

  const routerWallet = provide.loadAddress();
  const refundAddress = provide.loadAddress();
  const excessesAddress = provide.loadAddress();

  const deadline = provide.loadUintBig(64);

  console.log(
    "routerWalletAddress:",
    formatAddress(routerWallet)
  );

  console.log(
    "refundAddress:",
    formatAddress(refundAddress)
  );

  console.log(
    "excessesAddress:",
    formatAddress(excessesAddress)
  );

  console.log(
    "deadline:",
    deadline.toString()
  );

  const nested = provide.loadRef();

  printCell("NESTED PROVIDE_LP PARAMETERS", nested);

  const nestedSlice = nested.beginParse();

  const minLpOut = nestedSlice.loadCoins();
  const receiverAddress = nestedSlice.loadAddress();
  const bothPositive = nestedSlice.loadUint(1);

  const dexCustomPayloadForwardGasAmount =
    nestedSlice.loadCoins();

  const hasDexCustomPayload =
    nestedSlice.loadBit();

  let dexCustomPayload: Cell | null = null;

  if (hasDexCustomPayload) {
    dexCustomPayload = nestedSlice.loadRef();
  }

  console.log("\n===== PROVIDE_LP PARAMETERS =====");

  console.log(
    "minLpOut:",
    minLpOut.toString()
  );

  console.log(
    "receiverAddress:",
    formatAddress(receiverAddress)
  );

  console.log(
    "bothPositive:",
    bothPositive
  );

  console.log(
    "dexCustomPayloadForwardGasAmount:",
    dexCustomPayloadForwardGasAmount.toString()
  );

  console.log(
    "dexCustomPayload:",
    dexCustomPayload ? "REFERENCE" : "none"
  );

  console.log("\n===== REMAINING BITS / REFS =====");

  console.log(
    "forward payload remaining bits:",
    provide.remainingBits
  );

  console.log(
    "forward payload remaining refs:",
    provide.remainingRefs
  );

  console.log(
    "nested remaining bits:",
    nestedSlice.remainingBits
  );

  console.log(
    "nested remaining refs:",
    nestedSlice.remainingRefs
  );

  
  console.log("\n===== FINAL SDK VALUES =====");

  console.log(
    "ROOSTER amount:",
    amount.toString()
  );

  console.log(
    "minLpOut:",
    minLpOut.toString()
  );

  console.log(
    "routerWalletAddress:",
    routerWallet.toString()
  );

  console.log(
    "refundAddress:",
    refundAddress.toString()
  );

  console.log(
    "excessesAddress:",
    excessesAddress.toString()
  );

  console.log(
    "receiverAddress:",
    receiverAddress.toString()
  );

  console.log(
    "deadline:",
    deadline.toString()
  );

  console.log(
    "forwardTonAmount:",
    forwardTonAmount.toString()
  );

  console.log(
    "bothPositive:",
    bothPositive
  );

  console.log(
    "dexCustomPayloadForwardGasAmount:",
    dexCustomPayloadForwardGasAmount.toString()
  );

  console.log(
    "dexCustomPayload:",
    dexCustomPayload ? "REFERENCE" : "none"
  );

  console.log("\n=================================================");
  console.log(" DECODING COMPLETE");
  console.log(" NO TRANSACTION WAS SENT");
  console.log("=================================================");
}
